**T-051 (cellar half), 2026-10-22: Map 0's Old Cellar (Update 02 §6.1).** The bigger cellar is the inn's own cellar under the taproom (zone `OLD_CELLAR`), reached by Door 5 (1250) in the taproom's west wall down a stone lean-to stair; the Kar98k wall-buy moved along that wall to z 3.1. Rooms: wine cellar (drips), coal room with Jakub's cage (`HoldingCage`, `JakubSpawn`, `JakubWait`, `CoalShovel`), root cellar with the cousins' jam jars, the cousins' ice cellar right under Alix's trapdoor (bricked off until HB1 files INT_HB_01, then walkable so it is never a safe camp; replaces v3's ladder-only room), and a barrel vault with the training loop (corridors 4.9/3.2/4.4/3.2 m). It connects to the Helvetia perk cellar through the installers' breach, which falls in by itself once both Door 3 and Door 5 are bought; Map 0 is baked twice (breach shut / open) and the breach swaps the navmesh, because a NavigationLink3D made zombies dither. Two light-well windows with half-height crawl-ins and a 1.9 m drop (`Barricade.inside_drop`). Wall-buy: Gewehr 43 (the Garand and Thompson are already on the map). One ammo box (Map 0 now has 3). No perk machines (all 9 are in the Helvetia cellar). Hidden nav blocks are capped at 1.6 m (the bake left walkable islands inside taller blocks). The baseline "stuck" reports were mostly the playtest bot's own waypoints; fixed in `playtest_gl.gd`.

**T-052, 2026-10-22: Map 0's living world (Update 02 §7).** The land, sky and war beyond the play space are built at load from code by two nodes `gen_map00`'s `build_landscape()` adds — `Landscape` (three terrain layers, MultiMesh spruces with impostors, roads with wrecks, telephone line, frozen stream and bridge, farmhouse, church, crash-site detail) and `MapAmbience` (bombers, fighters, the once-a-match plane down from round 6, searchlights, artillery with sound delayed 2.9 s/km of virtual distance, flak, flares, the burning-town glow, gusts, crows, the dog that stops at round 10, the midnight bell at round 9, chimney smoke that dies with the hearth in the Cousins round, breath fog, frost, the farmhouse going dark at round 8, ammo cook-off, Dutch's empty-pilot-seat line), tuned from `map00_ambience.json`. The tscn stays small and the navmesh is untouched; everything fits inside the 400 m far plane; the far layers use a capped custom fog so the map's own fog is unchanged; the war escalates up to round 15 and sits to the east (the lore's front). `Props.lantern` now swings (`LampSway`). Reaction lines are tier-2 squad VO (VO_SCRIPT §14). Presentation only, local to each co-op peer. Cost: ~0.6–1.0 s more load; ~184 FPS looking out the east window at 24 zombies (vs 220 without).

**T-054, 2026-10-22: bot metrics and the AI soak (Update 03 §0/§9).** The bots are measured from outside the brain: a `BotMetrics` node reads GLBus, the co-op state and the Players, so any brain is scored the same way; only stuck time, stuck recoveries and teleports come from brain hooks (no-ops without a logger). The soak is `tools_src/ai_soak.tscn` (wrapper `tools/ai_soak.sh`; Godot tools live in tools_src), headless at `--fixed-fps 60` (~6× real time; `Engine.time_scale` gains nothing there), local AI off, logs to git-ignored `logs/ai_runs/`. The scripted player is the real Player on a fixed Map 0 training loop (buys nothing, carries the bot kit, free reserve); `bots3` benches the human as an uncounted spectator. Baseline (pre-Update 03): highest round 9 with the scripted player / 6 bots alone, revive success 83 % / 0 %, 0 teleports, ~35 % of bot time stuck on the pistol (the "pistol trap"); full table in `docs/BOT_AI.md`.

**T-053, 2026-10-22: Map 0's small-detail pass (Update 02 §8).** Checklist in `docs/MAP0_POLISH.md`. The pass is dressing plus generic reactive components (`gravelight/world/reactive/`: `DecorHit`, `ShatterSet` — 79 bottles and glasses —, lamp hit volumes through `LampSway`, `DraftCurtain`, `SwingDoor`, `HayPuffZone`, `SnowFootprints` — one pooled MultiMesh, 90 s fade —, `AudioZone`), never collision. Reactive props carry hit volumes on a new physics layer 10 "decor" that only the player's shot ray sees; shots pass through without using penetration, and the reaction is local to the shooter's machine in co-op (bot shots use their own mask and don't break glass). The bar's back-shelf collider is tagged `shot_through` so shots reach the bottles; it stays solid for movement. Bullet holes persist only on plain static world geometry, pooled at 96 per surface (oldest reused); holes on doors, machines and invisible colliders still fade; explosions leave pooled scorch marks; all clear at match start. Story items follow the canon (the patrol fled mid-meal with their map circling Kaltenbrunn; Lene's apron; a December 1944 calendar; Käthe's frequency slips — deliberately not the egg's frequencies; the cousins' room with childhood drawings and modest, period clothes per HOMAGE_BOSSES §1.2; the empty stall "FANNY": the horse is only heard, in rounds 1–2). The navmesh is unchanged (both bakes byte-identical). Not done: grime on window glass; footprints don't sink into drifts.

**T-056, 2026-10-22: bot locomotion (Update 03 §3).** Bots move through a separate locomotion layer (`BotLocomotion`) on the human's own movement code: player movement now reads a `MoveIntent` (`gravelight/player/move_intent.gd`); the human fills it from Input, bots through `Player.drive_bot`, so bots share acceleration, stamina, forward-only sprint, sprint-out (`fire_ready()`), slide and mantle, and turn at `skills.<skill>.turn_rate`. Paths are navmesh queries that reject shut doors; danger (zombies, traps, Bell Dome rims, `fire_hazards`, the blackboard's `danger_zones`, lift cages) is a sampled path cost with side detours, because region costs can't express small moving dangers. Avoidance is our own steering, not NavigationAgent3D: a 1.2 m bubble stretched ahead of a moving human, a predictive step-aside, no parking in chokes or on the human's training line; a bot within 2 m of a downed teammate never yields (revives stay safe). Training loops are JSON data (`data/gravelight/ai/routes_<map>.json`, not `.tres`), validated on the navmesh by `route_tool.tscn`: Map 0 taproom, taproom-to-stable (a lollipop through the kitchen door), stable (east half), Old Cellar barrels; Kaltenbrunn headframe, pews, turbine 1. The stuck ladder repaths at 2 s, nudges at 4 s, teleports at 8 s only when no human can see the bot or the spot. On by default (bots.json `locomotion: "v2"`, tuning in `data/gravelight/ai/locomotion.json`). Measured v1 → v2 on the old brain: player3 stuck 10.0 → 1.9 s/run, blocking 148 → 0, sustained 5.7 → 0; bots3 stuck 169 → 0.8 s; ~0.1 ms per bot (half of it the shared move_and_slide). Not done: bots don't ride the lift; nothing is in `fire_hazards` yet (BurningGround only hurts zombies).

**T-055, 2026-10-22: the bots' utility-AI brain (Update 03 §1–§9).** The T-031 mode machine and wishlist rules are replaced by a utility AI on a shared squad blackboard: perception, utility (19 actions, each a weighted product of response curves over 38 normalized inputs, hysteresis +0.1, a commit bonus mid-task, decision noise, minimum action time), aim, economy, tasks, pings and a debug overlay, in focused files under `gravelight/bots/`; all tuning in `data/gravelight/ai/*.json` (actions, curves, combat, economy, ai_counters, skills, seven personality files; JSON, not `.tres`, per project convention). Bots perceive only what they see (110° cone, budgeted LOS rays), hear (groans 9 m, gunfire, barricades, boss cues) or a teammate reported (4 s memory). The local-AI planner's directive is the Objective action (the scripted planner now finishes the Map 0 egg in 60 s vs 117 s). Cheats removed: the free pistol refill, a hidden 10 % damage cut, omniscience, teleport-to-leader; the visible spawn kit and bonus perk stay (both asked for by the user). The "pistol trap" is fixed (683 → 68 s of pistol per run). Bots buy a wall gun only when it beats what they carry (never a Kar98k after round 10), never touch power, traps or easter-egg objects unless ordered (the planner bot excepted), and buy doors only when ordered, after the human opened that path, or when no human leads. Skills are Recruit/Veteran/Elite (Veteran default; old names alias). Nine-order command wheel; 82 new callout lines with world pings (VO_SCRIPT §15). `ai_debug 1 | heat | panel | 0`. Jakub runs on it through `add_brain` options. Results vs baseline (docs/BOT_AI.md §7): stall time 124 → 3.6 s/run (player3), blocking 25.7 → 1.7 events, 0 sustained, 0 teleports, bots-alone revives 0 → 91 %, bots-alone rounds 5.7 → 7.0 (max 10), Kaltenbrunn 5 → 7, 0.35 ms/bot. Not met: bots alone reach rounds 7–10, not 20–25 (no power → no perks); player3 revive success 78 % (≈94 % outside the final collapse); Peg's flanking and Wren's high ground have personality fields but no movement yet.

**T-057, 2026-10-22: Update 02 follow-up fixes.** Bellringer packs no longer rise at the portal point when a slot fails: each member takes a free slot in the same open zone with room for a body (`spawn_spot_ok`), portal paths through a window are refused (`window_crossed`), and the fallback is the open-zone ground spawn nearest a player — this ends the stacked zombies pushed onto roofs and packs stranded outside (soak `--specials`: 1–9 idle per run → 0). Squad and Operator lines that name a place carry a map (an M0/M1/M2 category token, a "Map N" condition, or rules.json `map_lines`) and are drawn only on that map (Peg no longer says "Kaltenbrunn" at the inn). Lamp glass follows its light through `LampGlow` (`gravelight/world/lamp_glow.gd`, added by MapRoot): each glass mesh gets its own material copy with emission scaled by the light's energy, so the Cousins blackout and the Bellringer flicker darken the glass too (shared wall-lamp glass dims with its brightest light; Map 0 not regenerated for it). Map 0's stairs: no zombie could climb from below (0/12 — the 15 s stuck-respawn hid it): the agent cut the corner at the balustrade end and the ramp-to-floor seam stopped walkers; zombies now walk back to a skipped corner after 0.4 s without progress and step up 0.25 m (`soak_stairs`: 40/40 each way). A zombie that gets no closer to its target for 40 s while more than 8 m away is recycled through a window (enemies.json), as a guard against stalled rounds; the Kaltenbrunn stall itself didn't reproduce in 17 runs.

**T-051 (Jakub half), 2026-10-22: Jakub Ostrowski, the prisoner companion (Update 02 §6.2).** A bot-style Player (`is_companion`, pid 9) driven by the Update 03 brain through `add_brain` options and `personality_jakub.json`: no player slot, no lobby slot, no round scaling, no perks ("I've seen what they put in those."), and a match never stays alive on him alone. Own 60 s bleed-out; bled out, he is out cold (never dead) until the next campaign map, where he comes back wounded ("Not yet. Not tonight."). He breaks out at round 5 (data) whether or not Door 5 is open — the coal-window zombie (`Zombie.forced_target`) needs no door; with the Old Cellar shut he waits there calling out in Polish (subtitled, positional), which pulls players down. "Talk to Jakub" opens a small input-blocking card: give him the gun in your hands (not your only gun, never a wonder weapon; a second gift swaps), or Not Yet (shovel, mends windows, calls out sprinters, power-ups, the crate); a wall-buy with him beside it sells to him at the normal price. Armed: 1.4× aim error, prefers bolt-actions, half-price ammo boxes paid by the player who freed him. In pure solo the human keeps the solo rules (Jakub revives humans only with bots or in co-op). Online co-op keeps him caged until companions are replicated (data `online: false`). Joining sets the profile flag `companion_jakub = joined`, files his letter to his wife (Archive `INT_JAK_01`) and opens a 10-page dossier; `Campaign.snapshot["companion"]` carries him to Kaltenbrunn (Maintenance Stairs −500 while he's with you) and Totenhafen. 106 JAK lines + squad lines (VO_SCRIPT §16), baked offline on his own VO channel; three lines that name Kaltenbrunn as where he came from are rules.json `any_map_lines`. Canon (CANON §6.4): born 1901 in Wilcza Góra, tag KB-2210, Tomasz Nowak's gang leader in Gallery Nine; his chalk alphabet is Morgenrot's Witnesses' Marks; he stays at the rail siding during the Return Trip. Launcher "Map 0 — round 5 (Jakub)" = `gravelight/tools_src/play_jakub.tscn`.

**T-059, 2026-10-22: deflaking the smokes (both flakes were real bugs).** The bot stuck ladder counted a bot walking back from its nudge to the same wall as progress, so the ladder restarted after every nudge and a stuck bot never reached its unseen teleport: once a ladder has nudged, movement now counts only at least `stuck.escape_m` (4 m, locomotion.json) from where the bot got stuck. TEAM_DOWN chose its speaker before the reviver had started the hold, so the bot about to revive often said it: VOManager now excludes the blackboard's `revive:<pid>` claimers and only picks speakers who have lines for the category (so Jakub can't drop the line). smoke_vo's scripted triggers wait for a free chatter channel and retry (chatter is dropped inside the gap by design and the bots' callouts often hold it). loco_lab's budget check also passes when the layer's own cost stays under 2.5× the body movement measured in the same run, so a loaded machine doesn't fail it. smoke_m2 watches the Panzerschreck rocket for up to 2 s per shot.

**T-058, 2026-10-22: Update 03 tuning.** A soak down log (the last ten thinks of every bot that goes down) and a shot audit drove the fixes, almost all in `data/gravelight/ai/*.json`: no reloads cancelled by knife swings; a point-blank fire tolerance; fire only with the sights on the target, no hip spray at range (hit rate 25–35 % → 56 %); retreat sprints only from runners; trains shoot when stalled; quick target flicks without re-rolling the reaction delay; latched revive swarm blocks and a mid-hold abort under 40 % HP (the last-chance rule still overrides); speed-based backpedal; Bellringer rounds stand together; Trigger Tonic for SMGs; adds before the Cousins; the Crucible from round 5; a last-zombie hunt after 12 s with 1–2 left (the squad "hears" it; fixed the stalled Foreman on Totenhafen). Bots alone (no human in the match) lead themselves: the door on the path to the power, the power itself (round 2–3), useful doors only, a home loop, far errands between waves; with a human in the match the power stays theirs (even while they are bled out for the round). Peg flanks bosses and specials; Wren takes the hayloft high ground (`routes_map00.json high_ground`). The soak gains `--player-kit=realistic` (power at rounds 3–5, buys Ironhide and Second Wind), `--start-round`, `--homage=0`, `--character`, and a fair revive rate (a down with no bot standing for 10 s of it isn't counted). Results (docs/BOT_AI.md §6–8): bots alone reach round 10.0 on average (best 16; 12–19 with homage bosses off — the Cousins at round 10 are the wall), player3 with the realistic player 12.7 (best 18) with 96 % revives; with the baseline player 9.3 and 74 % (old rule) / 78 % (fair rule; the misses are downs still open when the squad wipes); Kaltenbrunn 9; empty-mag moments 9.5 → 2.9 per minute; 0.22 ms per bot. Not met: the brief's bots-alone round 20–25 and > 85 % revives with the do-nothing baseline player.

**T-060, 2026-10-22: the Cousins scale for bot-only squads (user call: "they should scale").** While no human is on their feet (solo with bots and the human down or bled out, or a bots-only squad), the Cousins Round switches to `cousins.json "bots_alone"`: the three at 50 % health (max and current rescaled together, so the bar keeps its fraction and switching back is exact), 60 % damage (`HomageBoss.hit_player`), 1.3x cooldowns (`cd_for`), 0.9x speed, and the horde still queued cut to 60 % once per fight (the down logs showed the runners, not the bosses, downing bots while they focused the three). On 4 s after the last human goes down, off 2 s after one is back up (a quick down and revive doesn't flip it). Humans in the match decide it, not bots or Jakub; a human standing gets the normal fight. Also fixed from the same soaks: bots no longer rebuild windows while the squad hunts a stalled round's last zombie (unless ordered to repair): that zombie was tearing at a window a bot kept boarding, and a bots3 run sat on round 2 for two hours. Soak bots3, 6 runs (seeds 11-16): the Cousins cleared 5 of 5 times reached (was 2 of 10), highest rounds 7, 11, 14, 16, 20, 20 (mean 14.7, was 10.0; best 20).

**T-060a, 2026-10-22: the lighting pass base (user: "all the maps need better lighting ... match the vibe").** Graphics presets now gate the costly lighting a map asks for: SDFGI (High only), SSIL (Medium and High), SSR (High). A map's generator turns an effect on in its Environment and the preset can only take it away, never add it (`GLSettings._apply_env`, the map's wish kept in Environment metadata; `test_graphics_presets.gd`). Low keeps each map's flat-ambient fallback. `tools/light_shots.sh` / `tools_src/light_shots.gd` shoots fixed viewpoints per map (`data/gravelight/lighting/shots_<map>.json`) with the power off and on (Map 0 also in the Cousins blackout), with an fps / GPU-time probe; before / after / after_low sets are in `morning/screenshots/lighting/<map>/` (git-ignored). Each map was judged by mean luminance and the share of near-black pixels per view, power off, before vs after; no view was allowed to get darker.

**T-061, 2026-10-22: Map 0's lighting pass.** The night outside is cold and huge and the inn a warm refuge: SDFGI bounce (High) and SSIL (Medium+) over SSAO, with the flat cold ambient kept as Low's fallback; shadow-casting lanterns with fog halos; shadowed moon spills that stripe the floor through the barricades; candles, hall and loft sconces, the installers' lantern in the perk vault and a lamp over the Old Cellar's ammo box, so no play space is black with the power off. The cellar lever's sweep now reaches the inn: 11 bare bulbs in the taproom, upstairs and stable. Lamplight spills from every window onto the snow, and GRAVELIGHT ONE glows orange in the field's fog and through the east window. Every light added that belongs to a lamp follows that lamp (`follow_light.gd`), and the new bulbs and sconces are in the Cousins' lamp_groups, so the blackout, the Bellringer and the dying fire still darken the inn properly. Pass 2: inn and cellar lamps x1.5 with wider reach and double GI bounce (SDFGI had replaced the flat ambient), mid-hall sconces, lit window cards and stronger spills so the inn glows from outside, moonlight stripes on the stable straw; power-off mean luminance went from 12–56 to 26–71 across all 15 views. High: 143 fps mean, 117 min at 1280x720.

**T-062, 2026-10-22: Map 1's lighting pass.** One Environment serves three levels, so `depth_grade.gd` (on the WorldEnvironment, presentation only) blends fill, far-fog colour, fog glow, exposure and saturation by camera height: moon-blue surface, neutral salt-grey depths, violet-black Core at full saturation. Fog density is left to the Bellringer. SDFGI and SSIL are switched on in the generator (presets remove them), and flat fill drops to 70% while SDFGI runs. Unpowered spaces are read by visible sources (lanterns, braziers, candles, emergency red, Nachtglas) instead of more ambient. Power lights glow in the haze (`power(... fog)`), the surface gets sodium goosenecks and warm floods, and the Core gets the GDD's amber cracks in the violet. Pass 2: the heart's halo in the vault haze and the violet fill are back (the Core is the climax room); with no volumetric fog (Low) `depth_grade.gd` adds Core fill and exposure; the Lab hall's cool light comes from a crate of Nachtglas specimen jars. Barracks / mess hall / lift house went from 85–93 % near-black to 16–46 %; no view darker than before on High. Light names, groups, the per-zone glow holders and the G→A sweep are unchanged. High: ~221 fps.

**T-063, 2026-10-22: Map 2's lighting pass.** Totenhafen was too dark because, before the power, its interiors had only small red emergency lamps and a flat ambient that the fog swallowed. Each zone now gets light from sources you can see: fire barrels, coke stoves, dockers' lanterns, moonlight shafts through the boarded windows and vents (shadowed spots inside the window closets), the sea's cold light under the pen-mouth curtain and through the pump-deck grille, and battle lanterns on both sides of the boat. The signal lamp's beam is stronger and shadowed, so it no longer lights rooms through roofs. With the power on, the hall's floods split sodium (west) and mercury (east) for warm-against-cold contrast, the flood beams light the fog, and the lamps that blew out walls and ceilings are softer. SDFGI, SSIL and SSR (graded by preset), sky reflections, exposure 2.0, a lighter blue-grey fog, thinner volumetric fog with a little emission so silhouettes read. Interiors went from ~8/255 mean (75–94 % near-black) to 20–50. The fire barrels and stoves have colliders (the navmesh routes round them); the quay's two spray emitters (brown blobs) are gone. High: 154–240 fps.

**T-064, 2026-10-23: Update 04 rule changes (work order bolt-and-sprocket/UPDATE_04_MAP_QUESTS_PISTOL.md).** Three earlier rules change at the user's request. (1) v1 Appendix A §A13 "no minimap" is replaced: a minimap is ON by default in the top-left, with a "Classic HUD" setting that turns it off; zombies stay hidden on it by default (only firing-revealed for 1 s, the Slender's Overwatch, or a setting). (2) v1 "objectives only from character quotes" is replaced: the pause menu gets an OBJECTIVES tab (quest log) with a spoiler setting (Hidden / Hints, the default / Full Guide). (3) UPDATE_03 "bots never touch easter-egg objects" is replaced: they can when the player allows it (setting "Bot Easter-Egg Help": Off / When Asked, the default / Full Partner); story choices, cutscene triggers, the Crucible handoff and the final commit stay player-only, and bots never sequence-break. The work order's `.tres` resources are JSON tables per the project's data convention. Built as five sections on branches u04-1-minimap (§1-2, T-065), u04-4-objectives (§4, T-066), u04-5-ee (§5, T-067), u04-6-pistol (§6, T-068) and u04-3-marks (§3, T-069, after the map data merges).

**T-066, 2026-10-23: the pause tabs and the quest log (Update 04 §4).** The pause page becomes tabbed: MAP · OBJECTIVES · LOADOUT · SETTINGS · QUIT, opening on MAP each time, with Q/E or LB/RB to switch. Solo pauses and co-op does not, as in T-040. The quest log is a pure model, `ObjectiveLog`, built from a snapshot of the match (`ObjectiveSnapshot`) and from text kept as JSON in `data/gravelight/objectives.json` rather than a .tres. The text covers each step at three spoiler levels plus per-map current-objective rules and flag-tracked side quests, so a future map only adds data, and a GUT test fails if a quest step has no text. Hidden lists only completed steps and hides untouched secrets; Hints (the default) shows in-character hints; Full Guide shows instructions, where to go, map highlights and seeded values the squad has already heard. No level ever shows a future step's words. The Map 0 radio mini-quest is Map 0's EASTER EGG entry; Jakub's escape and the Cousins' room are its side quests. The MAP tab is a host slot (`PauseMenu.set_map_view`) for the §3 pause map; "Assign to bot" calls the §5 BotSquad API when present. B&S gets the same view in the holo skin as an OBJECTIVES overlay in its own pause menu. SETTINGS opens MenuSettings as an overlay; pad B doesn't close the page (it removes marks in §3). Schema: docs/gravelight/OBJECTIVES.md.

**T-068, 2026-10-23: M1911 attachments and the Armorer's Bench (Update 04 §6).** Attachments live on the WeaponInstance (`kit`: slot → option, plus `finish`), so they travel with that pistol: through the Crucible (the forge carries the kit; Last Rites, `m1911_up`, applies each option's upgraded block: Penitent's Magazine, Rosary Drum 40 rds, Vesper Match, Censer Compensator whose flare ignites zombies hit within 6 m, Vow of Silence, Vigil, Steeple, Pallbearer, Confessor's, Last Word, Nachtglas Benediction), to Jakub, through the campaign carry and co-op inventory (`to_net` entries 7–8; inventory ops `fit_attachment`, `set_kit`), and away with a wall-buy that replaces it. All numbers are JSON (`data/gravelight/weapons/m1911_attachments.json`, the project convention, instead of the order's `.tres`). Stat effects are multipliers inside `WeaponInstance.f()`, so they stack with the Crucible tiers; `ads_check` covers every sights × stock build (60/60 aligned). Benches stand in the stable's tack corner on Map 0 (the inn has no walled tack room), at Kaltenbrunn's gatehouse, and on Totenhafen's harbourmaster's hut near spawn (not in the order; every built map gets one); a future map only places the node. Nachtglas rounds need the power; Jakub refuses them at the bench and in a pistol handed to him. Suppressed shots are flagged through `GLBus.shot_is_suppressed` (read during `weapon_fired`) so the minimap skips their reveal. Finishes (blued 25, battle-worn 100, nickel 250, engraved 500, Nachtglas inlay 1000 M1911 kills) and the Loadout Pick (Extended Mag or Checkered Grips only, from 75 kills) live in the profile's `equipped` block, chosen in Settings → Gameplay. The upgraded Suppressor gives +15 % head damage (no quiet-kill system exists). The bench UI doesn't pause the world; taking a hit closes it. Known: the drum and ammo bands sit below the frame at the default hip pose.

**T-065, 2026-10-23: map data for every level and the minimap (Update 04 §1–2).** Every playable level ships with map data: `data/maps/<id>/mapdata.json` plus one PNG per floor (JSON rather than the work order's `.tres`, per project convention). The list of playable levels comes from the campaign chain and the built B&S planets (`MapCatalog`), so a new map is required automatically. Floor images are baked off-screen (`tools/bake_minimap.sh <id>`; dev console `bake_minimap`): every visible mesh rendered from above, sliced at the floor's height, filled caps on cut walls, then styled per game (GRAVELIGHT ink sketch, B&S holo, a Swiss blueprint ready for Helvetia Works). POIs are read from the scene (GRAVELIGHT scenes are loaded but never run; Armorer's Benches count as workbenches) or the B&S level JSON. `MapCheck` (in GUT and `tools/check_mapdata.sh`) fails a map with missing data, images, floors or stale POIs. Floors: the Inn cellar / ground / upstairs; Kaltenbrunn vault / level −2 / surface / admin upper; Totenhafen lower pens & U-1047 / quay / catwalks / signal tower (my split); B&S one floor each. The minimap (top-left; brass compass bezel and a ledger-stamp zone name on GRAVELIGHT, holo ring on B&S) is on by default and "Classic HUD" turns it off, superseding v1 §A13. It draws pre-baked layers through a reusable `MapCanvas` (0.05–0.15 ms per frame against a 0.3 ms budget). `MapState` holds per-match discovery (buyables once seen within 16 m, fog per floor, the crate only at its current spot), live icons and marks. Zombies default to "Firing only" (an unsuppressed shot reveals nearby zombies for 1 s), since the order lists that as a default exception; "Off" means no blips. Rotation follows the view camera (B&S converts Bolt's yaw); `test_map_orientation.gd` pins north-up, east-right and the bezel letters.

**T-067, 2026-10-23: bots work on the easter egg (Update 04 §5).** UPDATE_03's rule that bots never touch easter-egg objects becomes a setting, "Bot Easter-Egg Help" (Off / When Asked, the default / Full Partner, up to 2 bots self-picking). The local-AI planner's egg step list moved into the quest data as a `bot_task` per step (collect, shoot_target, hold_zone, carry, interact_sequence, kill_with_weapon, escort, turn_key — the last ready for Morgenrot's two-key abort) with a `bot_knowledge` block per quest. BotEECatalog (pure rules), BotEEKnowledge (what the squad has discovered: items, lines heard, flags, a sight/hearing sweep), BotEE (one task per bot, a planner and an executor per type) and BotEEHub (setting, API, Full Partner, console) carry it out for every bot; the planner bot no longer works the egg unasked. Bots know only what the squad has discovered, only make the input the sequence expects next, never take the player's commit (`commit: last_input`: on Map 0 they lock 2 of 3 frequencies and hand the last to the player), and never trigger a stage that can fail a step unless ordered. Player-only steps (cutscenes, final commits, story choices, handoffs) get cover instead. Work pauses on threat, a teammate down or the round ending, then "Back to it." Jakub does Collect, Hold and Carry and refuses the Crucible and perk machines. 107 lines (VO_SCRIPT §18), baked. Soak `tools/ai_soak.sh --scenario=ee_full --runs=3`: 3 of 3 runs completed the Map 0 egg with the player's commit, 0 failed-egg events, 0.23–0.26 ms per bot. Map 1's later steps were not soaked end to end.

**T-069, 2026-10-23: the pause map, marks and bot orders (Update 04 §3).** The pause page's MAP tab is `PauseMapView` over §2's MapCanvas: floor tabs, pan and zoom, zone labels, a legend, hover state and price, round and power, and each bot's portrait with its Scrip and order line ("Going to buy Twin Barrel Bitters (1,600 short, 59 s)", "Defending Minehead Yard", "Doing: the radio notes (0/3)", "Idle"). Marks go through `MapMarks` (core/map, a child of the MapState): 3 personal marks, 3 danger, 1 regroup, 1 order per bot, the oldest replaced; Danger and Regroup belong to the squad. They show on the minimap, the full map and in the world (`WorldMarks`: a chalk X decal with a fading label for the 4 nearest on GRAVELIGHT, a holo pin on B&S, which has personal marks only). A mark for a bot becomes a `mark_order` utility action (weight 0.93; a revive at 1.25 wins and the order resumes with "Back to it."). The best bot is the closest one that's up, not reviving and able. Short of Scrip, a bot says the amount (rounded up into 17 voiced steps) and waits 60 s while earning. "For me" wall guns are bought with the bot's Scrip and handed over as a WeaponParcel at your feet; a gun the bot already owns becomes an ammo buy. Doors need the confirmation ("Open this door for 1,250?"). EE marks go through §5's `ee_assign_node`, bench orders through `ArmorersBench.buy`. Jakub never buys and refuses perks and the Crucible. The ping key is middle mouse / D-pad up (alt-fire gave up middle mouse and keeps B and D-pad right; B&S `bs_ping` is G / Back): a buyable means buy for any bot, a door asks and a second press confirms, a floor or wall means Go here, a double-tap means Danger, pinging your own mark removes it. Floors are on the D-pad and Shift+wheel / PgUp-PgDn (the bumpers switch pause tabs). Go Here waits 45 s, Defend lasts 90 s (data: `data/maps/marks.json`, `data/gravelight/ai/marks.json`). 247 lines (VO_SCRIPT §19), baked. Launcher: "Map 0 — Pause map & marks test" (`play_marks.tscn`), "Kaltenbrunn — Bots Full Partner EE test" (`play_ee_full.tscn`, Full Partner for that session only), "Armorer's Bench test" (`bench_shots.tscn` in play mode). Known: controller paths written but not driven; the chalk X is faint on Kaltenbrunn's snow.

**T-070, 2026-10-23: performance pass (Update 04 §2/§7).** `perf_marks` prints a breakdown (`--breakdown`), per-script costs (`--probe`), A/B experiments (`--ab=`) and `--preset`. Map 0's busy scenario (round 15, minimap, 3 bots + Jakub, 24 zombies) was CPU-bound: shadowed lamps re-rendering cube faces about 4.6 ms, scripts about 6 ms (24 zombies 2.7 ms, 4 brains 1.5 ms), and first-use shader compiles causing 100–320 ms spikes. Fixes: RenderWarm draws first-use materials (wound decal, severed piece, ReactiveFx bursts) invisibly at map load (`RenderWarm.add_provider` for more); ShadowProxies merges the static batches' shadow casting into a few depth-only meshes (about 5000 → 3600 draw calls, about 2 ms a frame, identical shadows); the minimap's bezel is retained and zombie dots take a lean path (about 0.19–0.28 ms against the 0.3 ms budget); HUD font colours are set only on a change. Rendered (before the user asked to stop rendered runs): avg 75.5 → about 87 FPS with instrumentation, worst frame 124–148 → 26 ms, 1% low 42–49 (target 45, borderline). The render thread stays Safe: Separate nearly doubles FPS but Godot 4.7.2 marks it experimental and it raced the headless renderer (launch option `--render-thread separate`). Not done: dual-paraboloid shadows (visual loss), shadow update throttling, landscape proxies; the final rendered check of the Safe build waits for the user's OK.

**T-071, 2026-10-24: player bot mode.** The user asked for "bot mode where a local agent will control his player ... train the zombies is most important". `PlayerBot` (one per match) hands the local survivor to the Update 03 BotBrain (`Player.bot_driven`, `BotSquad.add_player_brain`): F6 (rebindable `bot_mode`), the pause menu, the console `botmode`, `--botmode` or the launcher's "Bot mode" button; any real input hands control back at once; co-op host only. The agent leads (the T-058 self-led rules), revives, aims with the Elite preset on a damped view curve, shops with the player's Scrip (a real gun first, Second Wind then Ironhide, never a bolt-action), and can use the local-LLM planner (scripted fallback). `BotTrainer` (`lead_train`) trains hordes of 4+ round the map's loop with gap, safe-turn and 16-direction kite rules; small groups and special rounds go to the Fight action, which measured better. New agent-only Map 0 yard Well Loop. Measured (headless soaks): Map 0 solo 6.25 rounds, with 3 bots 10 (stops at the Cousins), Kaltenbrunn 8–10. **Not met:** the training targets (Map 0 beyond 14.7, Kaltenbrunn 15+); a second training pass follows.

**T-071 pass 2, 2026-10-24.** Pass 2 makes the agent train like a player once it can. A per-map game plan (`AgentPlan`, player_bot.json "plans") sets the spend order (Map 0: Garand, cellar, power, Second Wind, Ironhide, stable, yard, Thompson, Marathon, Crucible). From the moment the plan's loop is open, the trainer owns each round: it lets the round group up, then walks backwards round the loop firing into the train (no backpedal penalty), sprinting only when the head reaches it, steering by 5 lanes on the loop's line scored by simulating every zombie's pursuit for 1.5 s, and reloading on the move. Round-8 testbed: the yard takes 3 hits per training minute against 10-15 on the taproom and cellar rings, so the plan heads for the yard. The agent counts as a human for T-060 (it plays the human's survivor), so the Cousins come at full strength. Measured: Map 0 solo 6.25 -> 7.1 rounds (best 9), + 3 bots 10, Kaltenbrunn 7.7. **Still not met:** solo dies at 7-8 when it reaches the yard too late; Kaltenbrunn power never comes on; with bots the agent keeps the M1911; fixed in the same pass: a zombie outside that makes no 1 m progress towards its window in `window_no_progress_seconds` (30 s, enemies.json) is recycled, since the chase-state stuck checks never saw TO_WINDOW and a round could run forever.

**T-072, 2026-10-24: Map 0 made to feel alive.** A taproom wall post stood in the middle of the stable door (Door 2) and showed once it was bought; `DoorClearance` now lists every drawn triangle and solid collider inside any Map 0 doorway and `smoke_map00_alive` fails on any (it also caught scuff strips, wainscot and a yard snow drift, all cleared). Four more taproom tables with candles and the patrons' abandoned things; the clockwise loop keeps its width. Round 1 opening (first round 1 of a Map 0 match only): five period civilians (salt carters Rother and Kubiak, Frau Pohl, Trudi the barmaid, Gefreiter Sobotta on leave) flee through a new barred front door; two zombies rise from the snow; Kubiak, Rother and Sobotta are caught and rise 6 s later; all five come out of round 1's own count. Patrons are immune and ignored by bots; co-op host-started, every peer runs the same script. A gramophone plays until shot or round 3, the horse panics, the hearth flares. Data `map00_opening.json`; lines VO_SCRIPT §21.

**T-073, 2026-10-24: better voices.** The squad's, Jakub's and the Map 0 patrons' voices move from Windows OneCore to Kokoro-82M (offline, CPU, Apache-2.0) from a project-local venv (`tools/setup_tts.sh`); eSpeak NG only at build time. Each speaker is cast in `data/gravelight/vo/voice_cast.json` (a blend of voice styles, lexicon, Polish phonemes for Polish words, light accents from phoneme rules, delivery per category), loudness-normalised to −17.5 LUFS with a −1.5 dBTP ceiling. Radio/PA voices (the Operator, Fasbender, Reinholt, Liesl) stay on OneCore, also the fallback. Runtime: positional voices occluded by walls (low-pass, −5 dB), shuffle bags don't repeat across a refill, the squad no longer echoes a callout just made. Re-bake: `python gravelight/tools_src/gen_vo.py` (incremental). Docs: docs/gravelight/VOICES.md.

**T-074, 2026-10-24: the Armorer's Bench for any gun.** Attachments for whatever gun is in your hands, from `data/gravelight/weapons/attachments.json`: a table per weapons.json class (SMG, assault, semi-auto, bolt/sniper, shotgun, LMG, Panzerschreck, flamethrower) with per-weapon overrides (Thompson drum, Kar98k ZF39 and trench mag, Springfield Unertl, MG42 Heavy Zweibein, shotgun chokes and Dragon's Breath, a third tank for the flamethrower). Upgraded guns use their base table with Crucible names (benefits ×1.35, drawbacks 60 %). The M1911 keeps its T-068 table unchanged (a frozen fixture guards it). Wonder weapons, melee and tacticals are refused in character. The kit stays on the gun through the Crucible, Jakub, campaign carry and co-op; a wall-buy or Mystery Crate gun that takes its slot comes plain. Parts are built procedurally; scopes move the sight markers onto the scope axis; `ads_check -- --no-shots` checks aim headlessly (258/258). Needs a rendered look: the generic parts and the bench framing for long guns.

**T-075, 2026-10-24: perk upgrades and the machines' backstory.** Every tonic gets tiers II and III with a new effect each (data: perks.json `tiers` + `upgrades`). Bought at the perk's machine with the power on, tier II from round 8, III from round 15; profile mastery (1 per kill, 20 per round with the tonic) gates them (II at 250, III at 1200). Tiers take no cap slot, are lost with the perk, carry through exfil and co-op (the host's profile gates the squad); bots buy them per `economy.json perk_upgrades`. Backstory in docs/gravelight/PERKS_LORE.md (built into `machine_lore.json`): the three-chamber cabinets and the coin plate that reads the drinker, why each map's machines are where they are, the three Crucible crates, Fw. Ammer's Standing Order 17 for the wall guns. Shown on readable maker's plates, the Crucible's brass plate, chalk lines under wall-buy prompts, the Archive, the HUD's tier band and the LOADOUT tab; lines VO_SCRIPT §20.

**T-079, 2026-10-25: the Muster (out-of-match loadout).** The user asked for "better character improvement" outside the match and to choose "what pistol or perks they come in with", with the loadout perks named apart from the perk tonics. Before a match each survivor picks a Sidearm (M1911, Walther P38 r4, Webley Mk VI r8, Luger P08 r14: round-1 sidegrades with Crucible rows and procedural models) and up to 1/2/3 Field Habits at rank 1/10/25: twelve small, match-long edges (Pay Packet, Whetstone, Full Haversack, Grenade in the Boot, Carpenter's Pencil, Field Dressing, Cool Head, Spare Record, Quick Holster, Long March, Scrounger, Steady Hands) unlocked by rank, career stats, challenges or dossier level, never called perks and never doing a tonic's job. Data `data/gravelight/muster.json`, code `Muster`, screen `MenuMuster` (GRAVELIGHT menu, co-op lobby, dev launcher button); profile v3 keeps a kit per survivor. MapRoot registers each human's kit before spawn, so every way into a match (and the player-bot agent) uses it; bots and Jakub keep their defaults. Co-op: kits go to the host at join, sanitised, carried in the roster (the host trusts the peer's rank). A Commendation keeps all Muster unlocks; Classic Rules turns the Muster off; the extra sidearms don't count toward Nachtglas. Docs: docs/gravelight/PROGRESSION.md.

**T-076, 2026-10-25: the opening must be seen.** Playtest: no patrons, no turning. The logic ran on every entry path (`smoke_inn_opening_paths`: launcher with 0/3 bots, bot mode, hub Play and Campaign, restart, co-op host); the failure was presentation. The spawn stood among the patrons facing away from most of them, and round 1 began on a 6 s frame-time intro a load hitch and the title banner could eat. Now the spawns sit at the taproom's south end facing the room; round 1 is held ("look" block in map00_opening.json) until 11 s of settled view, the player facing the front door (22 s at most) and no patron mid-line, while the patrons chat; then a cue (pounding, "What was that?") and round 1 starts. Kubiak and Rother are caught in line with the open doorway, and Sobotta dies and rises mid-room. Round 1's total is unchanged. The squad's spawn lines wait for round 1 on Map 0 so they don't talk over the patrons (vo_manager).

**T-077, 2026-10-25: survivors upstairs.** Three travellers (Konrad Wiesner, Hedwig Brandt, Ida Kroll) hide in Room 2 behind the Upstairs door: absent until it's bought, then cowering, pleading, flinching at gunfire. Pay 750 to arm one with a Kar98k from the inn's rack (not your own gun): they hold a hall post and shoot what they see (no Scrip for their kills). Zombies target them via `Zombie.prey`; armed ones can be downed and helped up within 25 s, hiding ones die. The dead rise as extra zombies after a spoken warning, never silently. No collider or hitboxes (shots and bots pass through); host-authoritative; data map00_survivors.json; lines VO_SCRIPT section 22 (Kokoro, German accents). Not done: following the player.

T-078 Scrip boost (the user: 'the bot struggles and so do I… round 5 needs to be a perk and max ammo'). Reaching round 5 is now a data-driven milestone (`rounds.json milestones`): round 4's last kill drops a tonic bottle (our own power-up; a free random tonic from the map's machines for every survivor, with no power needed; with a full cap it gives the next unlocked tier instead, else 1500 Scrip) and a Resupply (our Max Ammo). They land next to the survivors on the navmesh, last 60 s and apply themselves if left, so the reward is guaranteed in solo, co-op, with bots and for the player-bot agent. Five in-match incomes reward skill and favour the early rounds: round pay, an untouched round, a round without a down, 10-headshot streaks, and dog-tag salvage. Each is in `economy.json bonuses`, paid by the host without Double Scrip, and shown as a named HUD slip (`GLBus.bonus_awarded`). Left out: a bank/loan, marked-zombie bounties and a per-round supply drop. Measured, the solo agent earns 6,461 → 8,171 by round 5 and survives 7.62 → 8.00 rounds; the scripted player earns 3,943 → 4,683. Docs: docs/gravelight/ECONOMY.md, GDD §5/§11, VO_SCRIPT §23.

**T-071 pass 3, 2026-10-25.** "See how long bot mode goes by itself." Measuring showed the agent hardly fired while training: its smooth first-person turn trailed moving targets past the fire tolerance. The turn now adds a feed-forward of the target's motion, and hip fire into a bunched train is allowed. Dry reloads wait for room to finish them. The solo agent gets a home loop (it used to idle in the cellar). The plan lets cheaper later steps go first while the current one can't be done, but never skips Ironhide. A crawler break (leg the last zombie, run the errands, come back, kill it) and a sprint-away after a Second Wind self-revive were added. The Map 0 plan buys the MP40 upstairs before Ironhide. Kaltenbrunn takes the maintenance stairs, since the lift is not a navmesh route, so the power now comes on in 5 of 8 runs. The round's last crawler is knifed, which removes the 40-minute stalls (4 in 24 baseline runs). Measured on the same merged game: Map 0 solo 7.17 → 7.71 rounds (best 10); Kaltenbrunn 7.75 → 7.0. **Not met:** Map 0 12+, Kaltenbrunn 10+. Next: the yard loop at rounds 9-10, unspent Scrip (Marathon, Crucible), Kaltenbrunn's Ironhide trip.


T-081: Perk-tonic cap 4 -> 6 (user: "make it so the player can have more than 4 perks"). data/gravelight/perks.json "cap": 6, "classic_cap": 4. PerkManager.base_cap() is read once per match (reset_match clears it). Classic Rules keeps the genre's four, like it drops traits and the Muster; the host's value rides the co-op snapshot ("cb"). cap_for() = base + bonus slots, so the Map 1 main egg's permanent slot makes 7 there (save key map01_perk_slot_5 unchanged). Bonus perks and tiers still use no slot. Tiers, the round-5 tonic bottle and bot buying already went through cap_for/capped_count, so only tests and texts changed. Bot nameplates squeeze their caps to fit 7+. Botmode soak (12 seeds): mean 8.08 / best 10 (was 7.71 / 10). The solo agent dies holding <=5 tonics with its Scrip saved for the Crucible plan step, so its unspent Scrip is a plan issue, not the cap.

T-080 (Map 0's Grounds; user: "the outside area on the first map needs some work, make it bigger and make it so you can go around the whole building"). The snow round the inn is play space: zone GROUNDS, x -20..60 by z -30..64, inside a 6 m invisible edge wall on drawn boundary fences and a spruce tree line, the four road exits closed just inside it. Two bought ways out: Out Front, the inn's front door (1250; GateDoor drives InnFrontDoor, held open once bought), and the yard's carters' gate (750, also opens the yard; Door 4 bought from the yard now opens the stable). Nothing outdoors in round 1. A 205 m ring goes right round the inn, stable and yard; the field has cover (GRAVELIGHT ONE now solid, its engine, wing panel and cargo, a burnt car, hay rick, MG nest, shrine, hay wagon, woodshed, oil drums, an ammo box under the parachute tree). The front doorway is no longer baked shut, so the navmesh joins inside and outside through bought doors like everywhere else. 13 edge spawns and 4 snow spawns (round 4+) are used only while a player is outside, never when an outdoor player can see them close up (ZombieSpawn.player_zone_only, hide_from_m + line-of-sight ray). On open ground (ZoneVolume.roam, enemies.json roam) a window-bound zombie goes for a reachable outdoor player within 20 m; one chasing someone it can't reach goes back to the best reachable window. Bots skip formation spots behind a wall (BotLocomotion.walled). New agent-only routes map00_wreck_loop and map00_grounds_ring; the agent's plan still buys the stable and yard (a 12-seed soak with the front door first: mean 7.5 / best 10 vs 7.71 / 10). smoke_map00_loop checks the ring, edge, doors and outdoor zombie behaviour.

T-083: The Crucible teaches, the boxes feed; reloading on the run. The user, bringing a half-empty taught gun for its second teaching, was asked for "upgraded ammo [2500]" instead (the refill offer came first so the two never shared a prompt). The Crucible now sells no ammo at all: Crucible.action_for answers upgrade for any taught gun under tier III, full at the top ("has learned all the Crucible can teach"), never ammo; economy.json lost crucible_upgraded_ammo. A taught gun refills at any ammo box at the box's existing upgraded price (2500; taught wonder 4000; any tier) or its own wall chalk (4500), and every teaching still hands it back full. Bots follow: the crucible_ammo errand is gone from the economy, tasks, marks, metrics and the agent plan's always-list; the ordinary ammo-box errand (which already priced upgraded guns) does the job; smoke_bots phase 12 watches for that box purchase. Separately, SMGs and pistols (machine pistols, revolvers, the Webley's break) now start and keep a reload while sprinting (movement.json "sprint_reload" per class / per weapon, WeaponHolder.sprint_reload_allowed); the viewmodel keeps 35% of the sprint pose under the reload (feel sprint_reload_pose, eased by the reload tilt), the sprint-out still gates the first shot, nothing new replicates. Every other class keeps the interrupt unless it wears the new Armorer's Bench grip option Paratrooper Sling (quick_sling, props.sprint_reload; 1000 Scrip, LMGs 1250; Pilgrim's Sling when taught; a Paratrooper Harness on the flamethrower), which costs the grip slot's foregrip / bipod / pads: a mobility trade. Tests: test_reload_sprint, test_upgrades, test_weapon_kit, test_bots, test_bot_metrics; smokes m2 / interact / map00 / ammo_box / bots pass. vm_shots gained --poses=sprint_reload and build-string ids.

T-082: Perk caps, pause perk list, perk buff (user: "list all the perks ... the ones they don't have grayed out", "better icons", "the perks in general need just a slight buff"). ui/perk_cap.gd is the one cap renderer (static PerkCap.draw_cap with tier and dim): a crimped enamel cap with a bevelled face and the tonic's glyph embossed on it, drawn from convex pieces so it reads at 18 px on a nameplate and 54 px on the HUD; the glyphs follow JINGLES.md's bottle-cap canon (flexed arm, swallow with red rim, hare, crossed barrels and moustache, winged boot with chrome rim, hawk's eye, mule with rifle lines, ram over a starburst, copper bolt with eyes over a cog), ink on pale enamel and cream on dark for legibility; the HUD row, bot plates, co-op ledger and pause menu all call it, "abbr" is only the fallback for a tonic without a glyph. The pause LOADOUT tab lists all nine tonics in perks.json order as focusable rows (PauseMenu.perk_row): held ones in colour with tier, bonus mark, next-tier state and the tier's description, unheld ones greyed with a new one-line "desc", the price and the machine's zone from MapData's perk POIs; squad rows show each bot's caps. Base measures raised ~10-15% with tiers moved proportionally (Ironhide 275/330, Second Wind x2.3/x2.9, Trigger x0.45/x0.4, Twin Barrel x1.5/x1.6, Marathon x1.10/x1.13, Hawkeye 0.45/0.7/x1.4, Packmule +1 grenade at base (II +2) with the slot unchanged, Blastproof 450 in 3.3 m (II 675, III 3.8 m), Overclock 2.5+4.0e m, 0.66xhealth, bosses 1700, 12 targets); PERKS_LORE 1.1a/1.3a, GDD 8, WEAPONS 20.2 follow. Botmode soak 6 seeds: mean 6.8 / best 10 (two pre-perk early wipes; no regression attributable to the buff).

T-084 (Map 0's edge and the barn; user: "when I'm on the edge of the map ... it seems like there's a wall or something ... allow us to shoot a zombie that's out there" and "the barn ... needs to be about 10% bigger because the staircase is all glitchy ... improve the staircase and some details on the second level"). The grounds' 6 m edge wall and the boundary fences on its line are on physics layer 11 "edge" (gen_map00.EDGE_LAYER): bodies collide (Player.MASK_MOVE; zombies never did, the navmesh holds them), while the shot ray, projectiles, grenades, blasts, flame and the aim and interaction rays mask layer 1 and pass. The 13 edge spawns stand 4.5 m beyond the wall in the tree line, each in a 3 x 7 m spawn pocket (edge_pockets): the baked wall and fence are cut there and the cut is filled by an EdgeGate collider on the edge layer hung off the map root, outside the navigation region, so the bake joins the pocket to the field and zombies walk in between the spruces while a player's capsule stops at the line; everything else beyond the edge is carved from the bake (bake-only NavigationObstacle3Ds), the pockets are GROUNDS roam volumes, and a kill out there drops inside the edge (MapRoot.drop_bounds / clamp_inside in PowerupManager.spawn and ScripBonuses.spawn_tag). BotLocomotion.walled sees the edge too. The stable is x 4.5..25.5 by z 22..34 (21 x 12, was 18 x 12; the bench, Trench Gun chalk and ammo box moved with the west wall; the great doors are 4 x 3 m). The hayloft is the west block (x 4.5..15.5) plus a gallery along the south wall over the great doors (x 15.5..25.35, z 29..34), 182 m2, the bay over the stalls, the hay pile and the north door open to the roof; a straight 2 m flight up the east wall (foot z 25.5, head z 31.5 through a well in the gallery, 16 risers, 30 deg) on a smooth invisible ramp along the nosing line with the treads drawn under it and a boarded store beneath; 1 m rails of three collider bars round every open edge (balusters dressing, bake-only panels for the navmesh) and a 1.4 m bale drop in the gallery's rail to walk off; bales and crates that are cover, the hay chute's trapdoor, a pitchfork, sacks, tack from the tie beams, a lantern on a hook (LoftHookLamp). The loft door over the yard is the stable's fourth window, W_STB_LOFT, its zombies on a hoist ledge outside (16 windows in all). smoke_map00_loop checks the pockets and gates; test_map00_grounds covers the layers, the pockets, the barn's fittings and a navmesh path up the stair. 6-seed bot-mode soak: mean 8.0 / best 9 (baseline ~8 / 10).

T-085: Zombies rise from the ground in stages (gravelight/enemies/zombie_emerge.gd, enemies.json "emerge"). The user: "Improve the animation for them coming out of the ground... that's what we want to see." The single 1.4 s slide-up (whose raised hands already poked 0.27 m through the floor at t=0) is replaced by five keyframed dig-outs baked on the shared rig: fast lunge-out (1.4 s), standard (2.1 s: one hand claws, the second, head and shoulders heave out twisting, elbow plant, knee, lurch up with a stagger step), slow clawing (3.0 s, a pause at the rim and a second heave), face-first-then-roll (2.3 s), and the crawlers' elbow drag (2.4 s, stays down; spec "crawler": true spawns one). Stage times are data and the contract between clip, effects and gameplay: the ground bulges (a mound mesh at the spawn, snow/earth/stone from a spawn meta, the floor collider or the map default), the crust breaks with a spray and a burst cue, hands scrape (rate-capped), the head tears free with a groan, clumps fall off the shoulders, a dust puff at standing; particles are a 24-emitter pool in Effects. Rules: hitboxes are off until head_out and on from then (and at any early exit; the InnOpening rise_from_floor path kept); navigation at done; a kill before stand runs the rise backward into the ground (no ragdoll); legs lost mid-rise switch to the crawler rise at the same progress; the variant is drawn per zombie from a pool RNG seeded by the match seed, weighted per tier (sprinters lunge, shamblers claw); every ground rise x a round ramp 1.0->0.7 (r1->r20) so the weighted mean goes ~2.0 s -> ~1.4 s; the director's pacing does not read the rise (player3 soak mean round 10.3 vs 8.0). Window, portal, chute and wade emerges unchanged. Sounds are the footstep samples slowed; a real crust-burst sample is wanted. Also fixed: zombie footsteps checked the bank's top level, so surface footsteps never played. Open: the pre-existing crawl pose sits ~0.3 m below the floor by the rig probe; needs a rendered look. GUT test_zombie_emerge; shots_zombie_anim --only=emerge captures every variant at eight stage times.

T-086: the guided egg tells its story; bots and the bot-mode agent do it. The user: "I'm not following the story... the bots should be able to do the Easter egg... bot mode... as well", and "a way to get all the perks". Map 0's map00_radio had no beats: the notes step opened at round 1 while Fasbender asked for notes at round 7, a note was a banner, the locks had no squad, the payoff was a banner. Every step now has a setup beat (2-3 lines, different speakers), progress beats (each note read by whoever took it), a payoff that says what changed and points east, and a reset beat, as quest-data actions (vo_squad, vo_actor, on_vo/after_end, at_n, while_active, patience, vo_retire) through GLVO.say_story_beat (tier 2, never over the story channel, speaker chosen at schedule time); 87 lines baked (VO_SCRIPT 24). The Objectives tab shows a story paragraph and the quoted setup line per current step (objectives.json story/said). Rules changed: Bot Easter-Egg Help defaults to Full Partner (old settings migrated once); a Full Partner bot offers a step's last move to the player for 45 s then makes it (EE_COMMIT_SELF), and a final step waits 60 s for the player then goes on (When Asked unchanged); bots let Fasbender finish before the next lock. The F6 agent does the egg through a separate egg block of its map plan via the shared BotEEHub.pick_for, in round breaks from round 2 and anywhere quiet (incl. crawler breaks) after Ironhide; soak 6 seeds: rounds 9/9/9/8/10/8 (baseline ~8), the egg complete in 2 runs by round 7, progress in all, 0 egg failures. Completing Glasmacher Calling grants all nine tonics slot-free to everyone as the Operator "credits every account" (the Requiem's grant_all_perks), lost on a down like any perk. Map 1's Requiem beats are a follow-up.

T-087: Gun intake (UPDATE 05A 1-3). The 22 conventional guns are downloaded, never modelled: Devin's three Sketchfab CC-BY models (Thompson by Artem Goyko, M1 Garand by Sky_Hunter, Kar98k by dsamuta) are the hero viewmodels and the OpenGameArt "Ultimate Low Poly Guns" set (TastyTony / Pichuliru, CC-BY 4.0; the WW2 zip plus the main set's Double Barrel and Cartridges) is the baseline for 13 more and the fallback for the heroes; luger_p08, m1919, type100, ptrs41, panzerschreck and flamethrower keep the greybox. Every asset lives in assets/third_party/<source>/<id>/ as model.glb + textures/*.png (external, 2K cap by texel density) + LICENSE.txt + SOURCE.txt (Devin's key: value format), converted by tools_src/blender/convert_to_glb.py (rigged OGA meshes split by vertex group into Mag/Bolt/Slide/Pump/Cover/Barrels/Cylinder/Clip parts) through the table in tools/intake_guns.py, which also writes data/gravelight/weapons/models.json (per gun: glb, parts, native forward/length/scale/bounds) and docs/WEAPON_MODELS.md. docs/ASSETS.md's third-party block and data/credits_art.json are generated by tools/assets_manifest.py and the Credits screen builds "Art credits" from that JSON; GUT test_license_gate fails any file under assets/ (user_supplied excepted and git-ignored) without paperwork or with a non-allowed licence, test_assets_manifest fails a stale manifest. *.import files under assets/third_party are committed (VRAM-compressed texture presets) so a fresh clone imports identically; 77 MB added, no LFS (repo ~640 MB). Also: test_zombie_emerge now seeds the global RNG (the body's height/sex roll made its bone-height checks order-dependent).

T-088 (UPDATE_05 0.3/1: the art pipeline). docs/ART_BIBLE.md is the style contract (stylized-realistic late-2000s round-based zombies, the palette the map already lights with, Nachtglas the only saturated accent, 1 unit = 1 m, prop <= 3k tris, 2K ceiling / 1K props, OpenGL normals, ORM packing, <kind>_<name>_<variant> names, the GLB node/meta contract). The CC0 library is fetched, not hand-copied: tools/fetch_cc0_materials.py reads data/gravelight/art/cc0_assets.json, pulls Poly Haven / ambientCG / Kenney into assets/third_party/<source>/<kebab-id>/ with the CC0 legal code and a SOURCE.txt in Devin's format, converts to 8-bit PNG and channel-packs AO/roughness/metal; 16 texture sets, 2 night HDRIs (reference; the sky dome stays), 24 props (Poly Haven scans decimated in headless Blender by tools/decimate_props.py); 183 MB. One shared world shader (assets/materials/shaders/surface_pbr.gdshader: grime, wetness, frost edge, triplanar) behind MaterialLibrary.get_mat(key) and data/gravelight/materials.json; Mats.get_mat hands it to the generators for the keys generator.enabled lists, so Map 0 switches over key by key; floorboards and plaster are on and the regenerated navmesh bake is byte-identical. Import settings are data: assets/import_presets/*.cfg + tools/apply_import_presets.sh; the *.import files under assets/third_party are committed (T-087's un-ignore) so a fresh clone needs no re-run. tools/asset_report.sh writes morning/asset_report.md; capture.gd --set=u05 renders 28 fixed Map 0 cameras for before/after pairs (tools/compare_shots.py); the BEFORE set is in morning/screenshots/u05/before. PropLibrary.instance(key) returns visuals only; collision stays the generator's. Also: smoke_polish counts 10 wind howls (T-084's loft door).

T-096: UI readability pass (UPDATE 05 7; the HUD must read in the dark cellar and on bright snow, one period style). Four bundled open fonts under assets/third_party/google_fonts with paperwork and generated credits: Stardos Stencil (OFL) for stamps, Special Elite (Apache 2.0) for the typewriter headings/buttons/prompts, IBM Plex Sans (OFL, variable) for paragraphs and subtitles, IBM Plex Mono (OFL) for counters and ledgers. data/gravelight/ui.json holds the roles, the type scale (MenuKit.size by key, MenuKit.px snaps free sizes onto its steps), the outline/shadow kinds, the HUD grid (margins with a safe-area fraction and every offset), crosshair/hit-marker sizes, the minimap bezel and open-ground rule, and the pause map's first fit; gravelight/ui/gravelight_theme.tres (default body font, typewriter buttons, gold focus outline, quiet checks, popups, tooltips) is applied at every HUD/menu/pause root and no SystemFont is built outside MenuKit's declared fallback and perk_cap.gd (test_ui_theme). The HUD sits on the grid with outline + drop shadow; the crosshair draws 2 px ticks snapped to physical pixels with movement spread, the hit marker has distinct hit/headshot/kill states; the boss bar is a framed brass-and-ink plate with the name stamp and the title card a ruled ledger page (timings unchanged); the minimap bezel is drawn (bevelled brass, screws, rose points, etched rose, glass glint) and a zone whose bounding box covers over half the map rect (Map 0's Grounds) steps the view out a zoom level. The pause MAP tab opens on the built zones (zones under 40% of the rect: inn, stable, yard) with the whole rect as the zoom-out floor and the last zoom kept for the match; zone labels follow their polygon's mid height. shots_hud.tscn renders the before/after readability set at 720p and 1080p; docs/gravelight/UI_KIT.md.

T-095: UPDATE 05 section 6 VFX. World-space gun and hit effects moved into the Effects autoload with data in data/gravelight/vfx.json and helpers under gravelight/world/vfx/ (FlashPool, CasingPool, ImpactFx, VfxAtmosphere); everything is preallocated and GUT test_vfx proves 500 shots' worth adds no nodes. Muzzle flashes are per weapon class (petal count/length/size/core/light/life/smoke/tint), three star textures, a pooled OmniLight budget of 4 (quality-scaled), driven from GLBus.weapon_fired for every shooter with the muzzle resolved per shooter; taught guns get a violet fringe, wonder weapons violet Nachtglas, the flamethrower keeps its flame. Casings are the OGA cartridge GLB (15 cases, one MultiMesh per kind, kinematic arcs with a bounce and a synthesized tink), chosen through models.json casing_for; GunFx is now a facade so WeaponHolder's calls stand. Impacts add per-surface debris to the T-053 decals (plaster crater and glass star new) and the surface comes from a data-driven name-hint table with per-map defaults because merged static meshes carry no material. Hits add mist and droplets (gore-gated) and a Nachtglas spark burst from Crucible-taught weapons; Effects.shatter and the Great Toll's world-space refraction ring (new GLBus.great_toll) are pooled. Atmosphere: camera-following near snow gated by open sky, a far snow layer, dust motes in the stable's moon shafts, a west field FogVolume, noise-based FlickerLight with fire/lantern kinds, breath that quickens when sprinting. GLSettings vfx_quality low/medium/high scales counts (Settings > VFX quality). The perf_marks bench held (51.4 before, 52.1 median after on a shared machine). Acceptance clip: tools/clip_map00.sh (60 s, round 8, 3 bots, the F6 agent playing, a taught Thompson, the yard): morning/clips/u05/map00_vfx_r8.mp4.

T-089: The downloaded guns are the viewmodels (UPDATE_05 2). WeaponModels.build hands every id with a models.json GLB to WeaponGLB, which builds it from the GLB normalised by its native block (forward to -Z, scaled to real length, the trigger 8 mm ahead of the grip origin) and regrouped into the holder's contract parts (Mag/Bolt/Slide/Pump/Cover/Barrels/Cylinder) at their pivots. Markers (Muzzle, SightPoint, FrontSight, LeftHand) and metas come from a vertex scan and the procedural twin (whose reload kind and hands stay), then a hand fit in data/gravelight/weapons/model_fit.json: offsets, pivots, part moves and scales (the Garand clip into the receiver, the Sawed-Off cut from the double), sight points, per-model eye relief, a procedural scope on the Springfield, per-slot offsets placing the T-068 M1911 kit on the OGA pistol. Every downloaded surface goes through one shader, assets/materials/shaders/weapon.gdshader (WeaponShader: object-space wood grain, edge wear, bluing, scratches, cavity grime) so the flat-palette OGA guns match the Sketchfab heroes; frost per map (Map 0 = 0.55), camo tints only the wood, the Crucible version is the same mesh with the Nachtglas veins. GLSettings greybox_guns / vm_shots --greybox restore the procedural guns for A/B; luger_p08, m1919, type100, ptrs41, panzerschreck and flamethrower stay procedural. The packs have no first-person animations, so the holder's choreography drives the real parts and every timing stays in weapons.json (test_weapon_glb proves GLB and greybox give identical reload/draw). Each survivor has generated arms (tools_src/blender/arms/make_arms.py -> assets/ours/arms/<char>.glb) on the unchanged IK rig, Dutch's as the fallback. ads_check 320/320. WeaponHolder carries the T-095 Effects hooks (zombie_hit, casing weapon id, first-person flash switch). Known rough: STG44 stock tint, Kar98k right hand large in ADS, Thompson bulky at hip.

T-093 (UPDATE_05 5, Map 0's environment): every Mats key gen_map00 draws with is on the CC0 library (materials.json generator.enabled, 27 keys; 10 new 1K Poly Haven sets plus snow_01 at 2K, replacing snow_02 whose twig marks read as cracks). The near terrain height-blends snow into frozen mud on the roads; the far hills' rock is textured. Library props are placed by the generator as transforms and built at load time by PropScatter ("LibraryProps", one MultiMesh per prop mesh, collision-free, over the existing invisible colliders, table tops at 0.75 m): tables, chairs, iron beds, nightstands, the casks and the KREUZWEG stack, the stall bucket, the chopping block. Spruces and bare birches (LOD0 <= 1.8k / LOD1 <= 150 tris), bushes and boulders are scripted in headless Blender (tools_src/blender/nature) with painted card textures (assets/ours/env/nature); they fill the edge tree line (NatureRows, no shadows) and the Landscape's near and mid forests (LOD0 inside 95 m); the far impostor cards stay. Half-timbering on the west and east faces, a snow lip on the eaves, split-rail fences. Lamp meshes, lights and the navmesh are unchanged (test_map00_art fingerprints both bakes). Round 15 bench: 68-75 FPS against master's 81-87, still over 60. Not done: window panes (the windows are broken and barricaded), a fieldstone yard wall (the yard is fenced, T-080), benches/bar stools/wardrobes/washstands (no library model), curtains and pictures. Maps 1 and 2 pick up the library materials the next time their generators run.

T-094 (UPDATE_05 5, world objects): the buyables and story machines are our own models. Headless Blender scripts (tools_src/blender/world/, shared wo_lib: Godot frame, bevels, metre UVs, slots named for MaterialLibrary/Mats keys) write assets/ours/world/<object>/model.glb with "Ours" paperwork; WorldArt instances them, maps the slots once per mesh, strips collision, and every script keeps its procedural build as the fallback (--no-world-art gives the before shots). The nine tonic cabinets keep their footprints and carry the tonic's PerkCap (drawn into a texture once) and stamp-lettered name on a lit sign, their own bottle on the shelf and in the hatch for the drink beat, and the JINGLES idle motion; unpowered they are dark. The Requisition Crate, an open ammo crate (still olive), six barricade plank variants, DoorArt plank doors for any opening with hinges, chain, padlock and a brass price plaque, the gate's hardware, wall-buys (the chalk plus the T-089 gun on two nails, dusty), the Crucible (brick hearth, iron firebox, Nachtglas buttresses), the bench, radio, lever and exfil beacon follow. test_world_objects_visual builds each object with art off and on and requires identical colliders and areas, no collision in the art, bounds near the old art's; the u05_objects capture set (23 cameras) is the before/after. No gen_map00 change. Over the prop budget: cabinets 5-8k tris, Crucible 6.8k (import LODs). Note: the raw Kar98k GLB has emissive 1/1/1 + alpha BLEND; the weapon shader hides it.

T-091: Modelled zombie bodies (UPDATE_05 3). The six base bodies (thin/medium/heavy x m/f) are built by script in headless Blender (tools_src/blender/characters/, tools/build_characters.sh), not downloaded: Quaternius UBC is CC0 but its rig and T-pose would have to be re-posed and re-weighted onto ZombieRig, at ~13k triangles. The rig is exported to rig.json (tools/export_rig.tscn). Each body is a skin-modifier block-out in an A-pose with build/sex radii from anim.json, subdivided and sculpted by displacement fields; the quad-sphere head has deep sockets with the violet glow, a real mouth on the Jaw bone, cheekbones, ears, clawed hands. Weights are computed per bone segment with side masks and unposed onto the rest pose; the body is decimated to ~7.2k triangles. One 1K skin set (albedo with frostbite, veins and lividity, alpha marking where the Nachtglas starts; normal; ORM) is Cycles-baked and transferred to every body. The nine kits are clothing fitted to each body (<= 3k triangles each), and the skin they cover is not drawn. ZombieBody.build_skinned merges a GLB into the same two surfaces (two draw calls) with Godot mesh LODs (bench median 77.8 FPS vs 67.1 for the old bodies, 64.7 without LODs). Gore pieces are cut by dominant bone; stump caps are sized per joint and build. HITBOXES and ragdoll shapes are unchanged and tested against the meshes. The crawl pose's hips, which sat 0.38 m under the floor, now lie on it (crawlers were invisible). The fictional armband emblem is added (soldier chance 0.3). ZombieBody.build_living and the human_base set give living humans for the people pass; docs/CHARACTER_PIPELINE.md.

T-090: The wonder weapons are ours, modelled from scratch in headless Blender (UPDATE_05 2): tools_src/blender/wonder/<id>.py on wonder_lib.py (lathes, bevelled boxes and prisms, swept wires and coils, rivets, raised lettering; one smart-UV atlas; Cycles bakes of AO and a curvature mask driving per-material procedural paint baked to a 2K albedo/normal/ORM set; GLB with external PNGs and an EEVEE turntable) into assets/ours/wonder/<id>/, registered by tools/wonder_register.py (models.json source "ours", paperwork, WEAPON_MODELS rows) and hand-fit in model_fit.json. WeaponGLB builds them; WonderLook puts the baked body in wonder_body.gdshader (the shared gun shader read painted steel, porcelain and rope as gun metal), glass/fluid/glow surfaces in their own shaders, shows the Crucible-only part (Corona's Halo, Mitternacht's Runes plus midnight-blue patina, Kugelblitz's Orb: same meshes) and drives new parts from the holder's state each frame: the Ashlight's Nachtglas fluid level equals the magazine and sloshes on a spring, the Totenglocke (rail-yard bell, bunk-tube frame, turret-clock crank) gets a striker that cocks over the wind and strikes on each toll, the Elmsfeuer's "crank" reload finally animates. Every timing stays in weapons.json (test_wonder_models); ads_check 6/6. The Siren Gramophone's world model spins its record and turns its crank while it plays, collision unchanged. The Ashlight's hip_rot is now [-4,22,8] (visual pose only). Design sheets and blockout stubs for Die Lange Nacht, The Return Policy, the Kinderhammer and the Omni-Blaster: docs/gravelight/WONDER_DESIGN_SHEETS.md. Rough: Kugelblitz subtle in hand; the Totenglocke's hammer small at the hip; no inspect animation (no inspect system).

T-092: The named people are modelled (UPDATE_05 4). Sixteen (Dutch, Peg, Deacon, Wren, Jakub; patrons Rother, Kubiak, Pohl, Trudi, Sobotta; survivors Wiesner, Brandt, Kroll; the Cousins Hammer/Reach/Alix) are built by script in headless Blender on the T-091 living body (build_people.py, make_face.py, make_kits.py kit_<person>; data/gravelight/art/people.json; tools/build_people.sh) into one GLB each (assets/ours/characters/people, Ours, 12-15k tris): per-person face knobs, eyes with lids, lash lines and brows, hair styles, skin paint (lips, cheeks, stubble, the Cousins' painted doll features), clothes and headgear from CHARACTERS.md and the Likeness Sheet defaults (COUSIN_1_NAME Mara, COUSIN_2_NAME Jo and the default looks), the skin under the clothes deleted. Six computed shape keys (blink, jaw_open, fear, determined, pain, smile). PeopleBodies merges a GLB into the zombie two-surface layout with blend shapes and mesh LODs. RemoteBody and PatronBody keep their pose solvers and timings and drive a PersonRig on ZombieRig (leg angles, IK to the same hand targets); the primitives stay as fallback and for -- --legacy-people. FaceDriver blinks, holds expressions by state (downed = pain, low health = fear, firing = determined) and flaps the jaw on the speaker's GLBus.vo_line_started. The Cousins wear their doll on their own skeleton (HomageBoss.modelled(); props, arms, HomagePoseMod and hitboxes unchanged and tested), never sexualized, Alix never blinks; a patron or survivor who turns rises as the same person (PeopleBodies.dress_risen). No collider, hitbox, capsule or timing changed. Not done: Bolt/Vendelin (not playable on Gravelight maps), Paul (not built yet). Known: squad gun hold reads as a ready stance, hair pokes under knit caps, necks long, co-op clients see the generic risen zombie.

T-098: The guns flagged rough in T-089/T-090 are fixed (user: "fix those issues you found"). The STG44's stock was the OGA palette's crimson cell, read by the shader as blued metal: a new model_fit shader.palette_remap {cell: rgb} repaints it walnut for that gun only (WeaponShader copies the palette image first). A new model_fit warp {Part: {z0, z1, dy}} bends one part of a one-piece mesh in rig space; it drops the STG44 stock 2.8 cm so its comb leaves the sight picture (the sight markers stay on the real sights). The Kar98k gets its own eye relief (0.42) and a lower, smaller firing hand. The Thompson gets its own hip pose (weapons.json feel, visual only). The winter camo is a capped, chipped, matte dirty whitewash (weapon.gdshader whitewash) instead of a near-white tint. The Totenglocke's striker Hammer and Pawl are scaled up about their pivots (part_scale). Kugelblitz has a brighter coil, arc and orb, and thin blue veins from a new vein function in wonder_body.gdshader. The Ashlight's capsule reload no longer swings the capsule past the camera; the fresh one comes up from below the grip (tested). The Kar98k Sketchfab GLB had emissiveFactor [1,1,1] and alphaMode BLEND, so plain loaders drew it glowing and see-through: tools/glb_sanitize.py fixed it, and convert_to_glb.py applies the same rules (--opaque for Sketchfab guns in intake_guns.py). vm_shots takes --camo=. ads_check 320/320; sight_clearance clear; no timings changed. Also the M1 Garand's clip sits 10 mm lower (it rose 7 mm above the sight line; SightClearance + test_sight_clearance), bots aim down the sights visibly (BotAim.ads_visual -> PF_ADS, the F6 bot's viewmodel, RemoteBody's shouldered pose; test_bot_ads), and a nearly dry main gun sends a bot to any reachable ammo box (it followed the player into the boxless cellar with an empty gun).

T-099: The T-092 known issues are fixed. Gun hold: RemoteBody measures each held gun (hold_of: a long gun turns about its butt, a pistol about its grip; sight height; support-grip axis and radius; firing grip) and places it on the body actually drawn (the rig shoulder and eye times the person's height). Long guns sit butt-tucked at the hip and in the shoulder pocket aimed, with the head lowered onto the stock; pistols are held out in two hands at chest height and raised to eye height aimed. Behind a long gun the body turns side-on so the support hand reaches the handguard, sliding back along it up to 20 cm if it still can't. hand_on_gun gives each wrist an offset and a hand basis, and PersonRig.drive turns the Hand bones to them (hand_basis / hand_w, wrist bend capped); the elbows hang down and out. Hair is trimmed under hats (make_face.under_hat). Necks read shorter through mesh-only reshaping (build_people.shorter_neck, people.json neck; no bone or hitbox moves). The zombie soldier, dockworker and nurse cuff bands take only arm vertices (no wrist-to-thigh ring); zombie_base, human_base and people GLBs were rebuilt. A risen person's severed pieces are cut from their own risen mesh (PeopleBodies.piece_mesh); the person id rides as field 9 of the zombie spawn event so co-op clients dress the same person; Zombie._reset_presentation clears the person meta on pool reuse.

T-100: The white blur near the camera was the first-person breath puff (eight 0.22 m sprites 0.3 m in front of the lens in every cold zone) plus world-fixed snow with no near guard: the local puff is small, faint and low (vfx.json atmosphere.breath.local) and every snow layer and the menu backdrop draw through snow_flake.gdshader (invisible inside fade[0] m, full past fade[1] m, never wider on screen than max_angle). The stable and hayloft are dressed (gen_map00 _barn_dressing: stalls, hay racks, mangers, troughs, a tack wall, hook lanterns, a ladder, hay scatter, loft sacks and library props), visual only, navmesh fingerprint unchanged; one small shadowless TackLamp. Map 0's round 1 now starts about 11.7 s in (look min 9 / max 10.5 s; look.interrupt_after 2.5 lets the cue's pounding cut a patron off mid-welcome); Kubiak and Rother are caught just past the threshold in the doorway's frame and Sobotta dies about 4 m in front of the spawn; the three pre-round chat lines play only if round 1 is held. Ultrawide: Settings > Video > Aspect (Keep 16:9 / Fill screen, default Fill) sets the root window's content_scale_aspect to expand; the HUD grid keeps to the real edges and past ui.json hud.max_aspect 2.4 to a centred 21:9 frame; menu pages, the pause page and the main menu centre in a 16:9 frame.

T-101: Voice acting with the cast (UPDATE 06 Part B). data/voice/cast.json maps every character to an actor (unfilled slots become slot_<code> so each slot still gets a script; PAUL = paul and THE SLENDER = devin, not in the game yet). tools/voice/make_scripts.py builds deterministic per-actor PDFs and checklists from lines.json, bosses/*.json and the B&S line sources, skipping on-screen text, ordering shouts last, with --since pickups; voice_scripts/RECORDING_GUIDE and VOICE_RELEASE. tools/voice/import.py: ffmpeg normalizes to 48 kHz mono; faster-whisper (the owner's existing ~/whisper-env, large-v3, out of process; GRAVELIGHT_WHISPER_MODEL/_PY override) adds word timestamps; spoken IDs match fuzzily (canon_id; every game ID tested unambiguous), takes split at pauses >= 0.65 s, a text-matching fallback handles files without IDs, scoring weighs text, clipping, noise and length; ffmpeg cleanup and loudness to voice_cast's -17.5 LUFS plus the delivery offset, -1.5 dBTP, per-character data/voice/fx.json from gen_vo's DSP; the OGG goes to the existing game path, the TTS is kept in gravelight/audio/vo_tts/ for revert, and the manifest records source/actor/take/hash.
