No consent.txt, nothing ships; withdrawn consent reverts to TTS. The review page writes data/voice/selections.json through a 127.0.0.1-only server. ElevenLabs is optional and off; cloning is refused without an explicit opt-in. gen_vo.py keeps recorded takes while the text is unchanged. Raw audio (voice_inbox), the work folder and the review page are git-ignored. Launcher: Voice review page, Map 0 voice test. 2,342 spoken lines across 46 slots.

T-102, 2026-10-26: UPDATE 07, Map 4 "Fernflug" design (docs/MAP4_FERNFLUG.md). The owner's beats are kept (Werk Thalbach → the Kranich takes off with the squad aboard → London hit by a second battery → the bomb run over the US coast, never all stopped → the crash at Groom Lake, where the Allies have their own Nachtglas program fed by Peg's orders and the Morgenrot crate → the rocket to the Moon → Darkmatter's relay → the squad cancels). Decisions made unattended: (1) `map03` stays reserved for Morgenrot (design-only, not built); Fernflug is `map04` with four act scenes `map04a_thalbach`, `map04b_kranich`, `map04c_groomlake`, `map04d_mondlicht`, chained after Totenhafen (a new exfil at round 40) with a `_doc` note on where Morgenrot slots in; `test_campaign`'s exact-chain assertion is updated. (2) Act round ranges are data-driven and act-relative (`act.length_rounds`), so a campaign arrival at round 41 and a standalone start both get Act 1 = 14 rounds; the HUD shows "ACT n · round". (3) The turn (round 15) and every later transition are round-clock exfils with egg shortcuts, never egg-gated; the autopilot key and the ignition key are handed out by the clock if their bosses were never fought. (4) Canon reconciliation, one line: Morgenrot's ABORT stays canon and the reserve rocket went to the Moon afterwards (Mondfahrt 6b after an Abort), which is what left the smile and lit the light; the light was Darkmatter's relay RR-7, there since 1938 as the probe's uplink, and cutting it is what stops the dead on Earth. (5) Jakub rides along only when the campaign carries him; his canon reading is that he turned back at Sankt Aurel because the transfer lists named Thalbach. (6) Named people are all fictional: Wittkamp (designer), Riedl/the Bordwart, Beck/the Kranführer, Jette Maurer (Skyhook's human), Hale (Peg's "Mr. Hale", the Totenhafen consignee), Dee Marchetti (Magpie's human), Rourke/the Test Pilot; Sable Vantage attends through her executive machine and is never named (CANON rule). (7) The Allies' program is a Darkmatter customer too (the Kranich's final waypoint is its delivery point), which is why the wing crashes where it does. (8) The bomb run: 6 racks, one every 35 s (45 solo), rack 1 (Boston) always drops, 2 of racks 2–6 are chalk-marked duds seeded per match, the rest are jammed or not; flags drive the Earth in the sky and the epilogue cards. (9) The finale's win is a vote + a key with no time limit after the boss "escalates"; nobody human is ever killed in the finale; the bad ending is a renewal timer (6 min from the boss's second phase, or act round 45) and leaves the match running in a "Retained" state. (10) Two perks: Skyhook Sekt (ELEVATION, DMS-VP-ELV-08: air control, a mid-air hop, safe landings, faster slides, push resistance) and Magpie Mead (RECOVERY, DMS-VP-REC-09: kills drop ammo/Scrip/grenades), both with tiers II/III; Magpie's cabinet is an Allied-painted Packmule cabinet with Vendelin's panel scraped clear. (11) The wonder weapon is the Lodestone (lift / slam / fling; Crucible "Escape Velocity"), buildable from three parts in Act 3; the fling into vacuum is the "into space" kill. (12) Four bosses, one per act, each dropping a key the clock also hands out; an HB4 homage slot is reserved in Act 3 and disabled until the owner names a likeness. (13) Phase 0 found only three built maps: Morgenrot is design-only, so its "extra attention" item is recorded as not applicable in docs/QA_REPORT_U07.md. (14) Blender's mirrors are blocked by the cloud network policy; the `bpy` wheel from PyPI is tried as a substitute, else the Lodestone ships as a procedural greybox viewmodel with its Blender script committed for the PC.

T-103, 2026-10-26: UPDATE 07 Phase 0, Totenhafen fixes (docs/QA_REPORT_U07.md Q-03, Q-10, Q-11). The quay's two landing stages were timber decks over a shingle and sea bed that had no colliders, so a zombie shoved off a stage fell out of the world (y < -15 at (22..26, 0, 0)); they now carry hidden kerb rails on their three open sides (dressed as rope on the posts), named Stage_10/Stage_24 so a test can find them. The BAR and Thompson wall-buys hung on the wrong faces of the pier lockers (over the basin, and inside a locker) and were unreachable; they hang on the lockers' free faces now. The playtest bot's errands judge a wall-buy by the side it faces (local -Z, since its prompt Area sits on its origin), so it no longer walks to the far side of a wall and stalls its shopping. The AI soak, the route tool, the training routes and the player-bot plan cover Totenhafen (`--scenario=totenhafen`, data/gravelight/ai/routes_map02.json, a map02 plan in player_bot.json). test_map02_edges holds the three rules: kerbs on the stages, navmesh in front of every board, every authored route on its navmesh. Found by the Phase 0 playtest (tools_src/playtest_gl.tscn --map=map02 --bots=3).

T-104, 2026-10-26: Map 4 Act I build (UPDATE 07 Phase 2.1). Map 4's acts share one generator base (gravelight/tools_src/gen_map04_lib.gd, the gen_map02 idiom lifted into overridable hooks) so each act is tables plus rooms; gen_map04a.gd writes gravelight/maps/map04_fernflug/map04a.tscn. The json for an act is data/gravelight/<first id token>.json (map04a), so ClimbLink, MapScan and Campaign derive the table from the id's first `_` token instead of substr(0,5); story-prop/intel keys come from a `map_key` field (old maps keep their hard-coded keys). Rounds on an act map are act-relative (RoundManager.act_start is 1 standalone or the arrival round; act_round()/act_label(); the HUD shows "ACT I · n" under the stamp). campaign.json gained a `standalone` list so a built act bakes map data and is launcher-playable before it joins the chain (the chain changes only with the boarding step). Buyable doors can wait on a flag (door.gd requires_flag): the Great Hangar opens on act round 12 or the three Fernflug files. The Fliegeralarm runs on the Bellringer scheduler through a ZombieDirector.special_handler hook (searchlight-marked impact circles with a 4 s telegraph, a shockwave that hurts players and kills zombies, landers rising on the circles, a Resupply on the last one); landers wear the soldier kit until an aircrew kit is modelled. Der Kranführer is a HomageBoss that never walks: it rides the hangar's crane rail at +12 m (casing drops, the hook sweep at 60%, parked over the ramp with a casing ring at 30%, the cab shot from the catwalks), data in bosses/map04.json, reserved at act round 13 and summoned by the act's ActController, with the Cousins' bots-alone scaling, a 360 s fail-safe and the hangar_key flag as its drop. Verified in the worktree: full GUT (30,279 asserts), smoke_map04a PASS, check_mapdata PASS, a round-8 playtest with 3 bots and no nav snags.

T-105, 2026-10-26: UPDATE 07 Map 4 systems: the two tonics and the Lodestone (docs/MAP4_FERNFLUG.md sections 7-8; branch u07-perks-lodestone). Skyhook Sekt (`skyhook_sekt`, 2500, ELEVATION DMS-VP-ELV-08) and Magpie Mead (`magpie_mead`, 3000, RECOVERY DMS-VP-REC-09) are full tonics on the T-075 tier system (Thermal / Stall Turn, Scavenger / Pawn Shop); the cap stays 6 and there are now 11 tonics (test_perks counts 11, test_perk_caps counts perks.size()). Decisions: (1) Skyhook's hop is one extra jump at 60% of jump_velocity, re-armed on every landing (tier II: on a slide too); it never beats a mantle (a jump that can catch a ledge mantles, as before); safe landing drops land_speed_loss, the head dip and 70% of the shake. (2) A new outside-force API is shared by everything that shoves a body: `Player.external_push(v, s, kind)` (Skyhook's push_resist 0.3 / pull_resist 0.5; the Lodestone's holder ignores pulls: Ballast; bots take it through `push_velocity()` in their adapter) and `Zombie.external_push` (a fling carries landing damage, x1.5 into a wall, and a kill plane `Zombie.kill_plane_y` = -15 that the act jsons may lower: a flung zombie below it is a "returned" kill worth economy.json `returned_kill` 100) plus `Zombie.lift / drop / slam` (a new State.LIFTED appended to the enum, so co-op state numbers are unchanged). (3) Magpie's drops are a small world object (MagpieDrop) rolled on the killer's kill (12% / 18% on heads and melee), holder-only until Pawn Shop; its salvage bonus multiplies the economy's dog-tag roll for that killer only. (4) Magpie's cabinet is Packmule's shell under olive paint with Dee's pencil line under Vendelin's sign-off; both machines are procedural (shapes `glider` / `bandit`) until the PC builds them (perk_cabinets.py tables carry them, on the apothecary shell). (5) The jingles are gen_audio sketches (a waltz, a Western swing); the 18 first-drink lines (DUT/PEG/DEA/WRN x2 x2, JAK refuses) are extracted and listed in the manifest as "bake on the PC"; Fernet's missing PERK token was fixed on the way. (6) The Lodestone is `mode: gravity` with a `gravity` block beside the Totenglocke's `bell` pattern (a RefCounted of pure static rules, the holder's tick, bot hooks): tap LIFT (a well 5 m ahead, 4 m wide, 8 zombies, 2.5 s, limb x1.5), tap SLAM (2.2 x health(r) and a 2 m wave), the hold ending = DROP (0.8x), alt-fire FLING (a 0.6 s wind-up that throws by itself; 12 m, 30 m in low gravity, landing damage); Escape Velocity pulls from 10 m, lifts 12, flings 18 m with 2 bounces. Bosses are staggered, never lifted. The kill-plane half of "into vacuum" is built; the vacuum-zone half waits for Act 4's zone flag. (7) The buildable system is generalized by id (BuildablePart.BUILDABLES: the quest, its random key, the parts, a label; Workbench.buildable_id); the Totenglocke's callers are unchanged (parts_held() defaults to it); the console gives `ls_part_*` and `lodestone_parts`. (8) The Lodestone's model is built through the bpy shim (Blender 5.2.3 as a module; the project's pinned 5.1.2 is unreachable from the cloud); its greybox fallback keeps the same markers; the sight line runs under the lens (ADS is a tighter well, not a zoom). (9) Audio: five Lodestone sounds and four perk sounds are gen_audio sketches. Known gaps for the acts: the cabinets, the parts and the workbench are not placed anywhere yet; no quest steps; GameState.gravity_scale does not exist yet (Lodestone.gravity_scale() reads it if present).

T-105b, 2026-10-26: the Lodestone's lens hangs UNDER the yoke's bar, not on top of it. The first model put the lens and its cradle on a head post above the bar with the sights under them; `test_sight_clearance` (every GLB weapon: no mesh above the SightPoint-FrontSight line near the bore) reported the Cradle +190 mm, the Sphere +149 mm and the Yoke's head +20 mm over the line, i.e. the lens would have filled the ADS picture. Options weighed: exempt the gun (meta "scope" is the only exemption the check knows and it turns on the HUD scope picture, wrong for a lens you watch open), a 30 cm ladder sight over the lens (ugly, and the aim pitch with it), or re-hang the lens. Chosen: the "lantern on a pole" reading the design already uses (MAP4_FERNFLUG.md section 8): the bar runs out over the lens to a bead at its tip (z -0.44), the rear notch sits on the bar at z -0.02, the sight line is y about 0.054 along the bar's top, the lens centre is (0, -0.11, -0.34) hung from a brass fork to the cradle's side pivots, and the relay coil's rings were trimmed to 28 mm so they stay under the line. Changed together: `tools_src/blender/wonder/lodestone.py` (rebuilt GLB), the greybox `WeaponModels._w_lodestone`, `model_fit.json` (muzzle, sights, pivots, length 0.56), WEAPONS.md section 28 (moved to the end: the first draft had been numbered 25, a number the Armorer's Bench already had). Gameplay numbers (the well, the lift, charges) are untouched. The bots' spending brain also gets `weapon_tiers.lodestone` 6.5 in `data/gravelight/ai/economy.json` (the Totenglocke's tier; `test_bot_brain` requires a tier for every base gun).

T-106, 2026-10-26: UPDATE 07 Phase 0, Crossroads Inn (docs/QA_REPORT_U07.md Q-14). The navmesh bake left a 0.2 m walkable island on every table top (a 1.2 m table eroded by the 0.5 m agent radius), so a bot that climbed one was "on the navmesh": its path never left the table and the stuck ladder random-walked on it for minutes (Wren, 37 stuck events in 4 minutes at (16.5, 0.8, 11.5)). Map 0's bake now drops regions under 8 cells (0.5 m², `region_min_size`), bots only mantle where the landing has navmesh under it (BotLocomotion.mantle_land_ok; vaults still land on the floor), and a bot marooned above the navmesh steps down onto it after 2 s (BotLocomotion.marooned, logged as a drop, never a teleport; tuning in data/gravelight/ai/locomotion.json "stuck"). The player's vault and climb are unchanged. The loco lab/smoke gained `furniture` and `marooned` phases; the hay-stair-head rail hug (two 1.5 s stretches in round 20) was judged not worth geometry. Before/after on the same seed: 42 stuck events / 19.8 s (37 on the table) → 3 / 18.6 s, none on furniture, the round cap reached. Maps 1 and 2 use the same bake defaults and may carry furniture-top islands (the bot-side rules protect them now); their bakes get the same region_min_size when their generators next run.

T-107, 2026-10-26: UPDATE 07 Phase 0, Kaltenbrunn bots (docs/QA_REPORT_U07.md Q-13, Q-15, Q-16, Q-17). Bots alone bought nothing on Kaltenbrunn and wiped in the yard at round 9: the power plan asked the navmesh for a path with only the open zones' layers, and on a map with one region per zone that is a partial path to the yard's corner, never a door; the squad now plans the way to the switch across every zone but the lift's (data/gravelight/ai/economy.json power.plan_avoid_zones, since bots cannot ride the lift) and buys the nearest closed door that path crosses, as Map 0's single region always let it; Map 1's training routes name the doors they need (requires_doors). The Admin stairs got closed strings on both sides (the navmesh climbed the ramp's low east edge and a bot stood against its face for 16 minutes at (-11.8, 0, 27.4)). A chasing zombie whose path end stays more than 6 m from every living player for 45 s once nothing is left to spawn is relocated to a ground spawn near the players (rounds.json "stranded"; the bot-mode soak's round 12 hung 140 minutes on crawlers jammed at the Admin door): generic, for every map and every Map 4 act, bosses excluded, counted in the soak's STALL report. Zombie.can_walk_to and the soak's stall report query the navmesh with the open zones' mask (the default mask is one layer, which made the stall report blame a connected navmesh). The yard's "stuck" spots were bots body-blocked at the window stands while the whole match stayed in the yard, not geometry. Before → after (same seeds): bots alone r9 / 0 Scrip spent / stuck 101 / teleports 7 → r12 / 28,800 spent (four doors) / 42 / 3; the open-map run's stuck 19 / 42 s → 4 / 4.4 s. Reaching the power in time is the next tuning item (they still wipe at 12 with ~66k unspent).

### T-108 — The boarding (Act I → Act II): begin_at, the chain, exfil flags and stages, cutscene fx
Campaign runs now start anywhere: `Campaign.begin_at(map_id, round, kit)` (the launcher's "Map 4 — full run / skip to Act II–IV", the console's `act <n>` and `campaign begin <map> [round]`) loads the act with an arrival kit (`campaign.json arrival_kits`: Act II = Thompson II + MP40, Ironhide/Second Wind/Marathon, 3 grenades; Scrip from the launcher's round table) through the same hooks a carried arrival uses, and resumes the round count (`resume_at`) with the act's own numbering (`Campaign.act_first_round`: Act I 1, II 15, III 21, IV 30), so a mid-act start still reads "ACT I · 14". The chain is map00 → map01 → map02 → map04a → map04b (Totenhafen's beacon at round 40 goes to Thalbach; Morgenrot's `map03` slot is noted, not built). An exfil block may now carry `trigger_flag` (the egg's `{"exfil_now": true}` quest action lights the beacon before its round; the console's `exfil now` does the same), `requires_flag`/`requires_text` (the Kranich's autopilot takes the engineer's key), a first `stage_flag`/`stage_prompt`/`stage_hold`/`vo_stage` hold before the vote, `hold_seconds` for the vote itself, and `act_round` (act-relative, wins over the nominal `round` on an act map). Cutscene shots gained fx `ramp` (the Kranich's ramp hinges down), `kranich_takeoff`, `crash` (the wing diorama slides in with dust and shake) and `launch`; the map04a hangar dressing was regrouped into kept nodes `Kranich` and `KranichRamp` for them. The crash cutscene was trimmed from 46 to 40 s to keep T-033's 20–40 s rule (shots rescaled, nothing cut).

### T-109 — Act II "der Kranich": the arena, the flight systems, the Bordwart, the London window, the bomb run
Seven zones in a flying wing (aft bomb bay → centre body → port/starboard wing crawlspaces → port engine room / gun blisters → flight deck by two walkways; six doors, 6,750 Scrip, the cockpit pair one link group behind the aux bus and the `autopilot_key` flag); the aux bus is the power, the Crucible sits in the centre body, eight perks with the Skyhook Sekt's debut. Everything in the act block and in `flight` is act-relative (London act round 3 = match 17, the Bordwart 4, the bomb run 5, the fallback key and the autopilot 6 = match 20). The FlightController (data: `map04b.json flight`) runs turbulence (a warned lateral push on everyone through the T-105 `external_push`, a 4° camera roll, a stagger; Skyhook's push_resist applies), fighter strafes (three tracer lines over the zone the squad stands in, never the centre body or the cockpit; they kill zombies and blow breach panels they cross), breaches (suction toward the hole, zombies through it die, sealed by a 2 s hold, the Deacon trait shortens it), climbers (Bordschützen at the blister rims and open breaches), the Druckabfall special round (every panel in sequence, climbers instead of windows, the Resupply on the clear) and the bomb run (racks 1..6 = Boston..Washington; rack 1 always drops; two chalked duds among 2..6 per match; a 2.5 s jam hold, bots help; results as boolean flags `bomb_<city>_hit|saved|dud` + `bomb_run_done` for Act IV's ending). Zombies on their way through a window are never pushed (a push stranded them with no window: fixed in `Zombie.external_push`). The Bordwart is a tethered HomageBoss at the engineer's station (30,000 HP scaled; pull toward the core window → vent; the cable lash with the cable a ×2.5 weak point from 60 %; overspeed from 30 %: lights out, banks every 15 s, climbers through the shaft, only the gauges take full damage); he drops the autopilot key, or the round clock lays it on his station at act round 6. Two round-softlock fixes came out of the Act II playtest and apply to the Kranführer too: a Map 4 boss is only summoned once his arena zone is open (the ActController pushes his round on by one until then: the squad that never opens the engine room gets the fallback key), and a boss's retreat now pays both of his alive counts (reserve_alive + take_extra_spawn; a death already paid both, a retreat paid one and the round never ended). The playtest bot no longer reports a tethered Homage boss as an idle zombie, nor a player pinned by a bank or a breach as stuck (`Player.is_pushed`). The bay's rack bases moved 0.4 m inboard so the hatch-repair lane along the side walls is 2 m wide. The London window is a 50 s in-engine runner (the aft blister's camera, three battery plumes, searchlights and air-bursts over a far plate, violet snow, 4 s of silence, the Operator, one line per character, Peg last), skippable after the first viewing, and it sets `london_seen`. The autopilot is the exfil: key → unlock stage (2 s) → the 6 s rip-out vote → the crash cutscene → Act III (the "campaign continues" screen until map04c exists).

### T-114 · UPDATE 07 · Map 4's main egg "Kündigung", Acts I–II, and the carry across the acts (branch u07-egg)

**The shape.** One quest file, `data/gravelight/quests/map04_kuendigung.json`, spans the four acts (`"maps"` lists map04a..map04d). Every step names the act it lives on (`"act"`). Two ways into a later act exist and both are handled in `QuestManager.load_quest`: a launcher skip ("skip to Act II") marks every earlier act's steps complete (the act's index comes from `RoundManager.act`, or the map's `act` block before the match starts); a campaign arrival carries the real progress instead. The carry is `QuestManager.carry_snapshot(next_map)` (done steps, open progress, the rolled randoms, the save points, only for quests whose `maps` list names the next map) in `Campaign.snapshot` as `"quests"`, applied by `Campaign.restore_quests` after MapRoot loaded the map's quests. World flags travel the same way: `campaign.json "carry_flags"` lists exact names and `prefix*` patterns (`london_seen`, `bomb_*`, `hangar_key`, `hangar_code_known`, `peg_*`, `dee_*`...); `Campaign.carried_flags` is pure, `Campaign.restore_flags` runs after `match_started` reset the flags. Flags the next act must not inherit (power_on, tunnel_running, under_ramp) are simply not listed.

**Act I steps (Werk Thalbach).** s1 the Hauptschalter (flag power_on). s2 the three Fernflug files (QuestNote kind "file"): each shows two digits of a six-digit seeded extension (`"random": {"files": {"type": "digits", "count": 6}}`; the note reads `random_key "map04_kuendigung:files"` with `digit_from` 0/2/4), so the files spell the code in order and the code is the same on every act of one match. s3 the canteen's PA board: the Kaltenbrunn Switchboard class got `quest`/`step`/`sign`/`prompt_text` exports instead of a second class; a wrong digit resets the line (BELL_WRONG category), the full line opens Wittkamp's extension and sets `hangar_code_known`. s4 the wind tunnel (new `WindTunnel` interactable, `gravelight/world/map04/wind_tunnel.gd`): with the power on the fan runs 20 s (30 s cooldown), pushes everyone in the test section along +X through `external_push` (Skyhook's push_resist applies), staggers the dead every 4 s, and holds the flag `tunnel_running`; the step is a `kill_count` with the new `zone` + `world_flag` filters (20 kills + 5 per extra player, in F_WINDKANAL while the fan runs). Reward: `perk_tier_grant` Skyhook Sekt tier II for the match (a new action: the flag `perk_tier_free_<perk>` = tier, owners are raised now, `PerkManager.grant` applies it to later buyers). s5 the Kranführer: a `kill_condition` in H_HALLE with `world_flag under_ramp`; the boss sets `under_ramp` when he enters his park phase over the ramp and clears it when he retreats, so only a kill while he is parked hands `hangar_key`; a kill on the rails runs the `fail` banner and the step stays open (he returns after 2 rounds, as before). s6 the ramp switch (new `QuestInteract`, a plain quest switch with a locked prompt naming what is missing): `interact` steps may now carry `requires_flags`; the ramp needs `hangar_key` + `hangar_code_known` (+ s4 done) and fires `exfil_now`, lighting the boarding beacon before round 15.

**Act II steps (der Kranich).** s7a the three crew tags (QuestNote kind "tag": Riedl, Haas, Pohl; the crew was taken out, not lost). s7 the Bordwart killed at his station (D_TRIEBWERK; his own drop sets `autopilot_key`). s8 the London window: the step's `on_start` fires the new `act_event` action (`ActController.fire_event` → `FlightController.start_london()`), so the egg brings the window forward of its act-round-3 clock; the flag `london_seen` completes it. s9 the bomb run, fired by `act_event bomb_run` 60 s later (`bomb_run_done`). s10 the autopilot: `on_start` lights the cradle (`exfil_now`); the exfil's new `done_flag` (`autopilot_ripped`, set in `_depart` before the snapshot) completes the step, and the carry takes the finished Act I–II graph to Groom Lake. Acts III and IV get their steps when their maps exist (the same file; `"maps"` already names them).

**The side egg.** `map04a_jette.json`: three glider models (QuestNote kind "model") on Jette Maurer's desk through the office hatch (QuestInteract `jette_hatch`); reward the logbook Archive entry INT_M4_JETTE, Skyhook tier II for the match, the "Jette" calling card.

**Bots.** Every step carries a `bot_task` (collect / interact_sequence / escort / turn_key); the boss steps are `escort` (cover the human: the fight's positioning is the human's), `bot_knowledge` names the files (on_collect) and the code (on_vo WIT_M4A_SWITCH_01).

**Why these calls.** One quest file rather than one per act: the egg is one story and the Objectives tab shows it whole; the per-step `act` keeps a skip honest without a second data path. Flags by allow-list rather than everything: an act's own machinery flags (power, running traps, a parked boss) would mislead the next act's systems. `requires_flags` on interact steps rather than a flag step before the switch: the prompt can say what is still missing. The PA board reuses Switchboard rather than a new class: same board, same bot task.

**Tests.** `test_kuendigung.gd` (20 tests: validation and bot tasks, the seeded code and the files' slices, the PA line, act skips, the carry round trip incl. open progress, carried_flags, the ramp gate and its prompt, the tunnel's pure rules and filters, the crane driver's condition, perk_tier_grant, the side egg, every quest object by the navmesh on both generated scenes, the files' digit order). `smoke_map04_egg` plays Acts I–II end to end by console (power → files → PA → tunnel kills → the Kranführer twice → Jette → the ramp → the boarding → the tags → London by the egg → the Bordwart → the bomb run → the autopilot → the crash). Objectives text for every step (test_objective_log), VO rows in VO_SCRIPT §14.7 (41 lines, in the manifest's missing list for the PC bake).

**The hangar key moved.** Act I's build (T-104) had the Kranführer drop `hangar_key` on any death (bosses/map04.json `flag`); with the egg that would make s5's "over the ramp" condition meaningless. Now his death flag is `kranfuehrer_down` (nothing reads it yet; test_kranfuehrer and smoke_map04a updated) and `hangar_key` comes only from the egg's s5 (a kill while `under_ramp` holds). Nothing else used the key: the hangar door itself opens by `halle_unlocked` (act round 12 or the files), as before, so the round-clock boarding at act round 15 is unchanged.

# DECISIONS drafts from the Act IV builder (append to docs/DECISIONS.md; PROGRESS.md untouched)

**T-112 — Act IV's map-wide physics: gravity as data, the vacuum and the AIR gauge, the Earth.** The three gravity constants (`Player.GRAVITY` 14, the bot adapter's 14, `Zombie.GRAVITY` 18) are now fallbacks: the live values are `GameState.g_player()` / `g_zombie()` = `world.json "gravity"` × `GameState.gravity_scale`, set by MapRoot from the act json (`gravity_scale: 0.38` on Mondlicht, 1.0 everywhere else; `reset_match` clears it). The jump's take-off velocity is kept (`jump_velocity_exponent 0`): at 0.38 g a jump goes 2.6× higher and hangs 2.6× longer, as the design says; the orchestrator's note had "velocity × 1/sqrt(scale)", which gives 6.9× (7.6 m) and hits every dome ceiling, so it was not used, and the exponent is data if anyone wants it. Landing shake uses the real fall (`h = v²/2g` with the live g), so Moon landings are soft without a perk. Slides go 1.4× farther by `slide_distance_mult` on the act block. Vacuum: `ZoneVolume.vacuum`, `AirPost` (a 2 m circle of air), `Player.air` (25 s, refilled in 3 s in pressure or at a post, 20/s at zero, `vacuum` status refreshed every tick), the HUD's AIR gauge top centre and a visor rim, a low-pass on the SFX and Ambience buses for the local player (the suit's mix), and a bot short of air (under 8 s) marks every vacuum volume as a blackboard danger zone so it leaves. Spawns on a vacuum point are always the Suited (`vacuum_only`); the Solar Flare (the act's special round, `ZombieDirector.special_handler` like the Fliegeralarm) fails the posts, flickers the Earth and raises the Suited on the crater rim (plain Gängers through the domes' points while the yard is shut), the last wave's point taking the Resupply. The Earth is `earth_sky.gd`: a sky sphere with a dark hemisphere, violet points for London and every bombed city from the flags, a spread that grows per act round from act round 3 to a 60 % cap, `cut()` (freeze + fade over 60 s), `renew()` (the night side violet in 10 s), `flicker()`.

**T-113 — The finale: the columns, the Account Manager, the two endings, the Retained state.** The three retention columns (`RetentionColumn`, Interactable) each quote a fine-print panel (Skyhook §1–§6, Magpie §1–§6, Fernet §6); a read is a 4 s hold that plays the reader's `TERMS_READ_N` line (`GLVO.say_as`; the Operator's `OPR_M4D_TERMS_N` when nobody can read) and sets `column_N_dark`; a Lodestone shot at the cap sets `cap_lifted_N` and makes the read 1 s. Every read wakes the dead (a burst at the hall's ground points, Backorders by id when the Act III build defines them, Gängers until then). The sign (`CancelSign`, one Label3D per letter) drops letters per stage; the design's third stage was misaligned with the text and reads "N B D  C N  LS" now (7 letters left). Three dark: `account_open`, the Account's door (2000). The doors: 750 → 1000 (the yard airlock) → 1250 (the store, with its yard airlock as the second way in: one link group, one price) → 1500 → 2000; the design's 7,750 counted the store's airlock as a second purchase, the player pays 6,500 (T-112's economy note). The Account Manager (`AccountManager`, HomageBoss) is summoned when F_CORE opens (not on a round; the MondlichtController cuts the horde to horde_fraction and runs the bots-alone scaling), stands at her station, and never dies: ONBOARDING (the welcome arms, a 180° sweep like the Bordwart's lash; the console shielded), RENEWAL at 70 % (the renewal clock, 360 real seconds on the controller with the Operator's warnings at 3:00 and 1:00; the trial tray: every tonic for a minute, then all of them stripped; her Reinholt line when Dutch is present), COLLECTIONS at 35 % (the pricing stamp: 90 damage and a tenth of the account; the Kranich's crew through the pad; three shield-cells orbiting her as `ShieldCell` Zombies, so every weapon and the Lodestone's lift / fling work on them unchanged, ×0.2 damage while any lives, regrown after 40 s unless carried into the vacuum shaft over the console, which is for good). Each phase is "presented" for 3 s (and again every 20 s): the torso takes ×2.5. At 0 % (or the 360 s failsafe) she escalates: folds, `manager_escalated`, the console's cover, 2,500 Scrip per player and a Resupply; the round count lets her go. The console (`AccountConsole`): cover (2 s hold, `OPR_M4D_SURE_01` once), the vote (every living human; solo one hold), the key (`cancel_ending.gd` with the key-turner's id: their cancel line, the relay cut, the Manager's "I don't have a form for this", the Earth cut, six stock shots as greybox dioramas built far below the relay plus one per burned city, the Operator's last line over black, the epilogue cards from `cards_m4.json` by flags and presence, Wren's count, Dutch's last word, the credits, then GAME_OVER with the profile's `endings_seen` "kuendigung" and 40,000 XP). The clock at zero or the act's fallback round (16, match 45) with the Account never opened: `renewal_ending.gd` (40 s in the player's view: the lines, the Earth renewed, the squad's lines by presence, the AUTO-RENEWAL card) then the Retained state: `GameState.retained` (the HUD's violet tint, perks kept on a down in PerkManager, Second Wind removed, `rounds.json retained_health_mult` 1.25, the air posts dead, a Dust Storm every round, the Manager folded, `egg_closed`, `endings_seen` "renewed"); the match goes on. Console verbs `renewal now|clock` and `cancel now`. The act names its own first match round (`act.start_round 30`, `Campaign.act_first_round` honours it) so the skip-to-Act-IV start numbers right whether or not Act III sits in the chain; the chain tests assert "Mondlicht is last and follows a Map 4 act", not an exact list. Nobody human dies in the finale; the Woman in Violet is never named; the world text never says the two forbidden words (test_endings checks every card and credit line).

## T-110 — Map 4 Act III "Groom Lake, Nevada" (UPDATE 07, Phase 2 step 5)
Branch `u07-act3`. The design (`docs/MAP4_FERNFLUG.md` §5) is built as `gravelight/tools_src/gen_map04c.gd` → `gravelight/maps/map04_fernflug/map04c.tscn` with `data/gravelight/map04c.json` (`map_key "4c"`, act block index 3 / numeral III / 9 act rounds / `special_kind dust_storm` / the Test Pilot on act round 6 in the lab / the rocket fallback on act round 9). Seven zones on the ladder 750 → 1000 → 1000 → 1250 → 1500 (power) → 2000, 8,500 over the seven door ids: the wreck (spawn, the fuselage interior, the torn hull as the windows), the open salt (a berm all round, the six burrow points, the impeller core where it rolled), the hangar (the cannibalized bomber as loop 2, Dee's cage, Hale's glass office, the British crate, the relay object), the motor pool (the generator = the power, the fuel dump live-wire trap, the service lane east to the hangar's side door), the radar rise (the tower step = the Jumpjockeys' launch points, the shack), the underground lab at y −6 (the hatch ramp from the motor pool, the cable trench ramp from the radar rise, both doors one link group at 1,500 with the power; three specimen rooms, the Nevada stone's pit, the workbench at the hall's head) and the test silo at y −6 (the control room with the Crucible and the console, the open shaft with the LANTERN rocket as a kept dressing group named `Rocket` so `fx: launch` lifts it). Decisions:
- **The dawn.** The lib's night sky is overridden in `build_lights()`: a warm sun, thin fog, a pale horizon. The wreck burns (flames, smoke, a moving damage zone `groom.fire`).
- **The salt slab leaves the underground footprint out** (five slabs) so the lab, the silo and the trench are reachable; two same-zone navmesh regions (the motor pool and its lane; the hatch mouth) get explicit links, as the Kranich's walkway did (T-109).
- **The act's dead are tagged kits on pooled Gängers, not new pooled kinds** (`gravelight/world/map04/groom_controller.gd`, the Bordschütze pattern of T-109): no change to `zombie.gd`. BURROWERS = Gängers rising at the lakebed's burrow points, which the controller walks to 6–12 m of a player on the salt every 3 s (the staged rise, hitboxes off until the head is out, is the tell and the "immune underground"). CANARIES (lab + hangar from act round 3, 35 % of spawns, max 4): health ×1.3, torso ×1.5, a glass hood on the head; a head kill leaves a `GasCloud` (3 m, 6 s, 15/s, the `gas_slow` status). JUMPJOCKEYS (radar rise + silo from act round 4, 40 %, max 3): health ×1.2, a rig on the spine; within 6 m of a launch point (or after 8 s anyway) they leap at a player 6–20 m away as an `external_push` arc of 1.6 s; any damage in the air kills them ("the rig fails"); the landing hits 30 and shoves within 2 m. BACKORDERS (from act round 5 once `part_ls_part_coil` or `relay_coil_taken` is set, 20 %, max 2): physics off and moved straight at the target through walls while phased (no collision, no hitboxes, a translucent tint), solid for 2.5 s within 2.2 m of the target when the spot is on the navmesh, forced solid by a Lodestone lift, 150 Scrip on top of the kill; the stamp over its head prints the target's Scrip when it solidifies. The design's "attacks on the way up", "frag kills it underground", "×2 in the air" and "Overclock ignores the phase" are not built (the first two need the rise system, the last two a damage hook on pooled Gängers); logged as not built.
- **DUST STORM** is the act's special round through the director's `special_handler` hook (T-104's Fliegeralarm pattern): the fog closes to sand, Burrower packs of two rise at the burrow points every 2 s from the round's queue, the last burrow is where the Resupply lands. The special round's cadence stays the global `rounds.json` gap (the design's "every 5–7 act rounds" is not a per-act knob).
- **THE TEST PILOT** (`gravelight/enemies/map04/testpilot.gd` on HomageBoss, `bosses/map04.json "testpilot"`, 32,000 solo): the leap is a scripted arc (collision off in the air, the crane's rail idiom of T-104), a 3 m / 100 shockwave on landing (2,500 to zombies), 4 s vulnerable on the ground, ×0.5 in the air and ×0.25 between; at 60 % the next leap goes to the lake anchor (flag `pilot_on_lake`), leaps of 40 m, a landing on a player grabs and drags them 3 s toward the next anchor (the HB grab helper); at 30 % the next landing is the Stall (6 s crawl at ×2 damage, then ×1.3 speed). A stagger in the air (the Lodestone's lift staggers bosses) is the catch: he drops where he is, takes 3 × the round's slam damage, flag `slammed_once`. Reward 1,500 each, a Resupply, `ignition_key`. Console damage (`kind world`) is never scaled, so `kill_all` and `debug_kill` work.
- **The Lodestone's parts** (two spots each) go live from the quest's `ls_parts` pick when `map04_kuendigung` is loaded; when no quest is loaded the controller seeds one spot per part itself, so the act is buildable on its own. The workbench is the Lodestone's (`buildable_id`). `Zombie.kill_plane_y` is −25 on this map (the shaft, the trench), restored on exit.
- **Hale is a voice through the glass**, never a body: a `StoryProp` (the program log) on the office front, lines on the hangar opening and on the two documents. Dee is a voice from the cage: `StoryRadio` with four lines, flag `dee_radio_done` → the controller opens her side door for free.
- **Peg's choice** is a `CarryObject` (the core, the Totenhafen Ballast rules, `ballast` status) + a `CarryCradle` with `choice` (the office hatch) → `PegsChoice` (a panel on the decider's machine: Peg's human player, else the first human; keys 1/2; no clock; the host applies, clients mirror) → `peg_resigned` (keep: the core is hers again, bound for `RocketCradle`, flag `core_in_rocket`; "heavier the second time") or `peg_delivered` (the hatch takes it). The Act IV "fee" for `peg_delivered` is Act IV's to apply.
- **Dee's cage** (side egg): three `CageItem`s (the casing in the wreck, the bomber's coil, the honey) → `cage_<item>` flags → `dee_cage_done`. The gantry cutscene still plays `DEE_M4C_LAST_01`; the `_02` variant needs a conditional-VO shot field the cutscene player doesn't have yet (logged for the endings step).
- **Fasbender** is the radar shack's `StoryRadio` (power on): four lines, `burn_known`, three seeded two-digit numbers in `GameState.flags["burn"]` shown on the HUD (his `_03` line says "the three numbers I am about to give you" instead of the design's `{a}, {b}, {c}` substitution, which the VO manager doesn't support).
- **The rocket** is a `SiloConsole` with two stages (`ignition_key` → `rocket_key_turned` by a 2 s hold; `burn_known` → `rocket_ready` by a 4 s hold) and the gantry lift `ExfilPoint` from the json exfil block: `trigger_flag` and `requires_flag` both `rocket_ready`, 3 s hold, act round 9 / match 29 on the clock, target `map04d_mondlicht`, cutscene `exfil_m4c_m4d` ("MONDLICHT.", 40 s, `fx: launch` on the `Rocket` group). On act round 9 the controller gives the key, the burn and the ready flag if the squad never earned them, so the act cannot softlock.
- **Registrations:** the chain, scenes and display names; arrival kits (already there); `bake_config` floors `surface` / `lab`; `rules.json map_lines.map04c`; objectives; two fixed Archive documents (`INT_M4_MANIFEST`, `INT_M4_PROGRAM`) placed by `story_props.json`; `PERKS_LORE.md` §10 → `machine_lore.json` (the "localized" olive-drab cabinets, Magpie Mead as Dee's own); `progression.json map_unlock_rounds`; `enemies.json` kits per map, Glasshides, the four kinds as `ganger_variant` rows, the `gas_slow` status; the summon branch in `zombie_director.gd`; the map lists in `playtest_gl`, `menu_character`, `test_vo_maps`, `test_machine_lore`, `test_campaign*`.
- **VO:** `VO_SCRIPT.md` §14.7–14.9 (93 lines: OPR, RRK, DEE, HAL, FAS, the squad, Jakub, Sprocket, Vendelin), extracted to `lines.json`, all marked `missing` in the audio manifest for the PC bake. Two Operator exfil lines (`OPR_EXFIL_WAIT_M4C_01`, `OPR_EXFIL_MISSED_M4C_01`) are new (the design listed only open/go).
- **Tests:** `test_map04c_data`, `test_groom_enemies`, `test_testpilot`, `test_pegs_choice`, `test_rocket_sequence`; `smoke_map04c` (the whole act by console: navigation, spawns, the generator, Dee's door, the Lodestone build, each kind, the boss, the choice, the cage, the console, the lift, the cutscene); `smoke_map04b` now follows the crash into Act III and checks the carry-over.

## T-111 — The crash target and the rocket transition (Act II → III → IV plumbing)
`campaign.json`: `chain += map04c_groomlake` (after the Kranich), `scenes` / `display_names`, the `exfil_m4c_m4d` cutscene (five shots: the lift and Dee's line, the capsule and Jakub's, the silo rim with `fx: launch`, the ride up over the range, the Moon with Dutch's line; title "MONDLICHT."). `progression.json`: Mondlicht unlocks from the Kranich at round 20 (the crash), Groom Lake's own exfil unlocks Mondlicht in the profile. The Kranich's autopilot exfil already targeted `map04c_groomlake` (T-109); with the scene present the crash now lands the squad on the salt with the carry-over (weapons, perks, Scrip, the bots' brains) at the end of round 20, round 21 = "ACT III · 1", the gantry lift dormant at 29. The flow smoke `smoke_map04b` step 7 was rewritten for it (the old "campaign continues" branch stays for a build without the scene). Act IV's scene is still missing in this branch: the MONDLICHT cutscene ends on the "campaign continues" screen and unlocks `map04d_mondlicht` in the profile, exactly as the Kranich did before Act III existed.

**Measured (this fork):** `smoke_map04c` PASS (57 s under the runner), `smoke_map04b` PASS into Act III (217 s), `smoke_map04a` / `smoke_map04_flow` / `smoke_campaign` PASS; the bake (surface 679×1036, lab) and `check_mapdata --quick` PASS; the 8-round playtest with 3 veteran bots in god mode reached round 8 (732 s): bot kills 226, one bot down, one bot revive, Scrip 10,680 / 14,050 / 13,030, 27 % hit rate, brain 0.56 ms/bot/frame, no slide issues. The bots bought perks but no doors (there is no `routes_map04c.json` for the bots' door plans: the self-led squad on the range stays in the wreck and the salt), so the Test Pilot's round waited for the lab every round ("boss waits: F_LAB is shut"): a human squad opens it; a bots-alone range needs the routes file (open problem for the soak step).

T-115, 2026-10-26: Map 4's main egg "Kündigung", Acts III–IV, and the bots on all four acts (UPDATE 07 Phase 2.8; docs/MAP4_FERNFLUG.md §9). The quest data/gravelight/quests/map04_kuendigung.json now runs s1–s18 across the four act scenes (its state rides the campaign carry, T-114). Act III: Dee's radio (dee_radio_done), the impeller core carry and Peg's choice (completes on peg_resigned or peg_delivered), the Lodestone build, Fasbender's burn numbers (burn_known), the Test Pilot caught by a lift at least once (slammed_once; he returns if killed without it, no loss), the rocket console (rocket_ready). Act IV: the yard airlock, the three columns read (all three dark), the crew's tags matched (optional, Wren's lines), the Account open, the Manager escalated, the key (relay_cut); the quest's reward is profile-side: XP, the Archive card, the calling card, the "Low Gravity" mutator for every map, the "Cancelled" title, dossier page 10. The Account's step is a bot `interact_sequence` on its door (a held hall was the wrong task: the door is what the step waits on). Two side eggs as quests: Dee's yard rule (map04c_cage.json: the casing, the bomber's coil, the honey; her last line and her Act IV radio follow) and the smile at the yard's rim (map04d_smile.json: the Mondstein charm). Training routes and player-bot plans exist for all four acts (data/gravelight/ai/routes_map04a..d.json, player_bot.json), every route point on its act's navmesh (test_map04_bots). smoke_map04_egg runs the whole egg, Act I to the credits, in 163 s. Finishing pass (the worker was cut off by a usage limit): the smoke's closing `_finish()` call was missing (it ran every check, then hung to the runner's timeout); smoke_map04c, written before Act IV existed, polled the freed Act III map after the hand-over and now follows the swap; the transition cutscene's camera uses north as up on a straight-up shot (the rocket's climb warned "colinear"); the ending diorama's hearth used a material key that does not exist.

T-116, 2026-10-26: the Fernflug soak (UPDATE 07, "a full playthrough soak, Act 1 → the ending, with bots, headless"). tools/ai_soak.sh --scenario=fernflug plays Map 4 as one campaign run: Campaign.begin_at on Act I (or --skip-to-act=2|3|4 with the launcher's arrival kit), the act beacons, the cutscenes at 12×, the carry, to an ending, a wipe or --max-minutes (240). The soak node lives under the root so it outlives the scene swaps; each act gets its own BotMetrics CSV and a row in the batch's per-act table (rounds, minutes, downs, revives, stuck, teleports, stranded relocations, Scrip spent, doors, bosses, the power round, the transition kind: egg / clock / forced, the end). The scripted player treats a lit beacon as its first errand and opens the doors a path to it crosses; with --bots-alone the harness holds a lit, unlocked beacon through ExfilPoint.force_depart() (tools only; the act's own flag rules still gate it). ZombieDirector: a round that has zombies queued but no spawn serving the players for 20 s falls back to any active spawn (the first bots-alone run sat on Act I round 5 for 40 game minutes with the squad in a closed zone and every ground spawn in sight). smoke_map04_full (the whole chain with the player's dev skips, the carry checked at every arrival, the canon ending; then a skip to Act IV and the renewal into the Retained state; 74 s) and smoke_launcher (the four Map 4 buttons exist, are enabled, and "skip to Act II" lands on the Kranich at round 15 with the arrival kit) are in the smoke suite. Measured runs: docs/QA_REPORT_U07.md §7.

T-117, 2026-10-26: a bot with a full crate gun never refilled its dry Crucible-taught gun (docs/QA_REPORT_U07.md Q-05; it was first logged as a load flake, then failed twice on a quiet machine). The bot brain's resupply action weighed the "ammo" input, which is the fullest long gun the bot carries: a bot that had pulled a second long gun from the crate (an MG42 at 125/500 beside a taught Thompson at 1/0) read 100% ammo, so the resupply score was zero although the economy offered the box (two reachable boxes, no cooldown, no claim). The action now reads `resupply_ammo`, the ammo fraction of the gun its candidate refills (BotBrain.resupply_ammo_of; the fullest long gun when there is no candidate), in data/gravelight/ai/actions.json. smoke_bots' refill phase now always gives the dry bot a full second long gun (the case that broke), and explains itself if it fails (each box's reachability, cooldown and claim, the bot's guns and Scrip); `--refill-bot=N` picks the dry bot. Verified both ways: with the old input the hardened phase fails every run; with the new one it passes. test_bot_brain: test_resupply_weighs_the_gun_it_refills.

T-118 (numbered T-102 on master before the UPDATE 07 merge; the same fix as T-117, merged with it): Bots refill an empty taught gun even when they carry a second full long gun. The resupply action weighed the brain's "ammo" input, the best ammo fraction over all long guns, so an empty Crucible-taught Thompson next to a full second rifle read as plenty of ammo and the box errand (value 1.0) never beat follow; now it weighs "resupply_ammo", the ammo of the gun the best resupply errand refills (BotBrain._slot_ammo; actions.json). Also: while a nearly dry main gun's box errand is pending its price is held back from every other purchase (a bot rolled the crate for 950 and could no longer afford the 2500 refill), and a nearly dry gun may send the bot to any reachable box, not only boxes near its anchor. smoke_bots' refill phase sets the bot's Scrip to 3000 and prints a refill diagnostic (action, gun, scrip, scores, candidates) at 15 s; test_bot_brain covers the two-gun case.

T-097: Cloud-ready repo (UPDATE 06 Part A). Secrets scan over every commit and the tree (key/token/private-key/password patterns, suspicious file names, personal email): nothing found. Git LFS for *.glb *.gltf *.fbx *.blend *.png *.jpg *.wav *.ogg *.mp3 *.zip *.unitypackage *.psd under assets/, audio/ and gravelight/**/audio/ (git lfs migrate import --everything; a verified bundle of the old history was kept); the Kokoro TTS model, force-added once despite the ignore rule, was filtered out of history (tools/tts stays git-ignored, the files stay on disk); git history 935 MB -> 30 MB, LFS 685 MB. Private repo github.com/DBITDEFENSE/bolt-and-sprocket-ultimate with every branch and tag; a fresh clone gets real files. tools/env.sh gives every tools/*.sh its GODOT/GODOT_W/BLENDER/PYTHON per OS (tools/local_paths.env overrides); the Python tools default Blender per OS; tools/migrate_gravelight.py reads GRAVELIGHT_MIGRATE_SRC. tools/cloud_setup.sh (idempotent) installs apt packages when root, pulls LFS, downloads Godot 4.7.2 Linux and Blender 5.1.2 with checksums (Blender from official mirrors: download.blender.org refuses scripts), a venv with the voice-pipeline packages, writes .gdignore into the tool folders (Blender's own .blend/.tex inside the project made the Linux headless import give up: Map 0 never loaded), imports headless and runs GUT and every smoke. CLAUDE.md is the session guide; the work orders are copied into docs/work_orders. The WSL Ubuntu 24.04 run found two more Linux-only failures, fixed: the map POI signature used printf %.1f, which rounds exact halves differently on Windows and Linux (now roundi, and staleness needs a real POI diff), and smoke_interact shot a still-rising ground zombie (hitboxes off until head-out). Both platforms pass the full suite.

T-119: KÜNDIGUNG's dossier reward (dossier_page: 10, T-115) wrote the bare page number into ProfileSave's "dossiers", which holds dossier XP; for anyone with a dossier past 10 XP it did nothing. QuestManager now raises the XP to the threshold of that level (ProfileSave.dossier_thresholds()[page - 1], 130,000 for page 10), keeping higher XP. test_kuendigung starts from a fresh profile (it read the player's own, loaded at startup, and failed on the owner's PC while passing in the cloud) and checks the dossier level. Found by the first Windows run after pulling the cloud session's Map 4 work: otherwise 1,321/1,322 unit tests and all 44 smokes passed.

T-120, 2026-10-27: the invisible wall beside Map 0's bar. The owner: "in the starting room on the left side of the bar there is an invisible wall that players and zombies get stuck on". It was StairRailPanel, the hidden panel that keeps the navmesh bake off the stair flight's open edge (T-040): it ran from the floor to z 8.95, the flight's head, but the stairwell is shut at z 10 (StairN / StairNu), so its last metre stood in the taproom at x 3.6 between the bar's west end and the stair wall, across the way to the Old Cellar door, and the bake had a hole round it. North of z 10.15 the panel now starts under the flight's soffit (2.6 m up, StairRailPanelHead); the map was regenerated (gen_map00) and re-baked. test_map00_art checks the walkway is free of colliders below head height and on the navmesh (new fingerprints). Found with a capsule sweep of the taproom floor at runtime.

T-121, 2026-10-27: bots take perk tiers and the Crucible's second and third teachings; the tonic bottle's full-cap gift. The owner: "make sure the bots double and triple pack a punch their guns and get higher tiers of the perks as they get points", and "when a perk bottle spawns and the user has all perks, make it increase the level of a random perk they have and also make it permanent if they go down". (1) Perk tiers: a squad bot has no profile, so the mastery lock (T-075) is not its (PerkManager.can_upgrade checks GameState.is_bot); the bots' own gates follow the machines' (economy.json perk_upgrades: II from round 8, III from 15; they were 10 and 18). Still only once nothing new is left to drink. The bot-mode agent plays the human's survivor and keeps the human's lock. (2) Crucible: a bot feeds its best gun (BotBrain.best_to_teach: economy tier with each teaching counted, reteach_tier_bonus 0.6; it used to feed whatever upgradeable gun was in hand), switching to it first; once that gun is taught and can learn more, the crate and the wall guns leave the next teaching's price (7500 / 10000) on top of the reserve (BotEconomy.reteach_save, crucible.save_for_reteach), since the rich-crate rule (5000+ Scrip, best gun under tier 5.5: a Thompson taught once is 5.1) spent the money every round. Every agent plan with a Crucible ends with {crucible: *, tier: 2} and {crucible: *, tier: 3}. (3) Tonic bottle: with the cap full (or every map tonic owned) it raises a random carried tonic a tier with no mastery lock and no round gate, and seals it: that tonic and its tier survive every down, solo and co-op (PerkManager.seal / kept_on_down; a sealed Second Wind is not used up by a solo self-revive while uses remain). Sealed tonics carry through the campaign and the co-op snapshot and wear the gold ring on the HUD (as a bot's bonus perk); the loadout tab says "kept on a down". Every carried tonic at its top tier: the old 1500 Scrip. Tests: test_bot_crucible_tiers, test_milestones, test_perk_tiers.

T-122, 2026-10-27: two HUD bugs. The owner: "the round counter on higher rounds on ultra wide is not displaying correctly also perks will disappear randomly". (1) The round stamp's digit box (a clipped holder so the numerals fill the stamp) was one numeral wide (hud_stamp x 0.72), so round 10 and up lost digits; Hud._fit_stamp sizes it to the number each round and the panel shrinks back for one digit. (2) The perks did not go anywhere: the transition cutscene (campaign exfil, every Map 4 act change) parks every HUD layer 8000 px up, including the perk row, which lives under the PerkManager autoload and outlives the scene change; it was put back only by stop() (the cancelled case), so after a normal transition the row stayed off-screen for the rest of the run. The cutscene puts it back on _exit_tree, and PerkManager resets its offset on every match start. Test: test_hud_stamp_perks.

T-123, 2026-10-27: Kaltenbrunn's starting area. The owner: "improve the second maps starting area". The Minehead Yard's south half, where the squad spawns, was bare snow against a blank wall, and the headframe's base was a plain 10 x 10 x 6 m concrete block. (1) The base is dressed as the hoist house (_dress_hoist_house): brick plinth, corner pilasters, cornice, barred high windows on three faces, the shaft-signal box with its conduit and bell, the "Seilfahrt eingestellt" notice, a lintel, "Betreten verboten" and a bulkhead lamp over the north door, cable conduit to the winding engine; visual only and flush (0.12 m at most), so its collider and the headframe loop are unchanged. (2) Along the south wall (_dress_yard_south), outside the loop's ring (z > 29.5), 2.5 m+ from every player spawn and the ground spawn at (3, 29), clear of the doors and Pylon A's ring: a fire barrel and an unpowered lantern so the spawn is lit before the power, the miners' tag board (empty hooks for the men still below) and lamp rack, a pit-prop stack and a spare set, a workbench with tools, a tipped ore cart in a salt spill, crates and sacks, and a trodden strip. Their hidden colliders (YARD_PROPS) go into the Geometry before the bake, so the navmesh goes round them. (3) The yard gets an ammo box (AMMO_BOXES "Yard", south wall, x 20): the start zone had none, while Map 0's taproom has one. Tests: test_map01_edges (props clear of spawns, doors, loop and pylon; the ammo box; no navmesh under the timber stack); smoke_map01 and smoke_map01_systems pass; routes still on the navmesh (test_bot_tuning). Map 1's minimap data needs re-baking for the new ammo box POI (tools/bake_minimap.sh map01_kaltenbrunn, a rendered run).

T-124, 2026-10-28: Bots lost their gun in the Crucible. The owner: "the bot will go and pack a punch a gun and then not receive it from the pack a punch machine and it will lose the gun." The bot fed its gun, then something cleared its errand during the 5 s forge (a zombie in arm's reach drops the errand's stickiness, T-071; the trainer's lead_train override; an order; a down), and nothing sent it back, so the 15 s offer expired. Fix: BotBrain.crucible_holding_mine() finds a Crucible forging or offering this bot's gun; every think then forces the Buy action (only a revive or retreat emergency scoring over 0.8 beats it, and while it still forges a zombie within 2.5 m may be fought first), and start_task("buy") builds the errand back to that Crucible ("fed", already paid). At the pickup the bot first selects its weakest non-taught slot when its slots filled up meanwhile, and takes the gun with Crucible._take directly, so a quest step on the Crucible can't swallow the pickup. Test: smoke_bots (--crucible-only) pulls the first bot off its errand mid-forge and fails if its gun expires or it never takes it back (fails without the fix, passes with it).

T-125, 2026-10-28: invisible walls outside Map 0's barn. The owner: "outside the barn on the first map there are some invisible boundaries and walls we can't run or shoot through". Three causes, found with a headless collider-vs-drawing probe (tools_src/probe_invisible_walls.gd). (1) The main one: Props.fence (T-093's split-rail fences) drew its posts and rails as MultiMeshes, and gen_map00 runs headless, where the dummy renderer keeps no MultiMesh buffer: the scene saved each MultiMesh's instance count but none of its transforms, so all 71 fence MultiMeshes on Map 0 (the Carters' Yard round the barn's south side, the roadside fence, the edge fences) have drawn nothing since T-093. Their 2.3 m colliders stood there invisible. New BakedMultiMesh (world/baked_multimesh.gd) keeps the transforms as an exported array and fills the MultiMesh in _ready; Props.fence and Props.bottles use it (Map 1's three bottle shelves have the same loss and get it when Map 1 is next regenerated). (2) The yard's fences and the roadside fence were solid to shots for the full 2.3 m though the drawn rails are 1.2 m. Now _fence_box: world layer (shots, zombies, bodies) to FENCE_SOLID_H 1.25 m, the drawn rails' top; EDGE_LAYER above to 2.3 m (players can't hop into the yard past its bought gate; shots pass). Those fences are drawn tall (Props.fence tall: 2.35 m posts, four rails, no open bays), so what stops a player is visible; the yard's four vault gaps get a drawn lintel over the YardTop collider. The edge fences are unchanged (already EDGE_LAYER; the map edge). (3) Every crate spot kept its baked footprint (NavBlock_Crate, 1.4 x 0.75 m) after the crate left, e.g. an invisible box by the yard's well. They are in group "bake_only" now and MapRoot switches the group's collision off at runtime; MysteryCrate's own blocker is on the world layer as well as the door layer while the crate stands there, so a present crate still stops shots and zombies. The navmesh is unchanged (both fingerprints in test_map00_art hold). Test: test_map00_fences. Not checked in a rendered run (no GPU runs without the owner's OK): worth a look at the yard in game.

T-126, 2026-10-28: GRAVELIGHT ONE you can walk into, and its trapped pilot. The owner: "improve the plane wreck make it so you can go into the plane, have a pilot trapped in there that you can give a gun and he basically turns into a turret because he's stuck in the plane". (1) The hull (gen_map00 build_wreck_hull, was one solid box): a cabin floor (WRECK_FLOOR 0.2), walls, a roof (2.35), the aft bulkhead and the tail cone; two ways in, both on the starboard side because the hull is rolled 8 deg and the port side stands too high off the snow to step into (the real C-47's cargo door is on the port side; gameplay first): the cargo door aft (z 2.2..4.0) and a torn hole forward of the wing (z -4.3..-2.45, 1.85 m so the navmesh's 0.5 m erosion leaves a way through). Both openings reach the roof line: a 2.25 m lintel on the rolled hull, quantized to 0.25 m cells, came out at exactly the agent height and the bake shut the doorway. The wing stops at the hull. The cockpit sits behind a bulkhead whose doorway is choked waist-high (1.15 m) with wreckage, so players and zombies never get in but see and reach the pilot over it; its windscreen (sill WRECK_SCREEN 1.1 over the floor) and side windows (sill 1.4) are broken out, under the seated pilot's eye and over any jump (player jump apex 0.63 m). All hull colliders are no_mantle. Drawn (_dress_wreck_hull) as a panelled shell round WRECK_RING in 0.4 m bays with the openings left out and torn, charred flaps at their edges, a tapering nose with the windscreen frame bent, ribs, a planked floor, a soot-black ceiling, paratroop bucket seats along the port wall, the static line, a cargo net and a crate, the choked doorway's bent door and fallen radio rack, a dim fire glow inside; LandscapeProps.pinned_cockpit replaces the empty cockpit (his seat, the instrument panel driven down across his knees, the bent column, the co-pilot's seat torn loose and empty). The hold's fire and the cook-off points moved out of the cabin. Navmesh re-baked (test_map00_art fingerprints updated); smoke_wreck_pilot checks a short path in through each opening, a standing capsule in the cabin and both doorways, and no path into the cockpit. (2) Flt Lt Harry Selwyn (world/trapped_pilot.gd, data/gravelight/pilot.json): an Interactable at the cockpit doorway. Hand him the gun in your hands (JakubCompanion.offer_rule: never your only gun; never a wonder weapon, a launcher or a stream weapon from a pinned seat); handing him another gives you his back. He picks the nearest active zombie in 32 m he can see from his eye (a world-layer ray, so the hull, the wing and walls block him), within 100 deg of straight ahead, turns at 3 rad/s, fires at 0.7x the gun's rpm (capped 550) with the gun's damage, falloff and limb multipliers through hitbox rays (aimed spread x1.5 + 0.8 deg, a quarter of shots at the head), reloads from the reserve at 1.4x, and calls for ammo when dry; passing ammo costs that gun's ammo-box price (the payer's Packmule / Scrounger apply). His kills are scored to the player who armed him (it's their gun; an armed Selwyn is worth the 2-slot cost). Zombies never target him and he can't be hurt (out of reach in the nose). The host runs him; arming, refills, every shot's flash / tracer / report and his lines are mirrored. Bots don't arm him (not in this change). (3) His body: PatronBody (the patrons' articulated body) with a seated aim (new aim_upper_only / aim_weight / aim_yaw / aim_pitch / kick / hold_gun: the legs stay seated, the chest and head turn to the target, the real WeaponModels gun in his hands, a recoil rock), drawn by a modelled person: people.json "selwyn" + make_kits kit_selwyn (sheepskin flying jacket with the fleece collar, cuffs and hem, RAF blue-grey trousers, flying boots, a silk scarf, a close leather helmet), built with tools/build_people.sh --only=selwyn (11.4k tris). (4) Voice: speaker SEL (VO_SCRIPT 14.15, 15 lines: CALL while unarmed and someone is near, GIVEN, SWAP, THANKS_AMMO, DRY, KILL), cast in voice_cast.json (bm_lewis/bm_george, gb) and baked here with Kokoro; positional from the cockpit with subtitles "FLT LT SELWYN", per-category cooldowns in pilot.json. (5) Canon (STORY_CHANGES 2026-10-28): the co-pilot died, Selwyn lived pinned in his seat; LORE 9.2 and the timeline updated. The squad's WRECK_PILOT lines ("the seat is empty ... who unbuckled him?") are rewritten for a living Selwyn (Dutch had found no pulse) and re-baked; the look trigger aims at his head. His fate after Map 0 is [OPEN]. Tests: test_trapped_pilot (gift rules, rate, lines voiced, sills, placement), smoke_wreck_pilot (navmesh in, capsule fit, gift rules live, 3+ kills scored to the giver, dry -> ammo prompt -> paid refill). Not seen rendered (no GPU runs without the owner's OK).

T-127, 2026-10-28: Jakub drinks tonics. The owner: "make it so the boy in the cellar can buy some perks". T-051 had Jakub refuse perks outright; now, once he is with you, "Talk to Jakub" can send him for one. He still never shops of his own accord (brain perks / bonus_perk / shopping stay false). JakubCompanion.tonic_offer picks the nearest perk machine he can drink from: powered, his to buy under the normal cap (PerkManager.can_buy for pid 9), not mid-pour, reachable on the navmesh (so Map 0's machines only once door_cellar is open), within tonic_range_m 70 m, and affordable on the talker's Scrip. The prompt and the card (JakubChoice, [reload] GET YOURSELF A TONIC, "never mind" once he is with you) only offer it then; with a gun to give as well, the card opens. choose_tonic sets the brain's options.perk_payer_pid and gives it a buy_tonic order on that machine (not a wheel order: it runs 60 s, the errand +25 s). The errand is sticky like a fed Crucible gun, because his escort score otherwise pulled him back to the talker before he reached the machine. At the till, PerkMachine.sell_to_companion re-checks everything and takes the talker's Scrip at the normal price: same jingle and drink beat, then PerkManager.grant to him. A short purse gets PERK_SHORT, a dropped errand clears the payer. His tonics behave like anyone's: one each, the cap, lost on a down, gone with a new match. Player.hp_scale (his max_hp over player.json max_health, 0.7 when wounded) keeps Ironhide's health on his own base and stops a lost tonic resetting him to a player's 100. He is still wary, never a joke: the first-time-by-a-machine line is now PERK_NEAR ("...Ask me, and I will."). New PERK_ACCEPT / PERK_DRINK / PERK_SHORT lines in VO_SCRIPT section 16, baked here with Kokoro; JAK_PERK_NEAR_01 is an any-map line. PERK_REFUSE stays as his comment when a survivor drinks near him. Map marks still don't send him to a machine (their refusal points to the Talk card). Built by a parallel agent in a worktree and merged after T-126. Tests: test_jakub (tonic_rule, cap/owned/lost on a down), test_mark_orders; smoke_jakub NOT YET (no offer behind the shut cellar door; offer and prompt once open; he walks to the cellar machine and drinks Ironhide Stout; the human pays exactly its price; lost when downed).

T-128, 2026-10-28: gunplay feel: tracers, hit markers, sights. The owner: "improve the gunplay, make sure all sights look good and tracers and hitmarkers feel satisfying". (1) Tracers (Effects.tracer, vfx.json "tracer"): the old tracer was a muzzle-to-hit line shown for 0.04 s (a flicker). Now a streak (a hot additive core and a 3.5x wider faint glow) flies from the muzzle to the hit at a visual speed per class (pistol 340 m/s .. sniper 520), its class length (shotgun pellets 1.6 m and faint, a sniper's 7 m and bright), and drains into the impact; the first 2.4 m in front of your own camera stay dark so the streak never smears across the sights (0.6 m for anyone else's shots); no tracer under 2.5 m; a Crucible-taught gun's tracers burn violet (the Nachtglas). Pool 12 -> 48 (shotgun pellets). Callers pass the gun's class, whether it is taught, and whether it is your own shot (WeaponHolder, RemoteBody, the inn survivor's rifle, Selwyn). (2) Hit markers (Hud.HitMarker, ui.json "hitmarker"): a per-state pop (hit 0.35, headshot 0.5, kill 0.7), a streak (hits within 0.3 s of each other grow each mark 6%, up to 5 steps, and thicken it), a random +-6 deg tilt per mark so a burst doesn't stamp one shape, and a kill throws a ring racing out from 10 to 34 px (amber on a headshot kill); kill marks last 0.4 s. Sound: a kill now plays its own confirm (gen_audio.py "kill_confirm": a deep short thock, a bright double tick, a breath of air) with the headshot crack layered under it on a headshot kill; the plain hit tick got a muffled knock under it so it reads as landing. (3) Sights: tools/ads_check.sh (T-047) checks only that the sight markers sit on the aim point; all 322 gun x attachment builds passed, yet some pictures were wrong because a downloaded model's markers sat on the wrong geometry. New audit modes in ads_check.gd: --picture (from the aimed gun's triangles: the share of a 70 px disc round the aim point it covers, of its upper half, of the strip over the front sight, the front post's gap / height / width, and the parts in that strip), --ascii (an 81 x 41 character map of the sight picture, each covered cell lettered by mesh) and --fit=dx,dy[,front] (moves SightPoint / FrontSight by the rig step that brings a measured screen point onto the aim point and prints the model_fit value). With the owner's OK, the 52 base and Crucible guns were rendered off-screen (ads_check shots) before and after. Fixed in model_fit.json: the M1 Garand's rear aperture sat 24 px left of and 7 px above the aim point (the Sketchfab model's aperture is 8 mm off the centre line; sight_rear [-0.0083, 0.0525, 0.136]) and its front marker sat on the protective ears, not the post (sight_front [-0.0027, 0.0525, -0.5766]); the M1 Carbine's aperture hole sat 6 px high and its front marker on the ears (sight_rear y 0.06, sight_front y 0.0523); the M97 trench gun's rear marker landed on its cocked hammer, so the hammer's flat top hid the bead (part_offset Hammer 9 mm down: the scan now finds the receiver top, and the bead shows over it). Every other gun's picture checked clean (the pistols, Kar98k, G43, MP40, Thompson, StG44, BAR, PPSh, MG42, M1919, Sawed-Off, Type 100, PTRS, the Springfield's scope, the Panzerschreck's window, the wonder weapons). After: ads_check 322/322 aligned, sight_clearance 0 models with parts over the sight line. Tests: test_gunfeel (tracer flight and drain, the close-shot skip, violet for a taught gun, a look per class, the streak, the sounds, the fits).

T-129, 2026-10-28: UPDATE 08 section 2, perk tier IV "perma perks". Every tonic gets tiers["4"] in perks.json: bought at its machine once tier III is held and the power is on (upgrades: max_tier 4, min_round["4"] 15, no later than tier III's; mastery unlock["4"] = tier III's, so no new grind), priced by strength: Ironhide, Second Wind, Packmule 15000; Overclock 14000; Twin Barrel 13500; Trigger, Blastproof, Magpie 13000; Skyhook 12500; Hawkeye, Marathon 12000. Permanence (upgrades.permanent_tier 4; PerkManager.is_permanent / permanent()): on_player_downed keeps permanent tonics with their tiers exactly like a sealed one (a down, a co-op bleed-out and respawn, any later down), a solo self-revive no longer uses a permanent Second Wind up (its three uses still count down), the campaign carry already holds tiers; a permanent tonic still counts toward the cap of 6. One new effect each, all data: Ironhide IV "Cast Iron": once a round, the hit that would down you leaves you on 1 hp with 3 s of iron skin (filter_hit); Second Wind IV: revives effectively instant (revive_speed_mult 60), 5 s invulnerability for the revived, and in solo a spent self-revive comes back every 10 rounds (to the starting three at most); Trigger Tonic IV "Live Rounds": reloading a magazine with a third or less left loads 6 shock rounds (x1.6 damage, the target staggers; reload_finished now gets the fraction left, from WeaponHolder and BotAim); Twin Barrel IV: every 10th bullet hits twice (shot_mult); Marathon IV: sprinting into non-boss zombies knocks them down (1.4 m ahead, each zombie once per 2 s, a 10 Hz host tick); Hawkeye IV: a headshot kill sends a half-damage shot of the same gun to the nearest zombie within 15 m (with a tracer); Packmule IV: every round each gun gets 30% of its reserve and a full magazine; Blastproof IV: the slide shockwave hits twice as hard, reaches 5 m and kills crawlers outright; Overclock IV: 1.5 s cooldown and the aftershock rings at full strength from 40% spent; Skyhook IV: two hops, a 4 m harder stomp; Magpie IV: drops one kill in four, 40 s life, every 12th a power-up. The spec's "third slot refills 10% a round" for Packmule was weaker than its own tier III (20% every gun), so tier IV gives 30% and full mags. Classic Rules now keeps every tier off (can_upgrade -> "classic"; tiers II/III were not gated before). Presentation: the HUD cap gets a thick gold rim and a pale-gold IV band (perk_cap.gd); the machine plays a new "perk_tier4_sting" (gen_audio.py: a rising C-E-G-C bell arpeggio over a brass swell) and a gold glow and punch (PerkMachine._tier4_flourish, mirrored); every survivor says a PERK_TIER4 line each time ("This one's mine for good."), the Operator once (VO_SCRIPT section 20, 11 lines, baked: Kokoro for the squad and Jakub, OneCore for the Operator); the pause list shows the IV mark and its desc (it reads tier_mark). Tests: test_perk_tier4 (data and prices, the gate incl. power and Classic Rules, permanence through downs, a self-revive, the carry's tiers, the cap, each effect that runs without a map), test_perk_tiers updated (tier III is no longer the top). Launcher: "Perk tier IV test (50,000 Scrip, power on, round 15)" (tools_src/play_tier4). Balance in the 30+ rounds was not measured: no soak run reached round 30 (see QA_REPORT_U08).

T-130, 2026-10-28: UPDATE 08 section 3, the bots' late-round kit. bots.json late_kit {enabled, round 25, perk_tier 3, weapon thompson_up, crucible_tier 2, exclude}. BotSquad.apply_late_kit runs from apply_kit (spawn, respawn after a bleed-out, revive, join) and for every live bot at each round start: from round 25, with the Bot Bonus Perk setting on and Classic Rules off, a squad bot (never Jakub) gets one tonic from the map's machines at tier III (pick_late_perk: never one it holds, its bonus perk, a spawn-kit perk, another bot's late tonic or an excluded one) and the Chicago Lament taught to tier 2, full, in hand (it takes the weakest slot when full). Idempotent: a bot still holding its late tonic and the gun gets nothing more; a down loses the tonic like any other (it isn't permanent), the next respawn or revive gives one again. It says a LATE_KIT line (8 lines, baked). The nameplate shows the tonic through perks_changed. Tests: test_bot_late_kit (the round 24/25 gate, the toggles, the draw), smoke_late_kit (Map 0, 3 bots: nothing at round 24, all three geared at round 25 with different tonics, a respawn regears, the toggle off gives nothing). Launcher: "Round 25 bot kit test".

T-131, 2026-10-28: UPDATE 08 section 4, bots and every egg (in progress; see QA_REPORT_U08 and PROGRESS). Built: (1) the bot-mode agent is the player: BotEECatalog.gate gives it player-only steps as tasks (the cutscene, the last input, a hand-off, a choice's default), plan_hold lets it start a holdout that is the player's to start (the Heartstorm: it uses the Crucible), _anchor_state lets it start a pylon without "a human within 22 m" (it is the human). (2) Full Partner: a squad bot may take a story choice's default pick when the player is away or down (ctx player_absent); every other player step stays the player's; bots still never sequence-break or guess. Peg's choice (map04 s11b) is now player_only "choice", and PegsChoice takes KEEP (the canon pick) after 20 s when Bot Easter-Egg Help is Full Partner and the decider is down, out or played by the agent. (3) Fernflug's bot_knowledge gains `power` (new on_power trigger: the map's power on; the radar shack's radio needs it) and `burn` (on s12b), the two undefined needs that made s12b / s14 impossible. (4) Kaltenbrunn: the agent plan gets the Requiem egg block, the Lab door (Pylon H is behind it), a Crucible gun for the Chapel bell and the surface loop first (it used to train underground and never reach the surface tonics, stalling the plan). (5) While the agent holds a charging pylon or a running holdout, its trainer no longer takes it away (it used to leave the pylon empty, and a charging pylon holds the round open: the round never ended). (6) BotLocomotion rescues a bot in free fall for 2.5 s (seen: a Full Partner bot slipping through Kaltenbrunn's yard by Pylon A and falling for the rest of the match with its egg step) back onto the navmesh behind its leader. (7) Measurement: BotMetrics logs each player's Scrip per round, every perk tier change and every egg step; ai_soak --until-egg ends a run when the main egg completes and prints EGG / EGG RATE; EGG_DEBUG logs every egg plan change. test_bot_ee now covers Map 4's four quests (every step has a bot task or a stated player-only reason; every need is defined). Result so far: Map 0's egg completes with bots (baseline already); Kaltenbrunn's goes from s3 to s0 + s3 + the first pylon; the rest of the Requiem (the other pylons under heavy spawns, the bell and the Foreman, the drummer chase, the Totenglocke charge, the Heartstorm holdout) is not yet finished by bots, and in god-mode soaks the bot-mode trainer can kite two straggling crawlers for half an hour (nobody hunts them). Not met: the 3-of-5 Kaltenbrunn acceptance and a completion on every map; the per-map completion smokes wait on that.

T-133, 2026-10-28: smooth motion and the graphics settings. The owner: "figure out why it gets choppy on my rtx5090" then "improve the graphics and graphics settings". A new frame logger (tools_src/frame_log.gd, on with `--frame-log`; the launcher forwards it to a window it spawns) showed the setup: a 240 Hz screen, vsync on, the GPU idle (well over 200 fps), physics at 60 Hz without interpolation. The choppiness was the update rate, not the frame rate: the local player's camera update (_update_camera: pitch, bob, recoil, roll, FOV) and the whole viewmodel animation (WeaponHolder._animate) ran in _physics_process, so on a 240 Hz screen vertical aim, the bob, recoil and the gun moved in 60 Hz steps (only mouse yaw, applied in _unhandled_input, was smooth), and the eye moved with the body in 60 Hz steps. Smooth motion (GLSettings smooth_motion, default on; Player.set_smooth_view): the local human's camera update and the viewmodel's animation run in _process at the render rate (both are time-based: no gameplay timer moves), and the head is offset each frame to the body's position interpolated between the last two physics ticks (Engine.get_physics_interpolation_fraction; a jump over 2 m is a teleport and snaps). Mouse look keeps no added latency. Bots, bot mode and the tools keep the tick path (ads_check, smokes unchanged). Global physics interpolation was not turned on: many nodes are moved in _process (tracers, flashes, patrons, pickups, Selwyn) and would smear. Zombies still move at 60 Hz (a candidate for per-node interpolation later).
