ZoneAmbience (gravelight/world/zone_ambience.gd, MapRoot attaches it after AudioDirector.start_ambient when data/gravelight/ambience/<map_id>.json or <map prefix>.json exists; the Fernflug workers' contract): schema {"crossfade_s", "zones": {ZONE: {"bed": AudioDirector key or res:// path, "db", "oneshots": [{"key", "every_s": [min, max], "db", "spatial"}]}}}; it polls the local player's ZoneVolume zone 4x a second (a point between boxes keeps the zone it had, so no flapping at a seam), fades the old bed out and the new one in over crossfade_s (linear in gain, the old player freed when it reaches silence), fires each one-shot of the current zone at a random interval (spatial ones 5-14 m round the listener), is silent when the map's player is a puppet, never networked; the fade state machine runs without a stream so it is tested headless; AudioDirector now loops OGG/MP3 streams too. Map 2's beds (data/gravelight/ambience/map02.json, under a map-wide storm bed ambient_map02_storm in audio.json music.ambient): quay surf + wind + the halyard on the hut's pole (gulls, a loose shutter, single waves, gusts), cannery line hum + belt rattle + stove + a tap (hull creaks far off), workshop air hiss + cooling pipes ticking + a welder's arc now and then, pen hall room tone + dock water + long-tail drips + the curtain groaning (thunder), U-boat transformer hum + hull creaks against the fenders + muffled water, lower pens' pumps idling + lapping + drips, tower wind at height + rain on the glass (the teletype, a flapping paper, thunder, gusts); built by gravelight/tools_src/gen_ambience_map02.py (run with a numpy+scipy Python, e.g. the TTS venv) from two OpenGameArt CC0 recordings filed under assets/third_party/opengameart/ (jasinski's "Beach Ocean Waves", four crashes layered into the surf; RandomMind's "Sea and River Wave Sounds", a 120 s excerpt for the dock water) and gen_audio.py's synthesis (wind, hum, creaks, drips, gulls, teletype, weld, thunder), 40 s loops as OGG q4 (4.6 MB for 8 beds and 8 one-shot sets; freesound needs a login, so nothing from there). Materials: the one blue noise is gone: materials.json m2_* library sets from Poly Haven (concrete_wall_004 whose panel joints read as form-board lines for the bunker, concrete_floor_worn_001, dirty_concrete for the wet aprons and the tide-stained pier sides, metal_grate_rusty for catwalks and treads, metal_plate tread plate for the boat's deck and the dais, rusty_painted_metal tinted dark for U-1047's plating, red for the antifouling, grey for the curtain and blast door, pale grey-green for the pressure hull inside, corrugated_iron for the roofs and tarred for the hut) and ambientCG DiamondPlate008A for the zinc; the Map-2-only procedural keys (bunker_concrete, wet_concrete, pier_concrete, u_hull, u_interior, hull_red, zinc) map to them in materials.json's generator block (also used by the ending diorama's Totenhafen pieces); shared keys (steel_grate, concrete_floor) untouched, gen_map02 uses the m2_ keys directly. The visual-only CliffBack box was coplanar with the 3 m face and MassAbove and z-fought them (why the audit saw "one blue noise texture" on the bunker): split so it stops at the bunker's ends and above its top. Pieces (gravelight/tools_src/detail_map02.gd, DetailMap02 static builders; gen_map02's _dress_u09_* per zone): U-1047 is a Type VII lofted with SurfaceTool in the generator instead of a Blender GLB (parametric sections that keep the 6 m gameplay deck: casing, saddle tanks to 6.8 m beam, the keel 6.4 m under the deck, red lead below a boot-top, the casing's flooding slots, bow planes, twin screws and the rudder out under the curtain to z 96.6; the tower is a lofted fairing over the CT collider with the bridge railing, the wintergarten with a 2 cm flak, two tapered periscope standards with their heads, the DF loop, the spray deflector, U 1047 on both sides; the 8.8 cm gun on its pedestal, deck slats, cleats, jumping wires; the 1.2 m deck-edge colliders dressed as a capped spray bulwark), the quay's cutter is a clinker hull of six lapped strakes a side with a rubbing strake, keel, stem and a flat transom carrying the M1 Carbine's board, the wheelhouse with framed windows, the door hung open and the wheel lashed with two lines, mast and boom, shrouds, floats, nets; the hut got its tarred roof, the door hung open, shutters hooked back, a tide table, a hurricane lamp on a hook, a rain cap and gutter, the halyard pole (no flag); fish crates, a drum, jerrycans, life rings (Poly Haven props, decimated with tools/decimate_props.py), cleats, three gulls; the bunker face a sealed blast door with its counterweight in a track and water and rust streaks; the open sea its own rougher copy of the water shader (amp 0.55, chop 1.6, whitecaps), two blinking breakwater lights (BlinkLight, red and green). Cannery: the conveyor with cans riding the belt, riveted brine tanks with lids and hoops, a hook rail over the lines, scale glints on the zinc, a skylight row, the infirmary's folding screen and a barrel stove (Poly Haven) with steam, the delivery book on a desk. Workshop: torpedoes on wheeled cradles with saddles (warhead sections, tail fins, contra-rotating screws, two with the inspection hatch open), the chain hoist on the gantry, a tool wall, a belt-driven lathe, the welding bench (Poly Haven cart and gas bottles) with a flickering arc and sparks, two more coffins in the cage and its manifest. Pen hall: kerbs on every dock edge, cleats, caged fixtures on the pole lamps, the crane's cab and ladder and a real hook on two falls, the curtain in painted steel, the storm beyond the mouth (a cloud card brightened by LightningFlash), drips falling from the roof into the docks, the dockers' chalk tally on pier 1, puddles, streaks down the walls. U-boat: round hatch rings with dogs and open covers at the three bulkheads, overhead pipe runs with flanges and valves, gauges, the Crucible strapped in with webbing, Deacon's map and the boat's log on the chart table. Lower pens: the tide pumps as machines (volute, motor with cooling ribs, coupling guard, flanged pipes and valves; the flywheels unchanged), the mains along the wall, the Pumpenschalter's backplate and signs, the drowned sailors' effects left by a column (a sea chest with a cap on it, boots, a kit bag, a photograph), drips. Tower: the teletype with its paper strip curling to the floor, a chart with dividers, the signal lamp's drum with a visible lens, snow in the two cliff embrasures with the storm card on their back walls (lit by the same lightning), TowerSway (a faint camera shake in gusts, through Player._shake so the shake setting scales it); Petersen's spot on the ramp top (T-135), the bank branch and Ledger Lager (T-141) and the infirmary cot (T-137) untouched. Budget (contract C4): from inside the boat the camera's frustum covered the north hall, the quay, the cannery and the workshop (the census's 7242-call peak), so ViewCells (gravelight/world/view_cells.gd): gen_map02 splits its static batches into visibility groups (StaticBatches/VisGroup_A..G, DH = the hall round the hatch and tower), tags its loose lights, labels, decals, particles and spinners (meta vis_group, node group view_cell_member), and writes cells (camera box -> groups seen: the boat's interior sees only itself and the hatch's column of the hall, the quay sees the cannery and the workshop but not the hall, the cannery and the workshop see the hall through their passages, the lower pens and the tower see the hall; outside every cell everything draws); the moon's energy and shadow go to zero in the roofed cells; MapRoot builds ShadowProxies per group. Lights: zone + vis meta on every light, the four 60 m sea shafts at the mouth are two wider 48 m ones, the "every third hall flood casts" rule is gone, one key light per zone casts: A the flood over the hut, B the middle pendant, C a caged lamp in the warhead cage, D the high flood over the docks, E the Crucible's violet glow, F the Pumpenschalter's lamp, G the signal beam; shadowed lights per zone A 1 / B 4 / C 4 / D 6 / E 1 / F 2 / G 3 (+ the moon; before: 20, none with a zone, 7 in D). Census (WSL + Xvfb, Compatibility, power on, census_map --set=u09_map02): before 4651 draw calls / 627k primitives at E_wide (C_hero 2782, C_detail 2713, A_wide 2974 also over), after peak 2028 / 394k at C_hero, E_wide 1419 / 317k (within C4's 2500 / 400k). Cameras: u09_map02's D_detail (black) re-aimed down pier 0. Soak (tools/ai_soak.sh totenhafen, 1 run, 10 min, 3 bots): before (master scene) stuck 13 (13.3 s), 0 teleports, round 7; after stuck 11 (12.2 s), 0 teleports, round 9; the snags are the same south-east quay corner by the drums and the brazier (29-31, 24.5-29.2) both times, plus one at (21.0, 1.6), the corner between the parapet and the Cutter collider (neither changed: run variance, not a new snag). Smokes: map02, m2, bank, squads, npc_walls PASS; check_mapdata PASS (POIs unchanged; the minimap re-baked through WSL + Xvfb, tools/bake_minimap.sh opens a Windows window). Tests: test_zone_ambience (schema, the crossfade state machine, one-shot timers, every ambience file valid and every key resolvable, map02 covers all seven zones), test_view_cells (first cell wins, hide/show, ALL never hidden, the moon, Totenhafen's cells name real groups, every light zoned, <= 6 shadowed per zone). Not done: the Blender route for the hull (the loft in the generator does the job and stays editable); story_props.json pieces per zone beyond the existing intel (the delivery book, the boat's log and the manifest are dressing labels, not StoryProps); the pumps' volute reads as a box from the front in the captures.

T-146, 2026-11-01: Fernflug Act I, Werk Thalbach, rebuilt to the bar of Maps 0-1 (UPDATE 09 section 5, docs/MAPS_3_4_REBUILD.md 3 and 5.2; an art pass: no collider, door, window, spawn, prompt, timing, trap gameplay, Fliegeralarm circle or Kranfuehrer rail moved; every navmesh region has the same polygon count before and after, which is the proof, and check_mapdata passes). The shared detail library is gravelight/tools_src/gen_detail_lib.gd (class GenDetail, static, pure: it only adds nodes under the parent it is given; never a collider): corrugated_sheet, i_beam, truss, sawtooth_roof, rivet_strip, pipe_run (flanges, elbows, a valve), cable_tray, rails (sleepers instanced), chain_link (cut-out wire panels), railing, crane_gantry (girders, end trucks, trolley, hook block, cab), form_board (lift lines, instanced tie holes), trim, window (mullions, dirty / cracked / punched-out panes, curtains, a lit glow card with a silhouette, shutters, frost; `blind` stands a window proud of a solid wall, `open` frames a real barricade opening without touching it), silhouette (a flat dark shape: civilians appear only like this), door (frame, planked or riveted leaf, hinges, handle, sign; `leaf ""` frames a gameplay door, `blind` paints one on a wall), stairs, grating, poster, enamel_sign (text fitted to the plate), chalk_mark (Jakub's three strokes and a hook, CANON 6.4, the backwards hook, a tally, an X, an arrow, a hopscotch), card (rust / frost / soot / water / chalk weathering cards from one procedural shader, one MultiMesh per kind), rust_streak, frost_patch, puddle, snow_drift, sand_drift, crater, washing_line, camo_net (world-space cut-out netting that sags between supports, skirts), fog_sheets (ground fog that reads in Compatibility: the web build has no volumetrics), lamp_fixture (hanging / wall / flood / pole bodies that return the bulb so every light has a visible source), flying_wing_mesh + wing_surface (a lofted flying-wing hull from a planform, NACA thickness, a centre-body plateau and a bay notch), rib_mesh, wing_skeleton (instanced ribs, I-beam spars, part-skinned), merge_children (folds a kept group into one mesh per material), instance / fill_instances (BakedMultiMesh per id); three small shaders in gravelight/world/shaders (detail_card, cutout_mesh, fog_card) plus cloud_deck; test_gen_detail_lib (11 tests). gen_map04_lib.gd only gained functions (zone_at, tag_light_zones), so Acts II-IV regenerate as before unless they call them. Materials: eight Poly Haven CC0 textures as new keys only (fl_brick factory_brick, fl_corrugated corrugated_iron, fl_concrete concrete_wall_004, fl_floor concrete_floor_worn_001, fl_grate metal_grate_rusty, fl_yard dirt_aerial_02, fl_planks plank_flooring_02, fl_plaster peeling_painted_wall; the existing generator keys are untouched so no other map changes) and six CC0 models decimated in Blender to 1200-2500 triangles (barrel_stove, old_gas_mask, hanging/industrial wall lamps, metal_jerrycan, metal_toolbox); the act's walls get a material per block through mat_rules (rendered housing, works brick, board-formed concrete, corrugated sheds). Zones: H the Kranich is one lofted shell (span 86 m, root chord 52, olive paint) in the same kept "Kranich" group the boarding fx moves, with the crown, a glazed canopy, four engine bulges over the 5 m engine blocks, four violet intakes in the leading edge, nozzles at the trailing edge and the bay mouth over the ramp; test_map04a_art proves the shell covers its colliders (0.49 m clearance at worst) and leaves the ramp clear; the hangar has 12 trusses, purlins, a corrugated roof with a skylight strip under camouflage netting, form-board walls with pilasters, the south wall closed above the 5 m test-stand wall, crane runway hangers on the existing rail line, railings in place of the solid rail boxes (their colliders stay; their visuals are hidden), ribbed doors (kept group), the crane bridge rebuilt with the Kranfuehrer's cab (kept group), the tool cage in chain-link, ground-fog sheets beside the FogVolume. E two sawtooth halls (north lights) under netting, the 6-9 m slot over the canteen closed, the jigs with two half-built fighter-scale wings (one half-skinned), the gantry on its legs and the jig crane's bridge, rivet guns on balancers, factory windows, the Kraftzentrale's generator set, switchgear, breaker panel and exhaust stack. B track and sleepers, the flatcars with bogies, six lead coffins stencilled TOTENHAFEN (one cracked open on its violet), a coal-hopper truss bridge wall to wall, the goods shed in corrugated iron with sliding doors and a pitched roof, a station clock on a bracket, a water crane, a semaphore signal on the wall top, the yardmaster's hut (window, door, sign, the transfer notice; the Lohnkasse and Ledger Lager of T-141 untouched). A three-storey rendered facades on blocks 1 and 2 (upper storey non-colliding), roofs, chimneys, framed windows with curtains, lit windows with silhouettes, balconies, stair doors, the wash-house wing, the works wall with a fictional Volkssturm call-up notice (wording only, no symbol), the air-raid cellar with sandbags, LSR arrows, the barrel stove and gas masks, the crater, washing lines, the courtyard pump under the key lantern, a hopscotch. C a plank floor, a painted dado, trusses, tables with benches inside their colliders and the meal still on them, the hatch, the stage with curtains, a radio, a fictional concert banner (green on linen) and the PA horn, enamel signs, a coat rack of works coats. D the church tower (12 x 14 m mass over its collider, belfry openings, a stopped clock, a broken crown with charred beams and the bell exposed in its cage), the well with its windlass roof and lantern, the frozen fountain with icicles, the parish house, town-house upper storeys and gables on the east wall, factory windows on the hall's face. F the brick horn's barrel vault and bell mouth, the fan ring with eight blades round a violet hub, the test section's mullions, a 1:30 Kranich model on its sting above head height, Jette's glass office hung over the test section with Wittkamp's silhouette at the glass, a ladder, the hatch. G the four stands with thrust frames, three burnt engines, the Nachtglas impeller on stand 4, the blast wall with floods and stencils, the bunker's observation slits, periscope and steel door, instruments inside. Works: ThalbachSky (gravelight/world/map04/thalbach_sky.gd): four searchlights sweeping a low cloud deck whose hit spots glow, the dawn that never comes as a mauve band low in the east, forest ridges; a chimney and a water tower on the skyline; the four fire barrels. Traps: trap_chandelier.gd gained an art-only `style` (crystal / skip / wing) and `chain_m`; the Coal Hopper hangs coal skips, the Jig Crane a wing section; drop, radius, timing and kill unchanged (Map 1 keeps crystal). Lights: one key spot per zone from a visible fixture; tag_light_zones gives every light a zone meta and caps shadows at 6 per zone. Budget (contract C4; census_map, software GL, Compatibility, power on, set u09_map04a): before 1850 draw calls / 302k primitives peak; a first pass with every key spot shadowed and every piece casting was 4183 / 492k; three measured fixes brought it inside: only pieces with two sides of 2 m or more cast shadows, the dressing is pre-merged into 40 m cells (440 batches, each centred on its cell so visibility ranges measure from it; small-detail materials fade at about 58 m, frames and trim at 90 m, text at 30 m, instanced details at 45 m), and only the cheap, small-range keys keep a shadow (A pump lantern, C stage, D well, F office, H crane flood; B, E, G keys are shadowless: in the Compatibility renderer a shadowed spot re-draws everything in its cone, about +1200 draw calls in the yards); the moon uses two shadow splits. After (rebased, final): 2259 draw calls / 390k primitives peak (B_shed / B_wide; H 756 / 195k, F 2042 / 306k), 1 shadowed light per zone at most. Ambience: data/gravelight/ambience/map04a.json (ZoneAmbience schema; inert until zone_ambience.gd lands) with eight synthesized 20 s mono beds (gravelight/tools_src/gen_audio_map04a.py on gen_audio.py's DSP, Owned: Siedlung dawn wind and far sirens, Bahnhof steam idle and rail ticks, Kantine radio hum and PA click, Kirchplatz wind and clock tick, Montage dripping iron and a press, Windkanal rumble, Pruefstand impeller hum, Halle room tone and intakes) merged into audio.json, one-shots from existing keys; ZoneAmbience (T-145) attaches it from the file's name; music.ambient map04a_thalbach is now the plain wind_howl under the zone beds (it fell back to the inn's bed, wind and a burning C-47). Captures: u09_after/u09_map04a (22 PNGs, looked at; F_wide re-aimed from the dark door to the hall's south-east corner); fixes after looking: 19 stencils, posters and signs faced into their walls (now yaw = the face's outward normal), long enamel-sign texts overflowed their plates, the Kranich's pale grey read as a snow dune (olive), its nozzles floated off the thin trailing edge, the bay mouth was a black slab (a bay light), the Montage was too dark (brighter lamps). Shared with Totenhafen: it fetched the same corrugated_iron, concrete_wall_004, concrete_floor_worn_001, metal_grate_rusty, barrel_stove, metal_jerrycan and metal_toolbox under the same prop keys and decimation targets, so the rebase kept master's copies and Act I's fl_* keys point at those folders; the JSON tables were re-merged additively after the rebase (scripts, no reformatting). Verified: smoke_map04a PASS, smoke_map04_flow PASS, smoke_squads PASS (its 7 script errors are bot_ee_catalog.gd's, untouched here), check_mapdata PASS, the fernflug soak (tools/ai_soak.sh --scenario=fernflug --runs=1 --max-minutes=10; the positional `fernflug` the docs give silently runs Map 0, and the script's usage comment line is broken on master) PASS with stuck 85 events / 101 s against 25 / 30.8 s for master's map04a.tscn in the same soak: the same hotspots in both (the platform's 0.2 m edge by the coal hopper at (16-20, 40-44), the canteen tables at (-32, 52)) and no new location; the after-run diverged (7 player downs and revives against none) and the colliders are unchanged; tests test_gen_detail_lib (11) and test_map04a_art (6). Not done: the minimap image is not re-baked (only the POIs are current); the Montage still reads dark in Compatibility (the wings are silhouettes); the Kranich's panel lines are plain strips.
T-149, 2026-11-01: Fernflug Act IV, Mondlicht, rebuilt to the bar (UPDATE 09 section 5.5; docs/MAPS_3_4_REBUILD.md sections 3, 4.5, 5.5). An art pass: every collider, nav block, door, price, spawn, prompt, the vacuum and low-gravity systems, the Account Manager, the finale and CANCEL are untouched; the regenerated navmesh (14 regions) and all 99 collision boxes are byte-identical to master's (compared in the .tscn), check_mapdata and the POI refresh report no change. Where it lives: gen_map04d.gd stays the source of truth (dressing rewritten zone by zone: `_dress_shell`, `_dress_reception` .. `_dress_account`, `_presentation`), with the vocabulary in a new gravelight/tools_src/detail_map04d.gd (static helpers, so gen_map04_lib.gd, owned by the Act I worker, is not edited): `mat()` builds the act's materials itself (surface_pbr ShaderMaterials on the Poly Haven sets, world-triplanar so merged batches need no UVs, plus Darkmatter's flat palette: violet #c07dff glows, amber cracks, graphite, chrome), so materials.json (shared by every worker) is not touched; `lathe()`/`paraboloid()` make the capsule and the dishes as ArrayMesh silhouettes (no Blender needed; proportioned primitives on lattices for the towers); `sign()`/`text()` set Darkmatter's corporate type as IBM Plex Sans (the bundled OFL family) at weight 330 with wide tracking on graphite panels with chrome edges and a violet light line, with the Darkmatter mark (a violet lozenge split by an amber crack); `strip()` luminaires; `patch()` a feathered decal-substitute on moon_patch.gdshader (decals do not exist in the Compatibility renderer the web uses); `skin()`/`trim()` give a shared wall a second face and every room skirting, cornice, light cove and pilasters; `tag_lights()` puts `zone` meta on every light (C4 counts shadowed lights per zone; the generators never set it). Assets (CC0, Poly Haven, via tools/fetch_cc0_materials.py and cc0_assets.json, SOURCE/LICENSE in each folder, docs/ASSETS.md regenerated): moon_dusted_01 (yard regolith, scaled from its 0.37 m macro tile to 2.4 m; the rim and crater wall darker), moon_footprints_01 (boot prints on the yard's loop and the airlock paths), moon_track_01 (the field units' tracks to the dish), terrazzo_tiles (concourse, tinted violet), marble_tiles (reception light, the Account dark), granite_tile (the nave), concrete_floor_painted (the store, cold), box_profile_metal_sheet (the store's walls and every hull face the yard sees, its relief with a neutral albedo because the set's red paint cannot be tinted grey); metal_plate was dropped because the Act II worker files it as diamond plate. Per zone: A Reception, the LANTERN capsule as a lathe silhouette (scorched heat shield, paint band, open hatch with steps, portholes, three legs, the spent chute), the landing pad the field units left lit (violet ring, amber lamps, DELIVERIES), the desk with the bell, a tented card and a terminal, a hanging RECEPTION sign, WELCOME, VALUED CUSTOMERS over the airlock, cycle panels, ceiling ribs and white strips, the Terms intel kept on the desk top at 1.0 m; B Concourse, a violet hall with slogans as lit signs (REGIONAL RETENTION RELAY RR-7, EXCEPTIONAL RETENTION, WE'LL TAKE CARE OF EVERYTHING, THANK YOU FOR YOUR CONTINUED ENGAGEMENT, WE DELIVER BEFORE YOU ASK, THE YARD · PLEASE FASTEN YOUR SUIT, AIR IS PROVIDED AS A COURTESY: Darkmatter is sincere, FINEPRINT_LORE rule 1), the relay's 1:60 model under glass with the little Earth painted ochre (INT_M4_RELAY's note) and the plate's bracket the intel rests on, Crucible 001's lit alcove, floor ring and plaque (MOD. R-E · PAT. PEND. BLZ-40417 · UNIT 001), the prototype cabinets' plates, lobby benches, the yard airlock's vestibule (header, chevrons, ports), the ACCOUNTS window and Ledger Lager left where T-141 put them; C Yard, the regolith with prints and tracks, rounded boulder chunks over the cover boxes (the boxes stay the collision), broken slabs along the rim, a crater wall of tilted slabs and ridges with peaks behind, the smile-crack as a violet grin veined with amber on its own rock face high on the north wall (it was hidden behind the rim), the transmitter dish beyond the north-east rim (lattice tower, 12 m paraboloid aimed at the Earth, feed tripod, warning lamp) with the retention signal as a faint additive violet thread to the Earth, the dish's foundation in the yard (concrete ring, bolts, relay mast, repeater dish, junction box, the feed cable over the rim), the two air posts dressed (grated pad, a pylon with AIR · INCLUDED IN YOUR PLAN, a beacon, the hose), dome caps over the lids for the yard's view; the Earth moved from straight overhead (0, 230, 95; never in a frame) to high over the north rim (-10, 150, 255; about 34 degrees up from the yard, 56 m radius) and drawn by earth.gdshader (procedural oceans, continents, ice, a slow cloud deck, a soft terminator from map04d.json earth.sun_dir, an additive atmosphere halo, fog-free); earth_sky.gd keeps its API, its points and its tests (the night hemisphere is transparent until renew() floods it violet; the city points are a fog-free violet); the Sun is the scene's Moon node, low (15 degrees) over the east-south-east rim and white for long hard shadows, and the sky is moon_sky.gdshader (hard unblinking stars, a faint galaxy band, the Sun's disc and glare from the directional light); D Fulfillment, three racks of suited inventory, 27 suits on hangers facing the aisle, each with a PERPETUAL stencil and a tag at the wrist, rack 2 bays 4-6 the Kranich's three by name (RIEDL, HAAS, POHL, from INT_M4_CREWTAGS) with the scratched mark, still and upright (tags, not spectacle), a tag board at rack 2's end for the intel, bay numbers, rack signs, the fold pad (graphite disc, violet ring, amber fold-scar, emitters, the ring overhead), the conveyor with rails, rollers, hoods and nine parcels a ConveyorRun moves, trusses, cold strips, aisle lines, FULFILLMENT, RETURNS ARE NOT ACCEPTED; E Retention Hall, the nave kept with NOBODY CANCELS, now pilasters and pointed ribs above head height (the old diagonal struts crossed the walkable space), violet glass between the ribs, floor inlays (a runner, a ring and spokes round each column), dressed plinths, a graphite panel and light blades behind the sign, the columns' caps rebuilt (a stepped chrome capital, a violet drum with an amber crack, a crystal finial; retention_column.gd, the `Cap` node and its lift unchanged) and the terms plates on chrome lecterns tilted to the reader with the text about 2.5 times larger, four ridge pendants whose lights breathe with the hum (HumPulse; the pulse dies when the relay is cut); F The Account, the round room on its square plan (pendentives in the corners above head height, a ring beam and violet cove at the ceiling, radial ribs from the shaft, concentric floor rings), the dish's foundation ring of concrete round the vacuum shaft with glowing rings in its throat (the old solid violet column closed the shaft to space), the console's dais and conduits to the first retained and the crew pad, the Manager's station disc, Jan Pilch under the floor glass as a raised vitrine (hollow walls, a chrome frame, frosted over him, clear over his hand, covered to the chest, the tag engraved on the rim, a soft glow inside, the relay's threads dim and thin: a person, not a spectacle; the first build had a solid body and a solid rim that hid him, found in the captures), his hand a RetainedHand that slowly curls and stops when `cancelled` is set, the crew pad; the console script itself is unchanged. The doors: door.gd gains a presentation-only `art_style` export ("steel" dresses an unpowered door as the existing steel bulkhead, no lock lamp, the price labels kept); Mondlicht sets it on its six doors because Darkmatter's airlocks are not plank doors; every other map keeps the default. Airlocks: AirlockCycle (gravelight/world/map04/airlock_cycle.gd) stands on the reception, yard and store doors and only shows and sounds the cycle (red lamps, beacons, the house chime and pump hiss, a vapour burst toward the vacuum side, a clunk, green), once when its zone opens and again whenever a survivor steps through (9 s cooldown); the door and the zones' vacuum are untouched. Lights: one shadowed key per zone (A the capsule spot; B the model; C the Sun; D the fold pad; E the column; F the shaft spot down onto the console; the Crucible and aisle spots light without shadow), every light with zone meta (test_mondlicht_art checks every light and the cap of 6), the old SunShaft spot removed (the Sun itself casts) and the earthshine reduced; the domes get shadow-only shells (a 2 m slab over each 0.3 m lid, 1 m outside the east and south hulls) so the low Sun cannot leak through, and SunGate (world/map04/sun_gate.gd, moon_switch's idea) switches the Sun off while the camera is inside a dome more than 9 m from a yard airlock: the shells block it there anyway and its cascades were redrawing every interior caster; the visible fixtures are chrome housings with their glowing tubes; chrome and dark steel are only half metallic because the black sky gives metal nothing to reflect in Compatibility (they rendered black). Sound: gravelight/tools_src/gen_audio_moon.py (synthesized on gen_audio.py's DSP core, Owned) writes six beds (reception air handling, the concourse's relay hum, the yard's suit only, the store's conveyor, the hall's choir-like hum, the Account's single note) and the one-shots (amb_m4d_airlock, the Darkmatter chime, vent, parcel, the suit's valve, the hall's swell), 18 files merged into audio.json; data/gravelight/ambience/map04d.json follows the ZoneAmbience schema (validate() passes) with `vacuum: true` on C_YARD (the bed is the suit, its only one-shot the valve), and the system honours it: ZoneAmbience.set_zone calls the new AudioDirector.set_vacuum_bed, which fades the map's ambient bed to silence while the listener is in a vacuum zone (ZoneAmbience.is_vacuum; reset when the map leaves); Mondlicht's map bed (audio.json music.ambient map04d_mondlicht) is the relay's hum instead of the default inn wind. Cameras: only the u09_map04d set moved (A_desk now looks at the desk's front from the customers' side; F_retained looks down through the clear pane at the hand). Budget (C4), census_map with set u09_map04d on WSL software GL (Compatibility + WebProfile, so these are the web-path numbers): before 1174 draw calls / 195 k triangles peak (B_wide); the first after build measured 2065 / 359 k; the budget pass (a shadow diet in detail_map04d: pieces under 1.2 m, flat pieces and ceiling-high pieces stop casting, 1275 of them; faceted 48-triangle rocks and an 80-triangle sphere instead of Props' 144; one shadowed key per zone; SunGate) brought it to 1281 / 237 k; after the rebase onto master's ViewCells (T-145) the act uses them too: detail_map04d.merge_by_group merges the dressing itself per (zone, material, shadow mode, 22 m cell) before the shared lib's merge (whose cells mix the domes; the lib is not edited), tags the batches, labels, kept pieces and the act's lights with vis_group, and a ViewCells node with six cells shows each dome with what its doors open onto (A: A, B, the yard; B: A, B, the yard, D; the yard: B, the yard, D; D: B, the yard, D, E; E: D, E, F; F: E, F; the dome caps, the apron and the shadow shells are ALL). Final: 1054 draw calls / 223 k triangles peak (B_wide; A_desk 209 k, C_boulder 209 k, D_wide 217 k, every other view under 200 k, F about 100 k); every camera inside the desktop 400 k and 2500 draw calls and below master's draw calls; the four named views sit up to 11 % over the 200 k web line (master was at 195 k), the rest being main-pass geometry inside a cell's own view. The generator prints a triangle tally per dressing group (all dressing is about 70 k triangles). Soak (tools/ai_soak.sh --scenario=fernflug --runs=1 --max-minutes=10 --skip-to-act=4 --god; without --god the bots wipe at round 30 in 0.7 min): this branch 0 stuck events (3.3 s), 0 teleports, 0 stranded; master's map04d.tscn in the same soak 4 stuck events (3.0 s); smoke_map04d, smoke_map04_full, smoke_map04_egg PASS; check_mapdata PASS; the POI refresh reports no change. Known and left: a moire of teeth on the hall's west wall at distance in the Compatibility captures (in the before set too; not light leaking, it survived the shells). Tests: test_mondlicht_art (the cycle phases, the hum, the conveyor wrap, the hand, the Sun gate, light zoning, every light zoned and the shadow cap, the airlock nodes on their doors, the Earth in the eye line, the ambience table and its keys).

T-147, 2026-11-01: Fernflug Act II, der Kranich, rebuilt as the inside of a flying wing (UPDATE 09 section 5.3; docs/MAPS_3_4_REBUILD.md sections 3 and 5.3; branch u09-5-act2). An art pass: every collider, the 16 navmesh regions and the links are byte-identical to master's scene (checked by diffing the NavigationMesh resources, the 170 box shapes and the links), so no timing, hitbox, hatch cost, prompt, set-piece or the Bordwart's arena moved. **The shell** is one Blender script, tools_src/blender/world/kranich_shell.py (tools/build_kranich_shell.sh; wo_lib's export conventions), writing one GLB per room in the act's own frame (assets/ours/world/kranich_bay/body/wing_l/wing_r/nacelle/kanzel/cockpit, "Ours", 170k triangles in all, 13-39k a room): frames every 1.5 m (4/3 m in the wings, so every third rib stands on one of the generator's rib-stub colliders) as I-sections with octagonal lightening holes rising up the walls into an elliptic arch, the skin 0.6 m behind the wall's room face wherever the outside is not walkable ("deep") and on the face itself where another room is behind it ("flat": the wing roots, the bulkheads, a 5 cm stiffener), stringers broken at every opening, bulkheads with coamed hatches exactly over the generator's door/window/core-window/breach openings, four perspex blisters with rings and bars, the flight deck glazed across its whole front and brow, portholes, deck strips in the cavities, floors (bay doors with a torn breach under grating, catwalk grating over a sub-floor, riveted plates, diamond plate). gen_map04b hides the flat wall, lid, deck, old rib-post and glass meshes (their colliders stay) and places a KranichShell node (gravelight/world/map04/kranich_shell.gd) that instances the GLBs at load and maps the slots: the skin is kranich_skin.gdshader, our own, over Poly Haven "Blue Metal Plate" (CC0, Rob Tuytel; desaturated to the grey-green primer) drawing the rivet rows procedurally on each room's frame lines (the shell's UVs are metres along the frame axis and the arc), the stringer rows and the sheet seams; frames are the same primer without rivets; floors use Poly Haven "Metal Plate" (CC0) through MaterialLibrary key deck_plate; grating.gdshader is an alpha-scissored grid that stops discarding at distance; perspex never casts; the skin casts double-sided shadows so the moon only enters through the openings; each room has a 95 m visibility range and the generator's static batches 80 m. Blender was available, so the primitive fallback was not needed. **The outside** is FlightSky (gravelight/world/map04/flight_sky.gd, data map04b.json flight.sky, presentation only, local on every peer): a sky shader on the act's environment (stars, the moon's disc where the Moon light comes from, the violet east after London, which it now does itself because london_window.gd's tween targets the old ProceduralSkyMaterial, dawn for the crash), three camera-following cloud sheets scrolling aft at their own speeds and a camera-following ground (sea with the moon's glitter path, coasts sweeping past over coast_s seconds, blacked-out land, London's street grid and lights round the plate with fires after the bursts, the salt flat), all fading to the sky's haze before the player camera's 400 m far plane, no volumetrics, so the web profile's depth fog is all it needs. The flight clock is the act round (legs: the North Sea, the English coast, London on the plate's level with the clouds above it thinned, out over the Atlantic, the American coast for the bomb run, the dark west). London: by the LondonWindow's own clock (its `_t`, never changed) six searchlight cones from the city from 12 s and flak bursts (flash, puff, thump, the clouds lit) between 16 s and 29.5 s, before the window's 4 s of silence; the LondonPlate's base is the ground shader in city mode and the plate shows only on the London leg or while the window runs (it had floated over the North Sea's clouds). Fighters: three silhouette passes a round at deterministic times (every peer sees them together), plus one with every FlightController strafe: a generic unmarked single-engine shape with an exhaust glow, a tracer burst, war_fighter_pass following it, war_fighter_mg on some. Turbulence: the existing push and roll, a small camera shake held through the bank, and every node in the group flight_swing (caged lamps and their bulbs as twin pendulums, the bulb parented under its power light so it is dark until the aux bus, harness straps on the bunks and seats, the sheared shackle over the bay's breach) swings as a damped pendulum; flight_spin turns the impeller core and the Bordfunk's tape reels, flight_rod slides the wings' control rods. The crash: when the exfil cutscene starts the ground drops to the salt flat at dawn with a hangar and a flag and the WingDiorama (hidden in flight) is shown. FlightSky searches from its own map root because current_scene is still the previous scene while a map readies (the capture tool, change_scene): that was why the first captures showed the old black sky. **The zones** (gravelight/tools_src/detail_map04b.gd, static helpers called with the generator; everything on top of an existing collider, flush, above 2.1 m or in the shell's cavities): A the six racks as cradles with posts, a shackle beam and sway braces, rack numbers and crew chalk, the doors' rams and pipes seen through the grating, the ramp with its hinge, rams, locking dogs and stripes, the breach (torn door, grating, a swinging sheared shackle, wind streaks, a cold moon spot from below as the key light), parachute packs, oxygen, a first-aid box, the load list, a helmet; B the galley (painted cupboards, zinc top, hotplate, the still-warm kettle with steam, mugs, a loaf, a mess tin, wall cupboards), two-tier bunks with harnesses buckled over nobody, the Bordfunk deck (radio sets with dials, the amber tuning glow, turning tape reels, the chart table with chart, rule, map case and headphones), the cable troughs, oxygen bottles in the cavities, the Crucible's tie-down rings and its violet key light; the Bordkasse and the Ledger Lager machine (T-141) untouched; C fuel cells behind both spar webs, cable runs, the fuel line, sliding control rods on bellcranks, station placards on the stubs, swinging hand lamps, four caged bulbs under the main spar, emergency reds, an inspection lamp that casts, the breach panels now see-through when blown (BreachPanel.see_through: the plate covers the shell's real hole while shut); D the lead-glass core window as a deep bolted frame on the collider's face with the turning violet impeller in an open housing behind it, heat shimmer, the engineer's console with gauges, levers, the speaking tube and his log, the three rib frames over their colliders, coolant pipes and valves, lockers; E twin guns on ring mounts through three blisters, and at the aft one (LondonCam stands inside that mount's footprint) a low stub with its stowed guns, receiver and ammunition to starboard, outside the window's view; ammunition cans and belts, spent casings, a moon shaft through each dome; a pre-existing quirk the flat walls used to hide: Act II's wall-gun labels and chalk sit a centimetre behind the wall face facing the wall (yaw 180 / 90 in the buys table), so a painted backing plate now stands just behind each wall gun and covers them as the wall did (the table and the prompts are untouched; whether those five wall guns should face the room is for the lead); F two seats with harnesses, columns, pedals, a thin panel of 36 lit dials under a glare shield, the throttle quadrant, the autopilot cradle with the Werk's fictional crane-in-a-cog on its lid and the violet strap. Lights: caged fixtures you can see for every light, 6 shadowed in all (one per zone: the breach moon, the Crucible, an inspection lamp per wing, the core, the autopilot strap; E none), every light carries a `zone` meta (the generator's post-pass fills it for lamp()/shaft() lights from the zone boxes), omni shadows on cube maps. **Sound**: gravelight/tools_src/gen_audio_kranich.py (gen_audio's DSP, Owned) synthesizes the whole wing's drone (audio.json music.ambient map04b_kranich = ambient_map04b, replacing the inn's wind the act used to fall back to), six zone loops, creaks, rattles, Bordfunk crackle, gusts and flak; data/gravelight/ambience/map04b.json (ZoneAmbience's schema) gives every zone a bed and two or three one-shots; T-145's ZoneAmbience, on master by the rebase, attaches it to the act (config_path falls back to map04b.json), and test_flight_sky checks the schema and that every bed resolves. **Captures** (WSL + Xvfb, Compatibility, invisible): u09_map04b re-aimed E_london (through the aft blister, back and down) and F_glass (cradle, seats, glass, brow), gained E_window (the London window 22 s in, via FlightSky.preview_london) and a FlightSky.calm on the first camera (a bank shoved the capture camera across the deck); tools_src/capture.gd gained a generic `call:<node>:<method>[:<wait>]` camera op. **Census** (`sh tools/wsl_godot.sh --path . res://gravelight/tools_src/census_map.tscn -- --set=u09_map04b`: WSL + Xvfb, the Compatibility renderer with the web profile applied, every shadow pass counted, 19 cameras): before (master, MAPS_3_4_REBUILD section 4) draw calls peak 1702, primitives peak 372k, 38 omni / 0 spot / 1 directional, 1 shadowed light; after, draw calls peak 1482 (B_wide), primitives peak 368k (B_wide; 12 of the 19 views under the web figure of 200k, the centre body's long views and the flight deck's wide one above it), 48 omni / 5 spot / 1 directional, at most 1 shadowed light per zone (six, plus the moon and one gameplay light outside the zones), mesh triangles in the scene 742k (590k before). The first census of the pass peaked at 611k: the frame webs were double-sided plates with octagonal holes and the frames and floors cast shadows; single-sided webs (the skin shader draws both faces, the normal flipped on the back), hexagonal holes and a skin-only shadow took the shell from 170k to 96k triangles. **Checks**: smoke_map04b, smoke_map04_flow, smoke_map04_full PASS; check_mapdata PASS (no POI moved); a 10-minute Fernflug soak from Act II (seed 1) wiped at act round 3 with 45 stuck events / 57.5 s and 1 teleport against 52 / 81.6 s and 0 on master's scene with the same seed (`tools/ai_soak.sh fernflug` without `--scenario=` silently runs the default Map 0 scenario: the work order's form is wrong). Not done: the bank does not swing the hangings on online clients (FlightController only banks on the host); the inspection-hatch windows still look into their closets (painted grey), not the sky; Forward+ on the owner's GPU is unmeasured (the census is the Compatibility renderer with the web profile applied).
T-150, 2026-11-01: the web packs re-split after the section 5 map rebuilds and the new menu (UPDATE 09 section 2c). Problem: 219.3 MB to reach the menu (target under 150) and game3 at 97.9 MB (each pack must stay at most 70 MB for static hosts), because the main pack still held every people GLB (52.8 MB of characters), all the sfx and music (the per-map beds alone ~85 MB) and, since the maps were rebuilt, a lot of textures. tools_src/web_pack_lists.gd now (1) moves the people GLBs (except the squad the menu poses: dutch, wren, deacon, peg; MENU_PEOPLE) and the six human_base bodies into the game packs (zombie_base stays: the menu's walkers and tearers, and the Marke armband); (2) sorts every file under gravelight/audio/{sfx,music,jingles} by the sound-bank key that plays it: files of keys the menu may play (string literals in res://ui and res://gravelight/ui, the keys in map00_ambience.json that the menu patches onto the Map 0 war, plus music_menu and the ui clicks, with the same legacy dir fallback AudioDirector uses) stay in the main pack; files whose every key is named only by one map's data/gravelight/ambience/<map>.json or the music.ambient entry for it go to that map's pack (the shared map04 pack when only Fernflug acts play them), so the Map 0 and Map 1 beds (10.8 MB each) and Totenhafen / Fernflug zone beds leave the main pack; ambient_wind is only the fallback bed (AudioDirector.start_ambient) and does not count as match-wide use; any other key also named in a script or scene outside the maps is match-wide and goes to the game packs; (3) sizes the game chunks by an estimate of the exported pack (textures x0.56, everything else x1; first guess 0.45 gave game3 77.7 MB) at 60 MB per chunk, giving game1..game6 of 25-64 MB. Third-party textures needed only by one map's scene closure still go to that map's pack (T-139's rule, unchanged: a texture that data/gravelight/materials.json or props_library.json names stays out of the map packs because material_library.gd and prop_library.gd read those at run time for any map; those go to the game packs). The Poly Haven / ambientCG set the inn's menu_inn.tscn uses (63 textures, 44 MB) cannot leave the main pack. MapPacks needed no change: it reads packs/manifest.json and mounts every pack whose name starts with game in the background, so game5 / game6 are picked up from the data. Result (MB): engine 37.7, main pack 97.8, html 0.3 = 135.8 to reach the menu (was 219.3); game1..game6 48.0 / 45.7 / 63.8 / 37.3 / 24.9 / 56.5; maps 14.4 (map00) / 7.7 / 28.9 / 22.0 / 9.6 / 14.4 / 22.4; first match 420-441. Checks: the self-test from build/web (--main-pack cz-main-*.pck -- --packs-from=packs --packs-selftest) mounts and instantiates all seven maps, PASS 0 failures; the main pack alone boots headless to the menu with no script or resource error (the only ERROR lines are the desktop engine not finding addons/webrtc inside a web pack, and a flaky 'texture_2d_initialize Parameter t is null' of the dummy renderer in 1 run of 4 that does not occur with --path .); tools/serve_web.sh serves the page, manifest, wasm, js and every pack with 200 and application/wasm, text/javascript, application/octet-stream, application/json. Not done: no sound-by-sound runtime trace of the menu (a sound the menu plays by a key built at run time would be silent until the game packs finish); the web preset's own 1024 px texture cap is unchanged.

T-152, 2026-11-01: Totenhafen's UPDATE 10 design (docs/U10_TOTENHAFEN_DESIGN.md; contracts docs/U10_CONTRACTS.md). The lead's calls: the main egg LETZTE FAHRT follows Deacon's wish to hear U-1047's diesels and the boat's log (Kptlt. Arvid Lenz refused to sail with the coffins, scuttled the injectors and the cells, and went down to the pump deck: he is DER KIELHOLER, the drowned captain in the harbour's salvage diving dress with the Marke glowing under the helmet glass; Obermaschinist Rolf Brecht wrote the start sequence 'for whoever is not them'); steps s0..s10 with parallel threads (the Ballast choice: dump 3 / load 1; a fictional three-rotor cipher machine fed by three radios; four log pages; the Undertow's parts), three diesel holdouts with twists (the lathe during a flood, the battery in the dark, the sequence while the Kielholer pull), the boss, the key, the curtain, the moving holdout on the deck as she sails, the dive with the cargo, and the Hale telegram as the card Peg carries into the Basel thread. THE UNDERTOW (a pressurised Nachtglas harpoon gun; MAELSTROM upgrade) is the map's buildable wonder weapon. THE SLENDER moves from Morgenrot (not built) to Totenhafen round 25 as the Hafenwächter, the pens' night watchman who locked every door on the night of the Erwachen (a Morgenrot hook stays). Why: the work order asks for three 'only here' features and an egg, boss and wonder weapon at the Kaltenbrunn bar; the boat was always the map's hero and the log the owner's 'next pass'.

T-151, 2026-11-01: THE BAR (docs/THE_BAR.md, UPDATE 10 section 1). A 28-row scorecard (the owner's 18 seed rows plus ten the Inn and Kaltenbrunn earned since: relief squads, extra people with favours, the Jakub arc, an opening, zone audio beds, a current minimap, crate drama, test coverage, recorded voice, pays well), scored 0-3 with an evidence cell per map: Inn 71, Kaltenbrunn 68, Totenhafen 45, Fernflug I 43, II 37, III 43, IV 39 of 84. The target (section 1.3) is >= 71 per column plus three 'only here' features a player can act on and no seed row at 0. The lead's rulings on the structural rows (section 3.2): row 19 (relief squads) counts 'a living war or its equivalent' in flight and on the Moon (other bombers and fighters that matter in Act II; Darkmatter field units on the Moon in Act IV), so those acts are scored on the equivalent, not waived; row 27 (recorded voice) is bakeable on this PC with the Kokoro venv, so the missing Map 4 lines are baked in this update rather than waited for; row 28 (pays well) is measured by the soak once Q-19 lets a soak reach each act. The blind review at the end (section 6) re-scores with scores hidden.
