T-159, 2026-11-02: PAUL, the homage boss of Kaltenbrunn (UPDATE 10 section 4, HB2; docs/HOMAGE_BOSSES.md section 5, contracts C5). Built on the HB1 framework: `HomageEncounter` (gravelight/enemies/homage/homage_encounter.gd) is a new one-boss round director shared with THE SLENDER (the Cousins keep CousinsRound): the round is reserved so the Foreman and a Bellringer round move off it, and if one still sits on it at the round start (a late reservation, a debug jump) the boss moves +1 (`pick_round`); the horde runs at `horde_fraction`; `bots_alone` is HB1's switch per boss block; lines play baked VO when lines.json has them, else a silent subtitle; `title_of` / `fill_tokens` resolve the Likeness Sheet with its defaults and strip any `%TOKEN%` / `[TOKEN]`, so no placeholder reaches the screen (PAUL, "Pvt. Paul" with no surname while `PAUL_LASTNAME` is unfilled). `PaulRage` is the pure rage meter (damage +1 per 1% max HP, fleeing +4/s, a down +10 then +1/s, cooling -0.5/s after 8 s, a bottle -20, the tin cup -5; stages 34/67 with a 5-point hysteresis down). `Paul` (the MZ humanoid "soldier" heavy male, inst_tint iron-red, scaled 1.38 x stage 1.00/1.15/1.30, riveted plates that push out per stage, the six-bottle rack with IV lines, each bottle its own hitbox `bottle1..6` with 400 HP scaled, beard, cap, dog tags, the tin cup) has every move of section 5.4 with its telegraph, damage and cooldown from bosses/map01.json; the neck takes x2 from behind (> 120 deg), small hits never flinch him, a blast of 3% max HP knees him 2.5 s (x1.5), a Charge into a wall stuns him 2 s (x1.5), a Charge opens unbought doors for free; he waits in G (never enters the Core Vault) and takes the lift shaft between the surface and F (the shaft is 12 m in the geometry, not the 40 m of the doc). Arrival: the headframe pieces jitter with dust and the players' views shake, the PA roar, the Operator, the leap from the head platform (y 27.4) to the yard, a crater (a kept decal) that knocks everyone within 10 m down 0.8 s, the title card and the rage bottle on the bar (BossHud.add_gauge). Fail-safe = the designed retreat (6:00: headframe, north) and a return 8-10 rounds later at +35% with six bottles until killed. Death and the twist (section 5.6) play in full and are never undone (phase CLEARED, the round unreserved, no return): to his knees, the rage drains, the shrink and the red fading toward a human colour, his last line chosen by the nearest player's character, the cup pressed into that player's hand (`tin_cup` meta + the `trinket_tin_cup` unlock), the slow forward fall; then the Operator's quiet line, the squad's line (Deacon's prayer, Wren's name), 2000 Scrip each, a Double Scrip drop, the card, ARC_HB_PAUL + INT_HB_02/03 in the Archive (ProfileSave adds the boss files' "archive" rows), and one PAUL'S LAST ROUND per player: free Ironhide outside the cap until downed (new `PerkManager.grant_slotless`) or a one-time +100 overshield (new `PerkManager.add_overshield`, absorbed first in filter_hit). Calls: the bowled zombie is killed in his hands and a body proxy flies (a live zombie can't be carried by the nav agent); the ore cart / rail section he rips is a proxy prop (the yard's carts are dressing); the "first-person lift" holds the player at his fist's height in front of his face (the camera stays the player's own); Fasbender's radio room has no door node, so the torn door is a prop on the floor; no bugle sting exists yet (`bugle_phrase` plays if one is added); VO is OneCore placeholder TTS for PAU (David, low; the PA line through the PA chain), no real voice imitated. `PEG_PAUL_DEAD_NAMED_01` stays reserved (not in lines.json) until the surname is supplied.

T-160, 2026-11-02: THE SLENDER at Totenhafen, the Hafenwächter (UPDATE 10 section 4, HB3; docs/HOMAGE_BOSSES.md section 6). The canon move, from docs/U10_TOTENHAFEN_DESIGN.md section 9: Morgenrot is not built, and his design (a very tall night watchman with a lantern and a ring of keys who works the night shift) fits the harbour's night watch exactly; he is the pens' night watchman, who walked the catwalks with a lantern and a ring of keys and locked every door behind him on the night of the Erwachen so the dead would stay in the pens; the Marke kept him walking; he appears at Totenhafen round 25 standalone (45 campaign), walks the catwalks, locks the Pen Hall doors and the pump stair as he passes (free to reopen when he leaves the zone or dies), and his lantern beam is the danger through the Watch system; the Morgenrot hook stays as a placeholder entry in bosses/map03.json pointing at the same script; likeness fields stay default and no placeholder token is printed. Built: `WatchMeter` (pure, per player: every fill and drain of section 6.3, tiers with whispers, CRITICAL puts him behind you, 100 quarantines; bots cap at 94; a second player waits under 100 while the room is busy; 10 s immunity after release), `Slender` (the MZ "dockworker" thin male, 2.6 m by the pivot plus lengthened upper arms and legs in the pose layer, pale with violet eyes, a long grey greatcoat with brass buttons, collar and scarf, a peaked cap, a brass lantern burning violet on a short pole (hitbox `lantern`, 3% HP, x2, breaks into a 6 s groping stun and relights), the keys at the hip that jingle as he walks and go silent before the Key Ring Lash, the watch-book under the arm (hitbox `book`, 2%: shot out, x1.5 for 4 s, 30 s lock)), the Walk 1.6 m/s, the Teleport (ObservedFreeze: only unobserved, 15 m closer, 4 s), Mirroring, the Lantern Sweep (1.0 s rise, a 70 deg 20 m cone over 90 deg for 2 s, +35/s), the Key Ring Lash (0.6 s silence + 0.4 s backswing, 85 + 0.6 s knockdown), Shift Change below 50% (two decoys with flickering lanterns; their hitboxes are his, limb `decoy`, so hitscan reaches them; a hit logs +15, no damage; they dissolve after 25 s or 5% HP in light; recast every 40 s), End of Shift below 20% (every map light out 30 s, the pools off until one of three fuse boxes is held 5 s). The lit rule at Totenhafen: he bleeds only in the powered lamp pools (quay floods, the pen hall key light and wall floods, the catwalk bulkheads, pole lamps; map02.json the_slender.lights), in the signal tower's battery searchlight cone or any `searchlights` node (the C2 Searchlight hooks in through `lights_point`), to a shooter within 5 m, or while frozen; in the dark a bullet is a smoke puff. He therefore needs the power on (the first round >= 25 after power-on), never comes while `sailing` or another boss holds `boss_fight`. `BuyDoor.boss_lock()/boss_unlock()`: a locked door shuts and blocks, can't be bought or used; it re-opens free when he is out of its zone boxes and > 9 m away, dies or walks off. `WatchRoom` (built once at y -60 under the pens: shelves, a green-shaded lamp, the clock at 3:00, three books, his figure walking in at 1 m/s): tear your page (1.5 s hold) within 25 s (35 s solo) to be let go standing with 3 s invulnerability; too late: returned downed; teammates' lantern hits release you (20% each). Fail-safe: after 8:00 he walks off the quay's east surf steps into the storm, every door he locked opens, the meters clear, he returns 5 rounds later at full HP. Death: the look up, the book closed and set down, the lantern on it, blown out, "Shift's over. Someone else has the watch now.", he sits and stays (DEAD, harmless, nothing targets him); rewards: 2500 Scrip each, card_night_shift, charm_violet_lantern (record only, D9), ARC_HB_SLENDER, OVERWATCH (a depth-ignoring violet rim overlay on up to 24 zombies within 12 m of a player, softer with reduce-screen-effects), the Watch-Book INT_HB_04 whose pages are this match's events in a 22:00-onward night clock. The originality checklist (section 6.2), honoured item by item: faceless head -> he wears the MZ body's real face, pale and tired, with two violet eyes and a cap; nothing hides it. Black suit and tie -> a grey watchman's greatcoat, brass buttons, scarf, peaked cap; no suit or tie anywhere in the costume code. Tentacles or extra limbs -> two arms, only longer; the costume has nothing on his back but coat cloth; the decoys are whole separate watchmen, not limbs. Collect 8 pages in a forest -> one page about you in one of three bound books, torn out to be let go. TV static on proximity -> the `logged` status's violet screen distortion from MEDIUM up and his tier words; no static, and he talks, calmly. A silent stalker who kills on sight -> a watchman on his rounds: he patrols the catwalks, the pump stair and the quay, logs, warns, mirrors, and takes you in for review; his only damage is the Lash and a failed room. Not built (cuts, documented in HOMAGE_BOSSES "HB2 / HB3 build notes"): the coat's bone-chain cloth, the HIGH tier's audio dropouts / echo footsteps / HUD flicker (the status FX and the pen-scratch line stand in), the room's live window render, the Leuchtpistole flares (a `flares` group hook exists), the SkyClock nudge at his death (Totenhafen has none), the cable-car ending egg (Morgenrot only). VO: OneCore placeholder TTS for SLN (Mark, slow, close), no real voice imitated.

T-154, 2026-11-02: Totenhafen's living world, dressing and sound (UPDATE 10 section 2.2; contract C1; design docs/U10_TOTENHAFEN_DESIGN.md sections 7-8; branch u10-2-totenhafen-world). MapAmbience (gravelight/world/landscape/map_ambience.gd) now runs on any map: MapRoot._attach_map_ambience (the lead's loader, 25a93f1) gives it data/gravelight/map02_ambience.json; "needs_landscape": false runs it without Map 0's Landscape, and every Map 0 section only runs when its key is in the file (Map 0's file and behaviour unchanged). New kinds are data blocks that default to off, written as `_kinds_setup_<kind>()` methods the node finds and runs at setup (a registry of ticks, round, power, shot, explosion and flag handlers, random timers and debug triggers), so the Fernflug act workers append their own blocks without touching the main body. Timed beats count "beat rounds" (the match round standalone, rounds on this map after a campaign arrival, so a campaign squad arriving at 21+ still gets them). Totenhafen's kinds (the "U10 Totenhafen" block): gulls (Map 0's CrowFlock with perches, colour and flight points from data; they scatter at the first shot and come back after quiet), lighthouse (a beam every 9 s from the breakwater's end, the foghorn every few minutes, gutters out at round 10), bell_tower (the cliff church's bell swings in the wind and tolls once on the quest flag egg_lf_started), ships (a burning freighter, a destroyer's searchlight sweeping the bay, its guns, star shells over the water), low_pass (two RAF Mosquitoes at round 6, a new PlaneModels.mosquito, flak from the cliff), raid (round 12: sirens, flak, seven bombs walking across the pens' roof; the hall shakes, dust from 45 ceiling points, the hall's new swinging pendants and chains swing, icicles fall, drips triple for a minute, the Operator's OPR_RAID_M2_01), chains (pulley blocks on chains, a second LampSway on group swing_chains: wind and explosions), drips, conveyor_jolt (the cannery belt jolts and runs at power-on: one can mesh scrolled by its spacing), radio_chatter (five radios; RADIO_UBOAT_01..06, speaker UBR, spoken in German, subtitled in English: gen_vo gained a per-line "speech" field and a cast flag german_all, both used only by these lines, so no other line rebuilt; fictional traffic, no real boat numbers but U-1047's), cutter_slip (round 15: the wreck creeps, rides over the parapet and slides down the shingle into the surf with a groan and a splash; its cradle, torn-off wheelhouse and nets stay on the quay over the unchanged collider so the M1 Carbine's board still hangs on something; the host sets cutter_slipped, the hidden sailor's letter appears and a node named Surf_Cutter is enabled if the systems worker registers it), footprints (one MultiMesh of fading wet prints behind every player on the wet concrete), frozen_spray (ice on the parapet, mist bursts on big waves), fish_crates (four crates tip and the fish slide out when the dead run through, tweens only), storm (GLBus.tide_stage / spring_tide / tide_flood_end from the systems worker's TideController, else its water level polled: the sea's and the docks' wave amplitude, both LightningFlash nodes' rate and strength, the rain on the tower glass) and power_spectacle (the pumps thunder, the docks drain 0.4 m through a new level_offset uniform of the water shader (visual only; the tide's own level is untouched), the pen lamps come on dock by dock from the pump room through a per-light sweep_delay meta PowerSwitch now honours (absent = at once, Kaltenbrunn unchanged), the perks wake in that order with 2.6 s of their jingles, the signal lamp spins up with the power instead of always, U-1047's red battle lanterns dim to 15 % as the white lamps come on; then the existing burst and the Operator). Dressing (gen_map02 _dress_u10_*, DetailMap02): the cliff town (twelve ruined façades, two fires) and the church with its bell on a kept pivot, the lighthouse, two harbour cranes and the freighter in silhouette against a new north storm card with its own lightning, the destroyer, the dead U-boat half sunk off the breakwater (the Type VII loft, rusted), the hut's interior (stove, cot, sea chart, oilskins, wireless), the hall's pendants, chains and icicles, the cutter as one kept piece; the U09 "pump reads as a box" was the lower pens' plain columns standing in front of the pumps in F_hero (close captures showed the pumps reading as machines), so the columns got a plinth, chamfers, a numbered band and a conduit, and the motor's end got an end bell, fan cowl and dome. Checked: the regenerated map02.tscn has the same 22 navmeshes, 20 NavigationLinks, 359 collision shapes and 112 Gameplay nodes as before (content-identical, only auto-numbered names differ). Story props (LORE.md section 13.6, intel.json, story_props.json; StoryProp gained hidden_until_flag): the sailor's letter (quay, after the cutter goes), the Hale telegram (two lines, the teletype's table), the cannery pay slip, the child's drawing beside the shelter door (a curator's one-line description, shown with care), Hinnerk's ration book (the chart table). Sound: ten new synthesized one-shots (gen_ambience_map02_u10.py: foghorn, siren, chains, conveyor, radio static, cutter groan, pump start, icicle, crate spill, ship guns) merged into audio.json without reformatting it; the beds got the far ship guns, chains and radio static; the round-bound sounds are MapAmbience events. Loudness: gravelight/tools_src/amb_loudness.py measures each bed as it plays (BS.1770 integrated loudness + the zone db + the key trim) and with the storm bed under it, against the footsteps' loudest 400 ms (-33.0 LUFS); the U09 mix had every bed 7-8 dB over a footstep, so the storm bed's key went to -17 dB and the zone beds to -20..-25 dB: every bed with the storm under it now sits 9.0-9.3 dB under a footstep (rule: at least 8). Why: the work order's list, the design's beats, presentation only, the systems worker owns every gameplay value. Not done: Surf_Cutter itself (the systems worker's spawn; not on its branch yet); the lead must re-run gen_map02 after merging both Totenhafen branches (both touch map02.tscn).

T-158, 2026-11-02: UPDATE 10 section 3, Fernflug Act IV "Mondlicht" (branch u10-3-act4; the Bar's Act IV column, docs/MAP4_FERNFLUG.md 6.10). Everything of T-112/T-113/T-149 stays (low gravity, vacuum and suits, the domes, the yard, the dish, SunGate, ViewCells, Pilch's hand, the airlock cycles, the finale, CANCEL, the renewal, the Account Manager); this extends it with data blocks in map04d.json (`terminator`, `heaters`, `low_g`, `meteor_shower`, `shaft_grate`, `earth.reverse_steps`) and the C1 living world (`map04d_ambience.json`, `_ev_*` kinds appended under `# ---- U10 Act IV ----` in map_ambience.gd, every node lookup guarded, Map 0 unchanged). Calls made: (1) the terminator is a vertical plane facing the Sun that crawls from all-day at act round 1 to all-night at round 16 (a 20 s glide after each round start), with a shadow-only wall on the line so the regolith and the crater show the night as a real shadow rather than a shader trick; night zones cost the suit +0.6 s of air per s in vacuum (x0.4 after the heaters), dim the domes to 55 % and multiply that zone's spawns by 1.5 through `ZombieDirector.set_spawn_boost` (the "+1 pressure"); the Operator speaks once per zone crossed, simultaneous crossings share one line. (2) The visor is its own action (`visor`, H) rather than a flashlight long-press, because the flashlight's press timing is shared with bots and the web build; the glare only exists on the day side of the open yard facing the low Sun. (3) The act's power-on is the relay heaters' breaker (`HeaterSwitch`, the concourse by the yard airlock): domes light one by one, the mast (`RelayMast`) surges violet, the airlocks cycle once, the perks spark, four Gangers burst, the Operator. (4) Earthrise reverses by the Kuendigung's completed steps 15-18 (a quarter each); CANCEL turns the night side dark blue; two Operator and two Wren lines notice. (5) Low gravity: five `JumpPad` plates (three in the yard around a new crater with one east gap, two in the hall), each a ballistic arc solved under the act's gravity; leapers rise at the yard's catwalk points and two new `Catwalk_E_HALL` points (the Moon has no GroomController, so Act III's Jumpjockey rule never ran here: `MoonLowG` carries it); a Lodestone fling in vacuum at >= 15 m/s (every plain Moon fling is 20.8) sends the zombie to orbit, a credited kill plus 120 Scrip, counted as SENT TO ORBIT. On the host a remote player's puppet never reports the floor, so pads use the height check for remote players. (6) The meteor shower is a round event on match rounds 33, 39, 45 (never a special round): a 10 s warning ("scheduled maintenance"), 40 s of telegraphed strikes in the yard only (every third aimed near a player in the open, nothing under a roof), each strike a blackboard danger for the bots; the Account's vent (`ShaftGrate` + `ShaftGrateLever`, 1000 Scrip, open 8 s, cooldown 60 s) pulls the dead into the vacuum shaft and throws players clear for 25. (7) Row 19's equivalent: three Darkmatter field units (constructs with lamps, never people) walk the far rim and never engage. Row 5/20: Obergefreiter Pohl, the Kranich's wireless operator, a retained crewman not yet inventory, awake in rack 2 bay 6: a Talk card and a favour (read his tag into the record at the Account console's `TagSlot` before CANCEL: his line, 750 Scrip, flag `pohl_tag_read`); he never fights; his body is the kubiak kit as a placeholder (a crew model is an art follow-up). Row 21: Jakub's console line when he is with the squad. Row 6: four new fixed story props (the field units' delivery log, Haas's tag, the relay's maintenance card, the LANTERN's flight card). Row 7: the smile gets a minimap note on first sight. (8) Transitions: exfil cutscenes start loading the next act on a thread (`LoadingScreen.preload_scene`), and the endings drop the act's card as a curtain. (9) Online: the line, the strikes, the pad launches and the fling are host-picked and mirrored (`Net.mirror`). (10) The SFX bus is muffled through an AudioEffect while in vacuum (`AudioDirector.set_vacuum_bed`: a 420 Hz low-pass and -12 dB on the SFX bus; voice, music and UI stay clear, the suit on the radio). The one-shots are synthesized (gen_audio_moon.py, Owned); 29 lines baked (OneCore Operator, Kokoro Wren/Jakub/Pohl). Soak (`--scenario=fernflug --runs=1 --max-minutes=10 --skip-to-act=4 --god`): 5 stuck events (7.5 s), 0 teleports, 0 stranded, 141 bot kills; the bots bleed out in round 30 on the arrival kit (as in T-149), so the god player holds the round open and the ring past 30 is not proven by bots; with `--open-doors=all --power` (the hall-concourse ring open) 13 stuck events (18.4 s), 0 teleports, 0 stranded, the stretches at the hall's middle plinth (28, 5.4) and the Reception corner the scripted player circles, none at a pad or the crater. The bots surviving 30+ on the Moon is left to the bots worker (s7, T-163).

T-157, 2026-11-02: UPDATE 10 §3 Act III "Groom Lake" (map04c; branch u10-3-act3; as built in docs/MAP4_FERNFLUG.md §5.10). The calls: (1) act rounds are the act's own count, so the work order's "rounds 22, 23, 26, 27" are act rounds 2, 3, 6, 7 (match rounds in a campaign that starts the act on 21). (2) The dust storm stays the director's special round (T-110) and is pinned to every 5th act round by the GroomController (RoundManager.next_special), so the loop past round 9 keeps the rhythm; the fog is set from a visibility (3/vis, 15 m) and the sky, ambient and sun dim with it. "Stalker" is not a new enemy: it is the role the Burrowers and the plain surface Gängers take while the storm runs; hearing is a small static rule class (`SoundHunter`, gravelight/enemies/variants/) and one hook in `Zombie._update_chase` (`hunt_by_sound`, reset on activate): 6 m, ×3 while sprinting or 1.5 s after a shot, else walk to the last heard spot. (3) The collapses really take ground off the navmesh: the wreck's region (`Nav_A_WRECK_0`, static colliders in its own AABB) is re-baked at runtime round the debris (~7 ms); the debris is solid only after the shove (a collider appearing round a player threw them 50 m up in the first smoke); the second section seals W_A_L and tears a plate out of the bulkhead (a generator opening filled by `BulkheadPlate`) for the new gap. (4) The living world runs on master's U10 kinds framework (T-154): the Act III block at the end of map_ambience.gd holds only its own kinds and shares the framework's `storm_k` (rebased onto db90e88: the branch's verbatim copy of the framework and its second `storm_k` are gone); the act counts act rounds itself because the framework's campaign `round_base` is one low at setup (current_round is still the previous map's last round), which the lead may want to fix there. The B-29s are their own key (`low_pass_b29`) so the Totenhafen `low_pass` kind never runs on this file. (5) No player ladder exists in the game, so the perch is a `TowerLadder` interactable (a 3 s carried climb, net_local) plus the zombies' climb link; the dish was raised to 22 m so it turns over heads on the deck. (6) Dee and Hale are ExtraPeople with three small generic additions (talk_at for a body behind a counter or glass, `fixed` bodies checked at the talk spot, flag `reactions`, talk-only cards) and a `supply_drop` reward; DEE/HAL are OneCore placeholder voices (T-166), so the people test accepts rules.json story_speakers; five new lines were baked on the PC. (7) The fuel dump kills on contact like the live wire (per-second damage did not kill a round-21 Gänger in its first second). (8) Online: the storm, the collapses, the pings, the spread and `act_start` (not in the round snapshot: clients counted act rounds from 1) are host events mirrored with Net.mirror; ExtraPeople stays offline-only as before. (9) The jukebox tune, the act's base bed and every new sound are synthesized in gen_audio_desert.py (our own; no real song); audio.json was spliced by hand-script to keep its format. (10) The wildlife keeps a `rabbit` flag per critter rather than reading node names (Godot renames a second sibling `@Node3D@N`, which sent the second jackrabbit down the coyote path in smoke_map04c). (11) From the captures (morning/screenshots/u10_after): the ridge rings are fog_disabled (T-148), so they stood crisp through the 15 m haboob; the ridge shader now blends to the storm colour with the fog (`storm_k`); the searchlight towers pitch up and sweep the sky (pitched down, the tower at the hangar's corner swept its beam through the hangar) and set the beam shader's `beam_len` with the cone the right way round (unset, each shaft collapsed to a bright ring mid-beam); the dish is olive drab (grey disappeared into the dawn sky). The map04c minimap (image and POIs) was re-baked under WSL (tools/wsl_godot.sh), since master's image still showed the pre-U09 boxes and the new trap levers made the POI check fail. (12) Transitions: `Campaign.preload_next` feeds master's T-158 `LoadingScreen.preload_scene` (one threaded request; the web build skips it while its packs are fetched) and the card honours both `preloaded` and `Campaign.preloading`; its lore line is the transition's own (tips.json transition_lines) else `lore_key` else the map's.
T-161, 2026-11-01: Kaltenbrunn's living world (UPDATE 10 section 5). Own landscape, shared engine: KaltenLandscape extends Landscape (Map 0's sky dome, far shaders, tree meshes and impostor cards) with KaltenTerrain's own height function, installed through LandscapeTerrain.use_profile() (CENTER became a static var, with height/crest Callables; Landscape.build() resets it, so Map 0 is unchanged), and the old prism mountains and cone firs are gone from gen_map01 (the landscape replaces them; the tscn was regenerated and differs from the old one only in that and the Landscape node). MapAmbience stays one script: Map 0's inn-only pieces (crows, frost, breath, lamp sway, chimney, spindrift) are built only when the file lists their block, and the new kinds are appended under `# ---- U10 Kaltenbrunn ----` (dogfight, avalanche, cable_car, siren, snow_harder, dust_fall, chains, drips, salt_glint, plane_down.ridge) with a validate() the tests run over both ambience files; the file is loaded by MapRoot's generic loader (the tscn carries no MapAmbience). Calls: (1) the siren has no Operator line (nothing in lines.json is about a siren; OPR_SPECIAL_WARN_02 is the resonance alarm), vo_category stays empty. (2) There is no flashlight in the game, so the salt glint is lit by a headlamp cone from the viewer's camera (underground only); it is a next_pass on the halite materials, found by the resource name MaterialLibrary-less Mats now gives them (`mats_halite*`). (3) Harder snow is a second Snowfall layer added at round 20 whose amount_ratio ramps over 25 s, plus extra wind lean on the gravity of every layer, instead of changing the existing emitters. (4) Dust points are fixed ceiling positions in the zone boxes (E to I), the nearest to the listener first; chains hang in the lift bay. (5) Budget: see the census in morning/MORNING_REPORT.md. (6) Audio is synthesized (gen_ambience_map01.py, deterministic), not third-party, so no new licence files.
T-156, 2026-11-02: Fernflug Act II, der Kranich: the wing walk, turbulence you can use, decompression, a switch to find, a side quest, people aboard, story props, a trap, the living world (UPDATE 10 section 3; contract C6 Act II, C1, C8; branch u10-3-act2; MAP4_FERNFLUG.md 4.9, EASTER_EGGS.md "The Crew's Tags"). The act's round clock, hatch costs, the Bordwart's arena and the London window's timing are untouched; the set-pieces are new gameplay. **The wing walk** (`flight.wing_walk`, WingWalk, WingHatch, ClipPoint, WingChest): from act round 2 a strafing run tears BreachL2 off for good (BreachPanel.torn: its suction once, then a permanent hole, no seal prompt, out of the random breaches; its suction now ignores bodies 2 m above or below the hole). The way up is a hatch prompt that warns (a scripted climb on the user's own machine, net_local), not stairs: the shell GLB has no roof hole, and the closets' roofs (3.3 m) stand above the crawlway skin (2.85 m), so the walk is a grating catwalk at 3.4 m on stanchions with 1.05 m rail colliders, over the crawlway, aft round the nacelle along the trailing edge and out to a wingtip platform at x -117 (about 90 m), with outer-wing panels port and starboard and trailing aprons in the skin's primer, the nav lights. Its navmesh is its own zone box in C_FLUEGEL_L's layer (renamed Nav_WingRails; the crawlway's box now stops at 3.0 m) plus a climb link (ClimbLink meta) the dead use to follow; the region stays enabled because toggling NavigationRegion3D.enabled off and on left its polygons unreachable on 4.7, so the link is the gate. Clip states (OFF_WING / UNCLIPPED / CLIPPED, WingWalk.next_state) are host-owned and mirrored; a tilt throws the unclipped off 0.9 s in, and so does a fall below the deck: the body is hauled in at the hatch's foot and downed (co-op p.down(); solo a scripted 4 s down and get-up, because a solo down without Second Wind is game over and the brief says never a death; god mode skips the down); bots are never unclipped. Wind push and streaks, Bordschuetzen over the edge (at most 3 on the deck), close fighter passes (FlightSky.spawn_pass_near). The wingtip locker gives a perk-tier discount token (PerkManager.discount_tokens, tier_price_for: perks.json upgrades.discount_token_mult 0.5, spent with the next tier at a perk machine) and has an empty Undertow-class part slot (WingChest.wonder_part). **The tilt** (`flight.turbulence_tilt`, KranichTilt pure maths, TiltSlider): the bank becomes 2-3 tilts per act round on a seeded flight clock, warned 3 s ahead (chime + PA_M4B_TURB_01), 8 degrees for 6 s eased in and out, the camera rolling with it; the dead slide up to 5 m/s and take 60% of their max health at a wall (a ray and a stall check per 0.25 s); nine loose sliders (four bomb casings, crates, drums) slide with a static/kinetic friction model and stop at the first collider; a moving casing kills the zombie it runs into (host); players get 1.6 m/s. The FlightController now mirrors the bank (`_bank_mirror`), so clients roll and their cargo slides: the U09 gap "the bank does not swing on online clients" is closed. Sliders have no character collision (the navmesh is baked without them). **Decompression** (`flight.decompression`, DecompressionBreach, RailGrip, AmmoDrop): DecompBay (the aft ramp seam, act round 2) and DecompStarboard (act round 5; never the London round): 3 s warning, 4 s suction (9 m, 6 m/s), the dead out with a scream; a player holding a rail (RailGrip holders, set by the hold itself or a bot's hold()) is anchored, the rest slide to the edge and are downed, never out; two free ammo tins (each a wall-box refill of the gun in hand) fall into the bay. **The aux bus** (AuxBus, a PowerSwitch): a breaker board with a pulsing red beacon and a 45 s hint, turned to face the cabin (the generic switch at yaw -90 faced the wall); the sweep flips a breaker per room, perk stings, the flight deck's `dial_lit` faces from dark to lit, the Bordfunk crackle and PA_M4B_POWER_01. **The crew's tags** (map04b_crew_tags.json): collect crew_tag x3 (Haas's tag moved from the top of the Bordfunk deck, which the bots never reached, to the second bunk: the main egg's s7a now completes with bots, so smoke_map04b pins bot egg help off to test the round clock), the roster board (CrewRoster), the last reel (BordfunkReel, player-only): three PA lines, Wren's reply as a squad beat, INT_M4B_LASTCALL (a new intel placement "quest": MapRoot never spawns it). **People**: ExtraPerson rows may have a body-less talk point (`"body": false`): Ruhland at the Bordkasse (her body is BankBranch's teller) with the ledger favour (the flight deck, 1500 Scrip); Riedl on the engine-room intercom (`until_act_round` 4; a new RDL Kokoro voice in voice_cast) with the engine-log favour (intel_ping). Story props INT_M4B_BORDBUCH / CREWPHOTO / AIMERCARD / AUTOPILOT. A second crate spot (the gun blisters' east wall). **The bay doors** (traps.json bay_doors, BayDoorsTrap, TrapLever): 1000, needs the bus, 1.5 s klaxon, 8 s open, the dead on the grating drop out, players pushed aside unhurt, 60 s cooldown. **The living world** (map04b_ambience.json; the `# ---- U10 Act II ----` kinds at the end of map_ambience.gd; after the rebase on T-154 they run through its registry: the file says "needs_landscape": false and `_kinds_setup_u10_act2` starts the kinds present and puts one tick on _ticks; the hook lines this branch first added are gone): flight_sun (FlightSky.set_sunrise drives a new `sunrise` uniform: the glow aft, the stars fading; the Moon light warms), formation (four bombers at fixed offsets, one shot down per act), flak_field, st_elmo, kranich_frost (a new `frost` uniform on kranich_skin, by act round), rivets, engine_pitch (AudioDirector's bed), london_hush (the Ambience bus -16 dB while LondonWindow runs), cables. **Transitions**: ExfilPoint._begin_cutscene calls Campaign.preload_next, which fetches the web packs (MapPacks.ensure) and then feeds T-158's LoadingScreen.preload_scene (idempotent: T-158's own direct call at the same moment and this one never load twice; on the web only this path finds the scene). **Online**: every event is one host-owned trigger mirrored (bank, opening, clip states, throws, breach phases, screams, drops, the trap); smoke_webrtc gained `--map=` / `--through=` (Jakub's check only on Map 0). **VO**: 35 new lines (VO_SCRIPT 14.16): all 35 baked: 28 with Kokoro (the squad, Ruhland, Riedl), the 7 Bordfunk lines after the rebase with T-166's PA profile (OneCore); the 50 existing Act II lines this branch also baked were baked on master by T-166 too, so the rebase kept master's files. **Checks**: smoke_wing_walk, smoke_turbulence, smoke_decompression, smoke_map04b_world, smoke_map04b, smoke_webrtc (default and map04b) PASS; GUT test_act2_setpieces (15). smoke_map04b, smoke_map04_flow, smoke_map04_full PASS; GUT 1586 + the fixes (mapdata re-baked through WSL: the footprint now reaches x -130; objectives.json texts). Captures (WSL + Xvfb only): u09_map04b 19 PNGs and the new set u10_map04b 10 PNGs (morning/screenshots/u10_after, uncommitted like every capture), every one looked at; fixed from them: the rail colliders drew as solid walls (hidden, the dressing draws stanchions and a tether tube), closet roofs stood through the wing skin (skin fairings), a respawn or teleport off the deck counted as a fall (now only within 3 m of the catwalk), FlightSky.calm did not stop the new flight clock, the formation was invisible at night. Census (C4, the T-147 way): u09_map04b draw calls peak 1621 (T-147: 1482), primitives peak 384k (368k), u10_map04b peak 1871 / 317k (the wingtip looking back), 70 omni / 5 spot / 1 directional, at most 1 shadowed light per zone, scene triangles 796k (742k). **Scorecard (the worker's estimate)**: rows 1, 3, 6, 7, 9, 14, 20, 25 to 3 or near; 8 (the wing) and 10 (decompression, the wing) up; 19 by the lead's ruling (the formation that matters); 26 and 27 up. Not done: the bots' wing-walk task is the bots worker's (C7); a soak (rows 16, 28) was not run.

T-155, 2026-11-02: Fernflug Act I, Werk Thalbach, to the bar (UPDATE 10 section 3, contracts C1/C6/C7/C8; branch u10-3-act1; docs/MAP4_FERNFLUG.md 3.10). The calls. **The air raid** (`air_raid`, ThalbachRaid, `map04a.json "raid"`, act round 8, 10 s in): sirens, two new flak towers on the skyline (board-formed concrete, no markings) firing for 40 s, the searchlights frantic (ThalbachSky.set_alarm), an eight-bomb walk across A, B and D each lit 1.3 s ahead (30 damage in 3.2 m, zombies die) and three roof hits; a 10 s Operator warning; 1.5 s before the fall a shockwave throws every player out of the roof bay (x -38..-22, z 86..110) and anyone who walks back in is moved out at the fall (nobody is ever under it: smoke-proven), zombies in it are crushed; the bay `Hall2Roof` (Hall 2's middle sawtooth bay, now a kept group) falls. "Assembly Hall 2" is the north hall, as the in-world signs say (the 2026-10 design text calls the Kraftzentrale hall "hall 2"; the as-built signs win). The contract's names are this map's: **`Door_E_south`** is the route from Montage 2 into the test stands through Door_Pruefstand (-30, 110), buried under the **`RubbleBlock`** (x -36..-24, z 104..109.8, 2.2 m); **`Breach_E_apron`** is blown through the Montage's north wall at x -55 into the test stands' west aisle (the run-up to the hangar apron) and is a BreachDoor: a Pruefstand door in the `pruefstand` link group, sealed until the fall, then open if the stands were bought or 1500 like the other two, so the raid never hands out a zone; blocking a redundant E-G route (rather than the C-E door) keeps the power switch reachable whatever was bought. Navigation (the bots' names): before, `Navigation/Nav_E_MONTAGE_4` and `Navigation/Link_5_G_PRUEFSTAND`; after, `Navigation/Nav_E_MONTAGE_raid` (baked by the generator with the heap carved out as a projected obstruction: a parsed 2.2 m box is hollow to Recast and left floor inside it) and `Navigation/Link_Breach_E_apron`; regions are parked out of the tree, not just disabled, because a disabled region still owns its polygons for link attachment (the door links stayed glued to the old Montage and the new one was an island; ThalbachRaid.route_node_on() tells the bots worker which is live). The wall gap is baked shut by `BreachPlug` (a world-layer collider whose layer drops at the fall). Fires: six damage zones (the bay's two dead-end corners by the wall, the breach's flanks, two bomb holes; none on a route: a bot jammed at the jig corner when two stood on the squeeze past the heap) whose heat falls linearly to nothing over three act rounds (10 / 80 dps at full heat), smoke; a match started past act round 8 starts collapsed; all of it is in the "traps" group's danger_contains (the bay during the warning, live fires). Online: the host runs the clock and the damage, `start_raid` and `_net_collapse` are mirrored (the swap is one event), a late joiner collapses from the replicated `a1_hall2_down` flag. **The rivet line** (`rivet_line`, RivetLineTrap, traps.json: 1000, needs power, 25 s, 60 s cooldown): Montage 1's south aisle (x -46.5..-1.5, z 62.4..65.6), the lever on the Kraftzentrale's east wall, a carriage shuttling every 5.5 s with four hammers (3 x 3.2 m kill box, sparks, a synthesized hammer loop), bots kept out of the strip while it runs. **The wind tunnel** (`wind_tunnel`, WindTunnel, traps.json): the fan still blows +X (the egg smoke and T-114 expect it), so the "screen" is the outlet grille at the far end: zombies are blown at 6 m/s and shredded there (the kill to whoever started it; the wind parts round the model's sting, which jammed them dead centre), players drift at a true 3 m/s through move_and_collide (Player.external_push adds its vector every frame, so "6" had meant about 18 m/s) and are never hurt; while it runs the plenum hatch (`TunnelHatch`, `TunnelHatchPlug`, `Navigation/Link_TunnelHatch`, flag `tunnel_hatch_open`) opens a shortcut F <-> E (84 m round becomes 5 m), only when both rooms are bought; the fan spins once on the power. **The living world** is `data/gravelight/map04a_ambience.json` with every kind under "act1" (the Totenhafen core reads top-level `low_pass`, `raid`, `bell_tower`... generically, so namespacing keeps them from running twice after the merge), functions `_ev_a1_<kind>` and `_kinds_setup_act1()` in the `# ---- U10 Act I ----` block, one boot line in `_setup()` for a map without a Landscape, and its own ticker that drains the core's `_after` list (the core only does that after a Landscape setup): the blackout (two lit windows a round from act round 2, `Gameplay/LitWindows` markers from the generator: blinds drawn, spill lamps out), the bell, the test jet at act round 4, four forest searchlights, the ammunition train at act round 3 and every 4th (signal `a1_train_arrived`), the Kranich's violet breath out of the skylight (the landmark from spawn: the 6 m works wall hides everything under about 16 degrees there, so the plane itself cannot be seen from the courtyard), sheet rattle, three swaying wing sections in the Montage (none in the hangar: the Kranfuehrer's bridge would clip them), welding sparks, six flapping posters with only the two fictional marks (ART_FACTIONS 1b now defines the crane in a cog), and the power-on extras (PA crackle hall by hall, the gantries' jolt, a sting per perk machine as its zone lights). Visuals are ThalbachLife (own geometry, procedural textures, static groups merged per material for contract C4); ten sounds synthesized by gen_audio_map04a_u10.py (Owned). **Verticality:** the Luftbeobachtung post in the church square, a straight plank ramp along the east wall (open underneath for W_D_E1's zombies) to a 12 m platform, its own D box above 7 m joined by a seam link like the hangar's catwalk ramps (a stacked switchback stair could not be baked: a zone box's top acts as a ceiling for Recast); from its platform the Kranich is seen through a bomb-torn gap in the hangar's upper south face (x -11..-0.4, y 5.6..11.6), the sightline clearing the church tower by 2.4 m. **People** (ExtraPeople, offline as before): Erich Wendel in the air-raid cellar (his wife and girls only as silhouettes behind a blanket; coal from the rail yard; reward `open_passage`: `CellarSideDoor`, a side-hinged TunnelHatch with `CellarSidePlug` / `Link_CellarSide`, opens for good: a loop through the cellar) and Otto Brack the signalman (item "signal" with `item_verb` Pull; reward `train_drop`: the next two trains each drop a Resupply in the yard); their 16 lines baked with Kokoro on this PC (cast WDL, BRK), the raid's 4 Operator and 4 squad lines too. **Room story:** INT_M4A_WORKSPASS, _RIVETLOG, _WINDOWLETTER, _LOADSHEET (fixed StoryProps). **Jette's logbook:** `SightNote_Jette` adds a personal minimap note the first time each glider and her hatch are seen; her eight lines are on master and checked. **The boarding:** master's preload (T-157/T-158: Campaign.preload_next from the exfil cutscene, LoadingScreen.preload_scene) superseded mine in the rebase; Act I adds its card's transition line (tips.json transition_lines "map04a>map04b"). bot_ee_catalog and bot_tasks cast or tested a freed node before checking it (42 and 134 SCRIPT ERRORs in the 10-minute soaks); both now check validity first (one line each). Verified: smoke_air_raid, smoke_rivet_line, smoke_wind_tunnel, smoke_map04a_world (new), smoke_map04a, smoke_map04_flow, smoke_map04_full, smoke_map04_egg, smoke_map04b/c/d and smoke_people_extra PASS, again after the final rebase onto d63418e (Act III merged); smoke_squads passed twice and failed twice on its Map 0 scenario ("no round ended after the rise"), both failures while the PC was loaded (a WSL capture; ~45 other workers' Godot runs), a timing flake outside Act I; check_scripts 671 / 0 failed and the unit suite 1667 / 1667 after that rebase; the full unit suite ran 1584 tests (master 1573 + test_thalbach_act1 11): its 5 failures were tables that pinned the old data (map04a had no people, the reward kinds, the fixed story-prop list and per-map spawn counts) and the minimap POIs, all updated (test_mapdata 11/11, test_people_extra 11/11, test_story_props 6/6, test_thalbach_act1 11/11, 146 asserts); the fernflug soak (tools/ai_soak.sh --scenario=fernflug --runs=1 --max-minutes=10 --god) PASS on the final scene: act rounds 1-6, stuck 25 events / 25.1 s, 1 teleport, 1 stranded (mid-branch: rounds 1-8, 51 / 62.4 s, 2, 0; T-146 measured 85 / 101 s); contract C4 census (perf_marks web preset through tools/wsl_godot.sh, round 15, 24 zombies, 3 bots) 1086 draw calls and 294k primitives a frame on average, 6 shadowed lights, at most 1 per zone; the minimap re-baked through WSL (8 zones, 42 POIs; the platform falls in the 9.4-13.4 m band); captures u10_map04a (13) and u09_map04a (22) in morning/screenshots/u10_after, all looked at; fixes from looking: lights for the tunnel's outlet screen, the blown breach and under the Kranich's wing, the jet scaled and its exhaust lit, bigger posters, re-aimed cameras. Not done: the train shot never framed the train (the smoke proves it stands behind the shed), the Kranich reads from the lookout as its violet column and a dark shape at 65 m rather than a clear silhouette, the jet is a speck at 150 m, online travel still changes scene without the loading card, ExtraPeople stay offline-only.

T-153, 2026-11-02: Totenhafen's systems (UPDATE 10 section 2, contracts C2-C5, C7, C8; the design is T-152). Architecture: nothing re-lays the map: Totenhafen (gravelight/world/map02/totenhafen.gd) is attached at runtime by MapRoot and places every new node under Gameplay from data/gravelight/map02.json (blocks tide, ballast, searchlight, crane_drop, kielholer, undertow, letzte_fahrt) with fixed names, so the host's Net.mirror paths match on every peer and map02.tscn only gained the quest file. Tide v2: TideController keeps its v1 rules (33 high, 34 low) and adds stages (stage_for_round: spring every 9th round, high every 3rd, half the round before a high one), holds per stage that the Pumpenschalter crank (PumpCrank, a HoldoutStation: 30 s under x2 spawns at the head of the pump stair, once per flood) shortens by 40 s, spring sheets over the north and east aprons (the piers stay dry islands; wade_min_y keeps the half tide in the basins), drifting power-ups lost past their box's edge, four sparking cables (live while wet and powered: zombies stunned, players 12/s), per-stage storm values for the living world (TideController.storm_for) and the C2 GLBus signals; the host decides when the water falls (fall_now is mirrored) and owns the flag flooded. Ballast v2: the carrier's hands are limited to melee and the pistol slot (WeaponHolder.set_carry_limit), the hook keeps taking coffins (the flag ballast_loaded holds the count, 500 Scrip per load), BallastDump puts one over the side at the quay parapet's sea stairs (the landing stage below it was off the navmesh, so the dump stands on the parapet: bots could not reach it), ballast_dumped counts, the next round gets +1 Glasshide (RoundManager.extra_spawn), the third dump leaves the Undertow's shard, and gone coffins respawn on a free pallet after 20 s (pallet_spots beside the baked three). The searchlight is a station, not a weapon: the player is held at the stand with the gun holstered (WeaponHolder.set_holstered), the beam (Zombie.set_lit: x0.6 speed, x1.25 damage taken, refreshed every 0.2 s) follows their view; MAN_SEARCHLIGHT is a mark-order kind "man" (any mark but Defend on the light); during the sail it tracks the boat. The Crane Drop is a TrapLever trap (traps.json crane_drop: 1000, 60 s, 6 x 4 m). The Kielholer are tagged Ganger variants (kits drowned / drowned_diver on the sailor's cut, a brass helmet from primitives, no Blender) that rise at Surface_* pier-edge points only while flooded (the dock-2 point moved onto the apron: it stood off the navmesh), grab within 2 m and pull toward the water at 1.2 m/s (melee, 60 to arm_grab or teammates free you), and the diver's helmet glass breaks to a violet Marke glow x2.0. DER KIELHOLER is a HomageBoss (bosses/map02.json: hp_base 28000 by HOMAGE_BOSSES 3.1 because the egg already gates him behind the Undertow and three holds, between the Cousins and PAUL; round 20 standalone, 40 campaign; the Homage Bosses setting turns off only the standalone visit so the egg can never softlock): submerges every 25% lost and resurfaces at another dock, x1.3 at high and spring tide, the chain sweep, the charge, the grab, the helmet glass that breaks after 15% of max HP; failsafe 420 s: he dives and the ignition key floats up at the dock edge, and the key also collects itself when any living player (bots too) walks onto it. THE UNDERTOW is built on the Lodestone pattern (a RefCounted logic on the holder's data key harpoon; pierce 5, reel 1.2 s to 2 m stunned, a whirlpool in a wet box, MAELSTROM's land vortex and chains) with a primitive viewmodel and generated sounds. Letzte Fahrt (data/gravelight/quests/map02_letzte_fahrt.json) keeps every contract id and adds sub-steps where one id had several actions: s0a_load before s0_power (the power wants a loaded coffin), s2a_radios before s2_cipher, and s5a_injector / s5b_battery / s5c_sequence under s5_diesels; every step has a bot_task (s10_ending is the cutscene runner's, as Requiem's s10). QuestManager gained a seeded random type cipher (three radio groups, the keying order, the depth mark and the curtain code; it also writes <key>_inputs so the plain sequence step works) and the reward actions scrip_all and tier_gift (the lowest-tier held tonic +1); the cipher's bot solver waits think_s 20-40 s (BotEE plan_inputs). Intel rows for the log pages and the Hale telegram use a new placement quest (placed or filed by the egg, never spawned as random intel). The boat does not move: U-1047's hull, deck colliders and navmesh are baked (T-145) and moving them would re-lay the map, so the sail is relative motion: the host owns one float sail_t (mirrored at 4 Hz), the scenery cards scroll past, a lightning flash hides the pen hall's visibility groups behind a ViewCells cell over the deck so she stands in open water, boarders climb over the saddle tanks, and anyone overboard is hauled back to the helm hurt; Hinnerk Petersen takes the wheel if he is with the squad. The dive is a campaign.json cutscene (m2_letzte_fahrt_dive) played through ExfilCutscene without an exfil, then the Hale telegram card. Economy: rounds.json map_overrides.map02 (interval x0.75, cap +4) plus two quay spawns; the 10-minute soak paid P1 10,385 Scrip by round 8 before and 9,605 after against Kaltenbrunn's 7,010 by round 7 under the same settings (one run each: noise; the knobs change pace, not zombies per round), so Totenhafen already pays above Kaltenbrunn per round and no zombie-count multiplier was added (ECONOMY 9). Online: every new value is the host's (the flood, the crank, sail_t, the searchlight's aim, the boss, the egg); smoke_webrtc gained --map= and --through=flood,sail. Bots alone on Full Partner (smoke_letzte_fahrt, three bots, the human only follows; its forcing is in the header): the first final batch stalled 0/3 at the cipher, and four reach bugs were behind it, all fixed: the signal tower is behind door_signal and BotBrain.stand_point's fall-back found a stand point on the north catwalk under the tower floor (now rejected: _same_storey), BotEE._blocking_door pathed with the open zones' mask so a door into a closed zone was never found (now over every layer, as T-107's power planning), the top of the tower ramp outside that door is the tower's own navmesh so a bot buying it circled under it (BotBrain.door_approach: the last open-zone point on the way), and the bots' route to the catwalk ran up the zombies' Ladder_CatwalkN, which a bot cannot climb (climb links now live on each zone's climb layer, which the director opens with the zone, so zombies are unchanged and smoke_zombie_anim still climbs, and the bots' mask leaves out); log page 3's two seeded spots stood inside the crane office's solid block and moved outside it (probe_lf_reach now checks every seeded spot), and the cipher machine moved off window W_G_N1. Further: one diesel hold at a time (the bots started two together, split up and were wiped), the engine-room hold at x1.5 spawns without ring spawns (its ring points fell in the hull walls and the basin), shopping breaks for bots on an egg task (ee.json shop_break), and BotEE keeps the price of the door its task waits on. Result: the final batch (three runs on this branch's last code) completed the egg 1/3 (29.8 game minutes, 16/16 steps: power 3.4, cipher 10.9, the diesels 15.3, the key 22.3, the curtain 26.5, the dive 29.8); the two batches before it also completed 1/3 each (30.9 and 26.7 game minutes), every batch before the reach fixes 0/3. The two failures of the final batch were squad wipes in the diesel holds: the Full Partner bots still carry 7,000-21,000 Scrip unspent at round 13 even with the shopping breaks (their economy buys nothing in the break), which is the bots worker's to tune (T-163, U10 7.4). After the rebase onto master (T-154's living world, the bosses, Acts I-IV) one more batch went 0/3 for the same reason (all three squads wiped in the holds, one with 15,905 Scrip unspent), so the egg is completable by bots alone (3 of 15 runs since the reach fixes) but not reliably.

Results, all PASS, from .smoke_logs/u10_online: map02 `--through=flood,sail`: the client was in the match over WebRTC after 2.5 s, the host's spring flood reached the client (level -1.30), the boat sailed on the client from the host's sail_t, a bank deposit round-tripped in 0.22 s, the dropped player's slot became a bot and they rejoined as pid 2 after 0.2 s (rejoin 1.3 s), and the client saw "The host left the match."; map04b `--through=turbulence,wingwalk`: the tilt arrived on the client (banking, the camera rolled 8.0 deg), the wing walk opened and the bot's CLIPPED state arrived, bank round-trip 0.03 s, rejoin 0.8 s; smoke_lobby: create, browse, join by code, full, start, bad code, the 15 s connect timeout, expiry, quick match and the rejoin window. T-162, 2026-11-02: UPDATE 10 section 6, the polish list (branch u10-6-polish; docs/PERFORMANCE.md "UPDATE 10 section 6", docs/AUDIO_MIX.md, docs/PLACEHOLDERS_U10.md). (1) Frame cost: measured `perf_marks --probe` headless on `low` and `web` for map00 and map01 (the machine shared with 30+ Godot processes, so +-15%); the T-133 smooth motion is 0.01 ms a frame of script and the invisible viewmodels of the bots sit inside `player.gd`'s 0.4 ms for six players (0.07 ms a player), so skipping them was not done (it would risk the reload cues); the T-134 people's posing already ran at the zombies' animation LOD (T-139), and the faces now do too (`PatronBody.lod_div`, `FaceDriver` steps with the time it skipped; in the perf scene most faces are inside 15 m, so the measured change is inside the noise). Zombies above 60 Hz: `Zombie.smooth_motion` (GLSettings `zombie_smooth`, default on, Settings > Video > "Smooth zombies", never on headless) offsets the Pivot each rendered frame to the position interpolated between the tick's start and end (Engine.get_physics_interpolation_fraction), within the full-rate animation distance, snapping on a jump over 2 m; the body, collision, hitboxes and every timer stay on the tick, so the visual leads by under 7 cm; global physics interpolation stays off (nodes moved in `_process` would smear). Cost per zombie per rendered frame, `tools_src/bench_zombie_smooth.tscn`: 0.44 us before, 1.05 us after (+0.6 us; +0.02 ms a frame for 32); the probe's zombie `_process` column agrees (0.945 -> 1.014 ms map00 low, 1.016 -> 0.929 web, 1.009 -> 1.046 map01 low, 0.999 -> 0.871 web: noise). Neither change adds a draw call or a node. (3) Wall-walking NPCs: `NpcWallProbe.static_only` + `test_npc_navmesh` load all seven maps and check every authored spot (inn survivors' hide/post, companions, extra people and their items, the player start, the squad bots' start) is on the navmesh within 0.3 m (was 0.6) and clear of walls: 7/7, 5 to 15 spots a map, nothing to fix. `smoke_npc_walls` twice on a quiet machine: PASS both (0 pressed, 0 spot issues, 11 bodies, 499 s each); under 30+ other Godot processes the same smoke failed on timing (1 then 2 bodies pressed for 3-6 s at different places each time): its pressed-run test needs a machine that keeps its x4 time scale. (4) All seven minimaps re-baked through WSL + Xvfb (`tools/wsl_godot.sh` + bake_minimap.tscn, three tries each; the first map00 and map01 bakes lost the bank POI because the Linux binary's class cache was stale, re-baked after an editor scan), `tools/check_mapdata.sh` PASS; `smoke_marks` has a `--map=` flag (phases 7 Danger/Regroup/Defend and 8 revive-and-resume run on any map; the inn's perk, gun, door and pause-map phases need Map 0's props): map04b and map04d PASS. (5) Audio mix: `amb_loudness.py` now covers every map and act plus the VO (the baked lines' mean -17.3 LUFS must sit 6 dB over every bed; footsteps measure -33.0 LUFS momentary); it found 44 of 54 beds over the rule (beds 8 dB under a footstep: map00 -27.0 LUFS = +6 vs the steps, map01 +11, map04a +9, map04b +9, map04c +9, map04d +13); `--apply` writes one uniform offset per map (map00 -15.0 dB, map01 -20.0, map04a -18.1, map04b -18.1, map04c -17.7, map04d -22.3) into `audio.json music.ambient_trim_db` (AudioDirector._ambient_db reads it; the menu and other users of the shared keys are untouched) and the zones' `db`; zone balance kept; Totenhafen was already inside (T-154). Result PASS, 0 beds over; one-shots (creaks, radio) were not touched. The table is in docs/AUDIO_MIX.md. (6) Transitions: online travel and the online match start (`Net._begin_match`) now go through `LoadingScreen.go` (the card, a tip, the transition's lore line; it reuses the exfil cutscene's preload) instead of a bare scene change; the preload fires from `ExfilPoint._begin_cutscene` for every act (one path, idempotent: test_transitions); `smoke_transitions` walks map00 -> 01 -> 02 -> 04a -> 04b -> 04c -> 04d with the card each time (card on screen in 16-60 ms; hop total / longest frame, quiet machine: map01 1.9 s / 0.69, map02 1.7 / 0.51, 04a 1.5 / 0.28, 04b 1.6 / 0.34, 04c 1.6 / 0.33, 04d 1.5 / 0.34) and `time_map_load.gd` breaks a hop into load / instantiate / _ready / first frame. `smoke_transitions` fails on a black screen (neither the card nor the new map drawn) over 2000 ms or a card later than 500 ms, and only WARNs on a frozen frame under the card (reported as "longest frame"). **map00's cold start holds a 3.5 s frozen frame under the card** (no preload; the card stays visible, not black): MapRoot._ready ~1.5 s, Landscape 0.57 s, first frame ~1.7 s (navigation sync); splitting MapRoot._ready across frames is the open fix. (7) First-time hints (`data/gravelight/hints.json`, `FirstHints`, once per profile in `ProfileSave.profile.hints_seen`, shown as the HUD banner): tide, ballast, searchlight (group `searchlights`: T-153 landed while this ran), wing walk, dust storm, air raid, terminator, visor; each files an Archive card (`ARC_HINT_<KEY>`); test_first_hints. (8) Placeholders: docs/PLACEHOLDERS_U10.md; the VO manifest's `missing` list was stale (341 listed, 318 baked) and is now 6; `gen_vo.py` drops a baked line from it; 18 Bolt/Sprocket/Vendelin lines and (after T-153 landed) the Kielholer's 4 got OneCore profiles and were baked (6 lines remain, all stage directions); Pohl got his own person `ogpohl` (Blender kit, 19.4k tris) instead of the kubiak stand-in; "(placeholder audio)" is gone from the song banner. (9) Citations (`data/gravelight/citations.json`, `Citations`, `ProfileSave.profile.citations`): 17 achievements (Took the Wing Walk, Survived a Spring Tide [fires on T-153's `spring_tide` signal when its round ends], Sent to Orbit, Dust Walker, Raid Survivor, Beat PAUL, Beat the Hafenwaechter, Letzte Fahrt [listed once `quests/map02_letzte_fahrt.json` exists, which it now does], and one per easter egg), a HUD toast (`bonus_awarded` kind `citation`) and a Service Record section; test_citations (every quest in data/gravelight/quests maps to one, every boss, the guards, once-only, the map rules). Tests: test_npc_navmesh 7/7, test_audio_mix 3/3, test_first_hints 9, test_citations 10, test_zombie_smooth 6, test_transitions 6; smokes: smoke_marks --map=map04b and --map=map04d PASS (21 s each), smoke_npc_walls x2 PASS, smoke_transitions PASS with a WARN on map00's frozen frame. Not done: the map00 hop under 2 s; recorded VO and a lighter `ogpohl` kit (docs/PLACEHOLDERS_U10.md); the viewmodel-skip (nothing to win).

T-163, 2026-11-02: UPDATE 10 section 7, the bots on the new content, the Kaltenbrunn egg and Q-19 (contract C7; docs/BOT_AI.md sections 14-15, docs/QA_REPORT_U10.md). Bot behaviour only: no round, enemy health, spawn count or human price moved. **Kaltenbrunn ("Requiem")**: soaked step by step with a new `ai_soak --egg-from=<step>` (the skipped steps' world effects applied) and whole, god and honest. Rounds that never ended: the bot-mode agent now hunts the round's last zombies (`BotTrainer.hunt_pick`: a crawler at once, a walker after 5 s of nothing near; closer in when its health doesn't drop), and a straggler nobody gets nearer to is left alone for 60 s (`BotSquad.mark_unreachable`) so the game's own no-progress recycle can take it (a Foreman wedged on the rail-yard fence held round 12 open for 30 game minutes while the hunters reset its timer). The agent's spending: an errand window (with bot teammates up, the round's last two zombies are the squad's hunt and the agent does the far errand a 10-15 s break can't hold; at most 45 s a round), purchase timeouts that count the walk, the plan's affordable perk/gun/door before egg work in a break (the U08-style agent sat on 12,000-22,000 Scrip with an MP40 at round 12, the Lab door 83 m off), training on the Turbines/Pews loops once the Core Vault is open. Full Partner: the main egg before any side quest (scores.main_quest 100, and a side quest is dropped when a main step opens); a task may list risky stages Full Partner does unasked (`full_partner`: the Chapel bell with a Crucible gun, the crate pulls for Herr Tamtam, a missed chase only resets) and carry its own pause_pressure; "nothing to do" counts from the last finished errand; while the bell is lit every free bot joins the Chapel hold and nobody shoots the Foreman until it is inside F_CHAPEL (any armed zone + flag kill condition); shoot_target counts per bell and per vantage and tries another angle, and `vantage_for` tests the guns' own blockers (the specimen-tank bell between decor-layer pipes); the drummer's crate plan stays on the bot's own roll; a Crucible gun, not a taught wonder, for the bell (a bot's first teaching is a gun); no bot feeds the Crucible while the shard slot or the Heartstorm start is ready; a scout task walks the open zones when the egg waits only on something not yet seen (the four little bells); every player takes a built wonder from its bench (the Totenglocke, the Undertow). Bosses: PAUL's counter is live (the IV bottles from rage 34, the neck from behind), `der_kielholer` has one, and a bot held by any boss grab knifes itself free (`BotAim.break_free`); an unseen zombie at arm's length (pushed into a wall) gets the knife. Measured: each step from s4 to s9 completed by bots in isolation (s9: the egg complete 13.4 min after the Heartstorm opened), a god run through s5 by round 15, and honest squads that wiped at rounds 11-13 before now live to rounds 21-30; the honest 3-of-5 target is **not met** (0/5 in each of three batches: the furthest honest runs did s0-s5 and stopped at s6, the four little bells not all seen; others wanted a Crucible gun for s4 or started the pylons late). **Fernflug**: a per-bot set-piece layer, BotSetPieces (setpieces.json; positions in routes_map04c/d.json "setpieces"), which either changes how the other layers behave (no sprint, hold fire, a tighter formation, blackboard danger zones) or takes the body through the brain's action `setpiece`: Act II a rail held through a breach's pull, sliding casings avoided, the wing walk as a risky optional task (one bot, quiet spells only, always clipped, the wingtip token); Act III no sprinting, a tight formation and no shot at a far stalker in the dust storm, warned collapse boxes and the burning hull as danger zones, the radar perch for Wren; Act IV the day side preferred, jump pads as shortcuts, shelter from the meteor warning on, the vent lever as a trap and Pohl's favour as an optional task; the aux bus and the bay doors needed no new code (an existing power switch and trap). Q-19 (bots alone, Act II into Act III): they wiped in Act II's first round (0/3); the fixes were a home loop on every act, doors bought in a break, far errands that must fit the break, retreat spots that are not dead ends, the Kranich as a stand-together map every round, traps allowed when no human is in the match (a change to T-058), Bordwart counters and the galley loop moved off the port window. One run in three exfiltrated into Act III on a mid-tuning commit; on the final code every run survives act round 1 but they wipe in act rounds 3-5 (0/3; the London and Bordwart rounds and the bomb run are the next lever). **Totenhafen**: the LETZTE FAHRT bot tasks, the MAN_SEARCHLIGHT order, the cipher's think time and the shop break are T-153's own; rebased onto it, this section adds the Kielholer's counter and grab break-free, the Undertow as a wonder taken from the bench (T-153's tier 6.5), and the searchlight-order case in smoke_bots_u10 (a bot mans the tower light on a map mark); the pause state carries both T-153's `shop` and this section's `pause_pressure`. Tests: `test_bot_u10_eggs.gd` and `test_bot_u10.gd`, smoke `smoke_bots_u10`.

T-170, 2026-11-02: the Totenhafen send-backs of the re-score (UPDATE 10 section 9; docs/THE_BAR.md 3.3 sent rows 20, 21, 5, 15 and 22 back because map02 scored below both Crossroads Inn and Kaltenbrunn; branch u10-9-sendback-m3). Everything is data on the existing systems, no new class, and nothing touches timings, hitboxes, the navmesh or prompts. Row 20 (extra people, was 0): two ExtraPerson rows in data/gravelight/people_extra.json in Kaltenbrunn's style (T-143). Karl Jessen, the Marinekasse's paymaster's mate, is the bank's own teller (bank.json tellers.map02 was already him, with his JES cast and bank lines), so his row is Ruhland's pattern (T-156): `body: false`, talked to at the pay window's east end on the crane office's north face ([47.1, 0, 59], outside the counter's nav_block), with a fetch favour (the harbour master's ledger page, a new `ledger` FavourItem prop, lying inside the Hafenmeister's hut at [13, 0, 12.3]; reward scrip_mark 1000, between the miners' 500 and Ruhland's 1500). Liese Harms, a gutting-line worker of the cannery's night shift (the pay slip INT_M2_PAYSLIP is her line's), hides by the clerk's desk and the delivery book ([-1.9, 0, 10.4], pose cower, the brandt kit with a kerchief and apron): a civilian, a person, never a joke, no insignia anywhere; her favour is the infirmary's storm lantern by the nurse's end cot ([-27, 0, 7.6]) and her reward is intel_ping (she listens to the hall), the map's first. A third person was not added: Hinnerk is already map02's companion (row 5) and the design names nobody else; two rows with Talk cards and favours is what the re-score asked for. Row 15 (the bank, was below both): Jessen also carries `reactions` (Hale's pattern, T-157) on the egg's flags lf_ballast_done and boat_key, and ExtraPerson now leads a favour card with the newest reaction's text once the ask has been seen (a Talk-only card already did), so the teller reacts to the ballast dump and to the ignition key at the window. Row 5 (a person to save): companions.json petersen gained a `scene` block and MapCompanion a generic `play_scene`: while he waits on the signal tower stair, the first flood (GLBus.tide_flood_begin, connected by name with the signal's arity unbound) or the first survivor within 18 m plays his category LF_TRAPPED (PET_LF_TRAPPED_01, baked in T-153, played nowhere before) once, positional at him, mirrored online, and the choice card carries the scene's text above his ask; the generic COMP_CALL timer is reset so the two never stack. Row 21 (Jakub): JAK_M2_ARRIVE_01/02 were not unused: JakubCompanion._join plays "M%s_ARRIVE" on every arrival (campaign and the profile's Jakub on a standalone map02), so the re-score's grep missed the format string; what was missing were beats of his own, so he gained JAK_M2_FLOOD_01 (the first flood, GLBus.tide_flood_begin, once) and JAK_M2_SAIL_01 (GLBus.sail_started) through the same _later_say path as M1_POWER / M1_FOREMAN. He gets no spot of his own: no map has one (he appears beside his escort's spawn on every arrival, _beside_spawn), so Totenhafen matches the others. Row 22 (an opening you remember): map02_ambience.json `arrival` and MapAmbience._kinds_setup_arrival: the first step onto the quay (the local survivor 2 m from where they stood, up to beat round 3 so a campaign arrival gets it) sounds the harbour siren from the cliff posts (far_sound, the raid's amb_m2_siren) and 1.4 s later strikes the storm kind's LightningFlash nodes over the pen mouth; once a match, a debug trigger for tools, no VO (no Hafenmeister line is written). Voices: 17 lines baked with Kokoro (JES on his T-141 cast; HRM a new cast entry, low and tired; JAK his own), audio_manifest.json updated by the bake (missing list unchanged at 6). The worktree held LFS pointers (the models, the .ogg files), so the first import wrote `valid=false` stubs for every .ogg; checking the LFS objects out, restoring the tracked .import files and deleting the stubs before the re-import fixed the suite's resource errors (nothing of that is committed). Evidence: test_people_extra 12/12 (the totenhafen spec, the counts), test_jakub 15/15 (his beats baked and wired), test_map_companions 5/5 (the scene), test_map02_ambience 10/10 (the arrival block), test_npc_navmesh 7/7 (the four new spots on map02's navmesh within 0.3 m), test_bank 10/10; smoke_map02_world RESULT PASS with the new section (two people, Jessen's card, favour and both reactions, Harms' card, favour and ping, Hinnerk's scene on the first flood played once, Jakub's arrival, flood and sail lines, the arrival siren and flash once). After the rebase onto master (40cb5676, T-163's bots): 744 scripts, check_scripts 0 failed; the full suite 1830/1831 with five Godot processes sharing the machine (the one miss, test_transitions' preload of every map, died on engine "mem is null" resource errors and is 6/6 run alone, so 1831/1831 in effect); smoke_map02 PASS, smoke_map02_world PASS (78 checks), smoke_vo PASS on its second run (the first missed "a DOOR line" under the same load, the miss .smoke_logs/m_brain shows on master too); smoke_letzte_fahrt one run: 7 of 16 steps (s0a_load 0.5 game min, s3_log 2.5, s0_power 2.7, s1_ballast 6.3, s4_parts 7.3, s2a_radios 12.9, s2_cipher 18.9), then the squad went down in the diesel holds at round 14 and the 55-minute cap ended it: T-153's known failure mode (3 of 15 runs complete), the bots worker's item, nothing of this section's.

T-167, 2026-11-02: the blind review's media (UPDATE 10 section 9, THE_BAR sections 5 and 6; branch u10-9-captures). The worktree was handed over at aa1807c5 (before the T-153 merge), so the branch was moved onto master 99611905 first; nothing in gameplay, data or scenes changed except the camera list and the tools. Capture: the camera `do` verbs moved out of capture.gd into tools_src/capture_do.gd (CaptureDo, shared with the clips) and grew `tide:<stage>[:hold][:wait]` (TideController.force_stage), `flag:`, `skipto:<quest>:<step>`, `use:<node>` (Interactable.interact as the player, the smokes' _use), `start:<node>` (HoldoutStation.start), `give:<weapon>`, `callv:<node>:<method>:<a,b>:<wait>` (arguments: `call:` had none), `round:` and `wait:`; a camera may `look_at` a node by name (the seeded log page, the stations placed from map02.json), a zombie camera may `make` its subject a Kielholer through KielholerController.make (the drowned kits, the diver's helmet), and `shot_on:<GLBus signal>` holds the shot for a signal (the cable spark fired 0.4 s in). The set `u10_map02` has 14 cameras in quest order (spring_aprons, pump_crank, cable_spark, ballast_dump, searchlight_lit, crane_drop, kielholer, cipher_machine, crane_office_page, diesel_injector, undertow_hand, curtain_lever, helm_ready, mid_sail); every one rendered, the spark, the crank's ring, the pallet mid-fall, the diver, the Undertow and the sail at sea (36 s in, past hall_hide_at) all read. What does not read: the spring sheet is a dark translucent plane and under llvmpipe the flooded apron is simply black beside the dry pier (three re-aims, kept the last); the dump has no visual of its own (the station and the sea stairs are the shot); the cipher's first eye stood outside the tower and fell 7 m (moved inside); mid-sail at (7, 1, 68) was inside the hull (the deck is y 1 only at the helm). Clips: gravelight/tools_src/clip_tour.gd walks a set's cameras with the round running (2 bots, god mode, power on, round 5), queues each camera's `do` list as the eye sets off toward it (the long waits, the tide's 26 s, play under the walk instead of stalling it), lerps feet, shortest-way yaw and pitch with a 1.6 s hold, thins a set to at most secs/6 cameras or walks back over it when there are too few, and `--sets=a,b` concatenates sets on one map (u09_ref's three Map 1 cameras joined u10_map01; u05 walks the Inn). The AVI from `--write-movie` DOES carry audio under the Dummy driver (pcm_s16le 48 kHz stereo, mean -16 dB on the test clip): the movie writer mixes the Dummy driver itself, so the master bus is left unmuted, and the mp4s have the beds, VO and the round in the mix. smoke_tour.tscn is the headless check (10 s, u09_ref's Inn cameras; run_smokes.sh picks up smoke_*.tscn by pattern). Rendering speed: 1280x720 at 24 fps took 1.3 s a frame with two renders sharing llvmpipe and a 2-minute clip 14-17 min alone (the first estimate of an hour was measured against two other renders; a 540p pass was made and then replaced), so every clip is 1280x720 as asked; crf 28 keeps each mp4 at 18-37 MB (tools/wsl_clip.sh re-encodes at a bitrate cap over CLIP_MAX_MB 48, so no clip trips GitHub's file limit; morning/clips and morning/screenshots are git-ignored since the morning kit, so the PNGs and mp4s are force-added as plain files, no LFS). Map 0 with the power on renders black rooms in the Compatibility renderer (the web profile gives its electric omnis dual-paraboloid shadows, which GLES3 refuses: 34,832 such errors in that run, 154 on Kaltenbrunn), so map00.mp4 is the lamplight walk with `--no-power`; the other six have the power on and no black camera: Act IV's yard, dome and Earth all drew. Environment: this worktree had never pulled its LFS files, and the first import pass ran on pointer files, which left `valid=false` import stubs that the editor never retries; `git lfs checkout` plus deleting the 2,506 stubs and re-importing (5,019 reimports) fixed 26 unit tests that failed on unloadable VO. smoke_launcher passes (exit 0, 15.9 s) when run from PowerShell; run from the Git Bash tool the console wrapper never exits after the smoke's own quit (three runs, all killed by the 1200 s timeout after RESULT PASS), which run_smokes.sh counts as a failure: noted for the lead, not fixed here. Results: check_scripts 731/0 failed, GUT 1742/1742, smoke_tour PASS, assets manifest unchanged (107 folders).

T-168, 2026-11-02: the re-score at FINISH (UPDATE 10 section 9, THE_BAR section 5; docs/THE_BAR.md 1.1b, 1.2b, 2.1, 3.3; branch u10-9-rescore). Method, as section 5 prescribes: the same 0/1/2/3 scale, unmeasured is 1, a feature in data that no run, test, smoke or capture shows is 1, shared systems out, evidence under 120 characters per cell naming a file, a test, a smoke RESULT line, a capture or a soak number; the data inventory re-counted at master db4c9ef6 (story_props and intel rows per map, quests and steps, bosses, traps, people_extra, companions, squads, both ambience files per map, DecorHit and swing nodes in each tscn, power blocks, climb links, the mapdata.json bake dates, the VO manifest's engines and its missing list, the launcher's U09_ACTS/U10_BUTTONS, smokes and GUT files per map); the lead's smoke logs in .smoke_logs/u10_* and T-164's online logs; the u10_before/u10_after capture sets (the u09 cameras for Totenhafen and each act plus the workers' u10_map01/04a/04b/04c/04d sets, looked at); the soak and egg numbers of T-153, T-155, T-158 and docs/QA_REPORT_U10.md (read from u10-7-bots: Kaltenbrunn egg 0/5 honest, Q-19 0/3 on the final code); and the scorer's own headless smoke batch on the PC (.smoke_logs/rescore: the class cache imported first, then tide2, ballast2, kielholer, undertow, sail, slender, paul, air_raid, rivet_line, wind_tunnel, wing_walk, turbulence, decompression, dust_storm, radar_ping, wreck_collapse, terminator, low_g, meteors, earthrise, the six *_world smokes, bank, launcher, people_extra, story_props: 28 PASS; smoke_map04c_world FAIL on master, "the zombies' climb link reaches the deck (navmesh up there)", the T-157 perch, so no cell cites it as a pass; smoke_tide2 PASS in 106 s alone and timed out at 900 s twice while three copies of the batch overlapped; smoke_story_props prints no RESULT line and is not cited). Map 1 and Map 2 were re-scored too, as section 5 says. Totals before -> after: Inn 71 -> 71, Kaltenbrunn 68 -> 76 (the living world T-161, PAUL T-159, the zone beds), Totenhafen 45 -> 67, Act I 43 -> 59, Act II 37 -> 59, Act III 43 -> 60, Act IV 39 -> 59; on the seed rows Totenhafen 29 -> 48 (above both references' baseline 44), the acts 28-30 -> 40-41. Rows 24, 26, 27 and 28 are provisional until u10-6-polish (T-162) and u10-7-bots (T-163) merge; from their paragraphs the expected finals are Kaltenbrunn 77, Totenhafen 69 and Act IV 61 (the minimaps and the Kielholer's lines). Pass/fail against section 1.3's target, the higher of the two re-scored reference totals (76 now, 77 expected): total FAIL on all five columns (gaps 9 / 17 / 17 / 16 / 17; against the baseline figure of 71 Totenhafen misses by 4, the acts by 11-12); the "three only-here features a player can act on" PASS on all five (10 / 7 / 10 / 10 / 10 counted, section 2.1); "no seed row at 0" PASS on all five. The decision taken: the FINISH merge stays blocked by the rule as written, and the shortfall is the same list on every act rather than one map's gap: rows 16 and 28 (no soak past act round 5 on the Kranich, past r21 at Groom Lake or past r30 on the Moon, so the loop and the pay are unmeasured: Q-19), row 13 (four act bosses that exist in data and tests and that no soak or capture has ever shown running), rows 20 and 21 (Totenhafen has nobody in people_extra.json, and Jakub's JAK_M2 and JAK_M4A lines are baked but played by nothing), row 27 (every line baked, the new speakers OneCore placeholders). Rows sent back by section 6's rule (a column below both re-scored references), with the owning section, in THE_BAR 3.3: 4 (Act I, section 3), 5 (M3, II, IV), 6 (I, II, III: Act III still has 2 story props against the order's 4+), 13 (I-IV: section 7 / 3), 15 (M3, I, III, IV), 16 (II, III, IV: 7.4), 17 (I-IV), 18 (I, II, III), 19 (IV), 20 (M3 at 0, I-IV: 2.2 / 3), 21 (M3, I, II-IV), 24 (IV, provisional), 25 (II), 26 (I-IV, provisional), 27 (M3, I-IV, provisional), 28 (II-IV, provisional); the three marked "first" in 3.3 are 20 (Totenhafen's people), 6 (Act III's props) and 16 (Q-19). Calls made while scoring: Totenhafen's row 4 is a 3 because the boat you take out is now the thing the map is remembered by (smoke_sail), even though the hull still reads as a box in the u09 cameras; the acts' power-ons are 3 where the sweep has perk stings, machinery that reacts and a line (Acts I, II, IV) and 2 where the switch itself did not change (Act III); Act II's row 21 is a 2 on the London and rack lines the baseline missed; Act IV's row 19 is a 1 on the lead's "equivalent" ruling because the field units never engage; the Ballast choice is Totenhafen's row 7 at 2 (a real loop with a reward, no quest file of its own); nothing was guessed up from a DECISIONS claim alone where a smoke or test exists to cite instead. Finalised after master 40cb5676 (T-162 and T-163 merged, 1827/1827, 744 scripts; `git merge master` into the scorer's worktree, DECISIONS kept both sides): row 24 is 3 everywhere (all seven mapdata.json baked 2026-11-02 14:29-15:01), row 27 is 3 on Totenhafen (KLH baked), rows 26 and 28 unchanged (T-163's tests, smokes and set-piece layer are shared; no Scrip figures), Pohl's `ogpohl` body noted in row 5; final totals Inn 71, Kaltenbrunn 77, Totenhafen 69, Act I 59, Act II 59, Act III 60, Act IV 61; the target is 77 and every target column still FAILs on total (gaps 8 / 18 / 18 / 17 / 16) while passing the only-here and no-seed-zero conditions; the send-back table is left as written until the u10-9-sendback-m3 / -m4 branches land (rows 24 and 27 for Act IV / Totenhafen no longer fall under both references).
