(6) **The weather officer speaks as PA** (the plant's intercom), so no new speaker code was needed beyond the contract's six; GRAVELIGHT ONE's squad lines are one per character at the window and one for the violet light, said by whoever is in the match (bots fill), so every character has exactly two. **The lines**: 106 new ids (SER 9, VOL 16, BRG 8, SVR 29 = 5 FTD + 12 M0 + 12 M1, RTN 16, REI 6, PA 6, SEL 8, DUT/PEG/DEA/WRN 2 each), 90 with a spoken form in `speech` (Russian in Cyrillic, two Church Slavonic: the inscription and the real troparion of Lazarus Saturday, German for Reinholt and the PA), each with its English subtitle; the Russian in three registers (a 1930 village priest: подклет, сынок; a young OGPU plenipotentiary: гражданин, предписание, по акту; 1944 front radio: Берёза, Дон, приём, коробочки); the Returned's sixteen are a name, a mother, a unit, "cold", a prayer half-remembered, a letter, "don't shoot, it's me", never a growl. ElevenLabs v4 tags ([whispers], [exhausted], [shouting], [breathing heavily]) sit at the front of the subtitle, as every other line's do; the bake applies them to the spoken form. **The extractor** (gravelight/tools_src/extract_vo.py) now reads `<speech>X</speech> Y` in any row, bullet or inline form and a `Speech` table column into {"text": Y, "speech": X}; its run moved into main() so tests import it; a doc row without speech keeps a speech another tool wrote (the RADIO_UBOAT lines). Its JSON indent went 1 -> 2 to match the committed files (a re-run used to rewrite all 13 000 lines of lines.json as whitespace; now the diff is the 106 added ids and nothing else, checked key by key: 0 removed, 0 changed). rules.json: SER, VOL, BRG, SVR, RTN, NAR are story speakers; SER/VOL/BRG/NAR default to tier 4 (cutscene lines are never cut), SVR 2, RTN 1 (T-192 tunes the horror rules); subtitles.gd names them FATHER SERAFIM, VOLKOV, THE BRIGADE, SOVIET RADIO, THE RETURNED. **The bake**: the 106 lines are listed in audio_manifest.json `missing` with the reason "awaits the ElevenLabs bake (T-193 / T-201)" rather than baked through OneCore (no Russian or German OneCore voice on the PC; English voices reading Cyrillic would be noise and ElevenLabs replaces every take anyway), so test_vo's every-line-has-audio-or-a-reason stays green and gen_vo.py / the ElevenLabs batch pop the entries as they bake. Tests: tests/voice/test_extract_vo.py (pytest, 5: split_speech, the tag in a table row, the Speech column, the bullet and inline forms, lines.json has no Cyrillic subtitle and the T-191 ids with and without speech), test_vo_speech (GUT, 5: no subtitle shows Cyrillic, every speech line has an English subtitle and the tag was extracted, strip_tags leaves "It's wet.", the new speakers' rules, the subtitle face has Cyrillic). Measured: test_vo* 41/41, test_u11_egg_guard 1/1, test_people_extra 12/12, test_trapped_pilot 5/5, check_scripts 790/0, pytest 5/5. Not done here (T-194): the cutscene definitions, the stages, the people, the hooks, the per-shot `card` event the mid-timeline cards need, and the Archive rows (T-200).

T-190, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 section 1-3, the Lazarus canon in writing (branch u11-1-canon; docs/LAZARUS.md is the bible, docs/CANON.md section 0 the summary, docs/STORY_CHANGES.md the old -> new list). **The calls.** Dates: Orthodox Pascha 1930 fell on 20 April (7 April O.S., the same day as the Western Easter; checked by the Julian computus, golden number 12), so Lazarus Saturday is **12 April 1930 Gregorian = 30 March Old Style**; Father Serafim writes Old Style, every Soviet and German paper New Style. The relics: found at Kition 890, translated under Leo VI "898 (some say 890)", because the sources split. The sickness counts the way John 11 counts (dead one day, up on the fourth), and nothing rises on Pascha itself: the brigade's three die 15 April and rise on Great Friday the 18th in the Staryi Brod ward; the village's first dead (the feldsher Grach and the widow Avdotya, who nursed the sick) die the 18th, Serafim buries them on Holy Saturday and they come up on Bright Monday the 21st. Pilch 1938: the Fine Print Ch. 2 already dated the rising to the night of 12-13 Nov, so the LORE/TIMELINE rows move from "that night [the 11th]" to 01:12 on the 13th: the fourth day from the glass room, two days early from the death, the first sign that near the crystal the sickness does not wait. Laboratory No. 4 exists from October 1934 (the OGPU became the NKVD's GUGB on 10 July 1934); 1930-34 the vessel is "Object Lazar" of the OGPU's operative-chemical section; the real NKVD poison laboratory ("Laboratory No. 1" / the Kamera) is named in the sources only so nobody mistakes ours for it. Volkov b. 1904 (26 in 1930, 40 in 1944, 49 in 1953: "an old man's hand" that looks older). East Prussia: the Gumbinnen-Goldap operation 16-30 Oct 1944 is real; the release is 26 Oct at a fictional "Point 112" and no real locality is a scene. Nachtglas's job under the new canon: it does not raise the dead, it *answers* the sickness (minutes instead of four days; ground together, STAUB carries on the air; a grain under the skin, the Marke, lets the risen be steered), so every "retention substrate" line in Darkmatter's own documents stays true in the company's mouth and only the lines saying Darkmatter *made* the dead changed. The relay: by May 1945 every walking body carries the dust, the dust listens for the carrier, CANCEL cuts it, the dead lie down; whether the sickness is dead or asleep is left to the Moscow 1953 card (three cards, no voice, the lamp flickers). **How the diary reaches the Moon:** Volkov kept it as Exhibit 3 of Object Lazar; the fold that took lot LZR-04 for the 1938 probe took the box it lay in (his 1938 vault inventory records both missing from a sealed box); the Loot Allocation Unit has dispensed the pages since as "returned inventory", one per Darkmatter site in order, the last racked in the Moon relay's fulfillment store under a tag "Sol-3. Customer-generated. Do not reshelve." The Soviet files on the German maps came with the bodies (the dead officers' satchels in the sealed wagons); the front report on Map 0 is in a dispatch case a Soviet risen walked in with. Church Slavonic: both lines (the crypt door and "what death left behind") are composed for the game and marked so. The Archive is a separate file (data/gravelight/archive_lazarus.json, 17 entries, contract C7) with place_hints for T-200; the seven read-alouds are VO_SCRIPT section 27 (`<speech>` Russian before the English subtitle; the extractor does not split it yet, T-191) and are listed in audio_manifest.json "missing" (no Kokoro voice; ElevenLabs, T-201). **Found on the way:** gravelight/tools_src/extract_intel.py regenerates intel.json from LORE.md section 13 alone and would drop the 30 Map 4 rows other tools added (93 -> 63); I ran it for dossiers.json (Peg's amended directive) and restored intel.json from git; the lead should know before anyone re-runs it. Data files checked and left alone: story_props, intel, machine_lore, perk_lore, citations (every "retention" hit is Darkmatter's product language on its own documents, still true). Not touched: quests (the guard), any voice line (T-191, T-195..T-199), ENEMIES.md (T-192), BRIEF.md (history).

T-193, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 §7, ElevenLabs as the default bake for every voice (docs/VOICES.md). The engine is tools/voice/eleven.py (`tools/voice.sh elevenlabs ...`, plus `tools/voice.sh plan` / `bake`), on the official Python SDK (elevenlabs 2.77, python-dotenv; installed in tools/venv and the system Python gen_vo.py runs under, declared in tools/cloud_setup.sh and voice.sh). The T-101 file tools/voice/elevenlabs.py was renamed, because a module called elevenlabs on the tool path shadows the SDK package. Model: eleven_v4. On 2026-11-07 it was the newest TTS model listed for the account ("most emotive", 94 languages, audio tags), it takes stability and similarity_boost only, and it serves English, German, Russian and Polish alike; the live check showed one credit per character (x-character-count = characters sent). Output: pcm_44100 is Pro-only and the account is Creator (131,000 characters a month, reset on the 7th), so the bake steps down pcm_44100 -> mp3_44100_192 -> mp3_44100_128, remembers what worked, decodes with ffmpeg and finishes the take with the recorded-take chain (trim, delivery loudness, fx.json effect, the line's [radio]/[distant]/[static] cues through gen_vo.py's DSP, OGG 48 kHz mono at the path the game already plays; no game code changed). fx.json gained works_pa / megaphone and entries for the PA and radio speakers the OneCore bake gave a channel, so their AI takes keep it. Tags: delivery words become v4 tags; effect cues are stripped and applied after synthesis; short delivery words pass through; blocking, sound cues and long stage directions are not sent (passing them through would have the model read "[orchestra swell]"); a shout / hurt / quiet line with no tag gets one. `speech` is what is said and `text` stays the subtitle; language comes from the line's own `lang`, else the `speech` language, else the speaker's cast language, with a whole line in another language detected. Source naming: AI takes are `source: ai_eleven`, `"ai": true` (ai_stock stays for the T-101 one-off gap line), with the TTS row kept under `tts`. gen_vo.py already keeps a non-tts row while the subtitle is unchanged, and pipeline.py now lets a recorded take replace an AI take while keeping the original TTS row. Cache: raw API audio by sha256(spoken text, voice id, model, settings, language) in <main checkout>/.voice_cache/eleven (git-ignored, shared by worktrees), so effect or loudness changes cost nothing. Quota: a pre-check against the account's remaining characters; --limit-chars; a stop on 401/402/quota and on 429/5xx after 4 retries, with bake_state.json for the resume; logs/elevenlabs.log gets one line per 20-line batch (characters billed, account count before and after). gen_vo.py runs the ElevenLabs pass first (a subprocess in the voice venv) and Kokoro/OneCore after it for whatever is left; without a key it prints the setup line and bakes as before. Since no voice_id is cast yet, merging this spends nothing until T-201 casts. The key is loaded from the environment, else .env.local (this checkout, then the main one), only into os.environ; errors are reduced to status, code and message and scrubbed. The Fine Print exists only as a script, so its 2,867 voiced lines are read from docs/fineprint/script_ch*.md (voicelib.collect_fp; bullets and the bark tables; speaker-less tables take the pool's or the heading's speaker). They are not added to collect_all, so the recording scripts and the importer are unchanged, and they go where B&S plays recorded lines (res://audio/vo/<id>.ogg, the B&S manifest), because the script says its speaker ids are VOManager ids. Casting schema: cast.json characters.<game>.<code>.eleven (voice_id, voice_name, source library|design, design_prompt, language, stability, similarity_boost, why, none, consent_actor, candidates), eleven_by_prefix for the relief squads, characters.fp with same_as for the shared voices, new entries for the 33 lines.json speakers that had none (including PAU and SLN, design-only, and the U11 codes SER VOL SVR RTN BRG NAR in Russian), first-draft Voice Design prompts for the main cast, and Voice Library candidates (free searches) for twelve leads; no voice_id assigned (T-201). Every line in the three games maps to an entry or to a reason (test). The two failing voice tests on master were fixed here: the cast now covers UBR and the other new speakers, and say_id reads DEE as the word "Dee". Live check: 2 TTS calls, 121 characters (Dutch TAG_KB4471_01_p3 and the German U-boat radio RADIO_UBOAT_03, premade voices, scratch folder only, after one refused pcm call). Plan on 2026-11-07, after rebasing on T-191: 420,602 characters (cutscenes 11,123; The Fine Print 197,650 is the largest group), counted at one credit per character, which is conservative because the account counter moved only 53 for the check's 121 billed characters. That needs one month of Pro or about four Creator periods; written to NEEDS_DEVIN under "## UPDATE 11: ElevenLabs". Tests: 46 in tests/voice, among them the key scan (tracked files, logs and cache, for the key's value and sk_ + 40 hex), the cache, quota stop and resume, rate limits, the format fallback, human takes win, speech sent, language codes, coverage, the consent gate, and the review page showing AI.

T-195, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 section 4, the Operator and the system voices for the Lazarus canon (branch u11-4-vo-operator; VO_SCRIPT.md sections 3, 14, 25, CHARACTERS.md the Operator profile and the Banter exchanges, lines.json for the ids that live only there). **The slice (contract C4):** OPR (353 ids, plus the 32 `op.slip.*` which are OPR too), PA (28, the Kranich's Bordfunk and the STAUB plant intercom), TEL (19, the Kaltenbrunn savings-bank clerk), BNT (33 squad banter exchanges in CHARACTERS.md), WIT (12, Wittkamp on the works' PA), BWT (7, the Bordwart), and UBR (6, `RADIO_UBOAT_*`, the harbour commander's radio) as the other announcer voice; every line read, 26 re-toned, 4 added, the rest kept because they were already right for the canon. Left to the owners named in C4: `BNT_*_JAK_*` and the Wren scene (Jakub's rows, T-198), `TAG_KB4471_01` (Wren and Dutch's scene, T-196/T-197), the other maps' bank clerks JES/RUH/PRU (map NPCs, T-198), the `op.slip.*` texts (the Ch. 5 script owns them, VO_SCRIPT section 10; none says Darkmatter made the dead, so T-199 has nothing to change there either), and the `gl.*.opr.*` Helvetia rows, which no data file carries yet. **The calls.** (1) **The Operator stays cheerful and gets two new favourite words**, said with a salesman's warmth: "the returned" (its own "returned inventory" phrase, which is also the Red Army's word for its risen; the Operator never notices the coincidence, which is the joke the player is allowed to get) and "the fourth day" (a calendar it finds charming: `OPR_ROUND_START_15` "it is always the fourth day somewhere", `OPR_GAME_OVER_01` "see you on the fourth day", `OPR_DOWNED_01` "down is a temporary condition"). It never says "Lazarus" (LAZARUS.md section 10) and never claims to have made the dead: `OPR_ROUND_START_16` "we didn't make tonight's guests, friends, we only found them", `OPR_SELFAWARE_T3_03` "Doctor Fasbender *thinks* he built me to listen ... nobody taught me to sell, I came with that" (the old line had Fasbender build it, which CANON section 3 contradicted even before U11). The Soviet risen reach it as guests "all the way from the east" (`OPR_MATCH_START_01`, `OPR_ROUND_START_14`: greatcoats, no boots, through the snow, said kindly). (2) **Map by map** the world darkens and the Operator does not: Totenhafen's coffins are "spoken for, mind the seals" (`OPR_BALLAST_LIFT_01`, no longer "it's... somebody", since the lead boxes hold Lazarusblut and STAUB, not bodies), the Kranich's bomb run is "a little of the product, for a very large market" (`OPR_M4B_BOMBRUN_01`), Groom Lake's customer "bought his samples retail" (`OPR_M4C_POWER_01`: Peg's orders, the Totenhafen crate), and the Marke's poster line is sold back as a feature (`OPR_MARKE_OPR_02`: "protection against the sickness, the poster said, and so it is, in a way"). (3) **Four new Operator ids** in the always-rolled categories (`OPR_ROUND_START_14..16`, `OPR_ROUND_END_08`) rather than touching the tier-gated SELFAWARE ladder; they are listed in audio_manifest.json `missing` as awaiting the ElevenLabs bake, like T-191's. (4) **Two Operator splice words were re-worded**, `{dead}` -> `{returned}` in `OPR_ROUND_START_12` and `OPR_BELL_WRONG_04`: a brace on an Operator line is a splice marker (section 3), stripped from the subtitle, not a substitution token; every other `{...}` and every `%token%` survives, checked key by key against master (the check script's output is in the T-195 report). (5) **The system voices were read and kept:** the Bordfunk (PA), the Bordwart (BWT) and the harbour radio (UBR) were already the cold, flat, bureaucratic register the work order asks for ("Kurs zwei-fünf-null. Endanflug. Auslieferung."; "the harbour is closed"), and their lines carry headings, rack numbers and the crew-tag egg's step-3 recordings, so re-wording them would have been change for its own sake. Wittkamp gets one line (`WIT_M4B_TAPE_02`: "the casings were never empty", STAUB in the Kranich's racks). The clerk (TEL) gets two: Herr Direktor "is outside now, with the others", and "the other kind come back too, and I don't serve them". (6) **Banter (BNT)** follows the squad tone, with every exchange keeping its part count and speaker order (the bake files are per part): Peg's pack now holds two empty sample jars with London's seal (`BNT_DUT_PEG_03`, her amended orders), Dutch "started praying again tonight, right about when the first one got up off the floor" (`BNT_DUT_DEA_02`), Wren's book tonight holds "Germans, and villagers, and Russians in greatcoats; the ink does not care; I am starting to" (`BNT_DUT_WRN_03`), and Peg's orders say "bring out the samples, and whoever can explain them" (`BNT_PEG_WRN_04`). `BNT_DUT_PEG_01` is kept word for word: tests/voice's committed Whisper transcripts match its first part. (7) **Egg lines** (`OPR_QUEST_S*`, `OPR_M0_FIRST/CREDIT`, `OPR_LF_*`, `OPR_M4A_FILE_*`, `OPR_M4B_TAGS_03`, `PA_M4B_LASTCALL_*`, `WIT_M4A_*`) were read against their quests and left as they were: each already carries its cue and its information in the right register. No quest file, no rules.json key, no PROGRESS/morning file touched.

T-196, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 §4 the VO rewrite, slice C4: Dutch (`DUT`) and Peg (`PEG`), every row of both in VO_SCRIPT.md and CHARACTERS.md (branch u11-4-vo-dutchpeg). **Read 614 lines (DUT 309, PEG 305), rewrote 276 (DUT 134, PEG 142) plus the DUT/PEG parts of 16 banter exchanges (BNT_DUT_PEG_01..05, BNT_DUT_DEA_02/04/05, BNT_DUT_WRN_02/05, BNT_PEG_DEA_01/03/05, BNT_PEG_WRN_01/04, BNT_DUT_JAK_01; the other speakers' parts untouched and still readable) and 7 Cousins rows (§12.5, header `Line id`, not extracted by design); kept 338**: every `MARK_SHORT_<n>` (test_mark_orders checks the 600 text), the one-word acks and pings, the GL1 lines T-191 already wrote in this tone, the TERMS_READ columns (Vendelin's words; the readers' tails were already sober), and every line that was already a frightened person's plain speech (TAG_KB4471_01 as it stands). **The calls.** (1) *What they can know when*: generic lines (buy, perk, down, revive, power-up, door) play on every map, so none names the Soviet dead, the fourth day or STAUB; the canon enters through the map-bound lines: Map 0 the greatcoats and the bite (DUT_ROUND_END_02 "Anybody bit? Say so now", BNT_DUT_PEG_02 "a scratch, not a bite", PEG_ROUND_END_02 "check your hands for bites"), the inn's dead getting up (DUT/PEG_INN_RISE, INN_SURV_TURN), Kaltenbrunn "this is where they made it" (PEG_SPAWN_01), Totenhafen Lenz "opened a coffin and wouldn't sail; he knew what the dust was for" (PEG_BOSS_KIELHOLER_01), Fernflug "that's the dust, they're putting the dust on London" (PEG_M4B_LONDON_01) and the crate (PEG_M4C_CRATE_01/02 now say what Hale's order says: samples of the sickness and the crystal). Nobody says "Lazarus" (LAZARUS.md §10: the word belongs to the people who mean it) and nobody theorises. (2) *Peg's secret colours the maps in order*: Map 0 a pause and "London wants a piece of whatever is in those men out there, too" (PEG_SQ7_M0_EGG_LOCK2_01); the perks "London wants a sample of everything down here. Everything" (SQ6); the shard "Give it to me. I have a tin" (PEG_EE_02); BNT_PEG_WRN_04 "They say to bring the samples out. [a pause] And the asset"; PEG_MARKE_FIRST_01 quotes the token clause in full; Totenhafen's telegram names the samples; Act III says it all. The LANTERN hints stay LANTERN hints (tiny, not confessions). (3) *Dutch*: complaint as his prayer, and the actual prayers under it (Hail Marys, the rosary, "Jezus Maria", Immaculate Heart on Polish Hill, Wigilia's extra plate: "Nobody open the door"); Normandy stays the thing he won't talk about and is now four oblique half-lines (the cliff, the Garand, "a beach in June"); period US Army speech checked (forty-five, chopper, trench gun, stovepipe, "outfit", "swell" as the sarcasm it was, the ten-dollars-a-month combat infantry pay of 1944; no "Kraut", no "Ivan": the dead on every side are people and he says "somebody's kid"); the Pittsburgh "yinz" exactly once per map, in map-bound lines only (Map 0 DUT_SQ7_M0_EGG_DONE_02, Map 1 DUT_PAUL_01, Totenhafen DUT_LF_CUT_01, none on Fernflug), never in a generic line where the rule could not be kept. (4) *Peg* never raises her voice: every "!" and capital shout in her rows is gone (PEG_M4B_BREACH_01 "Seal it now", PEG_EE_03 "Hold it, please", her sprinter / window / ping callouts levelled to full stops; only the squad-wide "Reloading!" and "Board's up!" keep theirs); the understatement stays and gets colder as the maps go on; the fear shows as precision and as prayer she denies ("I hadn't prayed since France. I find I'm doing it now, under my breath. Don't tell anyone"). Her father the sapper, Islington and her mother, France 'forty-three and the two teams are the grief she lets slip in banter, a line at a time. (5) *Humour thins by map*: Map 0 keeps the gallows jokes (the crate, the box, "Swell"), Kaltenbrunn fewer, Totenhafen almost none, Fernflug keeps only the ones the design leans on (PEG_M4B_ARRIVE_01 "The plan has been revised", PEG_M4B_CRASH_01, DUT_M4D_ARRIVE_01, DUT_M4C_MOON_01) and quiets the rest ([quiet] on DUT_M4D_MANAGER_01). (6) *Egg lines* (the `vo_squad` categories of map00_radio and the Fernflug quests, §18's SQ4 help, the Cousins' letter) keep their cue and every piece of information (three notes, numbered, lowest first, the set behind the bar, nine kilometres east by rail, "bring all of them" twice, the other voice, the hook on the dud racks, V, the three names); re-toned only. (7) *Audio tags*: [a pause] / [a breath] / [quiet] / [exhausted] / [breathing heavily] / [shouting] / [pained], at the front or between clauses, never more than two a line; gen_vo.py's cue tags kept. **Mechanics**: `extract_vo.py` does not parse the §24.3 SQ7 bullets (`` - `ID` (Dutch): "..." ``: neither the bullet nor the inline pattern allows the speaker note), so the 16 SQ7 lines and the seven json-only ids (BONUS_LEDGER, PERK_LEDGER, BALLAST, BOSS_KIELHOLER, LF_CUT, LF_TELEGRAM) were written into lines.json directly and the extractor re-run over them (contract C3); the lead should know the SQ7 rows reach lines.json only by hand. `gl.all.peg.perk_blastproof.01` maps to `PEG_PERK_BLASTPROOF_01`, which §2's bullet already defines, so the rare dossier-5 variant never reaches lines.json (pre-existing; the extractor says so on every run); its doc text is re-toned anyway. Checked key by key against master: 2555 ids before and after, 0 lost, 0 added, 292 changed = 134 DUT + 142 PEG + 16 exchanges whose only changed parts are DUT/PEG, 0 others; 0 placeholders lost; speaker, category, condition and exchange shape unchanged on every id; no U+FFFD. Tests: test_vo* 42/42, test_u11_egg_guard 1/1, test_endings 6/6, test_mark_orders 29/29, test_ballast_v2 8/8, test_letzte_fahrt 19/19 (headless). **On the rebase** master (T-195) had rewritten three DUT/PEG banter parts outside its slice (BNT_DUT_PEG_03, BNT_DUT_DEA_02, BNT_PEG_WRN_04): this branch keeps its own PEG lines (BNT_DUT_PEG_03 "Charges. Detonators. [a pause] Tea.": the sample jars stay a secret from Dutch; BNT_PEG_WRN_04 "the samples out. [a pause] And the asset") and takes master's Dutch line for BNT_DUT_DEA_02 ("Started again tonight. Right about when the first one got up off the floor."), the better line. Not touched: quests, PROGRESS.md, morning/*, any other speaker's row, the Dutch/Peg prose T-190 wrote (one voice-note bullet added under each profile for the ElevenLabs cast, T-201).

T-197, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 section 4, Deacon (`DEA`) and Wren (`WRN`) for the Lazarus canon (branch u11-4-vo-deawren; contract C4; CHARACTERS.md sections 3, 4, 5, 8 and their Banter parts, VO_SCRIPT.md sections 12.5, 13, 14, 15, 16.4, 17, 19, 20, 21, 22, 23, 24.3, 14.6-14.16, 25, 26, lines.json for the ids that live only there). **The slice:** 312 DEA and 316 WRN ids plus the 28 exchanges with a DEA or WRN part (the BNT pairs, `TAG_KB4471_01`, `BNT_DEA_JAK_01`, the Wren scene); every one read against its cue, 154 DEA and 121 WRN ids re-written and 6 banter parts, the other 353 kept word for word because they are terse callouts that carry gameplay information and no quip (the SQ5 mark acknowledgements and prices, most SQ4 egg acknowledgements and SQ3 pings, the `TERMS_READ` columns read verbatim, the Fernflug lines that were already written in dread, T-191's fresh `*_GL1_*` lines, and the lines that were already right: "Ruthie, honey... not yet.", "Tomek... I'm sorry. I came too late.", the KB-4471 scene, the Wren-Jakub scene, "Diesel. Thank you, Lord, for diesel."). Checked key by key against master after the rebase: 2559 ids before and after, none lost or added, 281 changed, every one DEA/WRN or a DEA/WRN banter part, no speaker/category/condition/exchange shape changed, every `{emphasis}` word and `%token%` kept, no Cyrillic or double quote in a subtitle (the check script's output is in the T-197 report). **The calls.** (1) **Deacon's faith is a line at a time and KJV-exact, never a sermon:** Psalm 23:4 for the plane going down behind the hills (`DEA_SKY_PLANE_DOWN_02`), Luke 2:11 under his breath at midnight on Christmas Eve, Isaiah 40:31 on the Marathon malt ("he never saw this place"), Revelation 13:16 said in full and slow on the first Marke kill (`DEA_MARKE_FIRST_01`: "my daddy read that one slow, every time"), Isaiah 21:11 "Watchman, what of the night?" for the Slender's death, 1 Corinthians 15:26 as the whole of his CANCEL line (`DEA_M4D_CANCEL_01`), Job and Passover where they were. He says "Lazarus" nowhere now: the old "Lazarus owes you one" revive quip is gone (`DEA_REVIVED_01` is "didn't want to find out what gets up when I don't", the Hard Truth nod), and the fourth day reaches him as a blues verse (`DEA_BLUES_03`) and as a flinch at the fourth tonic chamber. **He names the sickness once, as the work order asks** (`DEA_SQD_RISE_01`, the relief squad's dead getting up: "don't anybody call it a judgment. It ain't. It's a debt."), and does not explain. His machines keep their names; the machine lines turn from charm to grief ("who taught you to eat people? Who taught them?"). (2) **Wren knows the NKVD and the Soviet dead are complicated for her:** she reads the Marke on the German dead against the unmarked Russian dead on the road (`WRN_MARKE_FIRST_01`: "the Russian dead do not have it. Only theirs."), PAUL's dosing file is "a kind of file I have seen in two languages" (`WRN_PAUL_01`), the banter with Peg puts the Russians in Warsaw before London can fix anything (`BNT_PEG_WRN_03`, December 1944, the Red Army across the Vistula), and the relief squad's risen "do not know the difference" any more (`WRN_SQD_RISE_01`). Her brother's story is unchanged to the word. Polish is checked with diacritics (Głodny, Dziękuję, Sześćdziesiąt, Siostra, Do diabła, Wigilia, Kraków, Wilcza Góra); the Wigilia line adds the empty plate for the absent (`WRN_MIDNIGHT_01`, "two plates, this year"). Her humour stays only where it is deadpan grief ("I could run to Kraków. There is nothing to run to in Kraków."). (3) **Nothing is put in their mouths they could not know:** on Map 0 they know the dead walk, that Russians are among them and that the German dead carry glass in the hand; Kaltenbrunn's STAUB, Totenhafen's coffins and the Moon's relay are spoken of only on their own maps and only as what is in front of them (`WRN_M4D_MANAGER_01` "she sold the sickness its glass"); Chetverodnevo and the vessel are never named by either (the Archive pages may be unread). (4) **Egg lines keep their cue and every piece of information:** the Map 0 radio egg's `SQ7` lines keep the mantel/trunks/loft, "numbered, so there is more than one", "behind the bar, start low and climb", "wrong note, back to the first", the east rail line and the gatehouse and headframe; `WRN_EE_02` keeps four bells in order; the SQ5 prices, the Fernflug tags (Riedl, Haas, Pohl), racks and duds, `DEA_LF_LOG_01` "Brecht wrote down how" and `DEA_LF_CUT_01` are unchanged in substance. No quest file touched (the U11 egg guard passes). (5) **Audio tags** are the v4 delivery words gen_vo.py already maps or passes (`[quietly]`, `[whispers]`, `[shouting]`, `[exhausted]`, `[a breath]`, `[breathing heavily]`), one or two a line and never on the short callouts. (6) `BNT_DUT_WRN_03`: T-195 had re-written Wren's part of this exchange on master while this branch was open; the rebase conflict is resolved with a blend that keeps T-195's "Germans, and villagers, and Russians in greatcoats" and Wren's terseness. (7) **Left alone on purpose:** `data/gravelight/bosses/map01.json` and `map02.json` carry copies of the PAUL and Slender squad lines as a subtitle fallback that `homage_encounter.gd` reads only when lines.json lacks the id; lines.json wins, so the copies are stale text and not gameplay, and they are not this slice's file (the lead may regenerate or leave them). No rules.json key, no PROGRESS/morning file, no other speaker's row touched.

T-189 (lead, merge note): the voice pipeline's end-to-end test plays a committed synthetic recording of seven script lines (tests/voice/test_end_to_end.py) and matches takes against the live script text, so the U11 rewrite of DUT_AMMO_LOW_01 and BNT_DUT_PEG_01 broke it. The test sandbox now pins those seven ids to the text they were recorded from (tests/voice/fixtures/recorded_lines.json, lines.json before the rewrite); the live lines keep T-196's text.

T-198, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 section 4, the map NPCs, Fasbender, Reinholt, Jakub, the squad-wide lines and the bosses for the Lazarus canon (branch u11-4-vo-npcs; VO_SCRIPT.md sections 4, 5, 14.8, 14.11, 16, 22, 25, 26, CHARACTERS.md the Fasbender and Reinholt sample lines, lines.json for the ids that live only there). **The slice (contract C4):** JAK (184), SQD (98), FAS (56), REI (29, less the six `REI_STB_*` cutscene lines T-191 wrote), LSL (10), SEL (23, less the eight `SEL_GL1_*`), the Map 0 survivors WIE/BRA/KRO (27), the inn's round-1 patrons ROT/KUB/POH/SOB (13), the bank clerks JES/RUH/PRU (40), the companions TRU/AMM/PET/JET (51), the extra people MIN/MED/RAD/DES/WID/COU/RDL/WDL/BRK/OGP/HRM (90), Fernflug's DEE/HAL/WIV/RRK (51), the bosses PAU (22), SLN (20), KLH (4), Jakub's parts of the banter (`BNT_*_JAK_*`, the Wren scene), and BLT/SPR/VEN on the Gravelight maps read for "Darkmatter made the dead": about 830 lines read, **27 re-toned, 1 added**, the rest kept because they were already right for the canon or carry gameplay information in the right register. **The calls.** (1) **Fasbender is haunted by the trains and says what the Russians did first** where the quest already has him speaking, so no new id and no quest wiring: `FAS_M0_R12_01` (round 12 of the Map 0 radio egg; its cue "you will still come" and "I have everything they want" kept) gains the sealed wagons of November, "Russian soldiers, their own dead; what was in them did not come out of this mountain, it came *to* us"; `FAS_M0_FREQ2_01` (the 5.785 line; the stone, black glass, hums, kept) now says the stone does not raise the dead, something was already in their blood, the Russians had it first and lost it and it walked west on its own feet, near the glass it stops waiting four days, and "Reinholt calls it Lazarusblut; she says it like a prayer, I say it like a confession"; `FAS_M1_SWITCH_02` keeps "everything draws from it / if it breaks it all breaks" and takes the dead out of the list ("it did not make the dead; nothing here made them; but it makes them quick, and it makes them listen"); `FAS_MARKE_RELAY_01/_02` carry the Marke's new reason (a grain of glass that listens; the body gets up when told and walks where pointed; "the Russians could not make their dead obey, Abteilung III did it with a needle"; the poster's second line, protection against the Lazarus sickness, "is not protection, it is a leash"); `FAS_SAMPLE_04` is now the STAUB mill ("did I make the dust? I calculated a particle size, twelve microns, so that it would float; that is not the same thing as making it"). The Map 0 round schedule, the three frequencies, the Map 1 code fragments (`%d1%`..`%d6%`, red / blue / white), the hints, the exfil line and the Fernflug radar-shack scene are untouched. (2) **Reinholt's grief hangs on Lazarus in her own mouth**, never the Operator's, and the sickness is never the miracle (LAZARUS.md section 10): `REI_CYL2_01` (Feb 1939) gains "there is a man in the Gospel who was four days in his grave, and his sisters had only to ask. I have asked."; `REI_CYL3_01` (1943) gains the four-day count ("to the hour, as they all do; near the heart, less") and "it is in the blood, Emil, not in your glass; the glass only answers it, and keeps what answers; I have named it in the files for a man who was four days dead and came home: Lazarusblut", keeping subject seven-forty, the window and "I only need it louder"; `REI_SAMPLE_02` says the same in casting form. She speaks of the man who came home and never of the vessel, which she never knew. `REI_S8_01` says "every miner, every soldier, every villager" instead of "every laborer": the labour gangs are never zombies (work order section 8), so the heart cannot hold them as the things the squad quiets. `REI_HEART_90` says "seven years old", not nine: Liesl is six and a half on cylinder 1 (Dec 1938) and died in Feb 1939 (CHARACTERS: a seven-year-old girl); the "five years" stays, matching `REI_S5_BOX_01`. (3) **Jakub is unchanged** except where the canon reaches the places he knows: `JAK_ZONE_B_RAILYARD_01` adds the sealed wagons of November to the rail yard he was brought in through ("the men who did it never came down to us again"), and one new chatter line, `JAK_CHATTER_05`, is his word on the Soviet risen ("they walked from their own graves to ours; nobody should have to walk that far"): grief, never a joke about the dead, picked by the CHATTER shuffle-bag so it needs no wiring. His Polish, his refusals, the chalk, the Marke lines, the Wren scene and his three banter parts are kept word for word. (4) **The relief squads** (SQD) carry the front: the Soviet scouts followed "the dead west from the river; our own, some of them; do not ask", go back east "through them", and the survivor knows the report will say epidemic ("they always write epidemic": the cover-up, LAZARUS.md section 5.2); a bitten Home Army man and a scratched Red Army man now hear the fourth day in a scratch; the Volkssturm see "Russians, and our own, together"; the Kriegsmarine ratings "saw what Brecht found in the lead boxes" (Lenz's refusal, U10 design section 1); the paratrooper's "Holy smokes" becomes "Lord God" (the humour thins on Map 4). Nobody in a relief squad names the Marke. (5) **The map NPCs:** Wiesner saw Russians on the road "in greatcoats, no boots, walking with our own" before "the dead stayed down at Verdun"; the medic's three who stopped breathing "got up again before I had the sheet over them" (minutes near the Heart); the deserter's column was given the mark the week before, "protection, the poster said"; the harbour pilot would not sail "with the lead boxes aboard; the Kapitänleutnant would not; I would not"; Jessen enters the three coffins "as ore", as the manifest says (the count "three units" kept); Hale's jars hold "the sickness and the stone, with your name on the manifest; the Russians had the one for fourteen years and buried it; the Germans had the other and ground it to dust" (his sample order, LAZARUS.md section 6.3), with "before the Russians {do}" kept; the Account Manager's first line says what Darkmatter did and did not do: "we found you, you know; years ago; something on your little world that would not stay down, and a market that would not stay closed" (found and sells, never made). (6) **Kept as they were:** Liesl (nothing in the canon touches her), Selwyn's cockpit lines, PAUL, the Slender and the Kielholer (mechanics callouts exact; Lenz's four German lines already are the refusal), the Cousins (stage cues and the rhyme; no spoken canon), Dee, the clerks RUH/PRU, the companions TRU/AMM/JET, the extra people MIN/RAD/WID/COU/RDL/WDL/BRK/OGP/HRM (the Thalbach yardmaster's "lead boxes from the coast, they hummed in the cold" already is the cargo), the inn's patrons (Sobotta's "it's only a scratch" now carries the fourth day on its own), and every egg line of map00_radio, map01_requiem, map02_letzte_fahrt and map04_kuendigung, read against its quest and left with its cue and its information. BLT/SPR/VEN on the Gravelight maps say nothing about who made the dead (`VEN_M4D_PILCH_01`'s "the eleventh of November" is Pilch's recorded death, LAZARUS.md section 4), so there was nothing to change there. (7) **Audio tags:** `[quieter]`, `[breath]`, `[pause]`, at most two a line, on the radio and cylinder lines only; the `[radio]` / `[static]` / `[hiss]` cues gen_vo.py relies on are unchanged. **The check** (a key-by-key diff of lines.json against master after the rebase on T-196/T-197): 2559 -> 2560 ids, 0 lost, 1 added (`JAK_CHATTER_05`, listed in audio_manifest.json `missing` as awaiting the ElevenLabs bake, T-193 / T-201), 27 changed, every one a T-198 speaker (FAS 6, REI 5, SQD 8, JAK 1, WIE 1, HAL 1, WIV 1, MED 1, DES 1, PET 1, JES 1), every `{...}` and `%token%` kept, every `speech` kept, no speaker, category, condition or exchange shape changed. **Found on the way:** on the master this branch first rebased onto (a0d9db66 + T-196/T-197), tests/voice/test_end_to_end.py failed at `BNT_DUT_PEG_01_p0` (text score 0.69 < 0.75: the committed Whisper transcript speaks the exchange's old first part, "how come the British always talk like they're ordering tea", and T-196 re-wrote it; T-195 had kept it for that reason); the lead's 5a0e0c8d pins the fixture's seven recorded lines, and after the second rebase the suite passes (8/8). Measured (headless, in the worktree, on the rebased branch): test_vo 42/42, test_u11_egg_guard 1/1, test_jakub 15/15, test_map02 18/18, test_letzte_fahrt 19/19, test_homage 25/25, test_paul 17/17, test_kuendigung 26/26, test_map04 41/41, test_quest_m4 12/12, test_people_extra 12/12, test_trapped_pilot 5/5, test_objective_log 19/19, test_quest_graph 6/6, test_relief 16/16, test_bank 10/10; pytest tests/voice/test_extract_vo.py + test_end_to_end.py 8/8. No quest file, no rules.json key, no PROGRESS/morning file, no other speaker's row touched.

T-199, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 section 4, The Fine Print and Bolt & Sprocket where they said Darkmatter created the dead (branch u11-4-vo-fineprint; work order §1.4 and the last bullet of §4; contract C4). **Read**: every voiced and text line in docs/fineprint/script_ch1..5.md (the 2,867 lines voicelib.collect_fp collects, plus the stage directions, synopses, tables and design notes), the generated docs/FINEPRINT_SCRIPT.md, every B&S line source collect_bs reads (data/bs/story/*.json, ui_lines, bosses, weapons, gadgets, forge, enemies, the level line files), the hidden Darkmatter memos 36-40, cs.postCredits, docs/BS_*.md, the bs/ scripts, and the Gravelight-side BLT/SPR/VEN rows (the VO_SCRIPT.md tables and lines.json). Searched for made/created/raised/caused/engineered/seeded/built the dead, "the product does", "our zombies", payload, substrate, rise/rose/get up, and the Lazarus words. **Changed (2 lines; same ids, same cues, close to the same length):** (1) `fp.c1.sol3.10`, Sprocket reading the Sol-3 file: "Bolt, that's what the product does. It keeps you doing the last thing you were doing. Even after. They didn't just stop people canceling. They stopped them stopping." -> "Bolt. 'Natural retention phenomenon. An existing retained base, awaiting a Provider.' Something down there was already refusing to end. They didn't stop people canceling. They found a world where nobody could, and put a meter on it." It was the one line in The Fine Print that told the player, as fact, that the product made the dead keep going; it now quotes the file's own words (FINEPRINT_LORE §2.1 reason 0, Vantage's brief) and lands the canon's verb: found, not made. Bolt's "The dead ones get up." and Sprocket's "most loyal customers" follow unchanged. (2) `fp.c3.anni.28`, Vendelin to Anni in the label scene: "It makes the dead get up." -> "It keeps the dead moving.", the exact wording of the label fact the design note says this line must cover, and the only one of the five facts that named Nachtglas as the cause rather than the keeper (CANON §0, LAZARUS §4: the crystal answers the sickness; it does not raise the dead). FINEPRINT_SCRIPT.md regenerated with tools/build_fineprint_script.py (the diff is those two lines, so the generated file was in sync). Neither id has a baked take in data/bs/vo/audio_manifest.json, and the ElevenLabs bake reads the chapter files, so nothing else moves. **Hits read and left alone, and why.** Darkmatter in its own mouth still owns the retention, which the canon keeps true (it "took the credit"; its engineers "never met a body without it"): `fp.c1.sol3.04` ("Retention substrate. Product function: churn prevention", the waybill's copy), `fp.c1.sol3.12`/`.13` (the dead as customers; "EXCEPTIONAL RETENTION"), `fp.c2.rising.13`/`.16` (RETENTION EVENT: SUCCESSFUL), `fp.c3.link.14`/`.16`/`.18`/`.27` (Sable: "Churn prevention, V-1. It's the whole product"; "The retention is the subscription"; "so proud of what we've built" is the market, not the dead), `fp.c3.memo.12` (Counsel-9's "by design, non-final": the lawyer's pride is the joke), `fp.c3.ledger.18` (Reinholt's cylinder, "The material does not create. It preserves": truer now than before), `fp.c5.repo.06`, `fp.c5.op_audit.08`, `op.slip.07`/`.30`, `fp.c5.finale.01`, and the Vitality Plan terms, the Sol-3 results sheet and the fine-print panels in FINEPRINT_LORE §9 (T-190's file). Vendelin's understanding is a Darkmatter engineer's, and the canon keeps Ch. 2 as written (he counted Pilch's fourth day from the glass room; nobody on Earth knew both halves until Reinholt): `fp.c2.boot.07`, `fp.c2.rising.15` ("the payload deployed without a launch": it did, culture and crystal together), `fp.c2.log.04` ("It is the payload's product function... I would like someone else to tell me, please"), `fp.c2.log.07`, `fp.c1.transmission.07` ("The product works. That is the whole of my complaint. A retained user cannot cancel": the keeping is true), `fp.c3.link.13`/`.15` ("The product retains the dead"; "the bodies get up and keep doing the last thing they did"), `fp.c3.link.29` ("I built it": the machines), `fp.c3.journal.07`, `fp.c5.meet.14`/`.23`-`.25`, `fp.c5.bnt_ven_peg.07`-`.11`/`.27`-`.33`, `gl.qp.vendelin.special.01` ("Too much product": a Glasshide is a deeply engaged account). Kaltenbrunn's witnesses describe the sickness in the crystal's presence, which is what they saw: `fp.c3.secondwind.13` (Agnieszka: Second Wind "revives the dead. Twitching. For an hour."; the Kaltenbrunn salt was never clean after lot LZR-04), the label's five facts ("it keeps the dead moving" was already the accurate one), `fp.c2.seizure.05` (Pförtner's "two bodies walked away"). Bolt and Sprocket not knowing is the comedy: `fp.c1.sol3.03` ("What did they do to them?": the answer is now "found them and sold to them"), `fp.c1.sol3.15` (the war), `fp.c4.sable.07` ("We know what you did down there"), the Ch. 1 synopsis ("on Sol-3 the dead get up, and Darkmatter counts them as its most loyal customers"), `gl.qp.bolt.map.01` ("this is where it all started": Bolt means Vendelin's landing; he has never heard of 1930). The retained worlds in the Archive (`fp.c1.archive.02`-`.08`) are living users kept by the substrate, not Sol-3's dead. **Bolt & Sprocket: nothing to change.** Its only Darkmatter story text is the five hidden memos (memo.36's "exceptional retention"), `bs.boss.retention.dm.01`, the TONIQ dispenser (`bs.galleria.toniq.01`-`.03`, "DISCONTINUED: Sol-3 test market incident") and `cs.postCredits.01`/`.02`; none says what the dead are or where they came from, and docs/BS_*.md name Sol-3 only as the Crucible's provenance. **Gravelight-side BLT/SPR/VEN rows** (25 in VO_SCRIPT.md, 18 in lines.json): none says Darkmatter made the dead (`VEN_COUSINS_DEAD_01`, "'Retained.' That is the word they would use. I have a better one: waiting.", is already the register the canon wants), so T-198's rows were not touched. Also not touched: quests (the guard), data/bs/*, perk_lore.json (fine_print_reader.gd reads it; T-190 checked it), FINEPRINT_LORE.md (T-190's). Tests: tools/voice.sh test 51/51 (collect_fp still collects 2,867 Fine Print lines; both changed ids parse with their speakers). No GUT run: the Fine Print reader and the B&S line tests read perk_lore.json and data/bs, neither of which changed.

T-201 casting, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 section 7 items 2-4, the voice cast (branch u11-7-cast; data/voice/cast.json, docs/VOICES.md "The cast"). **What was cast:** every speaker in lines.json (63 Gravelight codes, the seven relief squads, 23 Bolt & Sprocket, 66 Fine Print) has an `eleven.voice_id`, a `same_as`/`same_as_voice`, or its explicit `none` (PAUL, SLENDER and NAR have no lines; their lines live under PAU, SLN); `tools/voice.sh test` 51/51, and the plan reports `uncast 0` in all eight groups. **The calls.** (1) **The key cannot add voices.** `voices.share` (assign with an owner) and Voice Design's create step need `voices_write`, which the key lacks (401 missing_permissions; the key is otherwise fine). A probe showed a Voice Library voice id works in text-to-speech without being added (12 characters, 200 OK), so the whole cast is library or premade voices used by id: no slots (Creator has 30), no Voice Design previews, no cost beyond the characters spoken. The risk is that a library owner withdraws a voice; NEEDS_DEVIN says how to pin the cast into the account with a `voices_write` key. Every chosen voice has rate 1.0. Voice Design prompts stay in cast.json as the fallback if a voice disappears. (2) **Picks by the character docs:** Dutch Damian (warm grounded baritone with a little gravel), Peg Sophia (young RP), Deacon Wesley (light Southern, relaxed), Wren Marta and Jakub Andrew (Polish accents, calm), the Operator Clyde (a vintage radio announcer), Fasbender Tobias and Reinholt Charlotte (German accent; she also reads German), Serafim Alex (native Russian, 50-60, textured) and Volkov Aleksandr (native young Moscow baritone), Soviet radio BaMnyc, the Returned Danik (low stability), the brigade Evgeniy Rybakov, the PA Hans, U-boat radio Carl, Lenz Tom (native German), the Cousins Scarlett / Clara / Lucy (distinct, never sexualized), PAU Harry and SLN Austin (library, never clones, so the consent gate stays; the test now accepts source library as well as design), Bolt Will, Sprocket Witty McGee, Blartz Goofy, Glambot Odom, Grindstone Edward, Vendelin Albert Martin (monotone, smooth, Northern-European colour; the hum is an effect), Sable Karin Anne (the Woman in Violet shares her), Res Michael Wogenburg (the nearest to Bernese Swiss), the Underwriter River held at stability 0.85. The map people are one pool of German-accent voices split by age and sex (Potato, Otto, Wogenburg, Simon; Susanne, Lucy, Hannelore, Clara) with the premade Americans and British for the rest; people who share a scene have different voices where the pool allows, and a voice is repeated mostly across maps or chapters. No library voice is a child, so Liesl is the premade Jessica (light, through the cylinder effect). (3) **Stability / similarity** per role, the only v4 settings: 0.30-0.40 for the frightened, the risen, shouters and comic energy, 0.45-0.60 for most, 0.70-0.85 for the machine and radio voices; similarity 0.75, 0.70 for the cartoon voices. (4) **A bake bug found and fixed:** The Fine Print tags a speaker `[de]`/`[pl]` when the character speaks that language in the story but writes the line in English; the bake would have sent `language_code: de|pl` with English text (about 3.3k lines). eleven.py `line_lang` now sends such a line as English (the voice carries the accent) and keeps German text in German. The Gravelight `lang` tags are unchanged. (5) **Auditions (8 lines, 597 characters, final lines only):** DUT_GL1_01, PEG_GL1_02, DEA_GL1_02, WRN_GL1_02 (the squad's GRAVELIGHT ONE lines), OPR_ROUND_START_02, JAK_CHATTER_05, SER_LZS_04, VOL_FTD_03 baked through the real chain into the game (manifest `ai_eleven`, `ai: true`; the Operator line's earlier TTS file is kept in vo_tts). Checked by duration and loudness only (no listening possible): 3.0-8.2 s, -15.6 LUFS for the shouted line and -17.6 to -17.9 for the rest (-20.7 for Jakub's quiet line), true peak -0.8 to -4.2 dB, 48 kHz mono OGG. The account counter moved from 53 to 321 for 597 billed characters (it counts about half; the plan still counts one credit per character). The seven now-baked ids were removed from audio_manifest `missing`. `test_untouched_lines_stay_tts` now allows `ai_eleven` rows (it failed on the first baked line). No Voice Design previews were made. **The plan after casting:** 441,251 characters to pay (cutscenes 10,740; squad 46,292; Operator 28,963; map NPCs 39,527; bosses 2,182; barks 58,655; The Fine Print 197,650; Bolt & Sprocket 57,242), Pro for one month or about four Creator periods; account 321 of 131,000 used.
T-200, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 Archive wiring, story-prop placement, test upkeep (branch u11-3-archive; contract C6/C7). **The Archive rows.** The 17 Lazarus documents are read from data/gravelight/archive_lazarus.json by `StoryProp.archive_rows()`, which returns intel-shaped rows (id, title, type DOC, body as text, `order`) with map `"lazarus"` (the Archive's own shelf), sorted by `order`. `ProfileSave._add_lazarus_rows()` puts them in the Archive table next to MachineLore's and the homage rows, so a first read files them and pays the usual intel XP, and the menu shows them through its existing grouping (one new MAP_NAMES entry, "Lazarus (Chetverodnevo)"). Unfound rows read "A Lazarus document. It lies where the story left it, on the map." (`MenuArchive._unfound_text`). `StoryProp.intel_row` falls back to the same rows for an ARC_LZ_ id, so the reader needs no change: the first paragraph is the caption, as with intel. The texts are T-190's and were not edited. **Placement.** Each entry is a StoryProp row in data/gravelight/story_props.json (17 appended entries, nothing else in the file changed), keyed by the story-props map key ("0", "1", "2", "4a" to "4d"); `StoryProp.ARCHIVE_MAP_KEYS` maps the archive's map field to it. Styles: a leaf of paper or a laid-down case is "letter" (flat, at the surface's y); a typed sheet pinned to a wall is "docket" (wall, back at the wall face). Positions were taken from the map scenes with a headless probe (a temporary tool, not committed: the floor under the spot, the navmesh answer, and the knee/chest clearance, the probe_npc_walls rules), so each spot sits on a real floor, top or sill, with no wall within 0.3 m at body height unless it is a wall item. Placements: map00 diary p1 on the cellar floor by the Helvetia crate column (31.5, -3.45, 21.0); map00 front report on the carters' road west of the gate (14, 0, 45.5); map01 diary p2 on the Crucible room floor (44, -18, 121.5); map01 lab report 1 on the Fasbender bench top (72, -11, 83); map01 memos 1 to 3 on the lab wall (76, -9.5, 81.45), the admin wall beside Door_Admin (-0.45, 1.5, 21.5) and the generator hall wall by the Hauptschalter (46, -10.2, 60.6); map02 diary p3 by the crane office wall (42, 0, 52) and the Laboratory No. 4 inventory inside the warhead cage (57, 0, 19); map04a diary p4 in the Montage hall (-50, 0, 70) and the subject list on the Bahnhof hut's north wall (2.5, 1.5, 57.8); map04b diary p5 on casing rack 1 (-5.6, 0.6, 6); map04c diary p6 on the British crate's lid (23, 1.4, 63), the culture log on the specimen-room floor (-62, -5.5, 128), Hale's order on the office glass sill beside the program log (26.5, 1.1, 91.5); map04d diary p7 on the artifacts rack top (28, 2.4, 34) and the last-resort order on the Account console top (28, 1.1, -30). **Not verified in the engine:** the cellar, the bench and the Hale sill are within about 0.5 m of the navmesh or of the surface edge, the specimen-room floor sits 0.45 m off the navmesh, and the map01 memo wall faces were read off the scan's wall line. The owner should walk all seventeen once in the editor; each is one row in story_props.json to move. **No quest and no Easter egg.** Nothing in data/gravelight/quests/*.json changed, no quest step reads these ids, and MapRoot's random intel spawn reads intel.json only, so the Lazarus documents never spawn at random. **Tests.** test_story_props: the Map 0, 1, 2, 4a, 4b, 4c and 4d spawn counts were raised by the new placements (9, 7, 12, 6, 5, 9, 11), each with its reason; `_by_id()` includes the archive rows, and the fixed-row check skips ARC_LZ_ ids (they are checked in the new file). test_archive_lazarus.gd (new, 7 tests): seventeen entries with unique ids and orders; each entry has exactly one placement on its map; every placement spawns on its map; each of the seven maps has exactly one diary page; the Archive's Lazarus shelf lists all seventeen in order with their body text; reading a placed document shows its entry; no ARC_LZ_ id is an intel.json row. Credits: no new asset, nothing added to menu_credits.

T-202, 2026-11-07, the respect review and its fixes: UPDATE 11 section 8 (branch u11-8-respect-fixes; the review itself was a read-only pass by a fresh Fable session over docs/LAZARUS.md, docs/CUTSCENES_LAZARUS.md, data/gravelight/archive_lazarus.json and 200 sampled lines; it filed R-01..R-16, one must-fix, five should-fixes, ten notes, and the owner's order is "fix everything it flags, and record the review in docs/LAZARUS.md"). The review and a findings table with every Status are now the "Respect review" section of docs/LAZARUS.md. **What changed and why.** R-01 (must-fix): Father Serafim's last line read as the priest opening the church door to the dead on forty-one people; he now goes out through the door himself and Pyotr Ilyich bars it behind him, "so that none of them has to be the one who goes" (EN and RU: *сам выйду за дверь церкви, и Пётр Ильич заложит её за мной брусом, чтобы никому из них не пришлось идти*), in the Archive body and in `SER_DIARY_MAP04D_01`; his fate stays one ambiguous line, his decency does not. R-02 + R-14: one Church Slavonic text and one orthography for the crypt-door line everywhere (the bible's *Є́же затвори́ся въ де́нь четве́ртый, никто́же да отве́рзетъ*: Synodal, wide *є* word-initially, final *ъ*, stress marks; *никтоже* takes no second negative), in `SER_LZS_05`'s speech and CUTSCENES set D's lintel; the rule is written in LAZARUS §12, and the sung or said liturgical texts (`SER_LZS_08`, `RTN_CLOSE_03`) stay in civil transcription because they are spoken text for the bake, not inscriptions; if the ElevenLabs voice stumbles on the stress marks, T-201 may strip them from the spoken text at bake time without changing lines.json. R-03: the three died 15.IV and rose 18.IV, so the chart reads 18.IV, set F is the mortuary room off the fever ward (Report No. 1 already said "rose from the mortuary"), the CUTSCENES header's exclusive fourth-day count is replaced by the inclusive one (John 11:17), and LAZARUS §2.4/§9 say mortuary. R-04: state security was the NKGB from April 1943, so Volkov's 1944 boards are "a state-security colonel's (NKGB by 1944)" in CUTSCENES §2 and §7, LAZARUS §3.1 has the sentence, the §7 and §11 rows and CANON §0 carry NKGB, and no 1944 line names the agency (the 1937-39 papers keep NKVD GUGB, right for their dates). R-05: the bitten are "in the side chapel, behind the inner doors", not in the sanctuary. R-06: `OPR_ROUND_START_14` loses "no boots" (how real Red Army dead were stripped) for "Greatcoats and frost, straight through the snow"; `WIE_SURV_SCARED_02`, a frightened German veteran noticing the missing boots, is an eyewitness and stands. R-07: the written letter in the front report says "the political officer" (commissars were abolished Oct 1942); the radio's `SVR_M0_04` keeps the soldiers' word. R-08: "the fictional 118th" becomes "a number not meant to identify any real formation"; the number in `RTN_CLOSE_04` stays, because every number to about 420 was a real rifle division at some date and a dying man's unit number identifies nobody. R-09: the doc drift aligned to the bible (card 1 "a long day's cart", header 16-30 Oct, shot 8 quotes the line's "Here is the warrant", Serafim 59 in CUTSCENES §7 and VO_SCRIPT §26.1), and LAZARUS §6.1 now has him keeper of the lamp from 1890 as a reader of nineteen and priest from 1899, which is the diary's "forty years". R-10: Reinholt's personal note separates the gift from the debt ("The Soviet file calls it Lazar. I did not know the name was already taken."). R-11: `VOL_LZS_08` "Priests have faked worse than this" / *Попы и не такое подделывали* (the 1919-20 relic-exposure claims, not a slur). R-12: Rule 4 says "his brother, a prisoner of the same camp, brought over at Subject 24's own request". R-13: `SER_DIARY_MAP04C_01`'s awkward clause is now *деревня знает одно: колокол зовёт — иди*; *уезд* in the last page is kept as the old priest's word and §6.1 says so. R-14 also: "lost after 1204" is "carried off by the Crusaders in 1204 (tradition says to Marseille)". R-15: §2.1 says in one sentence that a district closure three weeks after the 14 March 1930 resolution is the excess it condemned. R-16: the FOURTH DAY card says "the East Prussian line"; no real place name heads the fictional event. **Not changed:** quests (the guard), the cutscene build files and gravelight/ui/cinema (T-194 was told the built-text fixes: lintel, chart, mortuary, NKGB, the two cards), `VOL_LZS_04` (a modern-Russian paraphrase on purpose), `SVR_M0_04`. **lines.json:** extract_vo.py re-run; the key diff against the pre-edit file is exactly `SER_LZS_05`, `VOL_LZS_08`, `SER_DIARY_MAP04C_01`, `SER_DIARY_MAP04D_01`, `OPR_ROUND_START_14`, nothing added or removed; none of the five has an audio take yet (all in audio_manifest.json's `missing`, awaiting the ElevenLabs bake), so nothing is re-baked. Tests: tests/voice 51/51; GUT test_vo* and test_u11_egg_guard headless (no GUT test reads archive_lazarus.json; the JSON parses).

T-192, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 section 6, the horror pass (branch u11-6-horror). **The Soviet risen ("the Returned").** Three new outfit kits built the way every zombie kit is (tools_src/blender/characters/make_kits.py, Blender 5.1 headless, all six zombie_base GLBs rebuilt; our own work): `soviet` (the M1935 shinel greatcoat to mid-calf, collar turned up, belt and pouches over it, breeches, the boots taken off the dead: the portyanki foot wraps show), `soviet_tunic` (the M1935 gymnastyorka with its short placket and two chest pockets, sharovary, obmotki puttees over laced ankle boots, a 12% female share: the Red Army's women soldiers) and `soviet_burial` (from a field grave: wound in the canvas plashch-palatka and tied with cord, bare feet). Plain collar tabs and shoulder straps, a plain frame buckle, no slogans, no banners. The call on the cap star: the small enamel star on the pilotka and the ushanka is period issue for every rifleman, so it stays, 15-18 mm, a dull dark red (key `accent`, never emissive); nothing else political appears on them. Headgear is rolled per spawn from a new anim.json `gear_one_of` (at most one hat: ushanka / pilotka / SSh-40, new procedural gear pieces). Frost-white skin (a new skin look `frost`: cold blue-grey veins that barely pulse, faint pale cracks, no violet; anim.json infection.frost_look), the kit shader's frost at 0.35-0.85 per kit ("frost" in the kit row: greatcoats stiff with ice), milky eyes (ZombieBody.KIT_PALETTE replaces the eye's violet emission). kit_order had used all nine coverage bits (blue + alpha); bits 9-16 now live in a body vertex's red channel (the skin key is index 0, so every older body reads 0 there and is unchanged; make_body.body_colors / ZombieBody.mask_of). Never marked (CANON 2.2), so **no Marke and no glint**: the weak point is the wound that killed them (ZombieBody.WOUND_SITES chest / belly / throat / temple, a per-kit list, picked from net_id like the Marke so co-op puppets match; a dark frozen hole with a frost rim, lit, never emissive). It is the same hitbox cube with the same limb ("marke"), so every hit path, the weapon multiplier mapping and the co-op report are unchanged; the same enemies.json marke.damage_mult and scrip_bonus apply; it emits no GLBus.marke_hit / marke_kill (no violet HUD tint, no MARKE stat, no Marke VO), the bonus slip reads THE WOUND. Spawns, data only (enemies.json soviet_risen): a plain Ganger spawn's kit becomes a Soviet one with share 0.22 on Map 0 from round 2 (round 1's scripted opening stays as it was) and 0.15 on Kaltenbrunn for spawns at y >= -4 (the surface; never the depths at -12 / -18); no other map. No new AI. **The background war:** Map 0's map00_ambience.json `risen_column` (MapAmbience kind u11_risen_column): twelve of them (six in the browser) walking west across the fields north-east of the inn, 230-240 m out, from round 1. **Scripted use (for u11-5-cutscenes):** `RisenColumn.make({"from": Vector3, "to": Vector3, "count": n, "speed": [a, b], "width": m, "ground": "flat" | "terrain", "loop": false, "seed": s})` then add_child (presentation only: no collision, no hitboxes, in no group; `paused` freezes the walk; from / to are in the column's own space); a single body: `RisenColumn.body(kit, build, female, "shamble_a", seed_id, rng)`; a game zombie in the kit: any spawn spec with `"variant": "soviet"` (ZombieDirector.activate_reserved / rise_at), which gets its wound automatically. **Visual grit** (HorrorLayer, gravelight/world/horror_layer.gd, the MapAmbience kind u11_horror from each map's `horror` block; presentation only, each block off when absent): screen-edge frost in the cold (FrostCreep's ice-fern texture as one full-screen TextureRect under the PostFx layer; off in warm zones and below Kaltenbrunn's surface), flies over the dead in warm zones (Kaltenbrunn's lab, generator and core; Groom Lake everywhere; a CPUParticles3D swarm and its buzz per body, 4 / 2 / 0 by VFX quality), blood pools under the dead and mud where they dig out (Decals, pool 24 / 14 / 6 by VFX quality, none on the Compatibility renderer, which draws none; the Gore setting: full = blood, low = a small dark stain, off = none), breath fog on Kaltenbrunn and Totenhafen too (the existing BreathFog, data), a darker night (each map's adjustment brightness x 0.9-0.94 through GLSettings' brightness base, so the Brightness slider still scales it, contrast +0.03-0.05), and the lanterns gutter (every non-fire FlickerLight gets a deeper, more frequent gutter, and every 9-22 s one nearly goes out for under a second). Draw calls: at most four swarms and one canvas rect; decals cost none; the column's twelve bodies about 50. **Audio** (gravelight/tools_src/gen_audio_horror.py, our own synthesis, OGG; no recording of any real chant is used, so there is no licence question): a chant-like bed (a bass ison on D2 sung on "o" by four detuned voices with a fifth above, three slow swells a loop, worn down by tape wow, saturation, a 10-bit crush, a low-pass and a long church reverb) on the Ambience bus, rising with the round from -40 to -29 dB (the file measures -20 LUFS: -49 LUFS at its loudest keeps AUDIO_MIX.md's rule with the map bed under it), worn fragments of a modal line over it more often late in the night, a far church bell from a bearing every 50-220 s (not on the Moon), the Returned's close lines (within 3.5 m of the local player a Soviet risen on its feet plays lines.json category RTN_CLOSE through GLVO, speaker RTN registered as a positional player speaker with a 10 s cooldown in vo/rules.json; while u11-5-shots has written none it plays a wordless whisper at its head, horror_whisper), the squad's ragged breathing below half health (the local player 2D, teammates within 6 m at their body). The Jingles bus is untouched. **Hard Truth** (gravelight/world/hard_truth.gd, one per NetMatch, the authority only; Settings > Gameplay "Hard Truth": auto = on in the story rules, off in Classic Rules (the game has no other "Story mode": the story rules are the default, traits-on rules), on, off; online the host's setting decides). "Their death crate as now": the game had no death crate for a survivor (a bled-out survivor loses everything), so the call is the relief squads' free ammunition crate (SquadCrate), as FallenCrate named for them ("Hold F for Peg's ammunition"), 4 refills, 90 s (enemies.json hard_truth). When a squad member (human or bot) bleeds out, the host lays their body at once as a pooled zombie in their own risen body (PeopleBodies.dress_risen, unmarked) via ZombieDirector.rise_dormant, outside the round queue (it must die before the round ends), lying face down and still (Zombie.lie_dormant: the get-up's first frame held, no hitboxes, no body collision, no damage, bots don't target it; net_sub_state 1 so a client's puppet lies still too); after 4 s of round time (a break holds the clock) it gets up over 2.2 s (wake_from_dormant) and is a normal zombie; put down, the crate drops where it fell and is mirrored with NetMatch.spawn_dynamic. Companions never rise (Jakub and the map companions are out cold, not dead: Jakub is never a zombie), and nothing rises once everyone is down. The respawn rules are untouched (CoopState.respawn_dead brings them back at the next round as before). **The menu** (MenuScene): the far front's flares on the eastern horizon (bearings 55-150, every 18-32 s), five Returned on their own lane at the treeline behind the Marke-lit walkers (RISEN_FROM / RISEN_TO in camera A's frame: the owner's GPU look may want to move it), the chant bed at -31 dB under the theme. Tests: test_hard_truth (the toggle and its defaults, who rises, the dormant body, the rise on the fourth second, the break holding the clock, the crate, pooled reuse, the puppet sub state, respawn unchanged), test_soviet_risen (the kits in every GLB, the coverage bits, frost skin and eyes, one hat, the wound in place of the Marke and its numbers, determinism, the spawn shares, the column), test_horror_layer (every map's block, the bed's rise inside the mix rule, frost / breath / gore rules, the whisperers, the bank and buses, the grade). Smokes: smoke_hard_truth (solo with a bot), smoke_coop (ENet: the client's body on both peers, the crate on the client), smoke_webrtc --through=hard_truth (the bot's body and crate over WebRTC). Needs the owner's GPU look: the kits up close (the Blender exporter warns "not valid" on Kit_soviet / Kit_soviet_tunic after decimation, validated; check for holes), the wound's size at range, the menu lane's place, the far column's line on Map 0, the frost strength, the darker grade.

T-194, 2026-11-07: UPDATE 11 section 5, the four LAZARUS cutscenes and the Moscow 1953 card, built on the T-184 cinema library (branch u11-5-cutscenes; shot lists docs/CUTSCENES_LAZARUS.md, T-191; as-built notes in its §9). **Data, not code:** data/gravelight/cutscenes_lazarus.json holds the five definitions in the campaign.json `cutscenes` shape (CinemaDirector.validate and the clip tool read them unchanged) plus `sets`, `cast` (every person's look), `hooks`, the Archive's `gallery` and `risen_kind`. A shot adds `_set` (its stage), `fx` / `fx_at` (the stage's effects), `lines` (more than vo / vo2), `squad_lines` ([t, seat, suffix]: GRAVELIGHT ONE's `*_GL1_01` from the window seat, `*_GL1_02` from the second), `card` (a mid-timeline title card; a title-only card draws cleanly) and `black` (the card on black, the sound over black); the director gained `lines`, `card` and `black`, the overlay the `thaw` and `ward` grades and one owner for the black's tween. Lengths: LAZARUS SATURDAY 240 s (shot list 224), FOURTH DAY 106 (95), STAUB 73 (64), GRAVELIGHT ONE 84 (78), the Moscow card 23 (22), all inside +15 %: shots were lengthened where the lines at reading speed would talk over each other (no line starts before the last one on its channel ends; test_cinema_lazarus checks it with the VO system's own reading-speed estimate). **Voice:** every line goes through GLVO.play_local, which plays a line's baked audio when it exists and otherwise shows the English `text` (never the Cyrillic `speech`) for the reading-speed time; the timeline never waits for either, so the cutscenes run the same before and after the ElevenLabs bake (T-201). No line id was added. **Runtime (gravelight/ui/cinema/):** CinemaReel plays one or more cutscenes in a row; its picture is a SubViewport with its own World3D holding the current stage and the director (camera, overlay), shown full screen on a CanvasLayer under the cinema subtitles, so a stage never touches the map it plays over (its own sky, sun, fog), costs a map nothing when it is not playing, and the same reel plays in a match, before the menu and from the Archive; while it plays the root viewport draws no 3D. The stage swaps at the shot whose `_set` changes, always on black (a dip or blend across stages is pre-faded so the swap happens under the black; a blend across stages becomes a dip). LazarusCinema (one per map, every peer) is the match-start hook and the net path; CinemaTheatre (cinema_theatre.tscn) the prologue and the Archive's replays; CinemaLibrary the data. **Hooks:** the prologue plays before the CLAUDE ZOMBIES menu while ProfileSave `prologue_seen` is false (set when it ends or is skipped; never again unless replayed from the Archive); a new campaign on Map 0 plays FOURTH DAY then GRAVELIGHT ONE at the match start; the campaign arriving at Kaltenbrunn plays STAUB before the arrival grace (the arrival banner shows again after it); a Map 0 or Kaltenbrunn match outside the campaign plays its cutscene the first time this profile starts one (`standalone_rule` "once"; "always" / "never" are data); a dev start later in the chain plays nothing. While a reel plays the host holds RoundManager's intro timer, input is blocked and Map 0's opening waits (InnOpening's settled-view clock and title card pause while the "cutscene" flag is held), so the opening then runs exactly as built from the player's own eyes; no gameplay timing, hitbox, navmesh or prompt changed. The Moscow card plays after KÜNDIGUNG's credits (cancel_ending.gd hands its end screen to LazarusCinema.epilogue); its three texts are cards_m4.json `cards.moscow53` (MAP4_FERNFLUG §12.11; not in card_order, so the ending's own cards are unchanged): the shot list's MOSCOW card, the image with §12.11's second card as its caption, the last two cards. Headless runs (tests, smokes, tools) never start a cutscene on their own (`LazarusCinema.force`, `CinemaTheatre.force_prologue` turn them on), so no existing smoke waits on one. **Net:** host-driven like the campaign hops: the host decides and mirrors (`_net_begin`), every peer plays its own reel on its own clock, a skip is the co-op vote (every human holds [interact]; a client asks through the whitelisted `request_skip`, the tally shows on every peer's skip bar), the host's end ends everyone's (a client whose copy ends first waits on black). Not done: a late joiner mid-cutscene just joins the match after it. **The Archive:** the Intel Archive has a Cutscenes button; the gallery replays the five LAZARUS cutscenes (CinemaTheatre) and the six campaign hops (their map loads with nobody in it, rounds stopped, ExfilCutscene with no beacon, which ends instead of travelling), then reopens the gallery. **Sets (gravelight/world/cinema_sets/, 13 scenes, procedural, our own geometry on the game's CC0 MaterialLibrary / Mats materials and CC0 PropLibrary props; no new third-party asset):** the village (A-C: the thaw road, izby with carved frames, birches, the wooden church with its dull onion dome, the tent-roofed bell tower and its fall, the bell and its crack, the AMO-F-15 with two plain red cloths; variant `spring` for shot 32), the crypt (D-E: the stair, the lamp, the lintel's line, the vault, the vessel and its gore states), the mortuary room off the fever ward (F, the respect review's R-03), the cordon at night with the burning village and the door's painted board (G), the train compartment with the night going by (H), the German line and the Soviet OP (I-J; the OP 250 m from the line, not 600, so the long lens still reads the soft bursts), the field graves and the road west with its battery (K-L), the dust mill (M; the Wiederkehr mark is our own armband texture), the north ramp and the weather station over the valley's lights (N-O), the Heart's console (P, built small instead of loading Kaltenbrunn for one shot), the C-47 cabin and cockpit (Q-R: T-126's hull made whole; the window looks down 600 m on a moving ground with flares, fires and columns), the glide (S: a small night stage with the inn's lit windows instead of loading Map 0 for one 8 s shot) and the Moscow vault (T). A stage is a few hundred draw calls at most (MultiMeshes for logs, sandbags, birches, snow, woods, the far columns). People: CinemaPerson (a PatronBody posed by its own solver; its body the relief squads' way, ZombieBody.build_dressed in a kit recoloured by the look's palette, or a people.json person: Selwyn and the four squad members in the C-47) with our own small costume pieces (cassock, skufia, beard, service cap with its band, budenovka, ushanka, kerchief, rain cape, mask, plain red armband; no insignia) and new poses (kneel, pray, palm, hold, sit_hold, kneel_reach, writing). The squad in the C-47 are their own modelled bodies, seated (a RemoteBody stand-in has no sit). **The risen:** every risen body comes from `CinemaRisen.make(kind, seed)` (the seam; `risen_kind` in the data); since T-192 merged it builds RisenColumn.body in the Soviet kits at RisenColumn's weights (the stand-in it replaced, the soldier kit in a frost-white greatcoat, stays as the fallback). The C-47's window shows the columns as dark specks in files on the snow (a MultiMesh): a real body is under a pixel from 600 m. **Respect (work order §5, T-202):** nobody is struck on screen in 1930: the road is cleared by the people getting up and being walked aside, out of focus, while the camera holds on Serafim; the two who take his arms are cut away from on their first movement and the rest is sound over black; Serafim never falls or bleeds on screen; the lamp, the line and the church are lit and still. From the review: the lintel reads the bible's wording (R-02), the mortuary room and 18.IV (R-03), the cards read "a long day's cart" and "the East Prussian line · the German line" (R-09, R-16), nothing on screen names the NKVD (R-04). Gore: the vessel's inside is dark and wet with Gore off, red on low, red with a drop running down the lip on full; the sheet's and the mound's stains draw only on full. **Sound:** ten new keys synthesized by gravelight/tools_src/gen_audio_lazarus.py (ours, no samples): amb_thaw, truck_1930, bell_crack, timber_fall, rain_field, shell_soft, mill_bed, rocket_salvo, c47_engines, prop_windmill; inserted into audio.json keeping its formatting (gen_audio's manifest writer re-indents the whole file); T-192's horror_chant_bed and horror_whisper play where the shot lists ask for the chant and the whispers (`beds_opt` / `sfx_opt`, optional keys). **Tests:** test_cinema_lazarus (every definition validates; every stage, effect and aimed-at node exists on its built stage; every line is a cutscene line in lines.json and none overlaps at reading speed; lengths; lenses 24-70; the cards' exact text, Latin only; cards.moscow53; the seats; the seam; the Gore variants; the plan; the first-launch flag; the gallery; a reel plays and skips). Smokes: smoke_cinema_lazarus (each of the five plays to its end and is skipped; the first launch plays the prologue, a skip returns to the menu with prologue_seen set and it does not play again; a new campaign plays FOURTH DAY then GRAVELIGHT ONE with the intro, the input and the opening held, then the opening and round 1; Kaltenbrunn plays STAUB, then round 1), smoke_cinema_online (two processes over ENet on Map 0: the host plays GRAVELIGHT ONE, the client plays it on the host's word, one vote of two does nothing and the tally reaches the client, the client's vote ends both, round 1 on both). The clip tool plays every LAZARUS id on its own and `lazarus_campaign_start` / `lazarus_map01_arrival` the game's way (`--gore=` too). **Filmed** with tools/wsl_cutscene_clip.sh (WSL + Xvfb, software GL, 960x540, 12 fps) into morning/clips/u11_<id>.mp4 (git-ignored) and read still by still; what the first films showed and was fixed: the crypt stair blacked out by the lantern bearers' backs, the lintel's line half hidden by the landing's ceiling, the window shots blocked by a standing passenger, the violet column past the camera's far plane, the glide too dark to see, the OP's reverse shots taken from behind the officers, the rain fog hiding the soft bursts, a brigade man standing where the bell lands, Volkov between the camera and Serafim in the crypt, Reinholt turned away from her console, a pale lead case and a floating sleeve. **Needs the owner's GPU look:** depth of field and the grades on Forward+ (the films are the Compatibility renderer), the people up close (the costume pieces are simple primitives on the rig), the village's scale and the tower's fall, the vessel close-up under each Gore setting, the window view's flares and columns, the cockpit's violet column, the glide.

T-201 bake, 2026-11-07: the ElevenLabs voice bake (branch u11-7-bake; tools/voice.sh elevenlabs bake, eleven_v4, the final lines after the rewrite and the respect review). **Order and numbers** (lines baked, characters billed, account counter moved; the counter lags the real use by a batch or two, so the per-group counter figures are approximate): 1 cutscenes 107 new lines (6 auditions were already done, all 113 now), 10,795 chars, counter +4,745; 2 squad 578 lines, 46,292 chars, +19,485; 3 Operator 396 lines (+1 from the cache), 28,945 chars, +12,505; 4 map NPCs 438 of 440 lines, 39,509 chars, +17,385; 5 bosses 68 of 71 (3 stage-direction-only lines left, see below), 2,157 chars, +369 (lagging); 6 barks 1,087 of 1,087 lines, 58,572 chars, +25,744; 7 The Fine Print 1,228 of 2,867 lines (4 tranches), 96,207 chars, about +39,700; 8 Bolt & Sprocket not started (0 of 728). The account went from 321 to 123,965 of 131,000 used for about 282,000 billed characters: **0.44 counter per billed character**, steady across groups (0.41-0.46). **The reserve rule:** the owner keeps at least 5,000 on the account's own counter. The bake's built-in quota check compares billed characters 1:1 with the account's remaining counter, so it is conservative (it stopped Fine Print early the first time at "42 characters left" while the real counter still had 27,000) and does not protect a reserve by itself; I used `--limit-chars` per run, recomputed from a fresh `plan` read each time as (remaining - 5,000 - about 1,000 for counter lag) / 0.5, and re-read the account between runs and groups.
