HUD: subtitles break at a sentence or clause end inside the chunk's last 55 % (a chunk stopped on "Keep the lamps lit and"); the off-screen teammate marker keeps its 120 px name box inside the HUD margin ("Deacon 5m" ran off the left edge). Checked and left: the perk caps (redrawn in T-183; the contact sheet reads at every size on dark and pale grounds), the round stamp (a shot caught its 1.9x slam, not a layout bug), map select. No gameplay, timing, hitbox or prompt touched. Tests: test_ui_polish_t218.

T-219, 2026-11-08, the owner (UPDATE 13 Part 3): "Most perk machines, ammo boxes and other interactables face the wrong way. Fix them" (Fernflug only; the other maps report only; the Crucible is right; layout locked). A headless check, gravelight/tools_src/interactable_facing.gd, works from the scene file (the baked navmesh polygons and every box collider; nothing's _ready runs): for each Interactable (plus the bank counter from bank.json and the exfil beacon from the map json) it samples the navmesh 0.8-1.8 m past the body's front face with a clear chest-high line, for the four turns of the node, and classifies OK / BACKWARDS / SIDEWAYS / UNSURE / UNREACHABLE (front axis and body extents per script: most face local -Z; the bank counter, the Account console and the breach plates +Z; the heater cabinet, the vent lever and the workbench have their body behind the origin; perk machines use their tonic's footprint). tools/tests/test_interactable_facing.gd runs it per map (default the four acts; --report-only, --md/--json/--cams, a KNOWN list for problems that are not a facing), gravelight/tests/unit/test_interactable_facing.gd covers the rules. Finding: Act I was right except the Armorer's Bench (its bench ran into the wall); Acts II-IV were authored with the yaw sign flipped (the T-175 Crucible note was the same mistake): 55 objects there faced their wall. Fixed in the generators' tables (56 objects, all plain about-turns but the Lodestone workbench, which also moved 0.7 m onto Bench_Work: the node is the bench's front edge), scenes regenerated with node ids kept: only those transforms changed (navmesh regions, colliders, nav blocks, doors, spawns, windows byte-identical; the ammo boxes' nav blocks are the same for an about-turn). The Kranich's painted backing plates behind the wall guns went (turned round, they would have covered the chalk). Left and listed (docs/captures/fernflug/facing_after.md): Act I's hangar ammo box is buried under the west catwalk ramp; the Groom Lake generator switch stands in an off-navmesh strip with its front on the wall (its spot is the question: layout); the Lodestone gimbal part lies inside the radar mast's collider; the Kranich galley ammo box overlaps the Bordfunk desk (turned round like the rest; facing north would move its nav block); the silo exfil beacon is free-standing. Other maps: no wrong facing; Kaltenbrunn's Ledger Lager overlaps a filing cabinet collider (docs/captures/fernflug/facing_other_maps.md). Approach cameras for every interactable (2.75 m out on the open side, eye height): docs/captures/fernflug/interactable_cams.json, also as capture_cams.json-style sets.

T-220, 2026-11-08, the owner's plane pass on Fernflug Act II ("when you're out on the catwalks, it should look and FEEL like you're standing on a giant flying-wing bomber in the sky"; Parts 1 and 2; docs/MAP4_FERNFLUG.md section 4.10). Layout locked and checked: the regenerated map04b.tscn has the same 17 navmeshes, 191 collision shapes and 408 collision / navigation nodes as before (hashed); the only generator changes are two root nodes (KranichExterior, KranichAir, `_plane_pass()` in gen_map04b.gd's build_dressing) and the old flat outer wing panels removed from `_wing_walk_dressing` (their 3.04-3.08 m tops would poke through the new skin; they were visual only). Choices: (1) the hull is built at load from data (`data/gravelight/map04b_plane.json`), not a GLB: the planform has to fit a locked layout to the centimetre (under the wing walk's grating at 3.28 m, over every room roof, open behind the starboard blisters, the flight-deck glass as the nose), and the pure functions let a unit test prove those clearances; 82k triangles in culled chunks of 18 columns, about 0.45 s to build, nothing collides. (2) Faces cull from inside, so the blisters, the glass and the breaches never see the outer skin from behind. (3) The wingspan follows what the wing walk already implied (out to x = 117, nav lights at 129.5): 268 m tip to tip, a swept flying wing with the engines buried at |x| 84 and 100. (4) Markings: a fictional unit code (KR + 01) and plain German maintenance stencils; no emblem anywhere a period plane carried a national one (ART_FACTIONS: "the game shows nothing"); the Werk's crane-in-a-cog stays an Act I mark. (5) Night readability: the paint is a lighter grey-green than a real night bomber (top RLM-ish #5b6058 / #6a7063, the underside black), the moon gives it a specular sheen, and every tether post got a dim warm shadowless light; the exhaust glow is warm (fire family), not violet (violet stays Nachtglas). (6) The world banks, not the floor: the sky shader, the cloud sheets, the ground plane, the far deck and the key light roll up to 2.6 degrees and recover (two slow waves); this is a visual, so Camera Sway Off keeps it (Off removes camera motion only, as the order says; an option for the owner: make Off also stop the horizon's bank). (7) Dawn: the key light (the act's DirectionalLight "Moon") slerps to a low sun behind the wing as the act's sunrise passes 0.35-0.8, and the sky's disc turns into the sun. (8) Comfort setting `camera_sway` (full / reduced / off, default full) in GLSettings; CameraSway is the one place that scales flight camera motion: Player's ext roll (8 deg tilt -> 3.2 Reduced, 0 Off), FlightController's bank jolts, FlightSky's held bank shake, KranichAir's turbulence shake; ext_roll_deg itself (the gameplay value the smokes check) and the tilt's push are unchanged; weapon and explosion shake stay on Screen Shake; the hard landing after being thrown off the wing counts as a hit. The menu row is the lead's (menu_settings.gd is being restructured). (9) Turbulence events are rare (first after 50-90 s, then every 70-150 s) and wait for a quiet moment (no homage boss alive, no `cutscene` flag, no egg holdout, no tilt within 12 s, no decompression, bomb run or London window, at most 2 dead within 12 m, no hit in 5 s, no quest step in 25 s); after 45 s of waiting the event is skipped. (10) Audio: three new synthesized sounds in gen_audio_kranich.py (kranich_wind_roar, kranich_engine_outside, kranich_groan x3; audio.json keys inserted by hand so the file keeps its format); exposure zones are data boxes plus the live openings. (11) Captures: the fernflug_plane set (15 views) and fernflug_plane_dawn (after only; KranichAir.preview_dawn holds the dawn, FlightSky.sunrise_hold); docs images are 1600-wide JPEGs (the repo's 400 KB rule; a PNG at the window's 3440x1440 was 3-4 MB); tools/compare_shots.py --jpg. Performance (perf_marks on map04b, preset ultra, round 15, debug_spawn(24) [15-24 active], 3 bots, vsync off, 1280x720 off-screen on the RTX 5090; the machine was CPU-loaded: the owner's game and other agents' headless runs were up): BEFORE the pass 139.6 avg FPS at the spawn and 140.3 on the wing walk (--pos=-80,3.4,21,90, a new perf_marks option); AFTER, an interleaved A/B in one session (--no-plane, a new option: KranichExterior / KranichAir never build), three reps each, median avg FPS: wing walk 160.0 with the pass vs 178.2 without (6.25 vs 5.61 ms, about +0.6 ms; an earlier pair 169.7 vs 181.8, +0.4 ms), spawn 160.6 vs 163.8 (+0.1 ms); run-to-run noise was 10-25 FPS (one run of each kind dropped to 134-148). So: about half a millisecond when the hull is in view, nothing measurable inside, all far above the 60 FPS budget. One AFTER run died silently while loading (no error line; the retry was clean) and an A/B variant that freed the nodes at runtime hit the known "indexing did not unpair geometries from light" error and hung (T-208/T-214), so the A/B builds nothing instead of freeing.

T-220 b, 2026-11-08, the lead's legibility iteration ("the wing past the rail is a near-black plain slab; nothing on the skin draws the eye"). Choices: (1) a cold sky fill DirectionalLight that lights only the hull's own render layer 19 (layer 20 is the viewmodel), no shadow, kept out of the sky (sky_mode light-only, so the sky's disc stays the moon), 0.45 at night falling to 0.12 at dawn, with extra specular: the walk, the squad and the dead are not brightened, so the night stays the art bible's night everywhere the gameplay is. (2) The hull shader keeps every seam at least ~1.5 px wide at any distance (the line's world width follows the pixel footprint) and bevels it (a groove and a lit lip), turns far rivet rows into faint light lines, varies tint and sheen per sheet and age per group of sheets, wears the leading edge to bare metal and stains the trailing edge (the generated skin now carries its chord position in COLOR.r), darkens and widens the soot fans with a tan heat stain round them, and paints dark non-skid strips under the catwalk and a light walk-limit line 1.6 m round it (from map04b.json wing_walk.segments, so it follows the layout). (3) Bigger stencils: KR + 01 at 26 x 6 m on each outer wing, the airframe number 01 at each tip, NICHT BETRETEN every ~15 m along the walk's aft edge, HEISS by the nozzles. (4) Engines read from the hatch: a warm additive glow sprite at each nozzle mouth, a stronger heat shimmer. (5) Wingtip anti-collision strobes (a white double flash every 1.6 s; the red / green nav lamps stay steady, as nav lights are). (6) The sheets below get moonlit tops (lit colour, a little more opacity) and the far deck a brighter top; cloud banks are bigger, denser and higher (y 2-14 m) so they cross over the wing. (7) Triangles cut: the default SphereMesh lenses (4k triangles each) became 12 x 6 spheres and the skin went to 100 rows: 82k -> 56k triangles. Wing-walk cost: not measurable this round: the A/B (perf_marks --pos=-80,3.4,21,90 with and without --no-plane, interleaved, three reps each) ran while other sessions saturated the CPU (77-90 % load, 33-122 FPS, the same configuration differing by 80 FPS between runs, so no verdict either way). The changes that cost anything are a shadowless directional light on one render layer, four glow quads, two strobes that are mostly off and a longer hull shader, against 24k fewer triangles; the last clean measurement (T-220) was +0.4 to +0.6 ms on the wing walk. Re-measure on a quiet machine with the same A/B before calling it within budget.

T-221, 2026-11-08, the owner: "the lighting needs to be improved on the map, this is a great chance to make some crazy scenes for the trailer, make the sunset look great, the bombs dropping, and the lighting around it, too many dark areas and the textures on some hallways are messed up" (Act II only; docs/MAP4_FERNFLUG.md section 4.10). Choices: (1) The "sunset" is the act's own dawn: the clock still runs night -> dawn (MapAmbience flight_sun) and the story stays dawn behind the wing; KranichSunset turns it into a golden hour from act round 4 into 5 (sunrise 0.45 -> 0.7) and holds it at full while the bomb run is on, so the run (act round 5) is always gold and the night look of the early act is untouched. The key light (the act's DirectionalLight "Moon") goes deep gold (#ff8a34), brighter (2.4), glinting (specular 2.2) and low (sun_dir ~3 degrees up, aft); its volumetric-fog scattering drops to 0.1 and the depth / volumetric fog thin (x0.45 / x0.6) while golden, because the first pass washed the frame milk-white; the sky shader gets a golden uniform (orange horizon aft, a pink band, deep blue overhead and ahead, a tight sun glow), the cloud sheets and the far deck go orange over deep blue. (2) The look file (data/look/map04b_kranich.json): a cold ambient fill (#46526a at 1.8), exposure 1.7 -> 1.8, and a third keep band at hue 26 for saturation 0.55+ only (the sunlit sky and cloud keep their gold; the warm lamps, under that saturation, stay inside the bible's 0.65). (3) The bay: the low sun comes through the aft ramp's seam (one shadowed SpotLight: god rays through the racks in the volumetric fog; zone A goes from 1 to 2 shadowed lights of its 6), dust motes (260 lit GPU particles in the bay), and every dropped rack's release hatch glows from below (a shadowless spot, 14 s); the bay doors are not opened visually for the run (their aisle is walkable floor and opening it would lie to the player; the trap keeps its own doors). (4) The drop: BombRack.drop() now also calls the group bomb_drop_fx (visual only; the state, the flags, the timers and the jams are unchanged, test_kranich_lighting pins it); outside, a casing falls from under the hull with gravity and an aft drift, tumbling nose-down, with a vapour trail and a warm sun glint, into the cloud deck; a live one ends in a violet (Nachtglas) flash far under the clouds; no city is drawn. In the captures the casing and the flash were too small / too far to read at those framings (the owner frames them with `bombrun fx` from closer). (5) The interior: KranichInterior adds 50 shadowless practicals with visible fixtures (battery emergency lamps on from the start, bulkhead lamps the aux bus wakes, the flight deck's instrument glow, the impeller's violet through the engine room's glass), scales the generator's lights per zone (x1.15 .. x1.6 energy), and lays dark plates under the walkway gratings: the walkways' "messed up" floors were the sky showing through the grating as a bright white grid (the outer hull culls from inside, nothing was under them). Luminance (tools/look/luma_stats.py, the kranich_interior set, HUD border cropped): average mean 12.0 -> 25.8, 10th percentile 0.1 -> 1.3, near-black pixels 72.7 % -> 46.5 %; unpowered zones 2.5-15 -> 10-24, powered 4-31 -> 26-45 (the walkways 4.1 / 8.7 -> 37.3 / 39.6). Still under the bible's 30-60 unpowered / 45-80 powered in the big rooms: a further lift is a look-file number, logged for the owner's eye. Other hallway surfaces were checked in the interior frames and nothing else read as broken. (6) Trailer tools (gravelight/ui/trailer_commands.gd): hud off [subs] / hud on (every CanvasLayer but the post stack, the console and the settings overlay, plus the viewmodel's render layer), flyspeed (noclip eased, crouch sinks, real time under timescale), tod, bombrun now|drop|fx; a noclip camera is never thrown off the wing. (7) Perf: not measured this round (the machine was shared with other GPU and headless runs); the added cost is ~50 shadowless omni lights (clustered), one shadowed spot only while golden in the bay, 260 particles in the bay, a per-drop trail; perf_marks --no-plane now also disables KranichSunset and KranichInterior for the A/B.

T-221 b, 2026-11-08, the lead on the trailer frames ("a flat pink-grey milk, a small sun; a stray violet square; the bomber a grey slab; the drop unreadable"). (1) The sunset: the sky shader's golden gradient now puts a hot orange-gold band low round the sun (wider and stronger), a magenta band over it and deep blue overhead; the sun disc is 2.1x at the golden hour with a hot glare core (the environment's glow blooms it) and procedural rays fanning out; the key light is 3.2 with specular 3.2 (rails, seams and the nozzles streak); while golden the depth fog drops to x0.18 and the volumetric to x0.3, and the cloud sheets get crisper shapes (softness 0.1, coverage x0.82) with hot orange tops over violet-blue (#2a1d52) shadows. The U12 grade was flattening it (mono 0.45): the gold keep is widened (hue 24 +- 26 from saturation 0.5) and two keeps added for the sunset sky only at high saturation (magenta 322, the deep blue 228); the cabin's colours sit under those saturations. (2) The stray violet square was MapAmbience's flat-coloured billboard cards (the formation bombers' violet engine glows; St Elmo's fire is the same kind): KranichExterior hides the bombers' cards (their own soft engine glows replace them) and gives every flat MapAmbience card a soft radial falloff (map_ambience.gd itself untouched, it is shared with the other acts). (3) The formation bombers: near-black metal with a rim (the low sun outlines them), off the hull's fill layer, soft warm engine glows in their trailing edge, red / green nav lights; the T04 shot puts a noclip camera in line with the sun so bomber 3 crosses it. (4) The drop: a brighter metal casing, a 6 m sun glint, a thicker longer vapour trail, the violet flash 300 m wide inside the far plane and lighting the cloud sheets violet from under while it lasts; the trailer shots put the camera under the belly (capture_do gained noclip, hud_off and timescale verbs). (5) The trailer set is captured without the HUD; tools/look/kranich_drop_clip.sh records the drop at timescale 0.3 with Godot's movie writer (--fixed-fps 30) and cuts it to an MP4 with ffmpeg.
T-221 b, the framings: the capture camera runs at the owner's own FOV (110 degrees vertical in these runs, ~147 horizontal at 21:9), so every subject reads small in the stills; the trailer is recorded at whatever FOV the owner picks. T04 uses KranichSunset.frame_bomber_sun (a capture / trailer hook that stands the noclip camera 55 m short of bomber 3 on the line from the sun, held 1.15 s against the capture tool's own placement) and capture_do gained a `pos:x,y,z,yaw,pitch` verb so a clip's camera is placed before its action plays.

T-222, 2026-11-08, the owner: "the lighting needs to be improved on the map ... too many dark areas and the textures on some hallways are messed up" (Fernflug Acts I, III and IV; Act II is another worker's: no file of map04b / kranich_* / flight_* touched; branch worktree-agent-a970cfb4812438f4c off claude/fernflug-plane). **"Too dark" as a number.** A headless light survey (gravelight/tools_src/light_survey.gd: the baked navmesh sampled every 2.5 m, the direct light each sample sees at eye height from every visible Omni / Spot with a clear line and the moon, power off and on, plus a corridor flag from eight rays; tools/look/survey_report.py ranks the dark clusters and writes cameras) chose the views; the capture sets map04a/c/d_interior (hallways by hand + the survey's darkest spots, each with the power off and then on) and the u09 room sets were rendered off-screen under the gpu.lock (1600x900 asked, the window came up 3440x1440) and measured with tools/look/luma_stats.py (display luma on the frame without its HUD margins: mean, p5/p10, share under 12). Before: covered rooms and corridors averaged 4-30 of 255 against the bible's 30-60 unpowered / 45-80 powered (the Windkanal lane 5, the Kraftzentrale 18, the canteen 10-14, Groom Lake's hatch 4-6, lab 8, hangar 3-8, wreck 6-15, Mondlicht's store 12-14, hall 9-14, Account 13-16); on High the environment's ambient is replaced by SDFGI, so a room with no lamp in it was black whatever the ambient said. **What was added (lights and fixtures only, all shadowless, no collider):** Act I: 22 battery emergency lamps (caged bulkheads, always on, dim, warm: every hall has a source before the power, the Kraftzentrale's switch is found by one), 7 more canteen lamps, 6 Montage lamps (aisles, covered way), 4 wall lamps down the wind tunnel's lane, 3 more Windkanal lamps, 3 sodium wall lamps on the test stands' back wall, 4 ground-crew sodium floods on the hangar's side walls aimed at the Kranich, the cellar lantern brighter; Act III: 26 emergency lamps (hull, hangar, motor pool, hatch, trench foot, lab, control room, outside over the hangar's and the motor pool's doors) and a lamp over the silo door; on the power the specimen rooms' caged lamps (drawn, unlit until now), 7 more lab tubes, wall lamps down the hatch and along the trench, 5 hangar pendants and 2 floods on the bomber, a console pendant and shaft-side wall lamps in the silo; Act IV: a cold ridge strip and 3 lights down the retention hall, 10 pilaster uplights, the store's aisle strips lit (8 lights), a ring of strips and 4 lights in the Account, strips and lights by both yard airlocks. The look data grew interior exposure zones (LookZones boxes, contract C2; Act II has none): Thalbach halls 2.3 / hangar 2.15 (map 1.7), Groom Lake hull / hangar 2.4, shed 2.3, lab 2.5, hatch 1.75, trench 2.0 (map 1.35), Mondlicht store 2.4, hall + Account 2.5 (map 1.7). LookZones now writes only the Environment values its zones set (keys_of; before, leaving an exposure-only zone wrote back the fog from the entry snapshot, which would undo Groom Lake's dust storm and the director's fog tween; Map 0's inn zone writes the same fog keys as before). **After (power on):** the lane 68, the Kraftzentrale 49, the canteen 50-62, the bunker 49, the Montage 40-43, the hangar 40-44; the hatch 82-93, the trench 46-61, the lab hall 40-45, the silo 39-44, Groom's hangar 28-38, the wreck 32-35; Mondlicht's store 37-66, the hall 27-45, the Account 28-32 (it keeps its black-and-violet). Unpowered: the lane 27, the Kraftzentrale 35, the canteen 30, the hatch 64. Left dark on purpose or out of reach: the window closets the survey also found (zombie side), the motor pool interior seen from its door (17), Groom's specimen rooms (23, a cage lamp each), the Account's floor (deep black between violet rings). Numbers per frame: docs/captures/fernflug/lighting/luma.json; frames in docs/captures/fernflug/lighting/<act>/before|after|pairs (1600 px JPEG; the emergency lamps' volumetric fog share was set to 0 after these frames for the cost, invisible in them). **Hallway textures** (tools: gravelight/tools_src/texture_audit.gd lists stretched UVs, UV-less meshes with UV textures, coplanar overlaps of different materials and big flat surfaces; it found no stretched or UV-less surface on the three acts, the static batches being world-triplanar): Act III the lab's floor and ceiling and the hatch roof were the dry-stone wall texture (rule "Caliche*" -> stone; now fl_floor / fl_concrete), the hatch and trench ramps the procedural "concrete" that smears along a slope (now fl_floor), the lab's and the silo's floors overlap 2 m at the silo door with different materials (both fl_floor now), the lab's north wall F_N and the silo's G_S_W are the same box line (z-fight both faces, 97 m2), the shaft lining fought F_N too (moved to the shaft side), the hangar's and the motor pool's south walls stand on the salt berm's line (47 m2 each side), the trench walls against the salt slab's cut edge, two crossing sets of tyre tracks at one height (lifted); Act IV reception's east wall A_E and the hall's west wall E_W on x 12 (106 m2, white against violet). The walls are fixed by a new opt-in generator table `visual_adjust` (gen_map04_lib.box: the mesh's box moves, the collider keeps the table's box; empty by default, so Act II and the other maps generate exactly as before). Act I had no z-fight over 8 m2 (roof-line trims, pilasters; left). **Layout locked and checked:** tools/look/layout_fingerprint.py hashes every collision / navigation / Gameplay node (resource ids resolved to content, Godot's auto names by sibling order) and every navmesh's arrays: the three regenerated scenes are SAME against the committed ones (850 / 611 / 373 layout nodes, 29 / 20 / 14 navmeshes, 327 / 224 / 122 CollisionShape3D); the interactables' facing runner PASS. **Shared code:** gen_map04_lib.gd gained `visual_adjust` (empty default) and `fade_small_lights()` (called by Acts I, III, IV only: shadowless omnis up to 16 m fade past 42 m); look_zones.gd (above); no shader, MaterialLibrary or LookPost change. **Perf** (perf_marks --breakdown, ultra, RTX 5090, the same spawn, before = the committed scenes swapped in): GPU Act I 4.57 -> 4.98 ms with the lamps' fog share 0.15, 4.57 with 0; Act III 5.56 -> 5.53; Act IV 4.98 -> 4.92 (ViewCells hide other domes' lights); the FPS swing was the CPU (other sessions running). Shadowed lights per zone unchanged (most 1 in the perf census, cap 6). **Trailer views** (sets fernflug_trailer_a/c/d, frames in <act>/trailer): Act I the canteen under its lamps, the wind tunnel lane, the hero view from the watch post; Act III the hatch descent, the LANTERN in its shaft, the lab hall, the wreck on the salt at dawn; Act IV the retention hall's nave, a store aisle, the Account under the shaft light, the Earth over the yard. Left for the owner: the Great Hangar still reads as fog and a grey wing from the floor (the Kranich is a flat 86 m wing; the floods light its edge, not a silhouette); the air raid in a still frame is mostly searchlights: both want a moving camera.

T-223, 2026-11-08, the owner: "the rendering distance is short, theres like this hexagon around the player where assets like grates and other stuff dont load properly". Cause: the generators cut small geometry off at fixed distances, hard pops, regardless of the graphics settings: Werk Thalbach's dressing batches (40 x 40 m cells culled whole at 58 / 90 m from the cell centre, instanced details 45 m, props 35 m, text 30 m; 397 nodes) and every Kranich static batch under 60 m (gratings, rails, fittings) at 80 m (264 nodes, on a 268 m wing): square cells switching off whole leave a polygonal ring round the player. Fix (no scene regenerated): gravelight/world/detail_range.gd scales those plain cut-offs (an end, no begin; only under the generator containers StaticBatches / DressBatches / Dressing) with Level of detail: Low x1 (the authored distances), Medium x1.5, High x2.25, Ultra x3; LOD chains (the C-47's impostors, the forest's mesh pines / cards) keep their pairs. MapRoot applies it at load (prints "[DetailRange] <map>: N cut-offs xF"), GLSettings.apply_visuals re-applies it when the setting changes (the authored distance kept in meta range_base). test_detail_range; smokes map04a, map04a_world, map04b, map04b_world PASS (397 and 264 cut-offs scaled x3 on ultra). Perf: more of the small detail is drawn at High / Ultra (the draw-call cost the cut-offs were saving); Low and Medium keep it close to before.

T-224, 2026-11-09, the owner: "add a third story to the main building ... the bar area needs to look better and the bar needs to be bigger with more space" (Map 1 = the Crossroads Inn, map00). **The attic** (gen_map00 ATT_* / ASTAIR_*, build_attic, _dress_attic, _dress_attic_stairs): the roof space over the guest rooms (x 0..24, z 0..10, floor y 7.1 = the old UpRoof slab 0.3 m thick, 2.8 m to the boarded ceiling), zone UPSTAIRS (no door of its own: the attic is what Door 1 buys you now, with the rooms), reached by a straight flight along the upstairs hall's south wall (foot x 6.4, head x 12.4, 1.75 m wide, 15 treads, a smooth invisible ramp on the nosing line with the treads drawn under it, like the hayloft's) through a well in the attic floor (x 7.3..12.4) railed north and west. Decision: the flight along the hall rather than a dog-leg over the stairwell (the well's second flight would have ended against the south wall) and the well's west edge at x 7.3, not the 2.0 m-headroom line at 8.2: the first bake left a 1 m gap in the flight's navmesh there (Recast wants more than the 1.75 m agent under a slab edge; 2.3 m at 7.3 bakes continuous: smoke_map00's new path checks). Feeds: the stair and two gable windows (W_ATT_W, W_ATT_E at z 4.5) over hoist ledges outside the gables (navmesh islands; the window spawns stand on them like the porch roofs': the spawn offset runs along z for +-X-facing ledges). Buys: the BAR 1800 (north wall, x 18) and the Mk 2 frag 250 (east gable) - Map 0 had no grenade buy - both chalked as hauled up from GRAVELIGHT ONE (machine_lore chalk.bar / chalk.frag). Lights: two lanterns, the stair-head sconce, two bulbs (INN_BULBS; cousins.json lamp_groups.upstairs guts them). The hall's lantern and HallBulb1 moved from z 8 to z 7 and a painting from x 10 to 15.5, out of the flight's line. Outside: the gable 3.1 m higher, the chimney stack to 14.4, half-timber posts and braces up the north face, boarded gable ends, the stair tower's flat roof snowed. Data: bake_config floor "attic" (y 6.9+, cut 8.7; the minimap re-baked under WSL), the inn's LookZones box to y 10.2, DustMotes, wind-howl zones at both windows, Zone_UPSTAIRS_1 up to y 10.3. **The bar** (BAR_* / SHELF_*, _dress_bar): the L of counter moved 1.1 m west (back-bar x 3.5..3.9, side x 7.6..8.4) and grew north to z 2.4; the service walk is 3.7 x 5.1 m (was 3.2 x 5), the floor east of the counter gained 0.6 m to the taproom loop (routes_map00 unchanged). The front moved north to z 7.5..8.3: with it at 8.0..8.8 the Old Cellar walkway (T-120, test_map00_art) lost its navmesh (1.05 m to the stairwell wall after the 0.5 m agent); now 1.55 m. The look: a panelled counter on a plinth with an oak bullnose top and a brass foot rail on brackets, eight stools, the flap on brass straps, two taps over a zinc drip tray, the till and the bell, a cupboard base under three bays of shelving against a mirror, a carved cornice with two brass lamps, the tapped cask on its cradle (BarCask collider) behind the counter's north end, a chalked price board, steins on a rack; the bottle rows (ShatterSet "Bar") on the new boards, Kaethe's notes on the new back board, the wireless on its top board, the taproom ammo box against it at z 6.6, Trudi's and Sobotta's opening routes moved with it (map00_opening.json). T-225, 2026-11-09, the owner: "rework the starting area on map 2" (Map 2 = Kaltenbrunn, map01). THE BAR scored Kaltenbrunn's arrival 1 of 3 (row 22): the squad stood on bare snow in a 40 x 32 m yard. Now they start inside the WASCHKAUE, the miners' changing house built against the yard's south wall (gen_map01 KAUE_*, build_kaue, _dress_kaue: x 4..16, z 26..32, brick, a flat roof at 3.4 m, two 4.4 m doorways either side of a pier; the four spawns at x 7 / 13, z 28.3 / 29.7 facing north at the headframe). The fiction is the real thing: a Kaue is where every miner's clothes went up on a chain while he was below, so the baskets still hanging high are the men still down there; the tag board and the lamp rack T-123 put on the wall are its furniture now, with benches, lockers, the stove (its firelight lights the spawn before the power), the wash trough and the shift board. Outside, the squad's sled with its kit crate from the inn and their trail trodden from the gate down the gatehouse lane. Kept: the headframe loop's 6 m south lane (the Kaue's front is at z 26), the lane west of it with the ground spawn at (3, 29) and the way to the Administration door, every window and door. Accepted: Pylon A's 8 m ring now touches the Kaue's north-east corner (a wall at your back during the holdout; the T-123 prop rule of 8 m is kept for the loose props and the Kaue's benches are judged by their own rules in test_map01_edges: inside its walls, clear of the doorways and 1.5 m from the spawns). The T-123 brazier by the spawn is gone (the stove), the sled took the drum's slot in YARD_PROPS along the wall east of the Kaue; a gooseneck moved from x 12 (inside the Kaue) to 18.6 and two more light the Kaue's front; the yard's ammo box got a lantern. Checks: smoke_map01 / _systems / _world PASS, test_map01_edges (with the new rules), the facing runner.

T-226, 2026-11-09, the owner: "the pack a punch area in map 2 needs work, more details and perk machine down there". **The perk:** Skyhook Sekt (ELEVATION) stands in the Core Vault under the north wall between the F and G ramp doors (34.5, -18, 101.6), chosen over Magpie Mead because its lore is Werk Thalbach's consignment (Act I of Fernflug, where the campaign goes next) and because the vault's floor "lifts" under the heart: a soft-landing perk belongs beside the pit. machine_lore map01 perk.skyhook_sekt (plate, title, text; the Archive row joins through ProfileSave as the others). PERKS is a file-scope table now and **every Kaltenbrunn perk machine's footprint is baked into the navmesh** (build_perk_blocks, MACHINE_FOOTPRINTS as gen_map00's, capped under the agent height): since T-141 only Map 0 did this. Found by the facing runner on the way: Ledger Lager has stood inside the Administration's second filing cabinet (FilingA, z 10.55..12, drawn through the register) since T-141; the cabinet collider and drawing are cut back to z 9..10.45 and the machine is reachable (97 interactables: 70 OK, the 3 UNREACHABLE left are quest pickups on desks, as before). **The detail** (_dress_core_installers, CORE_PROPS colliders): the installers' corner on the east wall south of the Crucible's setting (bench, vice, gauge, tool board, retained-items crates, a hose coil), oil drums, the cable drum by the G door, four brass gauge stands on the pit rim cabled to the cage, conduit from the F door along the north wall (a junction box over the cabinet) and down the east wall to the Crucible's plate, caged lamps on the four pillars and over the cabinet (power), a red emergency lamp at each door, a jib crane with its hook over the apron, salt crust and puddles, luminous guide lines from every door round the pit to the Crucible. Nothing enters the Crucible room's approach (test_crucible_rooms PASS). Checks: smoke_map01 (the Core paths), test_machine_lore, test_map01_edges, smoke_map01_systems (counts one cabinet per plate: 11).

T-227, 2026-11-09, the owner: "make sure all wall weapon buys look good on both maps". No renders on the owner's PC (memory: headless only), so the check is geometric and built into both generators: `_audit_wall_buys()` runs after the dressing and before the batching and refuses to save the scene if anything drawn stands in the 1.8 x 1.2 m window in front of a chalk (out to 1 m) or if no world collider is within 0.1 m behind it. It found three real ones on Map 0: the spare planks leaning by the west window stood in front of the Kar98k (moved south of the window to z 7.3), the hoist ledge's middle bracket hung over the Thompson (brackets at x 20.4 / 23.5 / 24.8 now), and the new attic's rafter feet and the old wardrobe stood in the BAR's window (rafters raised, the wardrobe moved east of the chalk). Kaltenbrunn's nine were clear. Every wall-buy on both maps now has the same lamp: Map 0's BuyLight omni (0.6, 1.8 m) came to Map 1's nine buys, and both maps draw a bracket lamp over each chalk (_dress_lighting / _dress_buy_lamps), so a buy reads before the power too. The facing runner (tools/tests/test_interactable_facing.gd) passes both maps' buys (OK, reachable). Generation prints one WALLBUY_AUDIT line per buy. Left for the owner: a look at the attic, the bar, the Kaue and the Core in a rendered run (morning/NEEDS_DEVIN.md).

T-228, 2026-11-09, the owner, after looking at T-224..T-227 rendered: "touch up everything you added, it looks incomplete, stairs look bad and new building on second map looks bad". First the look: `tools/wsl_godot.sh` runs the capture tool under WSL + Xvfb (llvmpipe, invisible, silent), so the same cameras the owner was pointed at were rendered here (morning/screenshots/u05/touchup*, u10_map01/touchup*), and the Compatibility renderer's light caps (32 per frame, 8 per mesh: whole lamp-lit rooms went black that Forward+ lights fine) are lifted for those runs by a git-ignored `override.cfg`. **The attic flight** (`_dress_attic_stairs`): the treads were 5 cm boards on 3 cm risers over a hidden collider, the quarter-metre under-stair panels stood 0.5 m apart under a sloped string (daylight between them), and the under-stair's east end, a 3 m face toward the hall, was never drawn at all: the hall east of the flight looked straight into the cavity. Now: solid riser blocks with a tread board and nosing each, a footboard where the ramp leaves the floor, a closed string with 0.25 m panels whose tops tuck behind it, the cupboard door framed, the east end a wainscoted wall floor to ceiling with skirting, chair rail and cornice, fascia boards with a nosing over the attic floor's raw slab faces round the well, the wainscot and a sconce following the flight up the south wall, newels at every corner. The hall runner stops at the flight (it ran under it) and the x 10 ceiling beam stops at the well (it crossed the open well at head height). **The attic's light:** the renders read black; the three lamps are stronger and three more hang (over the well, mid-room, the sconce mid-flight; `cousins.json` guts them with the upstairs). **The Waschkaue** (`_dress_kaue`, `build_kaue`): from the yard before the power it was a black brick box with two black holes, flat-roofed. Now a lean-to roof against the yard wall (zinc sheets under snow on rafters with tails, a fascia, a gutter and two downpipes, barge boards, brick wedges closing the gables under the slope, the stove's brick stack through it; `_kaue_roof_y`, `_wedge_z`; the flat collider slab stays and is hidden), a plinth, corner pilasters, one lintel band, a string course, the name on a board over the pier, steel sliding doors slid back over the pier on their rail with hangers and wheels, a lantern on a bracket at each corner, the night shift's brazier in front of the pier (`NavBlock_KaueBrazier`, hidden, baked: the loop's lane loses 0.7 m at one point), a lit barred window in each gable end, locker numbers, duckboards, wire baskets instead of rusty drums, the boarded underside of the lean-to on its rafters inside. The two front goosenecks sit on the lintel band's face. Checks: both generators save with WALLBUY_AUDIT clear (7 + 9), the facing runner unchanged (Map 0 63 OK; Map 1 70 OK, the 3 desk pickups as before), probe_invisible_walls unchanged, test_map00 (60) / test_map01 (24) / test_map01_edges / test_capture_cams PASS, smokes map01 / map01_systems / map01_world / map00 / map00_alive / map00_cellar_bay / map00_loop PASS. Cameras added for the owner: u05 attic_flight, attic_well; u10_map01 A_kaue_corner.

T-229, 2026-11-09, the owner: "include a sniper tower with a zipline in map one, make sure ladders work on all maps". **The tower** (Map 0, the Carters' Yard's south-west corner; `gen_map00 TOWER_*`, `_dress_tower`): a timber Hochsitz, four legs with cross braces and ring beams, a 3.8 m deck 6.2 m up behind a boarded parapet on three collider bars (closed all round: the climbs and the zombies' link carry over it, nobody walks off), a little gable roof with a lantern under it, sandbags on the field side, a cartridge crate, the ladder up its north face. The deck is navmesh (a YARD zone volume covers it; it had to be 3.8 m: inside the rail the eroded square must keep over the 4 m2 the Inn's bake keeps for islands, T-106, or the deck is dropped like a table top). The zombies climb the ladder by `Climb_Tower` (map00.json climb_links, one way up: a drop link walks them down through the air; stranded ones are the director's stuck recycling), so the deck is a good spot, not a safe one. **The zip line** (ZipLine, `gravelight/world/zip_line.gd`; map00.json ziplines): from a bracket on the tower's north-east roof post (8.2 m) down to a 2.9 m post by the stable's south wall beside the yard door's lamp (the Thompson's audit window stays clear), 14 m, 7.5 m/s, the rider 2 m under the cable's sag; holding Use at the trolley hangs the player and runs them down, the trolley travelling with them and winding back; the landing is navmesh. **Ladders** (LadderClimb, `gravelight/world/ladder_climb.gd`, data-driven: map json "ladders", spawned by MapRoot with the climb links; the radar tower's T-157 TowerLadder is a subclass now): hold Use at the foot, the body is carried up the ladder line to `to`, a second node at the head carries it down; `zone` gates a ladder on a zone being open. Every drawn ladder on every map was audited with `probe_ladder_tops.gd` (a map's ladders', zip lines' and climb links' ends against the baked polygons, no server needed) and sorted: **works** when both ends are floor a player and a zombie can stand on, **shut** when the drawing leads nowhere (a guard plate with a padlock, a chain with a GESPERRT tag, or the ladder stowed on hooks): Map 0: the Old Cellar's ladder under the bar works (the taproom floor is cut round a 0.9 m hatch, HATCH_*, a Trapdoor leaf (`trapdoor.gd`) bolted until the Old Cellar opens then thrown back flat; both climbs and the zombies' link `Climb_CellarHatch` wait for the zone; the hall runner's T-120 walkway and Trudi's route are clear of it); the attic's ladder to the shut roof hatch is stowed on its hooks (the hatch leads onto the roof slab's bake island, not a place). Map 2: U-1047's engine room has an escape hatch now (the deck aft is cut, a coaming, the lid thrown back, NOTAUSSTIEG; `EngineHatch_Up/Down`, link `Ladder_EngineHatch`, zone E_UBOAT); the crane leg's 9 m ladder gets a locked guard (the cab is no floor), both U-boats' bridge ladders a GESPERRT chain (the bridge inside the fairing would stand in T-180's fitted hull), the landing stages' and the lower pens' ladders go down into the water and stay as they are. Thalbach: the two jigs' four ladders hang stowed on the jig ends (their 0.7 m plank decks are no floor), the signal post's ladder has a railway anti-climb guard, the hung glass office's ladder a chain under its own "Zutritt nur mit Erlaubnis". Kaltenbrunn and Acts II-IV draw no ladders (the Kranich's wing hatch and the radar tower work as before). Checks: `smoke_ladders` (both maps: every climb ends at its `to` on the navmesh, the body moves again, the hatch is bolted then opens with the zone, the links exist), the facing runner unchanged (Map 0 63 OK, Map 2 the two pen-hall doors as documented, Thalbach 70 OK), probe_invisible_walls unchanged on all three, test_map00 (NAV_FP re-set), test_map02, test_map04a, test_capture_cams PASS; smokes: smoke_ladders, map00, map00_alive, map00_cellar_bay, map00_loop (failed once under load, PASS alone), inn_opening_paths, inn_survivors, story_props, interact, cousins, map02 (failed twice under load with the rounds running 3x slow, PASS alone in 142 s), map02_art (after the hatch's deck pieces were tagged to draw from the hall), map02_world, map04a, map04a_world PASS; letzte_fahrt hit the runner's 20-minute cap as before (no result, no error). Cameras for the owner: u05 yard_tower, tower_deck, bar_hatch.

T-230, 2026-11-09, the owner after T-229: "zip line needs actual animation, also first room has a random piece of wood. ladder needs animation". **The carried body** (Player.begin_carry / end_carry): a ladder or a zip line now moves the body itself every physics frame instead of tweening it with physics off. The player's movement, interaction, fire, ADS and melee are off while carried, the mouse stays theirs (the controller clamps the yaw to the ladder or the line), and the camera takes the controller's extras: carry_head (a position offset), carry_pitch, carry_roll, carry_fov. **The hands** (WeaponHolder.set_hands &"climb" / &"hang"): the gun goes away and the two IK arms are posed to hand_l / hand_r in viewmodel space from shoulders lifted by hands_shoulder (the same two-bone arms, the same sleeves; the fists are refit to a 19 mm rung); back on &"" the gun rises as after a swap. **The ladder climb** (LadderClimb, PACE 1.4 m/s, RUNG 0.3): mount 0.45 s (onto the ladder's `line`, 0.4 m off the rungs, turning to `face`; a down climb steps out over the edge), the rungs (the body rises in pulls, PULSE 0.7: slow at each rung, quick between; the view bobs 3 cm and rolls 1.4 degrees toward the pulling hand; the hands grip the real rungs (rung0 + n * 0.3, 0.4 m ahead of the body's line, placed through WeaponHolder.vm_target, which maps a world point into viewmodel space along the same ray): each holds its rung while it slides down past the eye to 0.2 under it, then reaches in 0.22 s for the rung nearest 0.17 over it, the two a rung apart so they alternate; a rung sound per rung, wood or metal by `surface`), dismount 0.5 s (up and over the edge onto `to`, the gun back at half way, a step's landing). The json rows gained `line`, `face`, `surface` (the tower's two, the cellar hatch's two, U-1047's two; the Top ladder faces the tower). The radar tower's TowerLadder keeps its 3.0 s (T-157, a gameplay timing) and inherits the animation. **The zip line ride** (ZipLine): mount 0.4 s (under the anchor, half a metre back onto the deck, turning down the line, the hands up to the bar at shoulder height), then the rider steps off: the hang settles from the standing reach to `hang` (2.7 now: the feet under the cable with the arms up; the bar is 0.56 under it) on a stiff spring, the trolley rolls under gravity down the cable's slope (a = g sin slope - drag v^2, capped at `speed` 7.5, a bumper over the last 1.8 m: the 15 m line takes 3.6 s, not 2.0), the body hangs from the bar as a pendulum (rod = hang - 0.56; the first cut pivoted at the cable, which put the bar a metre ahead of the eye) lagging the trolley's pull (the felt acceleration is capped at BRAKE 5 m/s^2 so the cap and the bumper never jolt the swing) with a side sway that dies down, the trolley's wheels spin, the view rolls with the sway and pitches with the lag, the FOV opens 5 degrees and a wind loop rises with the speed; at the post the hands let go, the body drops to `to` with a landing. **The hanging arms** come in from the frame's sides (HANG_SHOULDER_*, the upper arms hidden: the real shoulders are behind the eye and an upper arm through the lens smears the frame), the hands on the real bar (bar_grips). **The capture tool** takes `use` (a ladder or zip line by name) and `shot_after_s`, and `bright` (a flat work light for a night frame, put back after), so a frame mid-climb or mid-ride is a camera like any other: u05 ride_climb, ride_zip, ride_zip_up, ride_zip_late. Two headless probes (the hands' rig targets against the rungs; the ride's body, camera and centre ray over time) found the two geometry mistakes the renders only hinted at; both are fixed above. **The random piece of wood**: the Inn's first room is the taproom; renders of its seven cameras under WSL showed the fire poker, a bare 10 cm iron block standing on the floor a step from the hearth, reading as a stray stick of wood. It is a set of fire irons now (a stand on the hearthstone against the left jamb with the poker, tongs and brush on its ring, the bellows leant on the jamb). Props has a probe for this (GL_PROBE_BOX="x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1" set when a generator runs prints every block / obox / cyl touching the box with its material and group): it pinned the poker in one run. The Waschkaue (Map 1's first room) was checked too: the one odd thing in its renders, a tan rectangle at the top of the right doorway seen from the back wall, is a lit window of the yard building north-east (x 15, 3.9 m up, on that sight line; two closer doorway cameras, u10_map01 A_kaue_door_r / A_kaue_door_r2, show nothing there); the probe (extended to the generators' geometry boxes, Props.probe_geo) lists nothing wooden in the corridor. Checks: smoke_ladders (73 checks: every climb and the ride end at `to` on the navmesh; mid-way the body is carried, between the ends, on the ladder's line, the hands on the rungs / the bar, the gun back after), smoke_ladders PASS (73 checks, four runs through the iterations), smoke_map04c_world PASS (the radar tower's climb, 3.0 s + the mount and dismount, still inside the smoke's 4.2 s wait), unit test_ladders_data 3, test_capture_cams 3, test_world_objects 16, test_map00 60, test_map02 18, test_map04c 8 PASS, check_scripts 877 scripts 0 failed; map00 regenerated (598 collision boxes, unchanged; the fire irons are dressing); renders here under WSL: morning/screenshots/u05/anim4 (ride_zip: the yard below the raised arms, the stable wall ahead; ride_zip_up: the arms up to the trolley), anim3 (ride_climb: the right glove on the lower rung), anim1 (taproom_hearth: the fire irons), u10_map01/anim1 (the Kaue doorway cameras).
T-232, 2026-11-10/11, the boss rework, W2: **PAUL, THE FOREMAN and the Screamer** on the T-231 kit (models, effects, battles). PAUL gains the Guard ("protect my guys": at BERSERK he stands over a downed player's body and slams whoever comes near, in front of him only, so a teammate can revive from the far side), the yard's floods dying under his roar, crack decals, a breath clock; the Foreman is rebuilt on `mat_ex` surfaces with a real dragging chain, sway strips for the coat's hem, a spot-light headlamp, and puts the gallery lamps out when ENRAGED; the Screamer's scream punches the dread layer. The detail, per boss, is `docs/boss_rework/w2_kaltenbrunn.md`. The worker's session ended before it verified: the lead committed its tree, merged it and ran the checks (test_boss_rework_w2, test_paul, smoke_map01_systems PASS; smoke_paul: see T-236 b).
T-233, 2026-11-10/11, the boss rework, W3: **THE SLENDER and DER KIELHOLER** on the kit. The Slender's coat and scarf sway, his eyes glow, the lantern swings over its pool; the Mirroring step, the whisper, End of Shift through the kit (the lights die and gutter, the fuse boxes spark); the Kielholer drips after surfacing, drags a real chain, wears weed and rope strips, sinks with the dock lamps dipping, dunks a player held at the water's edge (1.2 s at the edge, 1.0 s under, 20 damage once) and surfaces quicker at high and spring tide (6-10 s -> 3.6-6 s). Detail and numbers: `docs/boss_rework/w3_totenhafen.md`. Nothing of this worker's was committed when the session ended: the lead committed the tree as it stood and verified after the merge (smoke_slender, smoke_kielholer PASS after two smoke repairs: a 0.05 s timer given two physics frames, and the key check racing the held player's auto-collect; test_slender 16, test_kielholer 12, test_boss_rework_w3 PASS).
T-234, 2026-11-10/11, the boss rework, W4: **the Fernflug four** (der Kranführer, der Bordwart, the Test Pilot, the Account Manager) on the kit: `docs/boss_rework/w4_fernflug.md`, MAP4_FERNFLUG 3.8 / 4.7 / 5.7 / 6.7. smoke_act_bosses PASS (71 checks), test_fernflug_rework 6.
T-235, 2026-11-10/11, the boss rework, W1: **the Cousins**: the doll register pushed (glaze, glass eyes, cracks that spread with damage), the kit on every cousin, the last cousin hunting in the dark: `docs/boss_rework/w1_cousins.md`, HOMAGE_BOSSES section 4. smoke_cousins PASS (90 checks), test_homage 34.
T-231 b, 2026-11-11, **the kit's review** (the owner ran /code-review on the kit: ten findings, all real; fixed after the four merges, commit 3ad95961). Lights: `lights_restore` forgot the lamps at once, so nothing eased and a dim during the restore took the dimmed energies as the new base: the lamps now stay captured while they ease; a boss freed while it holds lamps (`_vanish` frees it in the same call) hands the ease to a tween of the tree (`_exit_tree`); a gutter that ends writes the plain level back; two bosses on one lamp share its true base through a static table and the last holder gives it back; a lamp freed mid-fight no longer raises a script error (W4's 266 errors). Telegraph: HomageBoss leaves the light to `fx.tele()` while its shape runs (`tele_active`); the weak-point flash never cuts a telegraph and restores the light's colour. Hits: throttled per boss (70 ms; the weak point 140 ms) with the process material cached; blood mist only with Gore on full; burn ticks and world damage are not hits; a weak point is the limb's own multiplier, not a state window's; a client's own hit on a puppet boss shows the feedback and a puppet's death plays the death burst; the flesh thud no longer plays under a non-flesh surface (W1's note). Dread: two bosses of one key report apart (`report_id`). An unknown emitter kind is dust with a warning. `strips()` names each group by its seed (a second "Strips" under one bone is renamed by the engine and name searches miss it: the two smokes counted 2 and 3 groups of 5). test_boss_fx +9. Decided against: restoring the lamps from `death_burst` (the Cousins' round and the Slender choreograph their own relight; the `_exit_tree` hand-off is the net). Open: the sixteen extra sound ids the four workers asked for (stand-ins play; listed at the end of each `docs/boss_rework/w*.md`).
T-236, 2026-11-11, the owner: "go through and make the movement feel better." A worker measured the real player on a bare arena through its input actions (`smoke_move_feel`) and ranked what it found; the report with the before / after table is `docs/u15/t236_movement.md`. (1) Anything 11-50 cm high stopped the player dead (the capsule rolls over about 10 cm, mantles start at 50): a **step assist** walks up and down steps to 40 cm with the eye eased (`movement.json` "step"; off for bots and companions, whose paths were tuned on the plain slide). (2) Jumps were eaten at ledges and landings: **coyote time** 0.1 s and a **jump buffer** 0.12 s. (3) Strafe reversals were soft: `turn_boost` 1.6 (15 -> 12 ticks to 95 % the other way). (4) Crouch eye height, the bob phase and the slide blend stepped at 60 Hz on a fast display: blended between ticks like the body. Walk 4.5 and sprint 6.8 unchanged, so the chase balance holds. A fall-gravity switch exists but ships at 1.0 (falling faster would shorten the sprint jump 4.4 -> 3.9 m: the owner's call). The worker's worktree was cut from master, not from this branch, so player.gd was merged by hand with T-230's carried body (the carry's head / pitch / roll / FOV extras beside the step offset; `begin_carry` clears the step offset); after the merge: test_move_feel 11, test_ladders 3, test_player 21, test_reload_sprint 10, smoke_move_feel, smoke_ladders, smoke_feel PASS.
T-236 b, 2026-11-12, **PAUL wedged in the Kaue** (found by smoke_paul after the W2 merge: the Guard began but he never closed the last 3 m; his fall speed had built to -277 at y 0.2). The Waschkaue's benches stopped 0.95 m short of the back wall and left a 0.55 m pocket in each back corner; he was resting on a bench end. The benches run to the wall now (T-239). Left: `Zombie._apply_gravity` lets velocity.y build without limit while a body is off a floor, and PAUL reads `is_on_floor()` false at y 0.2 on open ground; both are in zombie.gd, which the zombie overhaul (codex/zombie-overhaul) owns.
T-237, 2026-11-11/12, the owner: "while theres bots the game gets laggy, find a way to mitigate this". Measured headless at a fixed 60 (`perf_marks --hitch`, `tools/bot_perf.sh`, the new `BotProf`): the squad adds 1.7-3.3 ms to a 5-7 ms frame, and the bots' own code hitched on sound files, voice lines and weapon models loaded on the frame that first used them (2-45 ms each), on several brains thinking on one tick, and on the errand list asking the navmesh about every machine each second. Fixed: one think per tick (`BotBrain.think_slot`, a brain waits 0.1 s at most), a 4 s reachability cache dropped when a door or zone opens, `StreamWarm` loading the sound bank, squad voices and weapon models on loader threads (off on the web; about 100 MB + 15-20 MB a voice kept), AI bodies posed every tick only in view within 15 m (every 2nd to 40 m, every 4th out of view), the last four gun models kept, the squad's player list built once a frame, and the planner model asked far less often (`ask_gap_s` 8, `reask_every_s` 20: the owner's log showed gemma3:4b answering in about 1 s every 4 s on the card the game draws with). Result, honestly: frames over 16.7 ms on the Inn 31 -> 11 of 1800 and none over 20 ms (9 before), the brains 10-16 % cheaper, the mean frame 0-3 %. The GPU side (the model calls most of all) is unmeasured here. Left with numbers in `docs/u15/t237_bot_perf.md`: an explosion in a pack costs 9-35 ms and `zombie_director` 39-59 ms on single ticks (zombie code, not touched), a relief squad built in one frame (20-40 ms), about 0.4 ms a bot a tick of steady cost. `CZ_T237_OFF=1` switches the pass off for an A/B. The worktree was cut from master; perf_marks merged by hand.
T-238, 2026-11-11/12, the owner: "a final pass on map 1 and 2 ... the cellar is too cluttered, the haystacks feel half baked in the barn" (the Inn, map00). The crowded cellar in the renders is the Helvetia vault: floor-standing things 18 -> 8 (the crates between the Crucible and the lever, the north-east heap, the shelves in the machines' back walks, three of four puddles gone; the lever's approach 1.8 -> 2.6 m); the Old Cellar's stool, crate and three kegs out of the walks. Hay: `props_hay.gd` (bevelled bales with twine, course-laid stacks that fill their colliders, a mound with wisps, strewn straw, a pitchfork); the five walk-through bales in the stable's north-door lane became a solid stack on the north wall (`StableBales`); the loft stack fills `HayBales` (cover colliders unmoved). Taproom crates against the east wall and solid, the till modelled, the yard's loose things out of the open ground. Not changed: the twelve machines of the vault and its bay (positions were out of bounds for the pass; if the owner still finds it crowded, moving the south row is the lever). NAV_FP re-set, minimap re-baked, 29 cameras (u05, labels u15_before / u15_after). `docs/u15/t238_inn_pass.md`.
T-239, 2026-11-11/12, the same ask on Kaltenbrunn (map01). The map was not cluttered; most floor props had no collider (ore carts, drums, desks, the lab's gurneys, the pylons): 65 made solid and baked (`SOLID_PROPS`, `SOLID_ROUND`), each against a wall or flush with its neighbour. Drawn wrong and fixed: every pine piece rendered bright green (`data/look/_materials.json` tex_fix is keyed by scene sub-resource ids, which change on regeneration: the generator now names noise textures by content, `_name_noise_textures`; **map00 and map02 have the same exposure**; the Inn's T-238 renders show brown timber), sixteen door trims narrower than their doorways, the Kaue plinth and the lab's dado rail across doorways, the Admin stairs' bare black strings (a panelled boxed staircase with a shift board, a plan, a clock and a notice board on its lobby face). Snags: the Hauptschalter dais has a ramp skirt, body-sized slots closed. Lanterns in eight places dark before the power. `probe_snags` is the new headless tool (capsule sweeps + art with no collider). MapArtAudit 28 -> 41 (low prop colliders a little larger than their art, none in a lane). Minimap re-baked. `docs/u15/t239_kaltenbrunn_pass.md`.
T-240, 2026-11-11/12, the owner: "add a rideable horse in map 1 ... boss zombie horses on round 20 and 30". **The horse**: a model, rig and gaits in code (`gravelight/world/horse/`: walk, trot, gallop; a bay Noriker with saddle and bridle), tied at a rail in the Carters' Yard east of the Thompson chalk, spawned from `map00.json` "horses" by MapRoot (gen_map00 untouched; its colliders on the map-edge layer so nothing baked changes). Hold Use to mount (the carried body, T-230), steer with move, fire from the saddle at a trot or slower (`Player.carry_fire`), both hands on the reins at a gallop; zombies ignore it, it cannot die, it shies near the dead, a gallop through ordinary zombies flings them at the cost of the gallop, a downed or boss-struck rider is unseated, bots never ride. **Das Totenross** (round 20) and **der Fuhrmann** (round 30, the dead carter in the saddle with a whip, thrown at half health): `horse_boss.gd` on HomageBoss and the kit, run by `HorseRound` (a HomageEncounter: the round reserved, 60 % of the horde, a 300 s fail-safe after which it returns two rounds later), data `"round_bosses"` + `"round_boss_arena"` per map: on for the Inn (YARD, GROUNDS, STABLE) and Kaltenbrunn's surface (A_MINEHEAD, B_RAILYARD); players who go where a horse does not fit are besieged, not followed. Charge 1.4 s tell (a wall staggers it 2.6 s at x1.5), stomp 0.9 s, kick 0.5 s, trample of the knocked-down that a teammate breaks. 46,000 / 72,000 health solo (between PAUL's 30,000 and the Foreman's 50,000 / 75,000); no hit downs a healthy player. Decided: one horse with a rider at 30, not a pair; the encounter precedent, not "a third into the spawns". Known: a co-op client sees the framework's generic puppet where the horse is (the limit every homage boss has); whether bots leave a charge lane is unmeasured (the boss posts its lanes to the squad's danger map). The worker ran out of usage credits during its unit tests; the lead committed its last edits, merged and verified. `docs/u15/t240_horses.md`.
T-240 b, 2026-11-12, **the full smoke run after update 15** (101 smokes on the final code) found two bugs older than the update. The Inn's chimney: `MapAmbience` looked for the stack's smoke emitter within 1.5 m of a fixed point (y 11.6), and the stack rose 3 m with the attic (T-224), so the smoke never stopped when the hearth died; it is found by its column now (`chimney.stack_xz`, the highest emitter over it). Totenhafen: a Screamer on a climb link fell out of the world (y -56,530 when the smoke looked) and, as the round's last body, held round 33 open for good; `Screamer._physics_process` overrides the base and never had its fall guard (the base recycles below y -30): it is put down there now (`kill_silently`, so the round's count stays right). Why the link drops it is not found; the zombie overhaul's branch is where zombie movement is being worked on.

T-241, 2026-11-12, the owner's fifteen screenshots ("missing textures, no space in hallway. stair way has no space, area between bar no space, wall guns all fucked up"). Fixed: **the upstairs hall** (map00) is 5 m deep (the guest rooms' wall `HALL_Z0` at z 5, it was 6; rooms 5 m deep, Bed3, the two wardrobes, the cousins' pegs, drawings, stains, lamps and sconces moved with it; Room 1's wardrobe stands on its east wall now, clear of the MP40 chalk), **the attic flight** is 2.1 m wide (`ASTAIR_Z0` 7.75, was 1.75 m), so 2.6 m of the hall stays clear beside it; **the bar** front moved 0.7 m north (`BAR_Z1` 6.8): the walkway to the Old Cellar door is 2.25 m (was 1.55), the stein rack's hooks follow its length, the taproom ammo box keeps to the shorter service walk, and the cellar hatch's ladder lands (and the climb link and the way down start) on the open floor west of the hatch at (5.45, 5.6) (its old end, 0.35 m from the moved counter, was off the navmesh: smoke_ladders). Navmesh re-baked (test_map00_art NAV_FP re-set), minimap re-baked through WSL (`tools/wsl_godot.sh ... bake_minimap.tscn`; tools/bake_minimap.sh opens a window). **Wall-buys** (every map): build_chalk lays the outline from the gun's grip, so a Kar98k's ran from -0.21 to +0.90 m on a ±0.65 m board and the mounted gun hung off its end; the chalk is capped at 1.05 m and centred on the board (`WallBuy.CHALK_MAX_LEN`). **The white smears on the extra people** (Ebert, the courier, the deserter): the in-world map mark under each (a note mark, `WorldMarks`) is a Decal 2.4 m tall, which painted the chalk X down the person standing on it; it reaches 0.8 m now, fades on non-floor faces (`normal_fade` 0.6) and leaves out a new render layer the people's bodies draw on (`PersonRig.CHAR_LAYER`, render layer 18), which the maps' other floor decals (`cull_mask = 1`) skip too. Compatibility (the WSL renders) draws no decals, so test_owner_screenshot_fixes pins it. **The palette guns** (the M1911, MP40 and the rest of the OpenGameArt set): flat-shaded low poly, so the weapon shader's facet-edge wear (fwidth of the normal) lit every triangle edge as bright steel and the mottle read as a broken texture, worst on the owner's nickel M1911 finish; on palette guns the facet term is 0.18 of itself, the scratches half, the pitting bump 0.55 (the PBR guns unchanged). Not fixed, asked: the "box of sky" at the end of a Kaltenbrunn mine tunnel (the H corridor, the F/G/H ramps and their ends were rendered and probed: every wall drawn and solid, nothing culled); the beige panel by Ironhide Stout (most likely Second Wind's plain white enamel side); the arms are still flat untextured mitts and the low-poly guns stay low poly (better models are a download for the owner, as the three hero guns were). Checks: check_scripts 909 / 0 failed; GUT map00 66/66, mapdata, mark 41/41, people_extra, squad, jakub, the new test_owner_screenshot_fixes 3/3; smokes map00, map00_alive, map00_cellar_bay, people_extra, inn_survivors, ladders, cousins PASS; smoke_npc_walls FAILS as before this work (a bot pressed behind the bar at (4.9, 6.4), the spot the last full run named). Renders: morning/screenshots/u16_fix/{before,after1,vault,vault2,vm_old,vm_new}, u16_fix_m01/{before,probe}.

T-241 b, 2026-11-12, the owner: "fix this" (the three things T-241 left open). **The box of sky** (Kaltenbrunn): found with a scan, not a guess: from every walkable point underground, rays against a trimesh copy of everything DRAWN; where a ray escapes to the sky with a ceiling overhead, there is an opening. The H ramp's roof (DY + 3) is 5.5 m above the corridor's (CY + 3.5) and nothing closed the step where they meet (z 112): from the ramp and the corridor's east end you looked over the corridor's roof into the void, the sky dome behind (`RampHHead`, a rock header wall, now fills it); the F ramp's foot, 1.5 m wider than the Core's west wall, left a slit (`RampFFoot`). Left: single grazing rays through seams, and ledges outside the lift shaft nobody reaches. No collider anywhere underground lacks its drawn face (the scan's second mode). **The beige panel** by Ironhide Stout was each perk machine's FinePrintPanel (the readable terms on the back): one flat-coloured box held off the machine with nothing; a riveted brass plate now (the library's brass, a raised border, a rule under the heading, iron straps back to the cabinet), its prompt and hold unchanged. Second Wind's bare sides got framed, riveted panels and a maker's plate, its back the vent louvres and Ammer's slip. **The hands**: `Arms` draws every surface of a survivor's arms GLB with assets/materials/shaders/arms.gdshader (colour, roughness and metal kept; the ambientCG fabric on sleeves, cuffs and knitted or work gloves, the ambientCG leather on leather gloves, palms and straps, a pore grain on skin, triplanar in object space with the node's scale taken out; GLSettings "plain_arms" keeps the GLB's own). **The low-poly guns**: all 13 OpenGameArt models were flat-shaded (a normal per triangle); tools_src/blender/smooth_palette_guns.py welds them, shades smooth with sharp edges at 35 deg, adds a 0.7 mm one-segment angle-limited bevel with hardened normals and weighted normals, part nodes and origins untouched (tools_src/blender/glb_relink_images.py points the textures back at the palette PNGs); 4.6k..28k triangles each (MG42 the most), models.json's recorded counts updated, each SOURCE.txt notes the change (CC-BY: modified, credited). New, sharper models still need the owner's Sketchfab downloads. Checks: GUT weapon 104/104, model 11/11, map01 26/26, mapdata, perk 68/68, test_owner_screenshot_fixes 6/6; renders u16_fix_m01/after (the ramp down: a tunnel with a wall at its end where the void was), u16_fix/vm_smooth, vm_smooth_lit, vault3/vault4.

T-242, 2026-11-13, **a review of the zombie overhaul** (the owner ran /code-review on it, then: "fix what u think"). **The ragdoll cap froze falling bodies**: the overhaul changed a full budget (ragdoll.max_live 6) from "the new death keeps its clip" to "the oldest freezes and the new death ragdolls". But settled bodies are frozen and pruned at settle_freeze_seconds (3 s), so a full budget only happens in a burst (a grenade into a horde), and the oldest was then a fraction of a second into its fall: deaths 7 and 8 froze deaths 1 and 2 upright or in the air until they dissolved. Now the oldest is evicted only past `ragdoll.evict_after_seconds` (1.5 s, about the time a body takes to land); while every live body is fresher, the new death plays its recorded clip to the floor (the pre-overhaul fallback; with the mocap deaths that fall now carries its root motion). test_zombie_ragdoll_cap (fails on the old code, 2/2 on the new) and tools/zombie_ragdoll_check (burst: 2 denied, none frozen; then a landed body gives way). Also: ZombieClips reads the command line once (`_user_flag`; mocap_enabled / recorded_turns_enabled ran per zombie per tick and allocated the argument list each call), `_can_recorded_turn` uses const arrays. Not changed, for the zombie branch's next art pass: the zombie kit shader's triplanar weights come from per-triangle rest-space derivatives (stable under skinning, but they can facet on curved low-poly cloth: look at a close-up in the game's light); ZombieBody's second mesh builder still gives zombies the plain kit material. Checks: check_scripts 911 / 0 failed; GUT -gselect=zombie 52/52 (4,631 asserts); zombie_ragdoll_check PASS; smoke_zombie_anim PASS.

T-242 b, 2026-11-14, the owner: "ok fix them whats left!" (the two items T-242 left). **The zombie kit's triplanar weights** now come from a rest-pose normal packed into the unused CUSTOM1.w of every kit vertex (`ZombieBody.pack_rest_normal`: octahedral, 12 bits a component, +1 so 0 means none; an integer under 2^24, exact in the float32 attribute; the kit shader decodes it in vertex()). Smooth across faces and fixed to the body under skinning (the skinned NORMAL would make the blend swim), weights `pow(abs(n), 4)`. Meshes without it (people, who keep zombie_pbr off) take the old per-triangle rest-position derivative. Severed pieces copy CUSTOM1 whole, so a sleeve keeps it. **The procedural fallback builder** (`_skin_parts`, build_skinned's path when a GLB is missing; zombies only) packs the normal too and uses `kit_material(true)`. Renders (WSL Xvfb, Compatibility, the gallery's --material-closeup, soldier + sailor, mco_idle): docs/captures/after/mocap/materials/rest_normal/ (left before, right after). At that distance neither shows obvious facets; the change is a small shift in the cloth grain (max 10/255), no shader errors. Checks: test_zombie_rest_normal 4/4 (round trip under 1 deg, every GLB and procedural kit vertex carries its own normal, the zombie material); GUT zombie 56/56 (8,666 asserts), people 28/28; check_scripts 912 / 0 failed.

T-243, 2026-11-14, the owner: "make sure the player is able to see other players score without going into score board, also make the menu better and more scary". **The squad panel shows each teammate's Scrip again** (reverses that part of T-183, where the owner had asked for no scores on it): in gold in its own column after the name, as wide as the squad's biggest balance so the state words and caps line up, dimmed with the row while they are out; companions have no account and show nothing; kills stay on the scoreboard. `CoopHud._row` carries `scrip` (-1 for a companion), `SquadPanel.scrip_text / scrip_w`. **The main menu's dread** (MenuScene + the new MenuDread): *the Watcher*, a thin soldier zombie standing on the camera's side of the fence, facing the camera on a barely-breathing idle, that only ever moves while the lights are out: four steps from 8.4 m to 4.2 m (`WATCHER_STEPS`), then gone for two beats, then back far off; not counted with the walkers and tearers (the census and its budgets unchanged). *Dread beats* every 22-38 s (the first after 10 s): the inn's window glow, the window spill and the barrel fire stutter out, a whisper, and at the darkest moment the Watcher steps (a far groan); `MenuScene.dread_dark` drives MenuDread's dim of the frame. The flicker follows the Camera Sway comfort setting (`DreadFx.picture_k`): full three stutters, reduced one dip, off one slow 1.4 s dim to 60 % (never a strobe), and no beats while a page (Settings, Muster...) is open. *MenuDread*: a blood-rimmed oval vignette swelling on a 52 bpm double-thump heartbeat, under the menu's text, one pass with no screen read-back (fine on the web). *The menu*: the focused link's plate and the column rule in dried blood (`MainMenu.BLOOD`; the wordmark keeps the Marke violet), the plate throbbing with the heartbeat; the shade tinted red-black; the tagline types a new line every 9 s ("Silesia, winter 1944. The dead are keeping count." stays the first, then five more of ours). Renders: docs/captures/u17_menu/. Checks: test_menu_dread 6/6 (new), test_squad_panel 9/9 (its no-numbers test now checks the Scrip), test_menu_scene 9/9, test_ui_polish_t218 9/9, test_horror_layer 10/10; check_scripts 912 / 0 failed; smoke_menus PASS (10 screens), smoke_coop PASS.

T-244, 2026-11-14, the owner: "ok take over the cutscenes add what it has and push and merge everything". A new `cinema_horror` grade (`data/look/cinema_horror.json`) for every LAZARUS cut in the theatre and the campaign (`LookGrade.cinema_id`); explicit `reel:` replays keep the archive film. **The campaign now plays the 3D scenes instead of the archive reels** (`cinema_reels.json` in_game false; reels stay replayable from the Archive). Close-focus depth of field with focus pulls (`focus_to`), the shroud, vessel, vault and puddle craft (`cinema_craft.gd`, nachtglas / puddle / relic_liquid / shroud shaders), set passes on the ward, crypt, village, vault, rear, C-47 and heart, and CinemaReel frees detached cached stages on exit. T-245, 2026-11-14, the owner: "MAKE TRUDI BE ABLE TO GET PERKS, ALLOW THEM TO ALSO HAVE WONDER WEAPONS, THAT GOES FOR ANY OTHER BOTS LIKE HER". T-177 had switched companion tonic buying on, but a companion only shopped in the 10-15 s break near its escort with a pocket starting at 0, and every companion refused the wonder class (crate weight 0). Now: a joined companion drinks mid-round from its own Scrip when the fight around it is calm; Talk offers GET YOURSELF A TONIC on your Scrip (Jakub's errand reused); a banner and squad-panel icons show it; tonics are lost on a down as for players. Wonder weapons can be handed over on the give-gun card and the box is pulled from its own Scrip; GIVE A GUN swaps only the gun in its hands. Found on the way: since T-135 BotBrain setup reset `bot_damage_mult` to 1.0 after the companion set 0.6, so companions always took full damage; now 0.6 without an armour tonic, 1.0 with Ironhide Stout. Bots (companions and squad bots) fire real Ashlight / Elmsfeuer bolts (no self-splash). Details: docs/u18/t245_companion_perks.md.
