T-250, 2026-11-14, follow-up to T-248: the only red unit test on master was `test_sight_clearance` (bren / bren_up: Body +54 mm above the sight line). A vertex scan showed the culprit was not the ladder rear sight but the OGA model's carrying handle, a slanted hoop standing raised on the barrel just ahead of the magazine (its own mesh island in Body, x +-28 mm, up to 66 mm above the sight line), inside the 12 mm band left of the bore that the offset sight line looks through. The tall ladder is centred on the receiver, with the 32 cm magazine straight ahead of it, so no line through the ladder can reach the front post; the working rear sight is the small aperture ring on the ladder base's left side, where T-248 had already put `sight_rear`. Markers unchanged. Fix: a new model_fit key `fold` {Part: [{min, max, pivot, axis, deg}]} (WeaponGLB._fold_mesh: mesh islands whose rig-space bounds lie inside the box turn about an axis, normals and tangents with them, visual only, like `warp`). The Bren's handle turns -90 deg about the barrel axis at its hinge, down to the right side, as the real handle folds away from the left-offset sights. Bren feel `eye_relief` 0.34 -> 0.20 (aperture guns: Garand / Carbine 0.24, Thompson 0.12): at 0.34 the aperture was an 8 px dot; at 0.20 the ring reads, with the front post and its ears centred in the hole. ads_zoom 0.8 unchanged; no gameplay numbers touched; test TOL / HALF_W unchanged, no per-gun exception. Captures: docs/captures/u18_guns/ads/bren_ads.png and bren_fp.png (WSL).

**UPDATE 19, 2026-11-15 (T-251..T-254; the owner: "Make the textures for the maps higher quality and the models more detailed. snow sky planes tanks etc, walls make the mystery box better and the crucible more merged and immersed. expand the cellar in the first map by 75% make the crucible and power two additional doors down, spread perks randomly around the map").** Four workers, one branch each (`claude/u19-cellar`, `-textures`, `-models`, `-box-crucible`), merged to master; map00 regenerated once by the lead with all four in. The full notes with coordinates, tables and open items are in `docs/u19/t251_cellar.md`, `t252_textures.md`, `t253_models.md`, `t254_box_crucible.md`; renders in `docs/captures/u19_*`.

T-251: Map 0's cellar grows by 140 m2 on 187.4 (+74.7 %) as two rooms behind two new bought doors: the Lagering Vault (zone `LAGERKELLER`, `Door_Lager` 750) and the Dynamo Room (`MASCHINENRAUM`, `Door_Dynamo` 1000). The lever and the Crucible stand on the Dynamo Room's north wall, so power costs 3250 from the taproom (was 1500). Each new room has a loop island and a ground spawn from round 4. "Spread perks randomly" was read as: Map 0 only, 17 fixed `PerkSpot`s, the twelve machines dealt per match from `QuestManager.match_seed` (host-authoritative, NetMatch key `pl`), rules in `data/gravelight/perk_layout.json` (max 2 per zone, Second Wind one door from the taproom, cheap tonics only in the taproom), a nailed crate on each free spot, one bake-only nav box per spot so the navmesh never depends on the deal. Bot fixes that the new layout exposed: nothing behind a shut bought door counts as reachable; a door opening drops the stand-point and power-plan caches. Do not rebuild `machine_lore.json` from the markdown (rows differ).

T-252: surfaces. The shared `surface_pbr` shader gets macro variation, de-tiling and snow sparkle; baked materials follow `materials.json` at load (`MaterialLibrary.apply_runtime`, keys marked `live`). New CC0 sets (Poly Haven) for walked snow, frozen mud, the inn's plaster and Kaltenbrunn's render and rock; four sets raised to 2K. `sky_dome.gdshader` has a moon disc, a cloud deck with form and a veil; Totenhafen gets a dome. map01, map02, map04a-d regenerated (layout fingerprints unchanged). +132 MB in the repo, about +23 MB VRAM on Map 0. Do not keep the `NoiseTexture2D` entries `material_audit.sh --write-fixes` writes into `data/look/_materials.json` (they turned the taproom rug grey). Kaltenbrunn's brick is less red (held to the bible's saturation).

T-253: models, all still procedural (the rig, the event and the tests reach parts by name). The Sherman is rebuilt (44,658 tris near, 18,670 beyond 48 m; single road wheels, 13-tooth sprockets) with a paint shader `vehicle_paint.gdshaderinc`; the dive bomber has camouflage, crew, flaps and plain straight-armed crosses (`StukaPlane.CROSSES`, the owner to confirm; earlier precedent was no German markings); the six sky aircraft, `Props.cart` and `Props.crate` are rebuilt. Night readability by a measured `NIGHT_LIFT` (tank 3.2, bomber 2.2). All node names, attach points and colliders unchanged (`test_u19_models`). Open: the C-47 wreck is as dark as the night sky.

T-254: the Requisition Crate gets a locator column of light over the live spot (roof-aware), a roll with the real weapon models, a sink-and-rise move and a dressed empty spot. The Crucible probes the wall and ceiling behind it from its node transform and builds a housing into them in the wall's own material (`surround` per map in `crucible_rooms.json`; anchored coordinates like `"ceil-0.04"`); full housing on map00, map02, map04a, back-plate and flue only on map01, map04b-d (walling in would close walkable floor). Its own collider is unchanged. Found, not changed: `ArtCollider.fit` also hulls the hidden offer bar, an invisible chest-high collider in front of the Crucible on every map.

**T-256, 2026-11-15: movement feel and frame cost (the owner's brief: responsive, consistent movement at every frame rate; better frame rate in hordes, combat effects and co-op; profile first).** Branch `claude/movement-perf`, merged; the full note with tables and commands is `docs/perf/movement_perf_2026-11.md`. Movement: the body already ran identically at 30/60/144/240 FPS (60 Hz tick); the camera's recoil kick and landing dip, the viewmodel's kick, sway and inertia and the pad's turn were stepped with the frame's own time and differed above 60 FPS (recoil peak 2.07 deg at 60 against 2.77 at 240, sway 64.3 against 18.8, the pad turning the view in 25 % of frames). They now step on fixed sub-steps (`player/tick_spring.gd`, no class_name on purpose) and give the 60 FPS result at every rate; no tunable moved. Probe: `tools/move_rates.sh`. Frame cost (headless CPU, round 15, 24 zombies, squad, Map 0): horde 10.10 -> 8.74 ms, worst 37 -> 19; live round 8.96 -> 8.29 ms, worst 90 -> 30. Confirmed causes fixed: the chase's wedge check asked the navmesh every tick (now 5 Hz; a wedged zombie recycles at 3.0-3.2 s), the leap-hop's rays ran when no hop was due, severed pieces were cut in script at the first loss (now cut on a worker at load), bodies a kit can spawn were not all pre-built, the Inn's risen people and relief-squad bodies were merged in the tick they appeared (now at load / on workers; a squad arrives up to 0.6 s after round start). Load +0.55 s on Map 0. The owner reported 15 FPS at rounds 13-15 on an RTX 5090: the game's own CPU work there is 8-9.5 ms a frame and only the zombies grow with the round; the local-model bot is async, asks at most once per 8 s and cannot explain it on the CPU (a GPU dip of about a second per answer is plausible); the session coincided with other workers' Godot and WSL jobs on the same PC. Not met: "no frame over 16.6 ms" (left: bot first-shot and think spikes 8-39 ms, a director spawn tick 18-25 ms, a survivor's rise 17-25 ms, first-use effects). Not measured: GPU FPS on the owner's hardware (bench command in the note), a networked client.

**T-255, 2026-11-15: fixes after the owner's first play of UPDATE 19 ("this perk location is a bad spot, also while the lights and power are off the room in the cellar is too dark").** Branch `claude/u19-fixes`, note `docs/u19/t255_fixes.md`, frames `docs/captures/u19_fixes`. T-251 stood every perk spot 1.0 m clear of its wall for the fine print on the back; the taproom's crate so stood in the middle of the west walk. A spot can now be flush (`"flush"` in `PERK_SPOTS`, `PerkSpot.flush`; the fine print hangs on the cabinet's side): 16 of 17 are, the taproom's moved into the north-west corner (1.1, 0, 0.85), `old_vault_south` to the Old Cellar's east wall. Käthe's drawing hung 1 m in front of its wall since T-241 moved the hall wall (`story_props.json` holds plain coordinates); it and the Room 3 letter are back on their surfaces, with a new mount check (`story_prop_mount.gd`; 21 items on other maps reported, not verified). The cellar before the lever: two visible hanging lanterns per room, no fill light (Forward+ mean luma, lever off: Lagering Vault 3-15 -> 18-31, Dynamo Room 2-6 -> 16-23, bay 2.4 -> 18.7; the lever still about doubles it). Found on the way: `MapRoot._spawn_intel` asked the navigation map for points before it had regions, so every map's random Intel lay at (0, 0, 0) (the blue glint in the owner's screenshot); fixed. Map 0 regenerated, minimap rebaked.

**T-257, 2026-11-15: blood and gore rework (the owner: "re work the blood and gore make it scarier").** Branch `claude/gore-rework`, note `docs/gore/gore_rework_2026-11.md`, frames `docs/captures/gore_rework`. Before: per-hit particles only (red cubes, a bright mist), no mark left on the world by a hit. Now `GoreFx` (owned by `Effects`, data in `vfx.json` "gore"): spatter, pools, runs down walls, boot prints and crawler smears as instances of one MultiMesh with ageing in the shader; per-weapon-class hit profiles with exit spatter on what is behind the body; a spurting neck at head loss; torn stumps with a bone end; faint lens drops and blood on the first-person hands; four synthesized wet sound keys. Marks land only on plain static world geometry (the bullet-hole rule). Caps, oldest first: spatter 192, pools 28, runs 48, prints 64, smears 40, scaled by `vfx_quality`, halved on Compatibility, thinned from 12 live zombies. Presentation only. Honest state: clear gain on headshots and in lamp-lit rooms; no visible gain for shotgun kills, on snow, or rifle body shots near a wall; prints, smears and stumps never seen in a frame; dried near-black blood does not read on the Inn's dark boards, so the horror layer's kill decal (T-192) still lies under the new pool at Gore full. Frame cost not shown either way (runs spread by up to 1.8 ms on the shared machine). Do not regenerate `audio.json` with a full `gen_audio.py` run.

After both merges (lead): `test_cinema_lazarus` waits by the wall clock (GUT's `wait_seconds` sat for the rest of the hour in two full runs after a reel ended; cause unknown); `smoke_jakub` keeps the relief squad out (a patrol on the map shot the breakout zombie before Jakub's shovel reached it; in the game the scene carries on without the kill, open for the owner); `ReliefSquad._hand_out_prey` no longer hands out a zombie with a `forced_target`. Known intermittents on master: `test_movement_perf :: test_a_body_asked_for_before_its_merge_is_in_is_merged_on_the_spot` failed once in a full run with engine errors from the dummy renderer while a worker merged the same body (3 of 3 alone, clean in the next full run); `smoke_bots_shop_m0` "no squad bot did 'bench'" (fails about one run in three, before this work too).

**T-258, 2026-11-15: frame rate on the owner's RTX 5090 (the owner: "i am getting really bad fps ... CPU load is high GPU load is nothing").** Branch `claude/fps-shadows`. Measured with the owner's OK on the owner's PC (perf_marks rendered, off-screen and silent, Map 0 round 15, 24 zombies, 3 bots + Jakub, the owner's settings, logs `.smoke_logs/fps_review/`). As built: 33 FPS, 1 % low 18, worst 141 ms; headless game logic 8.9 ms a frame (unchanged since T-256). A/B: omni lamp shadows off 57, all shadows off 60, zombies and players hidden 37, animation stopped 30. Causes found: (1) 19 shadowed lamps, 12 of them cube omnis (6 shadow views each, prepared on the CPU in the game's thread), and the taproom lamps sway (`lamp_sway.gd`), so their shadows are redrawn every frame whatever moves; the Shadows setting only changed atlas sizes (GPU). (2) `GLSettings.load_settings` only read the keys of the defaults, and the individual graphics settings (`gfx_shadows`, `gfx_ao`, `gfx_gi`, ...) are not among them (`gfx()` falls back to the preset): they were saved but never read back, so every launch played the whole preset (the owner's "ultra" with SDFGI, SSIL, ultra AO, MSAA 4x + TAA) whatever they had turned down. (3) Rendering runs on the main thread after the game's work: with every shadow off one frame was still game tick 9.5 + per-frame scripts ~3 + render CPU 3.8 ms = 60 FPS while the GPU needed 7.8. Changes: `ShadowBudget` (`gravelight/world/shadow_budget.gd`, attached by `MapRoot`, re-read by `apply_visuals`): every 0.2 s the map's shadowed omni / spot lamps are ranked by how far the camera stands outside each one's reach and the nearest keep their shadow while their views fit the setting (Low 4, Medium 8, High 14, Ultra 24 views; within 20 / 30 / 40 / 60 m) with 2 m hysteresis; below Ultra the omnis use dual-paraboloid shadows (2 views); lamps a character carries and lamps whose own script turns the shadow off are left alone; Compatibility keeps WebProfile's cap. `load_settings` reads the individual graphics settings back (`test_graphics_presets` now clears them in `before_each`). `project.godot`: `physics/common/max_physics_steps_per_frame=4` (was the default 8: a slow frame ran up to 4 catch-up ticks of ~10 ms and made the next one slow; below 15 FPS the game now slows down instead). `Play.bat` starts with `--render-thread separate` (`Play.bat safe` = the old single thread; the launcher takes the same flag in its extra arguments). After, same scene, the owner's settings: 66 FPS (1 % low 36, worst 37 ms) on one thread, 92 (53, 54 ms) with the separate render thread, 88 in a live round (49, 36 ms). Part of the GPU drop (9.6 -> 2.6 ms) is the settings fix turning off what the owner had turned off; the frame was CPU-bound either way. Honest state: the separate render thread logs 10-14 "_texture_2d_update: image is null or empty" errors while a map loads (no script backtrace, source not found, no visible effect seen) and one "finalize can only be called from the render thread" at exit; dual-paraboloid shadows can show seams on low-poly geometry near the lamp; the game tick itself (zombies 3.2 ms, bots 2.0 ms per frame rendered) is the next limit; other maps not measured.

**T-259, 2026-11-22: the day (the owner: "have the map slowly become daytime one day is 24 rounds on all maps").** Branch `claude/day-cycle`. `DayCycle` (`gravelight/world/day_cycle.gd`, attached by `MapRoot`, numbers in `data/gravelight/day_cycle.json`: `defaults`, then `maps.<map id>`). One round is one hour: round 1 is midnight and is the map exactly as authored (tested), the sky greys from round 6, the sun rises in round 7, noon is round 13, sunset round 19, night again from round 21, round 25 is round 1. The hour on screen follows the round's hour at 80 s per hour, so a new round never snaps the light. How: the map's `Moon` light is the authored moon at night and the sun on an east-south-west arc by day (the swap is in the dark before first light; no second shadowed light, so the T-258 shadow budget is unchanged); ambient colour and energy, fog colour and exposure are blended over whatever DepthGrade, LookZones and LookGrade write (each frame the value found is taken as the night's unless it is the one DayCycle wrote last; LookZones and the Landscape ask DayCycle for the night's value); under a roof the camera gets 30 % of the daylight in the air, below -2.5 m none (the cellar, the mine and the vault keep their dark); the cloud domes (`sky_dome.gdshader`: `sun_k`), Thalbach's deck (`cloud_deck.gdshader`) and the searchlight shafts follow two shader globals `gl_daylight` / `gl_day_sky`, declared in `project.godot` `[shader_globals]` (0 = night, so the menu and the tools are unchanged). Presentation only: nothing reads it for gameplay, every co-op peer runs its own from the shared round. On: the Inn, Kaltenbrunn, Totenhafen (a grey storm day), Thalbach. Off, by choice, for the owner to decide: der Kranich (the flight has its own night-to-dawn clock by act round and the golden-hour bomb run), Groom Lake (authored at first light) and Mondlicht (the Moon): each has a story hour and would need its own day sky. Dev / captures: `callv:DayCycle:hold_hour:<h>`; capture sets `day_m00`, `day_m01`, `day_m02`, `day_m04a` (30 / 12 / 18 / 18 frames at 00, 06, 07, 09, 12, 18 h). Verified: `test_day_cycle` 9 tests; full GUT 2432 tests, 2431 passing, 1 risky (the known one); 21 `map0*` smokes PASS; software renders under WSL of all four maps (`docs/captures/day_cycle/`). Honest state: seen only in the Compatibility software renderer (no SDFGI, no volumetric fog), never on the owner's GPU, where the day's exposure and the sun in the volumetric fog may need a pass; the first renders showed Totenhafen and Thalbach too dark at noon (authored at exposure 2.0), fixed with their own day exposure 1.6; the inn's rooms get almost no daylight through the windows in the renders; Thalbach's story line is "the dawn that never comes" and the Inn's bell (round 9, written as midnight) now tolls in the morning: both left as they are. A fresh worktree fails 25 audio / texture tests until the git-ignored `.import` files are copied from the main checkout next to `.godot/imported`.

**T-260, 2026-11-22: the ElevenLabs bake finished (the owner: "I have a good eleven labs voice subscription now, finish any voice acting").** Branch `claude/voice-bake`. The account is on the Pro tier (600,028 characters a period, reset 2026-12-18). `tools/voice.sh bake` resumed at `fp.c3.outro.09`: 2,349 new takes (158,127 characters sent), 18 from the cache: the rest of The Fine Print and all 728 lines of Bolt & Sprocket; every group of `tools/voice.sh plan` now reads 0 to pay except 49 characters. Those are 6 lines that are a stage direction with no words (`REI_HEART_END`, `LSL_REC_DRUMMER_01`, `CSN_HUM_01`, `CSN_WHISPER_01`, `CSN_DARK_01`, `HAM_CATCH_01`: a scream, a giggle, a hum, a whisper); the API refuses empty text, so they have no take and want either a sound effect or a recording. Every take is `source: ai_eleven`; a recorded human take still wins. `tools/voice.sh test`: 52 passed. Not done: nobody has listened to the new takes; the new `.ogg` files need one headless import (`tools/voice.sh reimport`) in the checkout that plays them.

**T-261, 2026-11-22: the round-30 Sherman fights with judgement, fires its 75 mm, and a player can drive it (the owner: "Make the tank on round 30 smarter and make it use its cannon, I want the player to be able to drive the tank around if needed").** Branch `claude/tank` (a worker's), full note with the measured tables `docs/u18/t261_tank.md`. Before, measured with the new headless bench `gravelight/tools_src/sherman_bench.tscn`: in the round-30 assault it never fired (the gunner only ran once the assault was beaten off); in the assist rounds the 75 mm waited 11 s and was chosen only in the last 1.5 s (1-9 shells in 150 s). Now: it defends itself in the assault at 0.7 damage (1-6 shells and 42-77 MG rounds in about 45 s; unopposed it still loses), reload 6.5 s (11-16 shells in 150 s with the squad outside), targets scored in `ShermanRules` (`sherman_rules.gd`: a pack over a single, a burning zombie or the boss worth a shell alone, burning ones shot far out first), turret on the thickest sector of the dead, traverse 40 deg/s. Safety kept and tightened: no shell within 5 m of a player or 6.5 m of its own hull, none with a player near the line, no burning zombie set off on a player or the hull; no Scrip, no player damage. Driving: hold Use at the parked hull, one driver, no bots; the horse's actions (throttle, steer, the view lays the turret, fire = 75 mm, ads = coax), 3.2 m/s forward, pivot 34 deg/s; allowed ground is the GROUNDS horseshoe round the yard (`map00.json` `drive_zones` / `drive_boxes` / `drive_no_go`), never the yard, the stable or the inn (a parked hull or its wreck would seal a gate); navmesh, zombie paths and the hull's layer unchanged; the AI gunner stands down while a player drives; zombies under the tracks are run down (not bosses); the driver is put out before a wreck and at the start of round 40; host-authoritative in co-op, the driver's machine reports at 10 Hz and the host checks it. Verified by the worker: `test_sherman` 17 tests, full GUT 2430 tests, 2429 passing, 1 risky (known), `smoke_sherman` 74 checks PASS, both horse smokes PASS. Honest state: nothing rendered, not played by a person, co-op not run on two machines, real keys and gamepad not pressed; with the squad indoors the parked tank is still mostly blind; in the assault the cannon is a small part of the fight because the wave rises close; a client driver's hull does not slow when the host crushes a zombie; one existing bound moved (`test_assist_damage` 12 -> 14 kills a minute) and three T-249 smoke checks rewritten for the new gunner.

**T-263, 2026-11-22: the arms locker (the owner: "add a weapon locker").** Branch `claude/weapon-locker` (a worker's), merged; note `docs/u20/t263_weapon_locker.md`. `WeaponLocker`, data `data/gravelight/weapon_locker.json`: a steel two-door locker on a wall; one hold of Use stores, takes or swaps by what you carry. One compartment per player, per match, nothing in the profile (a tier III gun in round 1 of the next match would change the game far more than banked Scrip), so it is open in Classic Rules, Easter Egg Mode and online. 500 Scrip to store, free to take back (free both ways it would be a third slot for nothing; Packmule Porter sells that for 4000). Kept as they are: the weapon, its Crucible tier, attachments, finish, magazine and reserve, fire mode; no refill. The stored gun survives the down, the bleed-out and the respawn; a crawling player cannot use it; you must keep one gun on you. Map 0 only: the stable's north-east corner on the north wall (24.25, 0, 22.15), built at load from the json row like the bank branch, no regeneration and no navmesh change (measured by a probe: wall behind it, 4.7 m free in front, nearest machine 5.8 m). Host-authoritative, each player's own contents mirrored and in the state snapshot; bots never use it. Verified: `test_weapon_locker` 18 tests, `smoke_weapon_locker` PASS, `smoke_coop` PASS with a client storing and taking back, the four `map00` smokes PASS; on the merged master check_scripts 974 scripts 0 failed, full GUT 2457 tests, 2456 passing, 1 risky (known). Honest state: never seen rendered (model, stencil, card, lamp); not on the minimap; no voice line or hint teaches it; other maps have none (candidate spots in the note; a json row is all a map needs), so a gun left in it at a campaign exfil cannot be reached on the next map; the hay heap's round 6+ ground spawn is 1.9 m from where you stand at it; 500 / free / one gun is a first guess; in solo a down ends the run, so it is storage rather than insurance. A fresh worktree also needs `.godot/uid_cache.bin` from the main checkout.

**T-262, 2026-11-22: shooting from the saddle at a gallop, and tack for the horse (the owner: "allow player to shot gun while gallobing with horse, add horse upgrade").** Branch `claude/horse-upgrades` (a worker's), merged; note `docs/u20/t262_horse.md`. Before: `horse.json` `fire.max_gait` 2 blocked fire and ADS at a gallop and the gun was hidden for both hands on the reins. Now `max_gait` 3: the rider fires, aims and reloads at any gait; at a gallop the hands stay on the reins while the weapon is idle, any use of it brings it up in 0.29 s and it goes back 1.6 s after the last (a trigger press made from the reins is kept, `WeaponHolder.keep_fire_press`). The handicap is the stride: view pitch swing 6.1 deg peak to peak from the hip, 3.8 with the sights up (a trot is 1.1), and +2.4 / +1.2 deg of cone through a new `Player.carry_spread`; walk and trot are unchanged. Tack, three pieces in a fixed order at a tack chest 1.62 m east of the rail's middle (hold Use on foot, the horse tied at its rail; per match, on the horse, every rider has it; rows in `horse.json` "upgrades", `HorseRules.upgraded`): Studded Shoes 1,500 (gallop 9.5 -> 10.64 m/s, turn x1.2, hard-turn gallop 6.4 -> 7.36), Trooper's Saddle 2,000 (aim sway x0.6, gallop cone 2.4 -> 1.2 deg; carries no ammunition, so the ammo economy is unchanged), Carter's Barding 2,500 (trample 90 -> 225, speed lost 35 -> 17.5 %, cooldown 0.7 -> 0.42 s, shies at six in a crowd, was three). Shown on the model as bone attachments without colliders; the chest has no collider either, so the navmesh is untouched. Host-authoritative, a client can buy, bots never ride. Verified by the worker and again on the merged master: check_scripts 974 scripts 0 failed, full GUT 2460 tests, 2459 passing, 1 risky (known), `smoke_horse` 116 checks PASS, `smoke_horse_boss` PASS, `smoke_weapon_locker` PASS, `smoke_coop` PASS. On the merged master `smoke_sherman` failed 2 of 4 runs on one T-261 check ("the machine gun on the dead in its sight (4 rounds, 4 hits)": it asked for more than six rounds, and with the three zombies down after four hits the gun had nothing left); the check now asks for three rounds and a hit and passed 4 of 4. Honest state: nothing seen rendered or played (the swing, the draw from the reins, the tack on the model; the breast piece may clip the shoulders at a gallop); no one-hand-on-the-reins pose and no seated third-person pose; no two-machine co-op run; a client joining after a purchase is not sent the tack (the horse has no late-join state at all, T-240's included); the numbers are a first setting (`fire.view_rock`, `rock_ads_mult`, `spread_gallop_deg`).

**T-267, 2026-11-23: the menu (the owner: "what the heck is muster? ... make it more mainstream", "when you're customizing your starting pistol, you need to be able to see the pistol", "cutscenes are for the campaign").** Branch `claude/menu-rework`, notes and the full rename table in `docs/u21/t267_menu.md`. Player-facing words only (identifiers, data and profile keys unchanged): Muster -> Loadout, Field Habits -> Starting perks, Sidearm -> Starting pistol, Archive -> Collection, Dossiers -> Characters, Service Record -> Stats, Online -> Co-op, Citations -> Achievements, Commendation -> Prestige; Scrip, the Requisition Crate, the perk machines and Intel keep their names with a plain subtitle. The cutscene the owner saw in Solo came from the Solo page: its first, pre-focused card was the Campaign card; Solo now lists maps only and Campaign has its own page. `MenuModelView` (own World3D, three lights, no shadows) shows the chosen pistol with its finish and attachment and the four characters; `MenuMotion` holds every menu tween (worst press-to-settled 0.24 s, input never blocked) with a new Reduce motion switch. A Solo or Co-op load plays one clip from `assets/video/loading/*.ogv` (muted, looping, at least 3 s, skippable, fades out; never in the Campaign or the browser build; no clip = the old screen). The four clips there (inn, taproom, mine town, harbour; 15 s, 720p) were generated on the owner's Higgsfield account at the owner's request (420 credits) and are our own work. Verified: check_scripts 980/0; full GUT 2486 tests, 2485 passing, 1 risky (known); smokes match_start (new: solo plays no cutscene, campaign still does, no clip falls back), menus, transitions, coop, map00 (4), campaign, cinema_lazarus, inn_opening_paths PASS; WSL renders in `docs/captures/t267/`. Honest state: the motion, the 3D view on Forward+, the clips' picture on a GPU (the placeholder showed broken blocks in the software render, cause not established) and a real controller are unseen; the pause and game-over wording was not rendered after the change; the inn's ten-second hold before round 1 still happens in Solo (gameplay timing, `map00_opening.json`); the Luger has no real model and the Loadout now shows that.

**T-264, 2026-11-23: macOS and SteamOS (the owner: "we need to make this playable on macOS SteamOS").** Branch `claude/ports-mac-steamos`, notes `docs/u21/t264_ports.md`. Export presets "Linux" (x86_64, the SteamOS / Deck build) and "macOS" (universal, ad-hoc signed, not notarized, bundle id `org.claudezombies.termsandconditions`, macOS 11+; Intel Macs run Vulkan through MoltenVK); `tools/build_linux.sh`, `tools/build_macos.sh`, `tools/check_macos_build.py`. Three blockers fixed: the `&` of the project name broke the Mac Info.plist (`config/name.macos="CLAUDE ZOMBIES"`); pad A / B did nothing in menus because `ui_accept` / `ui_cancel` had keyboard events only (`GLSettings.ensure_pad_ui_actions`); every exported build crashed on quit (static caches and pooled nodes outliving the rendering server; new autoload `ExitSweep`). `import_etc2_astc` is on (the macOS exporter refuses arm64 without it; 325 `.import` files changed, a one-minute re-import). The WebRTC GDExtension got its macOS frameworks (same upstream release, LFS). New "Handheld" preset (FXAA, 100 % scale, Low shadows / AO / LOD, Low fog; V-Sync, cap 60 or 45) chosen on first launch by `SteamDeck=1` or the VANGOGH adapter; Apple silicon gets Medium (base, Pro) or High (Max, Ultra) with a new 3D resolution limit (900 / 1200 / 1440 lines); a saved choice is never overridden. Controller: on-screen letter keys for name / join code / LAN address, focus kept across rebuilds, right-stick scroll, new `smoke_menus_pad` with real joypad events. The separate render thread is NOT on in any export: with it the Linux build never exited on quit; it stays a launch option (`--render-thread separate`, `./start.sh fast`). The Mobile renderer was not adopted (it loses volumetric fog, fog volumes, SSAO and caps lights per mesh). Verified: the Linux build under WSL boots, loads a Solo match with its clip and exits 0, headless and on Vulkan Forward+ (lavapipe), 0 error lines; all seven maps load there with 0 shader errors; Windows re-exports and runs headless; the macOS archive passes the structure check. Honest state: the macOS build has never been started; nothing was run on a Deck or a Mac; the Handheld and Apple values are estimates; open controller gaps (the pause MAP tab swallows A / B, raw prompt labels like LEFT SHOULDER, no pad binding for lean / visor / weapon 1-3); mouse sensitivity depends on window size; no version handshake between host and client.

**T-265, 2026-11-23: the game's own cost per frame (the owner: "the frame rate still sucks at times").** Branch `claude/perf-tick`, notes `docs/perf/t265_tick_2026-11.md`. The aim (about 4 ms of scripts a frame on Map 0 round 15, no script frame over 16.7 ms) was NOT met: 9.1 ms became about 4 % less standing in the ring and about 10 % less in a played round. What changed: zombies, patrons and faces out of the camera's view animate every fourth frame and cast no foot rays (the largest gain when playing); walking bots ask the navmesh at 5 Hz, not every tick (9,177 asks -> 424); DayCycle writes only when a value changed; the sound bank is warmed from game start and a voice line heard for the first time loads on a thread (windowed game only; it starts a few frames late, 0.5 s cap); the first kill no longer draws the blood decal (26 ms) or compiles a script (14 ms) in its frame; no whole-map `find_children` during play; the column of the Returned and the idle horse rig sleep out of view. Counted: sounds loaded in the frame that played them 0 on every map; voice files 20-41 a run -> 0-1. No gameplay timing, damage, hitbox, navmesh or AI rule changed. Measurement caveat: the PC was quiet only for the first hour (other workers), so there is no clean after-table for all scenarios; the baseline survey of all seven maps and eight Map 0 scenarios is in the notes. What remains: zombies' `move_and_slide` 0.9-1.3 ms for 24, zombie animation in view about 2 ms, four bot brains 2.2-2.5 ms; hitches left: single bot thinks (5-22 ms), a ragdoll built per death, explosions in a pack. Estimate, not a measurement: a Deck-class CPU needs 16-19 ms for the tick with a full squad at round 15; the levers are the owner's (squad size on a low preset, a 30 Hz simulation, coarser animation LOD). `smoke_vo` failed 3 of 7 runs on the branch under heavy load (three different coverage checks), 3 of 3 PASS interleaved afterwards; the late voice start is not exercised by any smoke.

**T-268, 2026-11-23: the watchtower (the owner: "zombies need to be able to crawl up the watchtower ... make it taller ... 3 zip lines that go to 3 different spots ... if you go on the ladder, have the zombies be able to chase you").** Branch `claude/watchtower`, notes `docs/u21/t268_watchtower.md`. The old tower already had a climb link the dead used; what was wrong was the manner (diagonal through the deck edge, stacked on the ladder, no way down, crawlers shuttling at the foot for ever, killed climbers hanging in the air). Now a climb-link row with `line` is a ladder (data-driven, any map): one body at a time with 1.9 m between, the rest wait at the foot, up and over the edge, back down when the players leave; killed on the rungs the body falls; crawlers do not climb and are requeued after 8 s at the foot. The tower in `gen_map00.gd`: a landing at 6.2 m and the top at 12.0 m (ridge 15.3 m, a red lamp on a mast at 19.1 m), two ladders in stages, bracing above head height, a roofed top with a sandbag parapet, lanterns without shadows and one powered searchlight (the only shadowed light). Three zip lines from the top: to the hitching rail in the yard (always usable), north to the passage behind the inn, and south-west over the fence to the bank road (those two need the GROUNDS zone open: a line never skips a paid door); about 25 m, 26-27 degrees, 4.2 s, down only. Bosses: the undead horses do not climb; each siege scream now also shakes players on a ladder-served platform within 30 m for 20 damage (`horse.json` `siege.perch_damage`, 0 = off). Bots and companions still do not use ladders or zip lines. Verified: `smoke_ladders` 144 checks, a 12-minute soak on the top (rounds 2-7 all ended, about one arrival every 2 s once a queue forms, nothing left at the base), navmesh changed only round the tower and the two landing posts; costs +16,080 triangles, +8 lights, +1 shadowed light. Honest state: seen only in the software renderer (`docs/captures/t268/`); the wreck could not be reached by a line; a client's view of a climber's drop and fall is implemented but not asserted; `debug_spawn` with many bodies on one point can still launch one skyward.

**T-269, 2026-11-24: the zombies look like zombies (the owner: "what the zombies are wearing, they need to have more color. Right now they're all just gray ... they need to look like zombies now").** Branch `claude/zombie-look`, notes `docs/u21/t269_zombie_look.md`, renders `docs/captures/t269/` (before / after, same cameras and seed). Measured causes: all fourteen kit cloth colours were greys (mean saturation 0.096) and baked per kit, the skin was a cool grey with no face, each map's grade takes half the colour that is left, at night outside the figures were black, and on Map 0 six in ten walkers were soldiers in one uniform. Decided: a wardrobe in data (`zombie_wardrobe.json`, 49 outfits of three colours and an optional stripe / check / dot pattern, a weighted table per map and kit; period cloth colours, no insignia, no emblem, no real unit's colours); Map 0 is now half villagers; Mondlicht gets its own variant table (suits and lab coats; its grade stays black and white by the art bible's rule, so outfits differ there in value only). The colour reaches a zombie through per-instance shader parameters on the two shared materials (a colour slot per clothes vertex in `CUSTOM0.a`), so there is no material per zombie and no new texture; gear pieces merge onto the kit material (surfaces per walker 5.3-6.1 -> 4.4-4.8). Dead skin in four weighted kinds (pallid, grey-green, bruised, ashen) with dark veins, sunken sockets, blood on mouth, hands and clothes; the eyes keep the violet they had. A floor of light (11 % of the figure's own colour, corrected for the map's exposure) and a soft rim live in the zombie materials only: without them the outfits are invisible outside at night; the price is that the figures look a little flat there. No map light was changed. Only plain walkers are dressed; specials, the Soviet risen, bosses, the living, Jakub and the named people keep their look. Co-op: outfit, skin and the body's other rolls now come from the net id and spawn health already in the spawn event, so every peer sees the same figure (before, build and sex were rolled per peer). Standing rime on a dressed walker is scaled to 0.45 (on colour it read as mould). Not done: torn cloth. Verified on master after the merge: check_scripts 0 failed, full GUT 2533 tests, 2532 passing, 1 risky (known); smokes map00 (4), coop, zombie_anim, ladders, bots, bots_shop, bots_u10, map01 (3), map02 (3), map04 (10 of 11) PASS. `smoke_map04b` failed one check ("half his health: the cable phase"): the smoke read the Bordwart's phase two drawn frames after the hit, but the phase is set in his physics tick; the smoke now waits for it (no game change). `smoke_bots_shop_m0` 'no squad bot did bench' failed again (the known intermittent; the bench stands beside the new tower, so whether T-268 made it more frequent is open). Honest state: seen only in the software renderer; frame time on a GPU and the cost of the extra instance uniforms on the Compatibility renderer are not measured; other maps have no "before" render.

**T-270, 2026-11-24: one world on the map step (the owner: "maybe just merge this all into one map ... somehow make it feel like all the maps are just one for one ultimate map").** Branch `claude/one-world`, notes `docs/u21/t270_one_world.md`, renders `docs/captures/t270/`. Decided by the lead: the seven scenes are NOT merged into one (frame rate is the owner's first complaint, and the places are Silesia, the Baltic, an aircraft over the Atlantic, Nevada and the Moon); what is delivered is the feel. Solo's step 2 is one drawn paper war map, "ONE ROAD: Silesia to the Moon": the seven maps as numbered stops on one route (road and rail, a dashed flight line with the Kranich on it, a dotted rocket trail into a night-sky inset with the Moon), each with its name, date line, best round and egg state from the same sources the cards used; locked stops show a padlock and can take the focus so the unlock rule can be read; a side panel shows the stop's preview; pressing an open stop starts the match through the same path as the card. Drawn in code (`gravelight/ui/world_map.gd`), no textures. Left is onward (the sheet follows geography, so the story runs east to west). The co-op lobby keeps its drop-down; the Campaign page keeps its card; `MapCards` is unchanged (the loading screen reads it). Travelling between regions inside a running match is not built and stays the owner's call (three ways it could grow are in the notes, cheapest first). Verified: on the branch check_scripts 0 failed, full GUT 2546 tests, 2545 passing, 1 risky (known); on master after the merge `test_world_map` 13 of 13, `smoke_menus`, `smoke_menus_pad`, `smoke_match_start` PASS. Honest state: seen only in the software renderer on the Compatibility renderer; motion is in no still; the mouse is not driven by a test; 4:3 and ultrawide not rendered; "by road and rail" for Totenhafen to Thalbach is an invention; the coasts are rough shapes ("not to scale"); the T-267 captures of the old card page are out of date.

**T-271: the base of the map detail pass.** Branch `claude/map-detail-base`, notes `docs/u21/t271_map_detail_base.md`. Data rows and four small script changes, no generator and no scene touched. An arms locker on all seven maps. A rideable horse with rail and tack chest on Kaltenbrunn (the Minehead yard's east wall), Thalbach (the Siedlung's south wall) and Groom Lake (the lakebed behind its door); none on the harbour, the aircraft or the Moon. A raid-only mode of the Sherman event (`"tank": false`; `shelter_roof`; a map's own banners): Kaltenbrunn round 40 with a dive bomber on the locomotive, Totenhafen round 30 with the siren only (40 is its exfil). No tank on Kaltenbrunn: its walls have no way in that data can open. Three of Map 0's sky-war kinds on Thalbach. The opening and the survivors read `<map prefix>_opening.json` / `_survivors.json`; none authored. Left off: Groom Lake's day (its start hour would put the authored light out in round 1), zip lines (the cable and posts are generator dressing), round-numbered events on the Map 4 acts. Seen by nobody: all checks are headless. Lead's notes at the merge (2026-11-24): on master after the merge check_scripts 995 scripts 0 failed; full GUT 2560 tests, 2558 passing, 1 risky (known), 1 failing: `test_transitions.test_preload_next_is_idempotent_for_every_target` with engine errors 'Parameter "t" is null / "texture" is null' during the threaded preload; alone it passed 3 of 3, so it is logged as an intermittent under load, not fixed. Smokes map_detail (151 checks), weapon_locker, sherman, horse, horse_boss, menus, menus_pad, coop PASS. Open for the owner: the raid's voice lines on Kaltenbrunn and Totenhafen are Map 0's ("proceed to the cellars"), the Groom Lake horse wears winter dressing, the raid's bursts throw snow on the quay. `smoke_bots_shop_m0` 'no squad bot did bench': 1 of 3 PASS on the commit before the watchtower (cdd4075c) and 1 of 3 on master, so T-268 did not change it; the bots reach the bench's stand (the diag lines say so) but often do not choose it inside the smoke's window: a weak check, left as it is.

**T-266, 2026-11-24: the render load of every map (the owner: "make this playable on macOS SteamOS. the frame rate still sucks at times").** Branch `claude/perf-render`, notes `docs/perf/t266_render_2026-11.md`, sheets `docs/captures/t266/`. Counted, not timed (the PC was shared and rendered runs are software rendering): a headless frustum census of every capture camera and a rendered census of each map's heaviest views, before and after from one loaded map (`RenderTune`, `perf_marks --t266-ab`). Found: ShadowProxies (T-070) had silently stopped merging since the maps moved to `surface_pbr` ShaderMaterials (Map 0 at High, front road: 8,524 draw calls, 5,806 in shadow passes); props draw into shadow maps per surface (a tonic machine body is 22); nothing culls behind walls; the Shadows setting never touched the sun; no lamp had a draw distance. Decided, all runtime passes attached by MapRoot (generators and scenes untouched, `-- --t266-off[=...]` plays without them): ShadowProxies accept vertex-static opaque ShaderMaterials, far-cut batches and ViewCells groups across all batch containers; PropShadows gives multi-surface static props one shadow-only child; on Low / Medium only: OcclusionWalls (the maps' own large wall triangles as occluders for the player's camera), the sun 2 cascades 40 / 55 m with small casters dropped, and lamps fading out past 36 / 56 m. High and Ultra draw the same picture (sheets: weapon sway and snow differ only); occlusion stays off High until someone walks every map on a GPU with `--occlusion`. Map 0, four heaviest views, round 15: High 6,862 -> 4,074 draw calls a frame (shadow passes 4,579 -> 1,776), Low 5,018 -> 1,849. Price: shadow triangles on High up 15-20 % (cells, no LODs on shadow children): right for a CPU-bound frame, possibly wrong for a weak GPU at High. Verified on master after the merge: check_scripts 1004 scripts 0 failed, full GUT 2589 tests, 2588 passing, 1 risky (known); smokes map00 (4), map01 (3), map02 (3), map04a-d with worlds (8), map_detail, coop, menus, menus_pad PASS. Not done (usage limit): the last two Low census runs, a Totenhafen A/B/A control, the other maps' rendered tables. Open: no frame rate on any GPU, Deck or Mac (commands in the notes); the Compatibility renderer gets dual-paraboloid omni shadows it does not support (T-264's WebProfile; 2,534 errors in one run); the colour pass on High, many-material props (an atlas per tonic machine), per-room light culling for the Inn, Kaltenbrunn, Thalbach and Groom Lake.

**T-266 follow-up, 2026-11-24: cube omni shadows on the Compatibility renderer.** T-139's WebProfile and T-258's ShadowBudget (below Ultra) set omni lamps to dual-paraboloid shadows; Godot 4.7's Compatibility renderer has no such mode (T-266's capture run logged 2,534 "Dual paraboloid shadow mode not supported" errors), so those lamps cast no shadow in a Compatibility build. Now on that renderer both keep cube shadows (`ShadowBudget.cube_only`, from `GLSettings.is_compat()`), and ShadowBudget prices a cube at 6 views, so on Low (4 views) no omni keeps a shadow there and on Medium one does; spots are unchanged. Forward+ (desktop, Deck, Mac) is unchanged. Test: `test_shadow_budget.test_compatibility_keeps_cube_omni_shadows`; `test_web_profile` updated. Not seen rendered.

**T-273: Totenhafen's map detail pass (the geometry step).** Branch `claude/detail-map02`, notes `docs/u21/t273_map02_detail.md`, renders `docs/captures/t273/`. Round 30's raid opens with a dive bomber after all: its landmark is the patrol boat V 1507 (a Vorpostenboot, `DetailMap02.patrol_boat`) moored 20 m off the parapet, `stuka_target` its wheelhouse, its fire shown at the hit (`ShermanEvent.stuka_hit_show`), the raid's own banner and Operator lines (`OPR_RAID_M2_WARN_01` / `_CLEAR_01`, not Map 0's cellars), a stone burst on the concrete quay (`AirRaid.burst_fx` kinds; defaults unchanged for Map 0 and Kaltenbrunn). Drawn ladders at the three catwalk climb links with the players' LadderClimbs on the zombies' lines (now ladder links: one at a time, they drop back down; the east link moved off the east stair's foot to z 72, the north to x 15 and the west to z 55.5, clear of the tide's catwalk spawns). A signal balcony outside a new door in the tower's south wall and one zip line from it, 12.3 m at 27 degrees down to the east apron (no chain: it lands in the Pen Hall the tower is reached through); a second line from a 4 m catwalk was not worth a post. The perk deal on Totenhafen: twelve spots for the seven machines (the seven old places and five new wall spots found with locker_spot's scan), `perk_layout.json` `map02`, the scene saved with the seed-0 deal, the minimap rebaked. Not built: the round-1 opening (dockworkers flee the cannery), the sailors in the torpedo store and the optional U-boat surfacing (what each needs is in the notes). Cost: +31 meshes at most, +22 k triangles, one hidden unshadowed light. Lead at the merge: on master after the merge check_scripts 1005 scripts 0 failed, full GUT 2600 tests, 2599 passing, 1 risky (known); smokes map02 (3), map_detail (155 checks), ladders, sherman, weapon_locker, coop, map00_alive PASS. The round-1 opening and the torpedo-store survivors were not reached; the worker continues on them.

**T-274: the map detail pass on the Fernflug acts.** Branch `claude/detail-map04`, notes `docs/u21/t274_map04_detail.md`. The perk deal on all four acts (Thalbach 15 spots for 9 machines, Kranich 14/9, Groom Lake 16/10, Mondlicht 13/9; the old places moved back onto their walls; start / near from each act's zone graph; no outdoor spots), with gen_map00's audit in `gen_map04_lib.gd` keeping a blocked scene unsaved, and the mapdata POIs re-read headless (`refresh_mapdata_pois.tscn`). Reactive props on every act (door leaves, curtains, glass, loose papers, drips, salt and regolith dust; LoosePapers new), Kranich's decompressions taking papers and glass out (DecompReactive) and its slack cables swinging. Groom Lake: a zip line from the radar perch to the motor pool lane (50 m, 18 degrees, chained until the motor pool opens; T-271's line inside the radar yard would be 33 degrees), a buckskin horse on a rail without snow (horse rows' `rail_dress` and `HorseMesh.COATS`, defaults unchanged); its own day left off (no judged render).
