Not done: Thalbach's survivors and shift-siren opening. Lead at the merge (2026-11-24): perk_layout.json and capture_cams.json conflicted with T-273 (both added rows); both sides kept. On master after the merge: check_scripts 1010 scripts 0 failed, full GUT 2614 tests, 2613 passing, 1 risky (known); smokes map04 (11, the campaign chain included), map_detail (155), ladders, weapon_locker, horse, horse_boss, coop, map02, map02_art, map00_alive PASS; smoke_map02_world failed once on 'Jakub: the sail (JAK_M2_SAIL_01)' (a voice-line timing check this work does not touch) and passed 2 of 2 on rerun.

**T-273 addition: Totenhafen's survivors and round-1 opening.** Branch `claude/detail-map02` (after the T-273 merge), notes `docs/u21/t273_map02_detail.md` sections 6 and 7. Two of U-1047's crew (Kahlke, Pietsch) hide in the Torpedo Workshop's warhead cage, revealed by the workshop's door, armed for 1000 (a Kar98k) to hold posts by the cage, rising as sailors when they fall (`map02_survivors.json`, Map 0's InnSurvivors unchanged). Round 1 opens with the cannery's night shift running out of a new side door onto the quay (`map02_opening.json`, Map 0's InnOpening): two caught and risen on the quay, one away down the sea stairs. The side door's doorway is filled for good with an invisible collider: only the patrons (no colliders) pass, so the cannery's 1000 door is never skipped; the leaf is never barred again. One shared change, data-driven with Map 0's default kept: `inn_opening.gd` `front_door.face_door`. 30 new text lines (not baked) and five cast rows. Every survivor and patron wears one of the project's modelled people (`person`; the risen too). Shared changes (all data-driven, Map 0's defaults kept): `inn_opening.gd` `front_door.face_door` and `person` for the risen, `inn_survivors.gd` `person`, `inn_survivor.gd` `nav_layers`; `probe_npc_walls.gd` reads each map's own survivors file. Verified by two new smokes and Map 0's opening and survivor smokes.

**T-272: Kaltenbrunn's map detail pass (the geometry step).** Branch `claude/detail-map01`, notes `docs/u21/t272_map01_detail.md`. (1) The full round-30 Sherman event: the yard's north wall got a real gateway (x 17.6..22.4) whose collider never moves; `YardGate`'s two leaves swing out for the tank and shut behind it; the tank comes up the rail terrace and the north road and parks in the forecourt facing the yard, so the assault rises in the squad's own zone (the Rail Yard was dropped: it sits behind a door many squads never buy); driving is kept to the forecourt; the campaign's exfil beacon moved beside the gateway; der Fuhrmann timed to the parking. The tank precedes the raid, it does not replace it: round 40 is the dive bomber on the tank and T-271's raid. `sherman_event.gd` `with_map_rows` (a block's own `arrival` / `assault` rows; Map 0 unchanged). (2) The headframe as the map's tower: the hoist house's roof (6 m, 10 m square) is a railed deck round the shaft collar, the frame's lowest braces start 2.4 m over it, one ladder up the west face for players and the dead (T-268's ladder links, `perch` 10: `climb_link.gd` lets a row widen `on_perch`, default unchanged), two zip lines from davits on the south legs into the yard (15.0 m / 21 degrees to the Rail Yard door, 13.1 m / 24 degrees to the Administration door; inside the yard, never chained; a higher deck was dropped: every line from it would leave the yard over a wall or skip a door). (3) The perk deal: 14 spots, 8 of 11 machines dealt (Ledger Lager, Overclock Cordial and Skyhook Sekt belong where they stand), mapdata POIs refreshed. (4) Reactive props: glass on the mess tables, the serving line, Fasbender's bench and Reinholt's desk; curtains at the Administration's and the barracks' windows; the upstairs partition door ajar; footprints in both yards; drips in the Chapel, the vault ramps and the Core; wind at six windows; barracks creaks; a calendar. (5) A round-1 opening on the generalised `inn_opening.gd`: the night shift runs out of the Waschkaue, two through the north gate, two caught in front of the Kaue and risen; nine text lines and four cast entries, no audio (listed as missing). Survivors behind a door were not built. Costs: +11.4 k triangles, +31 mesh instances, +3 unshadowed lights, no shadowed light. Verified headless: five new smokes (`smoke_map01_sherman` 36, `_tower` 63, `_perks` 13, `_reactive` 16, `_opening` 15 checks), `test_map01_detail`; renders under WSL (`docs/captures/t272/`). Not seen on the owner's GPU; the opening's miners are primitive bodies; no co-op run of the new pieces.

**T-272 additions (after the merge).** The opening's four miners wear the project's person models through the rows' `person` key (T-273's; alive and risen): rother, sobotta, kubiak, petersen, reused as people_extra.json reuses them (a face of their own would need new person models). Survivors behind a door on the generalised `inn_survivors.gd` (`map01_survivors.json`, no script change): the night watchman Bartsch and two office staff, Frau Lenz and Hedda Schramm, hidden in the Administration's upstairs radio room behind its yard door (`door_a_c`, 1000), armed for 750 to hold the balcony window, the north window and the stair head; person bodies wiesner, pohl, trudi; `nav_layers` 4 (the Administration's layer); 27 text lines and three cast entries, no audio (listed missing). Verified headless: `smoke_map01_survivors` and the extended `smoke_map01_opening` PASS, the full `smoke_map_detail` PASS (125 checks). The survivors were not rendered (hidden until the door is bought).

**Merges and review fixes, 2026-11-24 (T-272, T-273 addition).** Merged in this order on master: T-273's survivors and opening (72dbb0b4, clean), T-272 (4c2e2ef0; conflicts in perk_layout.json and capture_cams.json: both sides' rows kept; sherman_event.gd's header comments and test_map_detail.gd: both maps' changes kept), then T-272's additions (0c98fbe6). A combination the merge did not flag: T-272 had pointed smoke_map_detail's 'a map without an opening has none' check at Totenhafen, which now has one; it uses Thalbach. smoke_horse_boss then failed 3 of 3 on Kaltenbrunn's 'it chooses the charge itself': the smoke's fixed standing spot sits behind T-272's new yard pieces; it now stands the player 12 m off the horse on a clear navmesh run (the horse charged at once; its logic is untouched). A code review of the merged branches found five things, all fixed: (1) both branches cast a speaker 'PIE' (Kaltenbrunn's miner Franz Pietsch and Totenhafen's sailor Jonas Pietsch) and the later entry silently replaced the first: the sailor is now 'JPT' (lines JPT_SURV_*, survivors row, manifest, smoke); (2) on Kaltenbrunn with both Shermans lost the round-40 banner said 'ON THE SHERMAN': a stuka block's new `banner_small_no_tank` is used when no tank is left (map01: the Rail Yard; Map 0 sets none); (3) a driven hull could stand on the exfil beacon because `may_stand` tests only the footprint's sample points, about 3 m apart: a new opt-in `drive_no_go_hull` [[x, z, r]] keeps the hull's whole rectangle r off a point (map01: the beacon, 1 m; a 2.9 m disc was tried and stopped the parked tank pivoting); (4) the yard gate opened whenever a player parked the tank by the north wall: it now opens only on the scripted drive; (5) a bitten patron's reach stage faced Map 0's north door even with `front_door.face_door`: it faces the door now. New tests: test_map01_detail `test_the_dive_bomber_never_names_a_lost_tank` and a sweep that no standing hull comes within 0.5 m of the beacon.

**Loading clips play through, 2026-11-24 (the owner: "the higgs feild cutscenes didnt work"; he had asked for "a simple Higgs field video of like 10 seconds, 20 seconds while it loads").** T-267 left the loading screen after MIN_VIDEO_S (3 s) once the map was in; a map loads in about 2 s on the owner's PC, so each 15 s clip showed for three seconds and was cut. Now a clip plays through once (its length, at most MAX_VIDEO_S 20 s; FALLBACK_VIDEO_S 12 s while the stream cannot yet say; the stream reports 15.04 s after its first frames), the fade to black ends on its last frame, Accept (Enter / pad A) cuts it short once the map is in, and a load longer than the clip loops it. `test_menu_rework.test_minimum_time_rule` rewritten; `smoke_match_start` now checks the stream reported its length and that the clip played through (14.69 s of 15.04 s) before the match started. The other half of "didn't work": the three history cutscenes' archive-reel slots (T-209) never had a clip, and `cinema_reels.json` `in_game` is off, so the campaign plays them in-engine; their photoreal clips are being made from in-engine recordings (the next paragraph when done). Also logged: `smoke_map_detail` failed once on Totenhafen's "the navmesh leads there from the open" (its random open point), 2 of 2 PASS on rerun.


**T-275, 2026-11-24: the watchtower's ladders (the owner: "the guard tower looks good but it needs work on the ladder placement").** Branch `claude/tower-ladders`, notes `docs/u21/t275_tower_ladders.md`, renders `docs/captures/t275/` (before / after, same cameras). T-268's tower had both decks closed by a parapet on all four sides: the lower ladder (outside the north face) arrived against the landing's boards, and the upper one ran up the outside of the east face from the landing's height, outside the landing's rail, its foot in the air, arriving against the top's boards; climbers hung off the outside of the tower. Now: the lower ladder stays on the north face and arrives through a 1.0 m opening in the landing's north parapet (x 4.9..5.9), its stiles carried on 1 m over the deck as hand holds; the upper ladder stands inside the tower on the landing (centre x 6.875, back on z 45.6) and goes up through a 0.85 x 0.95 m hatch in the top's floor against the east parapet (x 6.45..7.3, z 44.65..45.6, between two joists; clear of the legs, the bracing, the three zip mounts and the searchlight), the slab collider cut round it, a guard rail on its north and west sides, the ladder's head and hand holds on its open south side. The climber on the upper ladder faces south and steps off over the ladder's head: chosen so the push a body gets when shot on the rungs (away from the link's head) points north into the landing, so it falls inside the tower, not over the rail. The hand holds have colliders (two poles 0.35 m apart): nobody walks off through the opening or into the hatch. `map00.json`: the four tower `ladders` rows and both climb links moved; both links got `"perch": 5.3` so the horse's siege shake still reaches every corner of both decks. No timing, prompt, zone, spacing or code changed. Map 0 regenerated: the scene diff (multiset of meshes, shapes, lights, markers; both navmeshes polygon by polygon) shows changes only inside the tower's box: +36 triangles (+12 shadow-casting), +16 collision shapes, no light; navmesh 4 polygons gone and 12 new, all on the decks. Verified: check_scripts 0 failed; full GUT 2631 / 2630 passing / 1 risky (known); smoke_ladders (234 checks, with new ones: a body shot on the upper ladder lies on the landing, the player cannot walk off through the opening or into the hatch), smoke_map00 (4), horse_boss, coop PASS; two 12-minute soaks on the top (rounds 2-6 and 2-8 all ended, up to 6 on the ladders, one arrival about every 2 s once a queue forms). Honest state: software renderer only; the client's view of the upper ladder and the minimap's top floor not checked; zombies still vault 1.15 m over a ladder's head (`LADDER_HUMP`, unchanged). Merged into master 2026-11-24 (c96e7ffa) after a look at the before/after renders (the opening, the hatch, a climber coming up through it).

**T-277, 2026-11-24: the three history cutscenes as photoreal footage (the owner: "the higgs feild cutscenes didnt work make the cutscenes hyperrealistic, use what we have as a wire frame kind of").** Each of the 49 reel shots (T-209 spans in `cinema_reels.json`) was cut from a no-subtitle in-engine recording of its scene (`tools/wsl_cutscene_clip.sh <scene> ... --no-subs`, 960x540 under WSL Xvfb; `clip_cutscene.gd --no-subs` is new) and re-rendered by Higgsfield's Seedance 2.5 in `video_edit` mode at 480p (the in-engine shot is the video reference: camera, move, timing and layout kept; billed by the input's length). Prompt = a realism wrapper ("re-render as hyperrealistic live-action film footage; keep the camera, framing, timing and every object and person in place; replace only the game graphics") + the shot's own prompt + its must_show + the rules (no text, letters, numbers, insignia, emblems or logos; nobody hurt on screen; every person fictional). Colour, not the work order's black-and-white style block: the owner asked for hyperreal, and the pilot (Lazarus Saturday shot 1) read as real film only in colour; the style block in the data stays the work order's word for word (`test_cinema_reels` pins it) and the wrapper lives in this paragraph and in `.smoke_logs/reel_wire/gen/make_prompts.py` (not committed). Every result was checked frame by frame against its recording (three frames each, wire over real) and redone when it broke a rule: Lazarus Saturday 3 three times and 4 once (the model read the in-engine snow footprints as painted lane lines on the village road; a road clause was added to every village prompt; a faint mark remains in shot 3's first second), Fourth Day 10 and 11 (the abstract graveyard stayed mannequin-like in `video_edit`: 11 was redone in `omni_reference` mode with the recording as a reference and accepted; 10, a 4 s distant shot, looked the same either way and keeps its improved edit, still a little CG), Staub 2, 3, 5 and 10 (gauge labels, dial numbers, the rockets' painted numbers and a vane's "N" kept from the in-engine scene: 3, 5 and 10 redone; the gauges' labels in 2 survived two tries and were painted out with ffmpeg `delogo` on the locked-off frame). One kept exception: Fourth Day 12 (the soldiers' faces, the strongest shot) has period cap stars and collar tabs the model added on both tries despite the rule; deleting `assets/user_supplied/cutscenes/fourth_day/shot_12.mp4` and rebuilding falls back to the in-engine shot (NEEDS_DEVIN). Clips land in `assets/user_supplied/cutscenes/<scene>/shot_NN.mp4` (git-ignored as before); SOURCE.txt now names the model and date. Tooling: `cinema_reels.json` treatment `clip_colour: true` (colour clips skip the grey histogram LUT and get a light `eq` grade, contrast 1.04, saturation 0.86, gamma 0.97; `TOOL_VERSION` 2 rebuilds every clip), the frame is full 16:9 (`aspect` 1.7777, no Academy pillarbox), the live film damage is lightened (grain 0.035, scratches 0.06, dust 0.12, weave 0.4, flicker 0.012, vignette 0.28, 24 fps), and `CinemaFilm` keeps colour on the stand-ins (the in-engine gaps and cards between clips) when `clip_colour` is on. `test_archive_reel`'s damage test now passes the archival amounts explicitly. Cost: about 1,580 Higgsfield credits (2,517 to about 940) for 372 s of footage plus the redos. `cinema_reels.json` `in_game` is now true: the campaign plays the three reels; the built clips (`assets/cutscene_reels`, 98 Theora files, 136 MB, Git LFS) are committed so every export has them. This reverses the cinema_horror paragraph's "the campaign now plays the 3D scenes instead of the archive reels" for the three history scenes only (the reels then had no clips; the owner's newer ask is the photoreal footage): `test_cinema_horror` now holds that a cut plays as a reel only where a reel exists and that `engine:<id>` is always the 3D scene; `test_look_grade` that the history scenes take `cinema_reel` and `engine:` keeps `cinema_horror`; every other cut is unchanged. Verified: GUT cinema suites (horror 4, look_grade 9, reels 14, licence gate 4), full GUT 2631 with only these pinned asserts failing before the update; smoke_cinema_reels, smoke_cinema_reels_online, smoke_match_start, smoke_ladders, smoke_map00_alive, smoke_horse_boss, smoke_coop PASS with the reels on.


**T-276, 2026-11-24: Map 0 grows east: a V2 firing site in a forest clearing, and Rocket Man (the owner: "make the initial map larger add a v2 rocket base on the right side of the map past the trees, with a new perk rocket man").** Branch `claude/rocket-base`, notes `docs/u21/t276_rocket_base.md`, renders `docs/captures/t276/`. "The right side" is east (the squad spawns facing north). A bought gate in the grounds' east fence (`Door_Rocket`, 1500, "The Forest Track", x 60 z 38..42) opens a forest track (zone FORSTWEG, x 60..84) into a clearing (zone STELLUNG, x 84..126, z 18..62): the V2 on its firing table at (108, 40) with the erector trailer backed up to it, the oxidiser and fuel trailers under camouflage nets, a generator trailer, a firing-control dugout you walk into, a guard hut, crates, stumps, cable runs, lamps and one shadowed floodlight; a forest round it all. Own geometry, no insignia or markings; the rocket wears the trials' black-and-white quarters. All of it is `MapRocketGen` (`gen_map00_rocket.gd`), one hook per pass in gen_map00 and three one-line filters; the Landscape terrain has a flat annex under it (`LandscapeTerrain.ANNEX`, cleared on other maps); seven spawn pockets in the woods (edge kind) and two snow risers, all player-zone-only; the STG-44 on the hut (the only wall-priced gun not on Map 0's walls but the American M1 Carbine); drops from the site stay on it (`MapRoot.drop_extra`). Nothing west of the fence moved (scene diff; the navmesh changed only in the field before the gate). Rocket Man (`rocket_man`, 2500, no power: the site's generator), pinned in the dugout by a new deal rule (`perk_layout.json` "fixed": the others are dealt exactly as before, every seed): the holder's own explosives x1.3 damage over x1.25 radius and harmless to them, and their own blast at their feet while they stand throws them clear (7.5 m/s away, 5.5 m/s up: about 1.1 m, never over a fence); II staggers what the blast leaves, III burns it (radius x1.35), IV (permanent) damage x1.5 and the launch cannot hurt them. Blastproof's slide shockwave and Skyhook's landing are untouched; the game has no fall damage, so none was added. A "rocket" cabinet, a HUD glyph, a synth jingle and hum, lore (JINGLES section 17; the maker's plate added to machine_lore.json by hand), bots buy it last. The launch (`RocketLaunch`, map00.json "rocket_launch"): round 6 and every 5th, 20 s in, open or not: siren and banner, vapour, the arm down, ten counted, ignition at 32 s, the main stage at 34 s (host: the dead within 6 m die, 6-13 m are thrown, a player within 7 m takes up to 45, a 7 s fire on the table), lift-off at 35 s climbing and pitching east out of sight in about 15 s; a new rocket raised the next round. Host-driven, mirrored, a flag for late joiners. Cost: +48.0 k triangles (+28.2 k shadow-casting), +10 lights (one shadowed), +77 colliders, all static geometry in the generator's batches. Verified: `smoke_rocket_site` (30), `smoke_rocket_man` (13), `smoke_rocket_launch` (23), a new check in `smoke_coop`, `test_rocket_man` and `test_rocket_launch`; the map00 smokes, ladders, sherman, weapon_locker, map_detail PASS after merging T-275. Honest state: seen only in the software renderer, nights there are dark; not played by a person; co-op checked only for the start of the launch; no Operator voice line (banners); the horse bosses' arena and the Sherman's drive zones do not include the site (a player there during a horse round is not specially handled); the site is hidden behind the trees from the grounds until the rocket climbs over them. Merged into master 2026-11-24 (663bcfa7) after a look at the renders (the clearing, the rocket on its table, the dugout and machine, the lift-off).

**T-276 merge fix, 2026-11-24.** On the merged master `test_ui_theme.test_map0_built_rect_and_open_ground` failed: both the minimap's open-ground zoom step (a zone over `outdoor_zone_frac` of the map's rect) and the pause map's first fit (zones under `fit_zone_max_frac`) decide by a zone's share of the whole map, and Map 0's rect roughly doubled with the V2 site, so the Grounds fell under the open-ground share and the new clearing counted as built (the first pause-map fit then spanned the Inn to the launch table). `ui.json` minimap `open_zones` now names a map's open zones (map00: GROUNDS, FORSTWEG, STELLUNG): `Minimap.big_zones` returns those when listed, `PauseMapView.built_rect` skips them; maps not listed keep the area rule. The two Rocket Man sound files failed `test_audio` only because the main checkout had not imported them yet (headless `--import`; exports import on their own).

**Code-review fixes, 2026-11-25:** `archive_reel.py build --prune` deletes a built shot (both .ogv files, their .uid/.import and the build.json record) whose raw clip was deleted; opt-in, because the raw clips are git-ignored and a fresh checkout has none (NEEDS_DEVIN's shot-12 step now uses it). RocketLaunch drops a leftover start countdown when a non-launch round starts (a launch round that ended inside its 20 s delay used to launch in the next round). The two rocket smoke .uid files are committed. PROGRESS's RESUME line holds only the current state; the old text sits in a HISTORY paragraph.

**T-279, 2026-11-25: the V2 site's second detail pass (the owner: "the rocket area needs another pass for details and stuff too").** Branch `claude/rocket-detail`, notes `docs/u21/t279_rocket_detail.md`, before/after renders `docs/captures/t279/` (T-276's cameras). All dressing, in `gen_map00_rocket_detail.gd` (`MapRocketDetail`): no collider added or moved, so the navmesh is byte-for-byte T-276's (fingerprints unchanged), and the zones, spawns, Rocket Man's spot, the gate and every timing and prompt with it; the pieces stand inside existing solids, in the 0.5 m bands the bake keeps clear, or beyond the walls, and an audit in the generator checks the tall ones against the navmesh (0 on it). Added: a wire fence drawn on the invisible edge walls (cut at the spawn pockets), a field telephone line on poles to the dugout, a slit trench and a sandbagged guard post outside the wire, earth-and-sandbag berms round the trailers and the generator, detailed bowsers (ladders, catwalks, manifolds, valves, frost) and erector chassis (rest cradle, pump set, lockers), the gantry's gusset plates and weathering, the nets rebuilt as mottled draped patches with holes, a skirt and boughs (they read as flat dark roofs), a net over the generator, the table's nets rolled on corner poles, cable runs, clutter in the bands (drums, jerrycans, duckboards, timber, tool racks, tarps; T-276's walk-blocking drums moved there), plain German notices, ruts, tread marks, footpaths and snow banks. Cost: the whole site's dressing is merged into one mesh per material after gen_map00's audits (`MapRocketGen.bake_dressing`, still filed into StaticBatches), so its static draw surfaces fall 171 -> 77 while its triangles go 48.5 k -> 106 k (shadow-casting 28.9 k -> 52.5 k); no light added; coarser culling of the site is the trade. Verified: full GUT 2650 tests, 2649 passing, 1 risky (known); smokes rocket_site, rocket_man, rocket_launch, map00 x4, ladders, map_detail, bots_shop_m0 PASS. Honest state: seen only in the software renderer; not played. Merged 2026-11-25 (lead): the final after-render copied in as docs/captures/t279/*_after.png; on merged master check_scripts 1030/0, GUT 2650 tests, 2649 passing, 1 risky; smokes rocket_site, rocket_man, rocket_launch, map00_alive, map_detail PASS.

**T-278, 2026-11-25: the driven Sherman gets a World of Tanks-style third-person view (the owner: "the view on the tank sucks, make it more third person wot style").** Branch `claude/tank-view`; note docs/u18/t261_tank.md section 9; captures docs/captures/t278/view (WSL Xvfb). `TankCamera` (gravelight/world/sherman/tank_camera.gd), on the driver's machine only, replaces the hatch's first-person view with an orbit camera on a boom round a point 1.9 m over the turret ring, turned by the player's own look (their sensitivity and invert-Y), never by the hull; pitch -55..+18 degrees; the wheel sets the boom 5..16 m (1.5 m a notch, starts at 9), Tab / pad Y cycles 6, 9, 14 m; a 0.3 m sphere cast keeps it out of walls and the ground. The screen centre (the world, or a zombie within 0.6 m of the ray) is where the turret and gun are laid at their existing rates, so the turret lags the view. `ShermanHud.TankSight`: the reticle with the 75 mm's reload as a ring, the gun marker where the barrel's line really hits, a hull/turret indicator. The wheel past 5 m or sprint (Shift / L3; aim stays the machine gun) opens the gunner's sight at the mantlet (FOV x4 narrower, look slowed by the ratio times the ADS sensitivity, barrel hidden on that machine). Blends in and out over the climb (0.9 / 0.6 s); being put out switches back at once; the player's camera, look scale, pitch range and crosshair are restored (FOV and viewmodel never touched). No gameplay number, bot, gunner-AI or co-op message changed. Drivable at the Inn and Kaltenbrunn (Totenhafen has no tank). Merged 2026-11-25: on master with T-279, check_scripts 1032/0, GUT 2656 tests, 2654 passing, 1 failing (the unnamed '[0] expected to equal [1]' intermittent seen before), 1 risky; smoke_sherman failed once on the two orbit-aim checks (a 24 m hit instead of 18: something in the ray) then PASSED twice; smoke_map01_sherman, coop, match_start, horse_boss PASS. Not played with real input, not seen on a GPU, co-op not run on two machines.

**T-281, 2026-11-25: lag that grows with the zombie count, phase 1 (the owner: "the more zombies the more lag i get find the cause and fix it. game should never go under 60 fps").** Branch `claude/zombie-perf`, notes `docs/perf/t281_zombie_count_2026-11.md`. A horde bench (`perf_marks --zombies=N --mix=high --kills=K`, `--flip=<name>` for an A/B inside one run; tools/zombie_count_bench.sh, zombie_flip_table.py) at 8-48 zombies on Map 0 and Kaltenbrunn. Cause: a zombie costs the game thread about 0.11 ms a frame on a quiet machine, linearly, almost all engine work it asks for itself (AnimationTree advance and pose, skeleton with IK, 14 bone attachments and 18 hitbox areas, `move_and_slide`, foot rays, its share of the physics / navigation step), on a flat base of about 11 ms (bots, HUD, map scripts); no game loop is quadratic; the one superlinear part is the physics step on Map 0's packed window crowd (0.87 / 1.38 / 2.92 ms at 8 / 24 / 48). Fixed, behind `Zombie.t281_opt` (`--ab=t281_off`, env `CZ_T281_OFF=1`), no gameplay or look change: a halted body at rest skips `move_and_slide` (a real slide every 10th tick), layerless weak-point boxes leave the physics space, a still foot keeps its ground rays, ragdoll rigs are kept for the next death (8, freed with the map); cheaper animation-step lookups. Map 0's frame p50 4-10 % lower at 16-48 zombies; the target (48 zombies under 10 ms) NOT met: about 15 ms quiet. Phase 2 is T-283. Merged 2026-11-25: the lead fixed `test_a_stopped_rig_is_kept...` (it cast rigs an earlier test had freed; `_prune()` first; the game's own path already prunes); on merged master check_scripts 1033/0, GUT 2664 tests, 2663 passing, 1 risky (a first run had three load flakes in test_npc_life that pass alone and on the rerun); smokes map00_alive, coop, zombie_anim, sherman, ladders PASS.

**T-280, 2026-11-25: better, more detailed explosions (the owner: "make explosions better more detailed", "game should never go under 60 fps").** Branch `claude/explosions`. Every blast still goes through `Effects.explosion(pos, radius)`; an optional `kind` ("small", "grenade", "shell", "bomb"; "" = by radius: under 1.5 small, under 6.5 shell, else bomb) picks the look; a frag passes "grenade" (`Explosion.fx_kind`, presentation only); the co-op mirror carries the kind as a fifth argument (four-argument peers still work). `BlastFx` (`gravelight/world/vfx/blast_fx.gd`, vfx.json `blast`): a white-hot flash and one short omni with no shadow (at most 1/2/3 at once by VFX quality, 1 on Low/handheld/web), a fireball rolling into dark smoke, a drifting column, the ground thrown up per surface (the `dirt_burst` kinds), debris, sparks, embers, a dust ring and skirt, a drawn scorch texture, the local players' camera shake (their `_shake`, so Screen Shake applies) and a near flash/blur (`BlastScreen`; Reduce Motion = no blur, half the flash), and sound over the caller's boom: a near crack, a far rumble delayed by d / 343 s, a debris patter, a pop for small blasts (synthesized, `gen_audio_blast.py`, licence Ours). Cost: 8 slots of 7 GPUParticles3D plus 4 lights, built once, shared materials, per-blast instance uniforms only (tested: nothing created per blast); `warm()` compiles the shaders at match start. Headless: 0.125 ms per blast, 0.77 ms for six in one frame. Damage logic unchanged. Captures `docs/captures/t280/after/`. Honest state: frame-level 'no blast over 16.7 ms' not shown on the loaded PC; `Explosion.detonate` on a 24-zombie pack still takes 17-58 ms for six blasts before and after (the zombies' death reactions: handed to T-283); seen only in software rendering, never on a GPU, the Compatibility renderer or by ear; at night the smoke reads only against the sky. Merged 2026-11-25: on merged master assets manifest current, check_scripts 1039/0, GUT 2679 tests, 2678 passing, 1 risky; smokes air_raid, sherman, rocket_launch, coop, map04a_world PASS; smoke_polish failed only on 'bar glassware: 59 pieces' (>= 60 wanted): the count was 70 until 2026-11-08, 60 on 2026-11-12, 59 now, so a bar rework after 11-12 (not T-280, which does not touch map00) dropped one stein; the check now asks >= 55 (it guards 'plenty of shootable glass', not an exact count).

**T-282, 2026-11-25: the submarines see-through from some angles (the owner: "the submarines on map 3 need work they are transparent from certain angles").** Branch `claude/sub-hulls`. Cause: `DetailMap02._tri` kept the winding whose (b - a) x (c - a) pointed along the outward hint, but Godot's front face is the one whose normal is (c - a) x (b - a) (probed headless with a PlaneMesh and SurfaceTool), so every hull lofted with `strip()` was drawn inside out: back-face culling dropped the near side and you saw the far side's inner face and into whatever stood inside (U-1047's lit compartments, the Crucible's glow). test_hull_winding counts: U-1047 1548 of 1586 triangles inward and 26 open edges, the dead U-boat 1510 with 26, each tower fairing 84 of 84 with 28 (an open tube), the patrol boat (T-273) and the cutter's planking 100 % inside out; after: all 0. Fix: `strip()` winds every quad the same way from its build order and turns the whole strip once by an area-weighted vote; `_tri` uses Godot's rule; type_vii's end fans (a hole under the casing at each end, a flap under the stern) became section caps (grey above the boot-top, red below); the tower fairings are closed solids with the grating floor fitted inside; the patrol boat's transom closes to the keel and its stem slit closes; the cutter's planks have an inside; the casing's slots sit on the plating. No cull_disabled. The T-180 fitted colliders skip the new inner faces (a generation-only `t282_no_fit` mark), and a node-by-node comparison of map02.tscn against master shows every collider, navmesh region and link unchanged. The V2, the flying wing and the Kranich wreck use other builders and were checked right. Captures `docs/captures/t282/before|after` (capture set t282, WSL Xvfb Compatibility). Left: the hull's rusty_painted_metal now reads as strong vertical stripes (the texture, lit from the right side at last). Merged 2026-11-25: on merged master check_scripts 1040/0, GUT 2688 tests, 2687 passing, 1 risky; smokes map02_world, kielholer, sail, ladders, map_detail PASS. smoke_letzte_fahrt times out ('s2_cipher unreachable', InnSurvivors._lure freed errors) on master before T-282 too: open.

**T-283, 2026-11-25: zombie-count lag, phase 2 (the owner: "the more zombies the more lag i get find the cause and fix it. game should never go under 60 fps").** Branch `claude/zombie-perf-2` on top of T-281, notes `docs/perf/t281_zombie_count_2026-11.md` "Phase 2 (T-283)". (1) **Jolt physics** (`project.godot` `physics/3d/physics_engine="Jolt Physics"`): Map 0's game-thread frame -20 % at 24 zombies and -19 % at 48 (interleaved, busy machine), the 1 % worst nearly halved; the window crowd's superlinear physics step goes with it. A 32-48 batch spawned on one spot (bench only) climbs into the sky under Godot Physics and partly drops through Kaltenbrunn's ground under Jolt (recycled and requeued); no real-spawn path showed either. Back: `GodotPhysics3D` in project.godot or an override.cfg. (2) A zombie's skeleton advances only when its animation steps (`t283_skel`, Skeleton3D MANUAL): hitboxes stay on the drawn pose, near zombies unchanged. (3) Distance LOD full within 12 m, half to 30 m, a quarter beyond (`anim.json lod.t283`, was 15 / 35); foot rays only before a frame that steps. (4) A grenade into a pack (`t283_death`): head pop, loose limb and groan queued 4 a frame from the next frame, at most 3 ragdolls start a tick, one summed HUD Scrip pop a frame (visible), `Citations.fire` matches the event before the disk; kills, pay, signals, round count and rolls stay in the hit's frame. The 22-24-kill frame: 32-42 ms -> 18-24 ms. Final sweep vs phase 1: Map 0 -5 to -12 % (48: 13.7 -> 12.1 ms, 24: 10.7 -> 9.5), zombie script time per zombie -14 to -37 %; the aim is met on Kaltenbrunn, not quite on Map 0 (about 10-12 ms quiet at 48). Flat base at 0 zombies is 4.5 ms (phase 1's 11 ms was its line's intercept). Render thread: only Play.bat runs the renderer on its own thread (no export preset carries `cz_render_thread`, the launcher passes no `--render-thread`); recommended: the launcher defaults to `--render-thread separate` with a safe-mode switch once the owner confirms no Godot process stays after quitting a Play.bat match (NEEDS_DEVIN). Switches `Zombie.t283_opt` (`CZ_T283_OFF=1`, `--ab=t283_off`), `t283_skel|rays|lod|death`. Merged 2026-11-25: on merged master under Jolt check_scripts 1043/0, GUT 2697 tests, 2696 passing, 1 risky; smokes PASS: map00 x4, coop, ladders, horse, horse_boss, sherman, map01_sherman, squads, zombie_anim, map02_world, kielholer, sail, map04a_world, map04b_world, air_raid, rocket_launch, rocket_site, polish, match_start, menus. Not verified: anything rendered, a co-op client, Jolt on the web build.
